*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              1&2

A sanyaye matashiyar budurwar ta shiga dakin cikin yanayin damuwa, kayan jikinta sunyi wuluk saboda datti duk sun tsufa, kyakykyawar ja idan kayi musu zasu iya yagewa, kafarta kamar wacce ta shekara bata ga ruwa ba, hijabin jikinta kamar ankwato shi daga bakin kura, jingi a tayi da kyauren dakin nasu wanda tuni ya jima da karyewa, cikin karyewar zuciya mahaifiyarta take tambayarta.
        " farida yanxun ma ba'a dace ba ko"
Dan jim kadan tayi sannan tace, " wallahi inna naira biyar ban samo ba, duk gidan dana shiga neman taimako sai dai suce nayi hakuri, ga dai danyar taliya guda daya da naje wucewa ta wani layi na samu ana rabawa nima na  tsaya aka bani " ta karasa maganar tana mikawa mahaifiyarta taliyar.

    " to ay shikenan, Allah ya rufa mana asiri, dama malam nake ji kar ya tashi ba abinda zaisa abakinsa, amma hakan ma mun godewa Allah, zauna inje ingani ko zan samo mana abunda zamu dafa ko da mai muci " mahaifiyar tata ta fada.

     Farida zama tayi cen kusa da mahaifin nata, wanda yake kwance kan wata yaloluwar katifa wacce bata da maraba da tabar ma duk ta sid'e, har marmashewa take, tana kade masa kudaje, cikin zuciyarta tana tausaya musu irin 'kangin rayuwar da suke ciki.
Innar farida na fita rasa inda zataje tayi, saboda duk gidajen da zata je kowanne da kudin bashinsu akanta, gidan sahura ne ya fad'o mata, kai tsaye gidan ta nufa da sallama ta shiga cikin gidan, sahura najin sallamar innar farida tayi kicin-kicin ta tsuke fuska, ciki-ciki ta amsa mata sallamar kamar wacce akayiwa dole.

       Gefe innar farida ta samu ta zauna cikin sanyi jiki tana fadin, " sahura mun wuni lafiya ya yaran "
     " lafiya, yara kuma gasu " sahura ta fada tana dauke kai gefe.
          Innar farida tace, " Dan Allah sahura dama zuwa nayi ki taimaka dan girman Allah ki bani icce dauri daya wallahi zan hado miki da wadancen kudin "

     Kallan tsaf sahura tayi mata sannan tace, " haba sadiya yanxu bakiji kunyar zuwa karbar wani iccen ba, karki manta kudin da nake binki sun doshi wata uku fa, kullin sai dai akarawa barno dawaki, gaskiya na gaji wallahi wancen kudin ma naga alamar bazasu fito ba kije na yafe miki amma nida ke ciniki ya daina shiga tsakanin mu "
       Innar farida kamar zatayi kuka ta tsugunna tana rokon sahura, " dan Allah sahura ki taimaka mun wallahi bani da inda zanje da ya wuce nan, ki taimaka sahura kamar yanda Allah ya taimake ki " innar farida ta karasa maganar tana zubda kwallah.
      Sahura bata ce uffan ba fuuuuu ta tashi taje ta dauko wa innar farida icce  kullin hamsin ta shuro mata, tana hada mata da kananan maganganu alamar gunguni.
        Godiya innar farida ta shiga jerowa kamar wacce aka bawa kyautar gida, ta dauki itacen ta wuce gida.

       Kai tsaye gida ta wuce tana shiga inda tabar farida anan ta koma ta same ta, farida na ganin mahaifiyarta ta taso ta tarbe ta, wuta ta fara korarin hura, ledodi da yayi ya tadinga hadowa harta hura ta dora tukunyar girki.
      Mahaifin farida na kwance tari ya tirnike shi, ruwa innar farida ta dauko ta tallafo kanshi tana kokarin budewa ta bashi, da kyar yayi kurba daya ya kuma kwarewa sai bayan wani lokaci sannan tarin ya sake shi, shigowa dakin farida tayi cikin hadin baki suka fara jerawa mahaifinta sannu.

          Da ido ya bisu da kallo jikinsa na rawa bakinsa na karkarwa yace, " sadiya nikam na sare rai da wannan ciwon nawa, sai dai kumun fatan cikawa da imani, Allah ya tallafi rayuwarku "
     " haba malam karka dinga fadar haka, kana sare mana gwiwa malam kasan cuta ba mutuwa bace, anyi yafi haka ma Allah ya baka lafiya " cewar inna.
      " kayya sadiya inaji ajikina wannan ciwon bazai barni ba......... "
     Inna ce ta katse shi, " a'a malam ka daina fadar haka kana karya karyamun zuciya "

    " Baba insha Allah bazaka mutu ba, inka mutu dawa zaka barmu nida inna? " farida ta fada tana goge hawayen daya gangaro mata.
    Tari malam ya karayi sannan yace, " farida ko yanzu ba ahannuna kuke ba, kuna hannun Allah dan haka idan ma na mutu zan barmu ahannun Allah " inji malam.
      " Insha Allahu  ma zaka samu lafiyarka malam " cewar inna.
        Dan jinjina kai malam yayi yace, " Allah yasa "

    " Farida dubo tukunyar cen ko ta tafasa, inta tafasa sai ki debi taliyar ki zuba " inna tace.
        Tashi farida tayi ta fice daga dakin.

       Malam ne ya dan dago da kai yace, " sadiya ya akayi kuka samu abin girkawa? "
      " awani gida farida ta karbo mana sadaka " inna ta fada.

     " sadiya ki tsaya tayin daka akan tarbiyyar farida, nasan kina yi iya bakin kokarinki, amma ki kara kinsan duk fadin garin nan bamuda wasu yan uwan bare ince ko wani zai tallafa miki ta hanyar tarbiyantarwa, sadiya na bar miki Amanar farida duniya da lahira kiyi iya bakin kokarinki wajen ganin kin dora ta akan hanya mafi inganci, wasiya ta gaba da zan gaya miki itace, idan yarinyar nan ta samu mijin aure ki sanar da mutanen garin mu dukda nasan zaki samu kalubale daga garesu, amma ki kauda kanki mutumcin farida zaki nemo tunda kinsan mutumcin kowanne yaro dangin ubansa, idan bana raye kinga bazai yuwu ace kin aurar da farida ke kadai ba, watan wata rana dole agoranta mata, nasihata daku ta karshe itace, kuji tsoron Allah ku kyautatawa wanda ya kyautata muku, domin dan halak shi ke nuna halarci, wanda ya cuce ku  kubarshi da Allah, Allah zai isar muku Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarku ya duba lamarinku " malam ne yake yiwa inna wasiyya.

Gaba daya jikin inna yayi sanyi da maganganun malam, hawaye ne ya gama wanke fuskarta cikin sanyin jiki hade da kuka tace, " malam duk naji abunda ka fad'a kuma insha Allahu zanyi iya bakin kokarina akan tarbiyyarta, malam cuta ba mutuwa bace da ikon Allah zaka samu lafiyarka  "
    Farida ce ta shigo dakin ganin yanayin da mahaifiyarta take ciki yasa jikinta yin sanyi, gefe ta samu ta zauna tana karayiwa mahaifinta sannu.
   "  Farida Allah yayi miki albarka, farida kiji tsoron Allah kiyi biyayya ga mahaifiyarki kiyi koyi da kyawawan dabi'u, ki kare mutumci ki dana mahaifiyarki Allah ya tsare miki imaninki aduk inda kike " malam ya fada yana lumshe ido.
      Amsawa mahaifin nata tayi cikin wani yanayi tana karewa mahaifinta kallo.
Tun lokacin da mahaifinta ya kwanta jinya bai taba doguwar magana irin ta yau ba, tabbas duk wanda ya ga yanayin da malam yake cikin yasan yana jin jiki, mutuwa da rayuwa ta Allah ce amma ba wanda zai bawa malam awa guda ayanayin da yake ciki.
        Jugum sukayi bame cewa wani uffan kowa da abinda yake ayyanawa azuciyarsa.
       Sauran farau-farau inna ta dauko ta tallafo malam tana bashi, kurba daya yayi ya dauke kai, ta maidashi ta kwantar.
      
    Taliyar da farida ta dafa inna ta zubo musu ko mai babu gishiri kawai suka barbad'a suka ci ta a haka.
          Farida ce ta kalli mahaifiyar ta tace, " inna yanxu haka zamu kyale baba cikin wannan halin, ko zamu maidashi asibiti? "
        " hmmm farida kenan idan munkai shi asibiti dame zamu kaishi, kudin da zamu tafi asibitin ma ina muka ganshi kina gani wancen zaman da mukayi a asibitin sai da na saida komai namu hatta sitira saida na saida mana su, gidan nan bamu da komai banda yan tsummo karan mu sai abunda ba a rasa ba, haka muka dinga bara da kyar muka sa mu kudin magani, kina gani aikin da za'ayi masa na ciki baifi dubu saba'in ba amma sun gagare mu ba inda bamu je neman taimako ba, karshe kina gani likitocin da suka gaji da zamanmu a asibiti sai sallamar mu sukayi, farida bamu da karfi bamu da abunda zamu iya biyan kudin aikin malam dasu, kinsan kuma halin dangin mahaifinki sarai, mu cigaba da yi masa Addua Allah ya bashi lafiya, idan kuma ciwon bana tashi bane Allah ya sa ya cika da imani. "
       Ajiyar xuciya farida ta sauke, sannan tace "Aameen ya rabbi "

        Cikin dare ciwon malam ne ya tsananta jikinsa ya rikice, inna ce akusa dashi tana shafa masa ruwa tana tofa masa addu'a, ganin jikin ya kara tsanani yasa inna tashi ta fita, gidan makocinsu mlm maude take bubbugawa sai bayan wani lokaci aka bude kofar.

   Da fitila mlm maude ya haske ta cikin mamaki yake tambayarta, " sadiya lafiya naganki da tsakar daren nan "
   Cikin shashshekar kuka tace, " ka taimaka mun dan Allah mu kai mlm asibiti jikinsa ya rikice "
     " subhanallahi muje ingani " mlm maude ya fada.

   Tare suka wuce shida inna, yanda inna ta fito haka suka koma suka sameshi cikin mawuyacin hali.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              3&4

Da hanzari mlm maude ya shiga dakin yana kokarin tallafo mahaifin farida, sam baima san abinda akeyi ba saboda baya cikin hayyacinsa, asanyaye inna tace, " mlm maude dame za'a kai mlm asibiti ga dare yayi ko gidan mlm harisu zan buga ya taimaka ya kaimu?"
    Gyada mata kai mlm maude yayi, ya fara karantowa mahaifin farida kalmar shahada, farida ce ta shiga dakin cikin hawaye ganin halin da mahaifinta yace ciki gefe ta samu ta zauna tana goge hawaye.
     " inna ce ta shigo ita da mlm harisu, kamo shi sukayi mlm maude ya rumgumoshi suka shiga dashi adaidaita sahu, farida na tsaye mahaifiyarta ta bata umarnin komawa gida saboda babu gurin da zata zauna.

Kai tsaye asibitin murtala suka wuce da hanzari likitoci suka karbeshi, sai dai kafin su fara ma bashi agajin gaggawa rai yayi halinsa.
       Wani likita ne ya fito ya sanar musu, inna hawaye ne ya gangaro mata cikin dakiya da jarumta tace, " Allah ya jikanka ya gafarta maka malam halinka na gari ya bika, oh rayuwa wai yau mutuwa ta dauke mlm " sai kuma ta fashe da kuka me tsuma zuciya.
     Su mlm maude ne suka dinga rarrashinta suna bata baki ahaka har aka basu gawar suka juyo gida.
        Lokacin da suka dawo anfara kiraye-kirayen sallar asuba, jin karar adaidaita ne yasa farida tayi farat ta fita waje sai dai ganin andauko mahaifin nata arufe yasa jikinta yayi sanyi bata gama yarda da abinda take zargi ba saida taga mahaifiyarta na share hawaye, da gudu ta tafi ta fada jikin mahaifiyar tata ta fashe da kuka me tsuma zuciya.

Ruk'o hannunta inna tayi suka karasa cikin gida, kan shinfidarsa ta da aka maida shi ka kwantar dashi, su mlm maude su suka sanarwa mutane da rasuwar, kafin wayewar gari gidan ya fara taruwa da matan mak'ota, wajen karfe goma na safe akayi jana'izar malam aka kaishi gidansa na gaskiya.
      Duk wanda yaga halinda su inna suka shiga sai ya tausaya musu, sunyi kuka kamar ransu zai fita fuskarsu duk ta sauya kammani abunka dafarar fata tuni fuskarsu ta kumbura tayi jawur.

    Tun bayan rasuwar malam makota su suke ciyar da inna itada farida, abinci har bayar da shi inna sukeyi kafin wannan ya kare zakaji wani yaron yayi sallama ya kawo wani kwanon, ita kanta inna har mamaki take tasan dai duk fadin unguwar babu wanda baisan halin da suke ciki ba, amma babu me daukan koda gero ne ya kawo musu da sunan taimako, sai gashi yanxu abinci har almajiri suke bawa.

     _Mafi akasari abinda  yake yawan faruwa kenan, acikin unguwa zakuga akwai gidan da ansan mabukata ne suna bukatar taimako amma ko gero babu wanda zai dauka ya kai musu, idan ma rashin lafiya ce mutum zaiyi ta neman taimako amma masu taimakawar kadan ne, sai lokacin da mutum ya mutu sai kaga yan uwa da makota anata tururuwar kawowa iyalansa kayan abinci wasu guraren ma harda danye zaka ga buhu-buhu anashigarwa da iyalan mamancin, Allah ya bamu abinda zamu taimakawa yan uwanmu musulmai dan matsayi da isar Annabi muhammad (S.A.W)_

       Ahaka har aka shafe sadakar bakwai abinci daga na safe rana dare baya yanke musu, yau da gobe ahankali komai ya fara ja baya, dan kudin sadakar da ake bawa inna su take tarawa da aka daina basu abinci daga makota dasu suke cefane suna cin abinci, bahaushe yace zaa ra bata barin dami akwana atashi dan kudin hannu inna ya kare, su farida suka fara komawa yar gidan jiya.
       Malam yau ya cika kwana arba'in da rasuwa, inna da farida na zaune suna dan hira farida tace, " inna me zai hana  ki fara dafa mun abincin siyarwa ina kaiwa bakin titi kinga sai mu rage wata matsalar, amma zaman haka nake ganin bazai fisheshe mu ba"
       " farida kinsan dai tallah ba tsarin mu bane nida mahaifinki, kuma tun yana raye kunsan sarai bai lamunci tallah ba, zandai gwada sarar yan kayan amfanin ko su kuku da daddawa ne nagani ko zamu dace, idan ma abincin ne sai dai mu gwada agida " cewar inna.

       " to inna ko kosai zamu gwada ko kuma wainar gero?" inji farida.
     " ehto bari mu fara da kosai koda na gwangwani shida ne sai mu gani ko zamu samu ciniki " inna ta fada.

    Farida dan murmushi tayi tace,  " eh to inna kosan ma yafi Allah yasa mu fara cikin sa'a "
     " Aameen farida"

       Wata tsohuwar gingimemiyar radio su da wata bulalliyar tukunyar karfensu inna ta fito dasu, farida ta aika gidan hansatu dilalliya ta kira ta.
         Hansatu bata dau lokaci ba ta janyo mayafinta tayi gidansu farida.
       Kallan tsaf hanne tayi musu sannan tace, " sadiya gaba daya kayan nan zan siye su dubu daya da dari biyar idan kin siyar ga kudinki ahannu "
     " haba hansatu dan Allah kidan kara mana, tukunyar nan fa me kwari ce bula daya ce ma ajikinta kuma radio ma wlh ko gun masu gyara kika kaita tsab za'a gyara ta ki siyar da daraja " cewar inna.

    " gaskiya nikam haka zan siya na dauka ma na siya da daraja " inji hansatu.
     Inna tace,  " dan Allah kar muyi haka dake hansatu kudin nake da bukata "
      " to na kara dari biyu akai zan baki dubu daya da dari bakwai gaskiya daga haka ba abinda xan kara " hansatu ta fada tana kallan inna.
      Inna bata da wata mafita hakan ne yasa ta karba, amma tasan ba karamar riba hansatu zata samu akai ba, bayan fitar hansaci cefanan kosai inna ta bawa farida taje ta siyo sauran dan canjin ta sai musu abinda ba'a rasa ba, makota inna ta dinga aykawa ana gaya musu zatayi kosan siyarwa.
         Kamar yanda inna ta sanar haka suka wayi gari da kosan siyarwa, cikin ikon Allah tas ya kare murna suke inna tana fadin sun samu sana'ar da zasu rike kansu da ita.

       Haka inna ta dinga kosai tana ciniki tun tanayin na gwangwani shida har ta dawo yin na rabin kwano da gwangwani biyu, ahankali kuma sai cini ya dinga ja baya, wata rana ma baifi suyi cinikin dari uku ba, ahaka-ahaka inna ta rage mudu ta dawo yin na gwangwani hudu,  ciniki sai kara ja baya yakeyi tun abun baya damun inna har abun ya fara damunta.
       Wainar gero suka koma yi itama hakan take, wata shawarar suka sake tayin wainar shinkafa, ba laifi itama da farko anfara cini daga baya kuma ko ta dari biyu ba'a zuwa siya.
       Ganin haka ne yasa inna ta watsar  tunda wataran idan sukayi sai dai su cinye abarsu, rasa abinyi inna tayi komai ta kama da ta fara sai cinikin yaja baya.

_*WATA UKU DA RASUWAR MALAM*_

  Su inna suna zaune farida na yi musu kwad'on kanzo sallama sukaji ina kwalawa, hannu farida ta wanke ta leka kofar gida, Alh Ya'u ta gani tsaye cikin manyan kaya, har kasa ta tsugunna ta gaida dashi, cikin washe baki ya amsa sannan yace, " yanmata ina maman taki kimun sallama da ita "
     "  To " farida ta bashi amsa.

   Inna ce ta fito suka gaisa, sannan yace, " dama tun watan shekaran jiya nake sauraron ku har yanxu naji shiru, ya ake ciki ne kinsan kudin hayarku wata biyu kenan da karewa, dama na d'aga muku kafa ne saboda rasuwar me gidanki, yanxu inasan ko rabi ne ki bani zuwa watan gaba na dawo na karbi rabin kudin "
      Rass gaban inna ya fadi dama kullin da tunanin hakan take kwana take tashi, tattaro jarumta tayi sannan tace, " dan Allah Alh  kayi mana afuwa wallahi dama kullin kudin nan yana raina amma insha Allahu zanyi kokari inhada maka, dan Allah ka dan kara mun lokaci " 
     Karkace kai gefe yayi cikin kufula yace, " wane lokaci kike san na kara miki, dududu nawa ne kudin hayar dubu arba'in kudin gida ashekara banda ni dinma wane gida zaki samu azamanin nan daki biyu adubu arba'in, abinda nake so dake ki rage mun wani abu ko dubu goma ki mikomun na rage zafi "

          "wallahi Alh dubu daya tawa ta kaina bani da ita, dan Allah kayi hakuri insha Allahu zanyi kokari inhada maka kudinka" inna ce tsugunne gaban Alh ya'u tana rokonsa.
          Dan jim kadan Alh ya'u yayi sannan yace, " shikenan na baki nan da wata guda ki hada mun kudina wallahi idan na dawo bansamu ba aranar ne zaki gane asalin kalata " yana gama magana bai jira cewarta ba ya juya ya hau kan mashin din vespan sa ya kara gaba.

    Tashin hankali wanda ba'a sa masa rana, inna ta kusa minti guda agurin tana jimamin maganganun Alh ya'u, gida ta shiga ta zayyanewa farida duk abinda ya faru.
    Farida ce ta kalli mahaifiyarta tace, " inna yanxu meye abunyi ?"
     " farida abunyi yanxu fita zanyi neman aikatau agidan masu kudi " cewar inna.

Yatsina fuska farida tayi tace, " aikatau inna "
     " kwarai farida, wannan zaman da nake yi bazai kaini ko ina ba, kina gani kinfi wata guda rabonki da zuwa islamiyya duk saboda ba kudin wata, ita dama boko ba'a magana tunda yanzu sa'anninki sun tafi aji daya ababbar sakandire ( SS1) farida idan na zauna kina ganin zaman zai fishsheni? " inna ta fada tana kallan farida.
     " A'a inna ance ne gidan masu kudin wulakanci garesu musamman ga wanda yake musu aiki" inji farida.

      " Farida duk wulakancinsu zan jure indai ba duka da zagina zasuyi ba, komai da kika gani na rayuwa me wucewa ne, wata rana zai zama tarihi ilimi shi ne jigo arayuwa, idan bakiyi ilimi ba sai gaba  abin zai miki ciwo, insha Allahu farida zanyi duk wani aiki ba gajiyawa indai zai rufa mana asiri " cewar inna.

  A sanyaye farida tace "To Allah ya taimaka inna"

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              5&6


Washe gari  duku-duku inna ta tashi ta shirya ta fita neman aiki, kudin motar da zata hau ma bata dasu da kafa ta tafi tun daga unguwa uku har na'ibawa cikin layin masu kudin nan tashiga, duk gidan da taje sai dai suce Mata suna da me aiki, yawo take tayi ba nasara ahaka ta hakura ta dauko hanyar gida, agalabaice ta shigo gida kafafunta furu-furu ga yunwa da k'ishirya sun addabeta, adaki ta samu farida na bacci jin Motsin Inna ne ya tashe ta, barka da dawowa farida tayi mata take tambayarta ko andace, duk abinda ya faru haka Inna ta bawa farida labari, sosai ta tausayawa mahaifiyar Tata, sauran dumamen Daren jiya farida ta zubowa Inna, tuni Inna ta cinye shi badan ya isheta ba, ruwan rijiyar da farida ta debo musu shi Inna ta kamfata take ta mammak'a, Sai da Tasha me isarta ta sauke kofin ta ajiye.
    Sallar Azahar da la'asar ta gabatar tana tunanin abinda zasu ci da daddare, tunawa Inna tayi da wata ajiyayyiyar ramarta da ta taba siya, Ramar ta dauko suka Fara gyarawa itada farida, haka suka kwadanta Ramar Dan dole ita suka ci suka kwanta bacci.

      Inna tun daga ranar haka ta dinga zarya neman aiki Amma yanda take fita haka take dawowa ba sa'a, wata ranar lahadi itace ranar da Inna ta fita na karshe domin taci alwashin muddin ta fita Bata samu ba to daga ranar ta daina fita, sai dai ta gwada wani abun ko zata dace.
           Awannan Rana ne Inna ta shiga unguwar kundila ta zagaye gidaje sunfi goma sai ana gaba ne wata mata tace ta amince zata dauketa aiki, alokacin Inna kamar ta zuba ruwa akasa tasha.

       Wani hanzari matar ta kawo na cewar ita masuyi Mata aiki kwana suke basa jeka ka dawo, Sam Inna bataji dadin wannan maganar ba.
       Alfarma inna ta roki hjy ta taimaka Mata da ta dauko farida suyi aiki tare.

Kin amincewa hjy tayi dan dole Inna ta hakura da aikin wannan ake cewa ga k'oshi ga kwanan yunwa, Inna afadarta bazata iya tafiya aikin kwana da kwanaki tabar farida na kwana ita daya agida ba, hakan ne yasa ta yanke kauna Kai tsaye gida ta wuce jiki asanyaye tana kara mik'awa Allah lamuranta.

     Lokacin da ta shiga wanke-wanke ta samu farida tana yi, guri Inna ta samu ta zauna agajiye tana maida numfashin wahala, farida ce tazo take tabaryar Inna halin da ake ciki.
    Ajiyar zuciya Inna ta sauke ta zayyanewa farida duk yanda sukayi da hjy.
      Farida ce tace, " Inna meyasa Kika ki amincewa? "
     "Saboda ke farida " Inna ta fada.
    Da mamaki farida tace, " saboda nifa inna, bangane ba " inji farida.

    " Saboda bazan iya tafiya wani guri inje inyi ta bauta inbarki ke daya kina kwana agidan nan ba, kinsan fa akace kiwon mutum ake ba dabba ba, haka kawai wani yazo ya b'ata Miki rayuwarki abanza da wofi, gwara na hakura Allan daya hallicemu zai San yanda zaiyi damu, haka zalika Allah baya dorawa bawa Abinda bazai iya ba, nasan hakan Yana daga cikin jarrabawar mu, duk tsanani Yana tare da sauki wata Rana wannan rayuwar da muke ciki zai zama tarihi " Inna tayiwa farida bayani.

   Tun daga lokacin Inna Bata kara fita neman aiki ba sai yan kame-kamen sana'ar da takeyi yau suyi wannan gobe suyi wannan ahaka har aka shafe wata biyu da zuwan Alh ya'u, Inna ta saddak'ar da duk irin cin mutumcin da Alh ya'u zai musu, saboda basu da wata kafar samu kudin gidan sa, tabbas Alh ya'u tasan idan yazo ba makawa zai tashe ne tunda akallah sun shafe sama da wata uku basu bada kudin hayar ba, ita kanta tana mamakin yanda Har ya Kara musu kwanaki bai dawo garesu ba kamar yanda ya fad'a.

Inna da farida na daki suka jiyo sallama daga kofar gida, hijabinta farida ta dauka tasa ta fita, atsaye ta sameshi gaisheshi farida tayi, kafeta Alh ya'u yayi da idanu sannan yace, lafiya klau yan Mata kiyi mun sallama da mamanki kinji "
      Farida mamakin Alh ya'u take, gida ta shiga ta sanarwa da mahaifiyar ta, ba karamin firgici Inna ta shiga ba gabanta ya hau duka uku-uku, fita tayi ta sameshi akofar gida, Inna gaisawa sukayi da Alh ya'u sannan ta Fara cewa, " Alh ya'u Dan Allah Dan Annabi ka taimaka kayi Mana afuwa wallahi kudin nan.............. "
     A zafafe Alh ya'u ya katseta, " ke dakata mun tashi tsaye kibar tsugunna mun, wallahi ko ki bani kudina ko ku fice mun daga gida na "
     Duk da Inna tasan karshen maganar kenan, Amma ba karamin razanata maganar tayi ba, cikin magiya ta Fara rok'onsa, Alh ya'u kallan tsaf yayiwa Inna sannan yace, " kina San na yi muku afuwa ? "
      Kamar karamar yarinya Inna ta amsa da, " Eh"
      Shafa keyarsa yayi Yana gyara tsayuwa yace, " Abu daya Zaki mun, idan Kika yi munshi kuwa ba kudin haya ba gidan nan da kike ciki zan iya mallaka Miki shi baki daya"

Mamaki, al'ajabi da ta'ajibi ne suka kama Inna kamar wata sokuwa haka ta saki baki da hanci tana kallan Alh ya'u, ta kasa cemasa Koda uffan.
         Magana ya Kara yi Mata, " Inna magana kinyi shiru ko inkara maimaita Miki? "
     Ajiyar zuciya Inna ta sauke sannan tace, " naji abinda kace, meye bukatar taka? "

    " Yauwa wato abinda nake so, so nake ki bani auren waccen yarinyar taki shikenan abinda Zaki mu "
       Kafeshi da ido Inna  tayi sannan tace, " kasan me kake fada kuwa Alh ya'u? Inbaka auren farida fa kace, Kai bakaji kunya ba Dan Allah? Yanxu farida nawa take na tabbata sai anraba shekarunka gida nawa Zasuyi nata, ka dubeka ka dubi farida alalace dai nasan ka yi jika da farida, Alh ya'u shekarar farida goma Sha shida fa, gaskiya bazan iya baka farida ba "
           Azafafe Alh ya'u yace, to kuwa dole ku barmun gida na nida na dauka ke mutuniyar kirkice Ashe ba haka bane, wallahi ayau basai gobe ba sai kun fice mun daga gida " Yana gama maganar fuuuuu yayi gaba cikin sauri ya haye mashin dinsa yayi gaba.

      Hawayen bakin ciki da takaici ne ya zubowa Inna, gida ta juya ta shiga ta zayyanawa farida duk abinda faru harda maganar aurenta da Alh ya'u.
       Farat farida tayi tace, " Inna to meye aciki Dan na aureshi kinaji fa gida zai bamu, Kuma kina gani me kudi ne fa Inna ni wallahi................... Ke rufemun baki sakarya kawai mara sanin darajar kanta " Inna ce ta katseta cikin takaici, farida badan taso ba taja Baki ta tsuke Tana mita kasa-kas.

     Bayan Awa daya da fitar Alh ya'u, sai gashi ya dawo shida wasu mazga-mazgan samari, Kai tsaye gidan suka shiga, umarni Alh ya'u ya dinga bawa samarin ana fita da kayansu kofar gida, cikin isgili suke musu watsi da kaya.

Gaba daya kayan su inna sai da aka fice dasu, dududu kayan nasu basu fi ajuye su cikin buhu ba sai wata yaloliyar tabarma, fitowa sukayi Inna na kuka ta karasa gurun kayansu, suna fitowa Alh ya'u ya ja kofarsa ya datse ta da kwad'o ko kallan arziki bai musu ya sa Kai yayi gaba.
      Su farida kuka sukaci suka godewa Allah, bakin cikin duniya ya ishesu mutane hard sun Fara taruwa, ganin anfara sa musu idanu yasa suka, Inna ta hada musu kayansu Kai tsaye gidan hansatu me adashi ta nufa, gaba daya kayan ta Kai Mata Dan ta siya, hakan kuwa akayi  gaba daya tayi musu kudi gaba daya dubu uku ta siyesu, ta mik'awa Inna kudin, ba wanda Inna ta gayawa maganar tashin su daga su sai kayan jikinsu, suka tafi.

    Tafiya suka yi me nisa, tafiya suke ba tare da sunsan inda zasu ba, cikin gajiyawa farida tace, " Inna wai Ina zamu je ne "
      " Duniya zamu shiga farida, muje ko bakin gada ne gwara mu dinga kwana acen.

[8/23, 6:16 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              7&8


A sanyaye farida ta kalli mahaifiyar tata cikin tausayawa tace, " Inna bakin gada fa, acen zamu kare rayuwar mu kenan? "
      Farida bamuda wata mafita da ta wuce wannan, sanin kanki ne rayuwa tayi tsada, taimako yayi wuya, afadin garin nan ki gayamun ina zamuje mu tsugunna daya wuce bakin gada, cen ne zamu yi rayuwar mu bame tashinmu bamuda fargabar kudin haya sai dai mu dinga neman na Abinci, kinsan bakin da Allah ya tsaga baya hanashi abinci " Inna ta fada.
    " To Inna Allah ya kawo Mana dauki" cewar farida.

     Tafiya sukai sosai sannan suka karaso titi, Dan sahu suka tara ya karaso dasu gyadi-gyadi, a gadar lado suka yada zango, agajiye suka zauna suna karewa titi da mutanen dake rayuwa agurin kallo, wata Mata suka samu azaune da alama gurguwa ce ta sa robarta agaba, duk Wanda yazo wucewa da irin rokon da take Masa.
     Tashi sukayi suka karasa gurinta.

     Da sallama Inna suka karasa gurinta, cikin sakin fuska ta amsa musu, bayan sun gaisa tsohuwar ta Fara roko kamar haka, " Ataimaka sadaka fisabilillahi sadaka saboda Allah da Annabi ku bamu na Annabi bayin Allah, yan Mata Allah ya kawo miji nagari kinji "
     Sororo su farida sukayi suna sauraronta, Inna ce ta danyi murmushi tace, " iya nidai sunana sadiya wannan kuma 'yata ce mahallin da muke ciki ne aka tashe mu, Kuma Bamu da gurin zuwa shine muka zo nan Dan mu zauna har Allah ya kawo Mana mafita......." 
      Tun kafin Inna ta rufe Baki tsohuwar ta Fara sababi, " wato kuce zuwa kukayi ku samun Rani ko? Dan abin alherin da nake samu kukewa bakin ciki, shine kuka tarkato kuka dawo nan Kai duniya Allah ka rabamu da Dan bakin ciki "
     Farida ce ta cewa mahaifiyar ta, " Inna Dan Allah mu bar gurin tsohuwar nan, ga guri cen muje mu zauna inba haka ba wallahi sai ta Tara Mana jama'a "
   Hakan kuwa sukayi cen gefe suka koma suka zauna, zamansu keda wuya farida taga mahaifiyar ta ta fashe da kuka mara sauti hawaye na zuba, babu dadi taji cikin zuciyarta daurewa tayi ta Fara lallashin mahaifiyar tata.
   
   Cikin kuka Inna tace, " farida Wai yau mune zaune abakin gada, Allah ya jikan malam nasan da Yana raye da haka baza ta taba faruwa damu ba, tabbas Allah baya barin wani Dan wani yaji dadi, Allah ya gafarta maka malam halinka na gari ya bika " Inna ta karasa magana tana goge hawayen fuskarta.
    Yar tsohuwa lokaci-lokaci take kallan su Inna tana watsa musu harara, ganin haka yasa suka juya Mata baya.
      Dan kullin kayansu Inna ta bude ta dakko musu daddumar sallah, purewater ta sai musu sukayi alwala sannan suka gabatar da sallar la'asar.

   Suna nan zaune sun zubawa sarautar Allah ido har magariba ta kawo Kai, Yar tsohuwa ce ta hada Dan buhunta da sandarta tazo wucewa ta kusa da su Inna zata tafi, harara take watsa musu sannan tace, " to yan bakin ciki na bar muku gurin zuwa safiya sai ku tara bankin najeriya gaba daya "
        Da ido suka bita ba Wanda ya tanka Mata har ta samu ta tsallaka ta hau mota.
        Bayan sallar magriba Inna ce ta bawa farida kudi ta tsallaka titi gurin wata me shinkafa da wake wacce ta gefen gidan man AA. RANO abinci ta siyo musu ta kawo musu sukaci sukayi kat suka kora da ruwa.
      Suna nan zaune zuru suna kallan yanda mutane suke gudanar da al'amura har dare ya farayi, farida gajiya tayi da zama ta kunce kullin kayansu wani tsumman zanin gadonsu ta zaro ta shinfid'a ta kwanta ba jimawa bacci yayi gaba da ita.

      Inna tana nan zaune har kafa ta Fara daukewa, sai manyan motoci da suke wucewa, tuna irin rayuwar da tayi abaya tayi, da irin gatan da tasamu wajen mahaifiyanta har zuwa aurenta da malam, hawaye ta share ta jingina tana nazarin rayuwa.
    Inna na nan zaune har bacci barawo yayi awon gaba da ita.

    Nesa-nesa cikin bacci take jiyo Kiran sallar Asuba, sai alokacin ta tuna da basu tanadi ruwan alwala ba uwa uba kuma gurun da zasu yi tsarki, gefen wuyanta taji ya rike saboda baccin da tayi azaune, Dan shafa gurin tayi ta tofa yan addu'o'i.

Waige-waige Inna ta farayi tana hange ko zata hango wani gurin da zasu samu ruwa, tsallawa tayi ta tana duddubawa, ta dade ahaka Bata samu ruwa ba wasu masallatan har sun Fara sallah, wani tuka-tuka ta hango da sauri ta Kara gurin ta Fara bugashi, Tunowa tayi da babu abinda zasu dibi ruwan dashi suyi alwala.
      Komawa tayi ta taso farida suka koma gurin tuka-tukan, dube-dube suka shiga yi cen nesa suka hango wata leda daukota sukayi suka wanketa, suka Debi ruwa aciki kadan-kadan take zuba ledar, wani lungu suka hango da baya bulla  daga jikin wani shago, farida ce ta Fara karewa Inna tayi fitsari sannan farida ta Kara debo ruwan Inna ta kare Mata itama tayi fitsarin, sannan suka dawo sukayi Alwala suka koma suka gabatar da sallar Asuba.

       Gari ya Fara haske mutane sun Fara gilmawa kowa Yana harkar gabansa, koko da kosai Inna ta aika farida tsallake ta karbo musu gurin wata bayerabiya, hijabai suka shiga suka ci suka sha sannan suka koma gefe suka Dan Fara Hira.
       Zuwa wani lokaci Yar tsohuwa suka hango ta taho da Yar sandarta a hannu, wucewa taxo yi tana watsa musu mugun kallo.
      Yanxun ma ba Wanda ya kulata ta je gurin zamanta ta zauna.

       A kwana atashi ba wuya agurin Allah, sun Inna suna bakin gada har kusan kwana goma, Dan kudin hannun Inna ya kare tas, banda ma Allah yasa musu albarka dasu take musu komai ci da Sha, ga wani gidan wanka da ta roki mutumin suke zuwa, da kyar ya amince saboda yace Mata na maza ne basa barin Mata shiga, alfarma ta roka akan cewar da duku-duk zasu dinga zuwa suyi wanka da duk wani Abu da zai kaisu band'aki, hakan ne yasa ya amince suke biyanshi suyi abinda Zasuyi su koma bakin gada.

       Tun su Inna najin kunyar bara har suka zage duk mutumin da yaxo wucewa zasu fara rok'onsa, duk tsawon Rana baifi su Sami dari biyu zuwa dari biyu da hamsin ba, saboda mutane na ganinsu dakau da lafiya suke bara, hakan ne yasa mutane da dama sai su tsallakesu su bawa Yar tsohuwa sadaka.
         Ranar da suka cika sati biyu tashi sukayi ko na karyawa babu, ga kayan jikinsu kamar basu taba ganin ruwa ba, Inna ce ta cewa farida, " farida me zai hana ki dinga tsallakawa gidan mancen bara ko mafi samun na abinci, ni sai inzauna anan Kinga mun raba kafa ke inbaki samu ba ni ko Yaya ne zan Dan samu "
   
   Amsawa mahaifiyar ta tayi, hakan kuwa sukayi farida take tsallawa gidan mai ita kum Inna na bakin gada, ba laifi sukan dan samu kusan dari hudu dari biyar arana, ahaka-ahaka har suka shafe wata guda suna rayuwa akasan gada.

          SADIQ matashin saurayi ne da bazai wuce shekara Ashirin da bakwai zuwa da takwas ba, Yana aiki ne akarkashin gidan man AA. RANO dake gyadi-gyadi sune masu sayar da petir aciki, tun ranar da farida ta Fara shiga gidan mai ya d'ora idansa akanta, cikin zuciyarsa yake mamakin kyakykywar yarinya me kuruciya kamarta ace tana yawon bara, sai yayi kamar zai Mata magana sai Kuma zuciyarsa ta gargadeshi, abinda yake kara bashi mamaki magariba na karatowa zai nemeta sama ko k'asa ya rasa, sab'abanin mabaratan da suke kaiwa har kusan goman dare.

    Wannan Karan alwashi yaci sai ya sa mata ido yaga sanda take tafiya, idanunsa na Kan farida har ta tsallaka gurin Inna, duk atunaninsa yayi zatan zuwa wani lokaci suke barin gurin, Abinda ya daure mai Kai gani yayi kullin tana tashi saidai ta tsallaka kasan gada ta zauna.
     Yau ma kamar kullin farida ta tsallaka kasan gada, Sadiq ya ayyanawa zuciyarsa cewar ya tsallaka yayi Mata wasu 'yan tambayoyi ko kansa ya bude ya samu Karin haske.
     Bayan ya tashi yaje ya cire uniform din sa ya sako kananan kaya ya tsallako kasan gada gurinsu Inna.


_Da kyau masoya naji dadi kwarai da yanda kuka karbi wannan littafin da hannu dubu-dubu naga ruwan comments dinku inayinku irin sosai din nan ana mugun tare._💃🏼💃🏼💃🏼💃🏼

[8/24, 12:35 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

😭 *TSANTSAR BUTULCI*😭
                  2020
     
*Written By:-*
    _Ameera Adam_
                _AmeeraAdam60_
              *@Wattpad*

☀ *FIRST CLASS WRITER'S ASSOCIATION* ☀
   FCWA
*{Home of qualities and trusted writer's of The nation}*
https://www.facebook.com/First-class-writers-Association-111719967001810/?referrer=whatsapp
*____________________________________*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              9&10


Da sallama ya karasa karkashin gadar Yana bin wajen da kallo, amasawa sukayi cikin sakin fuska atunaninsu sadaka ya kawo musu, sai ayanxu ya lura da tsantsar kamar da farida takeyi da mahaifiyar ta, gaida Inna yayi cikin sakin fuska ta amsa Wanda yayi daidai da tahowar 'yar tsohuwa, shekeke ta kallesu sai ta bushe da dariya tace, " lallai dawa tayi nama har  anfara kawo samari kwana kusa zamuji sabon labari " ta wuce su tana Kara kwashewa da dariya.
       Da ido Sadiq ya bita Yana mamakin maganganun yar tsohuwa, girgiza Kai Inna tayi tana kauda Kai gefe.
       Inna ce ta katse Sadiq daga dogon tunanin da yakeyi tace, " yaro kazo ka zauna me yake tafe dakai Dan Allah ka tashi ka tafi kafin afara yi Mana mummunan zargi "

     Dan duburdur cewa yayi sai yanxu yake jin kunyar tambayar da yayi niyyar yiwa su Inna, murmushi yayi sannan yace, " am mama dama Dan tsallakowa nayi mu gaisa "
     " Yaro gaisuwa haka kawai, nikam bangane fuskarka ba " cewar Inna.
      Dan shafa Kai yayi sannan yace, " mama bari na wuce ga wannan kwa sayi wani abun " dubu daya ce ya mi'ka musu, zaro ido Inna tayi tana mamaki cikin jin dadi Inna da farida suka Fara yi Masa godiya.

    Ganin irin farin cikin da su Inna suka shiga ba karamin mamaki abun ya bashi ba, bayan ya Dan gifta ya jiyo Inna na cewa farida, " oh farida Kinga ikon Allah dama ance rabon kwad'o baya hawa sama, wannan kudin daya bamu ma kwana biyu muna kashe su, Wai farida ko kinsan yaron nan ne? "
      Farida tace, " eh to Inna ganinsa Yana saida mai agidan Mai, amma ni Bamu taba magana dashi ba"
         " Yauwa yar Albarka ki kama mutumcin kanki ki rike kimarki, duk 'ya ta gari Bata ballagazar da kanta atiti, Dan muna cikin wannan rayuwar karki amfani da wannan damar ki biyewa San zuciyarki, ko abun duniya ya rudeki Allah ya Kara rufa Mana asiri ubangiji ka kawo Mana karshen wannan rayuwar da mukeyi ta karkashin gada, Allah ka duba lamarinmu ba muda tsimi bare dabara ka kawo Mana dauki " inji Inna.
      Duk maganganun dasu Inna suke yi akan kunnensa, wani irin tausayinsu ne ya shiga zuciyarsa, yanxu ace Mata ne da kwanan karkashin gada tabbas mutanen nan suna bukatar taimako, girgiza kansa yayi sannan ya wuce Yana Kara jinjina halin da su farida suka ciki, adaidaita sahu ya shiga Kai tsaye aka wuce dashi unguwar hausawa sabon titi.
        Da sallama ya shiga gida mahaifiyar sa ya samu tana sallar Isha'i, gefen ta ya samu ya zauna Yana dafe kanta, har dawo gida Yana cikin tausayinsu farida.
        Mahaifiyar sa ce ta idar da sallah ta Fara yi Masa magana, " Sadiq andawo ne, ya hanya? "

     A sanyaye yace, " eh na dawo umma" daga Haka bai Kara ce Mata komai.
      " To ka tashi ga ruwan wankanka cen dana dafa maka acikin tukunya, sai kazo ga abincinka nan kaci " cewar umma.
        Shafa sumar kansa yayi idansa arufe sannan yace, " umma abincin nan ba yanxu ba Amma dai bari na je nayi wankan "
       Tashi yayi ya wuce dakinsa ya jima aciki sannan ya wuce band'aki yayi wanka, bayan ya fito sallar Isha'i ya gabatar, ya koma Kan katifa ya nutsa cikin tunani.
     Jin shirun nashi yayi yawa har goman dare yasa umma shiga dakin Sadiq, akwance ta sameshi akwance da sallama ta shiga Sam baimasan ta shigo ba, ta dade atsaye tana kare Masa kallo dama tun shigowarsa ta fahimci Yana cikin damuwa.
    
    Sadiq, Sadiq, firgit ya tashi zaune, cikin mamaki umma tace, " ikon Allah Sadiq me yake damunka ne, ka shigo daki ka zauna ka zurfafa cikin tunani haka, har goma baka ci abinci ba kana kwance yunwa na cinka, Gaya mun agurin aikin ne ka samu matsala  ko Kuma wani abun ne daban? "
           Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke sannan yace, " umma ba komai wallahi jikina ne kawai banajin dadinsa, muje naci abincin dama yanxu nake cewa zan fito naci"
        Umma badan ta yarda da maganarsa ba tace, " Da dai yafi maka wuce muje"
      Haka Sadiq ya bi umma badan yaso ba sai dai Dan kar itama ya sata cikin damuwa.
     Suna zuwa dakin umma ta Mika Masa abinci, haka ya Dan caccakaleshi badan yanajin dadin abincin ba, ya Dan zauna suka taba hira duk Dan ya kawar wa da umma tunanin da takeyi akansa.

Washe gari Allah-Allah Sadiq yake yi yaje yaga yasu farida suka kwana suka tashi, ranar sai akayi rashin dace Bata fita bara ba saboda zazzafan zazzabin data tashi dashi, Sadiq Yana aiki Amma gaba daya hankalinsa baya jikinsa, lokaci-lokaci yake waigen gurin data Saba zama, wayam Bata nan har zuwa yamma har yayi niyyar tsallakawa, sai wata zuciyar tace ka bari zuwa gobe man wata Kila wani uzzurin ne ya tsareta, Tunowa yayi da dubu dayan daya basu da Kuma maganganun Inna da take cewa, kudin Zasuyi musu kwana biyu suna kashewa, afili ya furta, " ko Dan suna dan kudin da zasu yi amfani dashi ne yasa Bata fito ba? "
       Ya jima a tsaye abakin titi Yana hango su, sannan ya tari abun hawa ya wuce gida.
     Sadiq mamakin kansa yake yanda yaga Yana yawan tunanin farida da mahaifiyar ta, wata zuciyar ce tace Masa, " Kai meye naka da zaka dinga damun kanka " afili Kuma ya furta, " Amma Kuma duk Wanda ya taimaki wani Allah zai taimakeshi, Dan guntun tsaki yayi yace, tausayin da nake musu ne yasa na kasa cire tunaninsu azuciyata"

     Maganar da Sadiq yayi tayi dai-dai da zuwan umma dakinsa, sai dai bataji me ya fad'a ba taji dai yana magana shi kadai, "Sadiq" umma ta Kira shi.
      Firgigit Sadiq yayi ya amsa, " na'am umma"
     Zama umma tayi a gefen katifarsa sannan ta kare Mai kallo har sai da ya tsargu, cikin tsarguwa yace, " ay umma dama yanxu nake San zuwa muyi Hira "

     Murmushin manya umma tayi tace, "Sadiq kwana biyu naga kana cikin damuwa me yake damunka?" 
         " Damuwa Kuma umma, ni ba abinda yake damuna kawai gajiya ce na dawo agajiye shiyasa " cewar Sadiq.
      " To ay shikenan idan tayi wari maji, ni kaga wucewa ta" umma ta fad'a tana kokarin tashi.
     Da sauri Sadiq ya ruko hannun umma cikin wasa yace, " haba umma niba abinda fa yake damuna ki zauna muyi Hira man"

"A'a sakarmun hannu idan Hira kake so ka biyo ni dakina" umma ta fad'a tanayin gaba abinta.
      " Angama ranki ya dade ay sai abinda Kika ce hajiya umma " Sadiq ya fada Yana bin mahaifiyar sa.


   Washe gari ma Haka Sadiq ya fito aiki, Yana yan kalle-kalle ko zaiyi tozali da farida Amma ba ita ba alamarta, bayan ya tashi daga aiki tsallakawa yayi gurin su farida, da sallama ya karasa gurin da suke zaune da mamaki yaga farida ce ita kadai ta shige cikin hijabi.
     Sallamarsa ce ta sata fitowa daga cikin hijabi, idanuwanta ya gani sun kada sunyi jawur ga fuskarta duk ta canja launi, muryarta na rawa rawa tace Masa, " ina wuni " wani Abu ne ya tsarga Masa tun daga tafin kafa har tsakiyar kansa, bai amsa Mata ba ya wurga Mata tambaya, " ina mama ta tafi naganki zaune ke kadai, me yake damunki naga yanayinki ya canja? " Lokaci daya ya jeho Mata wadannan tambayoyin?

        A hankali ta bashi amsa da," bani da lafiya ne Inna Kuma taje siyo mun magani " 
       Har tuntuben harshe yakeyi cikin sauri yace, " me yake damunki ?

Inna ce ta karaso gurinsu da sallama, har k'asa Sadiq ya tsugunna ya gaida Inna, cikin girmamawa ta amsa ta karasa kusa da farida ta zauna tana ajiye ledar maganin.
        " Mama ashe farida bataji Dadi ba? " Sadiq ya tambaya.
      " Wallahi zazzabi ne ya rufeta tun shekaran jiya da dare, to jiya nayita neman chemist bansamu ba sai da wani yaro dazu da yamman nan ya kwatanta mun sannan naje na karbo Mata gasu " cewar Inna.
 
        " Mama mezai hana akaita asibiti Ina ganin zaifi " inji Sadiq.
     Murmushi Inna tayi sannan tace,  " A'a basai munje asibiti ba insha Allahu tana Shan maganin jikin zaiyi sauki  "

Bayasan suyita Jan magana shida Inna shiyasa yaja bakinsa yayi shiru, magani farida Tasha ta koma cikin hijabi ta maida kanta ciki, zanin gadon da suke shinfid'a shi Inna ta dauko ta rufa Mata ita Kuma ta shinfid'a dadduma ta zauna akai.
    Kwallah ce  ta cika idanun Sadiq ganin haka yasa yayi ta maza, ya Mike tsaye Yana cewa Inna, " mama ni zan wuce sai da safe insha Allah, ga wannan " dari biyar ya mik'awa Inna da farko kin karba tayi sai daga baya ta karba tana jeromai godiya.

    Sadiq tafiya yayi zuciyarsa ba Dadi tabbas bayin Allah nan suna bukatar taimako, afili ya furta, "aykuwa  zan taimaka musu tsakanina da Allah.
      Da tunanin su farida ya karasa gida Ya kuduri niyyar fayyacewa umma duk abinda yake faruwa dasu farida da taimakon da yake San yi musu, sai dai tsoronsa daya kar umma taki amincewa.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              11&12



Da sallama ya shiga cikin gidansu, Kai tsaye dakinsa ya wuce ya je ya rage kayan jikiinsa ya fito, d'akin mahaifiyar sa ya shiga ya sameta akwance kan daddumar da tayi sallah, da alama bacci ne yayi gaba da ita bayan ta idar da sallah.
           Sadiq tunda ya zauna yake tunanin hanyar da zai bullowa mahaifiyarsa Dan ganin ta fahimce shi, nepa aka kawo musu hasken d'akin ne ya tashi umma daga bacci, tashi zaune tayi tana kallan Sadiq dake Kan kujera ya hada Kai da gwiwa, kiransa ta farayi shiru bai amsa ba harsai da ta kirashi sau uku sannan yayi firgigit yace, " na'am umma barka da dare "

  Kallan sa take ba tare da ta amsa ba, tsarguwa yayi ya Fara sosai Kai Yana sunkuyar da Kai k'asa.
   "  SADIQ " umma ta kirashi da babbar murya har sai da gabansa ya fadi.
   A hankali ya amsa, "na'am umma"
     " Na lura kana cikin damuwa na tambayeka kaki gayamun, Wai shin Sadiq ko kana da wacce zaka gayawa da ta wuce ni, duk kauce-kaucen da kake yi ina lura da Kai kana tunanin zakamun wayo ne, nifa na haifeka duk wata dabara da zaka mun ay saidai inkalleka " umma ta karasa fad'a tana kallan Sadiq.
      " Dama umma ina Shirin sanar Miki ne sai Kika tambayeni, wallahi wasu bayin Allah ne suke bukatar taimako, basu da hali ko kadan akarkashin gadar wajen aikin mu fa suke kwana ita da yarta, umma sitirar kirki basu da ita abincin da zasuci ma bara sukeyi, shine dama nace zan sameki inroki alfarma Dan Allah umma ki bari inkawosu gidan nan su cigaba da zama " cewar Sadiq.

    "  Toooo too to, wato Abinda yasa ka tunani kenan ba dare ba Rana, to idan ka kawo su nan yaushe zasu tafi, Wai ma intambayeka basu da dangi ne su? Ko su biyu kawai suka rage aduniya ? Sadiq shifa mutum ba abin yarda bane kaje ka janyo mutum daga Rana Kai ya turaka wuta, gaskiya bazai yuwu ka dauko mutanen da Bamu sansu ba bamusan asalinsu ba ka kawo su mu dinga kwana dasu ba, azo awayi gari su yanyanke Mana kawuna su tafi dashi Sam ka bazai yuwu ba, kama cire wannan tunanin daga zuciyarka" umma ta fad'a cikin bada umarni.
      Sadiq kamar zaiyi kuka ya Fara magana, " umma Dan Allah ki fahimci manufata karki musu mummmunan fahimta, duk Wanda ya taimaki wani Allah zai taimakeshi, wallahi umma bazasu cutar damu ba Kuma..........."
      Katse shi umma tayi, " Kuma me? Ni fa na gama magana karka cikani da surutan banza idan wannan maganar zaka cigaba da mun ka tashi ka bani guri " ta karasa fad'a cikin tsukewar fuska.

   Tashi yayi Kai tsaye dakinsa ya wuce Kan katifarsa ya fad'a, wani Abu nayi Masa d'aci a makogaronsa, kwana yayi Yana tunanin halin da su farida suke ciki musamman yanda yaga take jin jiki, kusan baccin Daren da yayi rabi da rabi ne, har Allah-Allah yake gari ya waye yaje yaga halin da take ciki.

          Yana karasa wa gyadi-gyadi Kai tsaye karkashin gada ya wuce, umma na zaune farida na kwance kudundune cikin bargo, da sallama ya karasa gurin Inna, tayi mamaki kwarai da ganin sa sassafe haka, amsa masa tayi ta Fara tambayar jikin farida, " mama ya me jikin ta kwana? " 
     " To jiki dai gashi da sauki za'a ce amma zazzabin har yanxu bai sauka ba, ciwo ne ke shiga lokaci daya sauki sai ahankali " cewar Inna.

       Cikin tashin hankali Sadiq yace, " a'a mama baza'a sawa farida ido tana cikin wannan halin ba, bari na tsallaka na dawo ku shirya kafin na xo sai mu kaita asibiti " ya karasa fad'a Yana tashi daga gurin.
      Inna ba karamin Dadi tajiba, ruwa tasa ta wanke kafa da hannunta, ta tashi farida jikinta duk ba kwari ko bude ido Bata iyayi, wani hijabin Inna ta dauko ta sa Mata itama ta cenza wani hijabin suka zauna jiran Sadiq.
        Inna cikin zuciyarta take fadin, " tabbas Allah ya kawo Mana yaron nan ne cikin rayuwarmu Dan ya taimake mu, Allah mungode maka Allah ka Kara rufa Mana asiri.

  Sadiq na karasawa gurin babbansu ya karasa ya dauki permission, ce musu yayi  kanwarsa ba laifiya zai kaita asibiti idan sun gana da wuri zai dawo inbasu gama da wuri ba Kuma sai gobe.
         Yana tsallakowa su inna suka taso, Inna ce ta rike farida har suka shiga adaidaita sahu, wani private hospital ya kaisu dake unguwar zoo road, shigarsu keda wuya likitoci suka karbi farida, ruwa suka Fara sa Mata da allurai sannan suka dauki jininta test kala-kala sukayi Mata zuwa wani lokaci zazzabin har ya sauka, bacci ya dauketa sakamakon alluran bacci da Tasha.

            Lokacin da Sadiq ya shiga dakin umma ta shiga toilet, tsayawa yayi Yana karewa kyakykywar fuskarta kallo, fuskarta tayi wasai ta fad'a tabbas zazzabin ba karamin kamu yayi Mata ba, wayarsa ya Ciro ya Fara daukanta hoto Motsin fitowar Inna yaji ya koma kujerar da ke gefe ya zauna.
      Inna ce tace, " Sadiq har ka shigo"
     Murmushi yayi yace, " eh mama na duba reception ne ay bakyacen shine nace bari na leko nan "
       "Sadiq" ,Inna ta kirashi, " banida bakin da zan gode maka mungode kwarai Allah yayi maka albarka Allah yaja da ran mahaifa........."
     Katseta ta yayi da, " Dan Allah mama ki daina gode mun farida kamar kanwa na dauketa, Abinda zanyiwa kanwata shi zanyiwa farida fatan dai Allah ya Bata lafiya.
     Gaba daya suka amsa da" Aameen"

Sai wajen yamma farida ta tashi ruwan tea Sadiq ya karbo Mata tun bayan da aka sa Mata ruwa flask ya ara agurin wata nurse ya siyo mata aciki, magani Tasha ba laifi jikin nata ya danyi kwari, alwala taje tayi ta gabatar da sallar azahar, tana Kan dadduma taji shigowarsa, tashi tayi zata nad'e daddumar yace, " barta agurin bari na zauna akai "
       Inna na zauna Kan kujera tace, " ga guri man kazo ka zauna "
    " A'a mama nan ma yayi " Sadiq ya fad'a.
       Inna taso ta fahimci wani Abu daga gun Sadiq Amma sai tayi tunanin halin da suke ciki, wata zuciyar ma tace Mata, " yaro Dan gayu kamar wannan me zaiyi da wata farida, kawai dai tausayi ne gareshi da jin Kai.
          Bayan zaman Sadiq Inna duk sai ta kasa sakewa tashi tayi ta fita reception ta zauna.
         Farida sunkuyar da kanta kasa tayi, tana San yi masa magana tana jin wani kwarjininsa azuciyarta.
      " Yah jiki? " Taji ya ce Mata.
        D'ago da kanta tayi karaf suka hada ido, saurin sunkuyar da kanta kasa tayi, ahankali tace, " da sauki " runtse ido yayi saboda wata irin kuwwa da yaji muryarta tayi Masa akunne, bai gama fita daga wancen tunanin ba ya tsinkayi muryarsa tace, " mungode da taimakon da kake mana Allah ya saka da alheri "

        Shiru ne ya biyo baya taji bai Bata amsa ba, dagowa tayi taga Yana dan Danna waya kamar bai ji Abinda tace ba, sai yanxu ta tab'a kare Masa kallo cikin zuciyarta take fadin, " tsarki ya tabbata ga Wanda yayi wannan kyakykywar halittar, gashi  black beauty Amma kyakykywa ne, nasan dai duk kyansa ba Abinda zai nuna mun " ta fada cikin zuciyarta.

        Duk kallan da farida take Masa Yana kalle da ita ta gefen ido, dariya yake cikin ransa afili Kuma ya tsuke fuska yace,  " yan Mata wannan kallan fa "

      A kunyace farida ta kunyar da kanta kasa Bata bashi amsa ba, cikin zuciyarta tace, " au dama Yana kallona, kema me ya kaiki Kika wani kare Masa kallo kamar yau Kika Fara ganinsa, haba farida karki bada mata Mana " dayar zuciyar tace Mata, " to ke wane bada Mata zakiyi bayan mutumin Nan yasan sana'arku ya Kuma San makwancinku, to Wai ma duk wannan tunanin da kikeyi na meye taimakonki kawai yayi fa ......... "
       Ringtone din wayarsa ce ta katsewa farida tunanin da take yi, daukar wayar yayi da murya  kasa-kasa yace, " Assalamu alaikum, ranki ya dade barka da wannan lokacin "
      Daga daya bangaren umma ce ta amsa da, " barkanka dai Dan Albarka ya aikin? "
         Ya amsa da, "wallahi alhamdulillah, kashe bari na kiraki kar kudin wayar taki ya kare "
   Kashe wayar umma tayi.

     Wani kululun bakin ciki ne ya tokare farida jin yanda yake waya kasa-kasa cikin zuciyarta tace, " da alama da budurwarsa yake waya har yana cewa Wai ta kashe wayar zai Kira " guduntun tsaki ne ya subuce mata Wanda batasan itama kanta tayi shi ba.

    D'agowa yayi ya kalleta, aykuwa ta tsuke fuskar kamar ba ita tayi ba, murmushin gefen baki yayi sannan ya Danna number umma, tana dauka ya tashi zai fita Yana cewa, " yau tun safe kinji ban kiraki ba wallahi abubuwa ne suka Sha mun Kai " yana gama fada yasa Kai ya fice.
        Da idanu farida ta bishi tanajin wani haushinsa azuciyarta, komawa tayi ta kwanta ta rufe idanta kamar me bacci, Amma kamannin Sadiq ne keyi Mata gizo a  idanta, uwa uba kuma sautin muryarsa da yanda taji Yana magana kamar bayaso, sai Kuma haushi ya kamata tunowar da tayi da budurwarsa yake waya, Kuma ga dukkan alamu yanaji da ita.
      Motsin kofa taji tana bude ido taga ya dawo, Kara rufe idanta tayi kamar wacce bacci ya dauketa.
    Sadiq zama yayi ya dau wayarsa ya cigaba da yan danne-dannensa.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                13&14

       

       Farida nasan bude idanta tana gudun karya ce tana kallansa, shigowar nurse ce ta Bata damar bude idanta, sannu nurse din ta farayi Mata sannan tace, " yanxu me yake damunki? "
       " Ba komai" farida ta bata amsa.
      " Ok, to ki gyara yanxu zamu yi Miki allura " farida kallan Sadiq tayi taga shi ko ajikinsa sai ma Danna waya da yake yi, labulen da suke kare patient idan zasu dubashi inda wani aciki shi nurse din ta janyo, farida na matukar tsoron allura yanxu ma Haka suka Fara da nurse din, nurse din ce take Mata magana, " haba ke kuwa da girmanki kike gudun allura, wato kice dazu ma da bansa Miki acikin ruwa ba haka zamuyi dake.
         
        Farida sai mutsu-mutsu take tana juye-juye, gajiya nurse din ta farayi tace, " yanxu ya kike so ayi da allurar nan nifa tsoro nake ji kar naji Miki ciwo ko kirawo zanyi arikeki ne ? "
      Karaf Sadiq yace, " nurse inbata tsaya ba kimun magana sai inrike Miki ita? "
      " Eh wallahi da dai yafi, tun d'azu muke Abu daya " nurse din ta fad'a.
     Motsin shi taji alamun zai taso da sauri farida tace, " ni kayi zamanka yanzu zata yi mun "
     Ba Sadiq ba hatta nurse din sai tayi dariya, sumul kalau farida ta tsaya akayi Mata allura, sai dai kafin agama idanunta duk sun ciko da kwallah.
     Bayan nurse din ta fita  Kan gado farida ta koma ta zauna, tana goge Yar kwallar data zubo Mata. 

     Inna ce ta shigo dakin, Sadiq tashi yayi yace wa Inna, " mama ni zan wuce inajin sai yamma zan dawo  sunce sai gobe zasu sallameta, saboda sun ce maleria da typod ne suka ci karfinta, sai na dawo anjima, Sadiq na karasa maganar yayi hanyar fita.
        Inna godiya take ta zabga masa idanunta har sun Fara cikowa da kwallah, Sadiq ne yace, " mama nace bana bukatar godiyarku ay yiwa kaine " yana gama magana yasa Kai ya fice.

        Inna ta dade bataji farinciki irin na yau ba guri ta zauna tana kara tambayar farida saukin jikin nata.
       Sadiq na fita gurin aikinsa ya koma, sai bayan ya tashi daga aiki ya koma asibitin, leda ya ciko musu da yan kayan da zasu bukata na amfni sai abinci da ya siyo musu Wanda zasu ci harda su kayan fruit Dana shayi, Inna godiya take yi Masa wannan Karan harda hawaye, lokacin da zai tafi ya kwatantawa Inna gurin da ake sai shayi Koda da safe bai karaso da wuri ba.
         Yana karasawa gida bayan sun gaisa da umma tace, " Sadiq yau abun hawa ka rasa? "
      " Eh umma kinsan karfen nasara ne ba tabbas muna hanya adaidaita sahun ya tsaya, Kuma na Dan jima ban samu wani ba" Sadiq ya fad'a.
         " Oho nifa ince har na dau waya zan kiraka ay naji shigowarka" cewar umma.
        Tashi Sadiq yayi ya wuce dakinsa, umma ta bishi da kallo.

          Washegari da sassafe Sadiq na fitowa aiki ta asibitin ya Fara biyowa, Alhamdulillah jikinta yayi kyau fuskarta tadan cuko ba laifi.
        Bill yaje akarbo Abinda aka cajeshi dubu goma Sha bakwai, ta account ya tura musu sannan aka bashi receipt,  tambayar nurse din yayi ko zasu iya tafiya tace, sai Dr ya karaso zuwa 10 'o' clock, Inna ya kawowa receipt din ya gaya mata yanda sukayi da nurse din, dari biyar ya zaro ya mik'awa Inna yace suyi kudin mota.
         Inna tayi wa Sadiq godiya har ta rasa bakin godiya, sallama yayi musu ya wuce gurin aiki, ahakan ma yaso yadan makara.

       Su Inna basu suka karasa karkashin gada ba sai sai wajen Sha daya,  ga mamakin su suna zuwa suka ga gurin zaman su wayam ba dan kullin kayansu, sororo suka yi suka karasa gurin zanin gadon da suka tafi dashi asibiti shi suka shinfid'a suka zauna, Inna har tayi niyyar tambayar Yar tsohuwa sai ta tuna da masifarta hakan ne yasa ta kyaleta.

         Tun daga ranar Sadiq kullin sai ya tsallaka ya duba jikin farida, ganin ta warware yasa ya Dan dakata da zuwa.
      Atamfofi kala uku-uku da hijabai yasa aka dinkawa su farida ita da Inna, tsayawa yayi daga cen nesa ya tura wani yaro da ledar yace ya Kai musu, Yana ganin yaron ya karasa gurinsu ya maza ya matsa daga gurin, zuwa wani lokaci yaron ya tsallaka gurinsa ya dauki Dan kudi ya bashi.
       Yana daga gefe yake hango su Inna na ta yan dube-dube, zuwan cen Kuma yaga alamar suna cikin farinciki, girgiza Kai yayi Yana murmushi ya wuce gida.

Atamfofin da Sadiq ya saiwa su farida ba karamin kyau sukayi musu ba, akwana atashi Dan kudin da Sadiq yake ba Inna suka kare, haka farida ta tsallaka gidan man sai dai Bata tab'a jin kunyar bara ba sai yau, wani irin nauyi take ji tana Kuma jin kunyar haduwarsu da Sadiq.
       Tana shiga ta hango shi, rass gabanta ya Fadi cen gefe ta nemi guri ta zauna, Sadiq tun shigowar farida ya ganta ji yayi ransa ya b'aci da shigowar ta da sunan bara, yi yayi kamar be ganta ba ya cigaba da harkar gabansa sai dai lokaci-lokaci yake kallan  gurin da take zaune ta gefen ido.
          

    Farida kamar wata mara lafiya haka ta zauna Sam ta kasa zuwa tayi bara kamar yanda ta Saba, tana Nan zaune wata tamfatsetsiyar mota ta wuce Tasha Mai, me motar na Shirin fita ya dawo yayi, ya yi parking a gaban farida da ta hada hannu biyu tayi tagumi.
       Wanni dattojon Alhaji ne ya zuge glass din motar sa, sai da ya karewa farida kallo sannan ya lashe leb'an bakin sa yace, " yan Mata wannan tagumi haka, meye damuwarki yarinyarki dake Zaki dinga damun kanki, kyawawa fa basu cika shiga damuwa ba " Alhajin ya karasa fad'a Yana kashe Mata ido daya😉.
     Duk Abinda yake faruwa akan idan Sadiq, farida ce ta d'ago Kai tana murmushi, ahankali tace masa, " ba komai"
    " Inane gidanku "Alhaji ya tambayi farida.
   Shiru tayi bata bashi amsa ba ta cigaba da wasa da hannunta.

      Murmushi Alh yayi yace, " to ko zaki mun rakiya ne? " Alh ya karasa maganar Yana bude Mata kofa.
          Farida zaro manyan idanunta tayi, tana kallan gurin da Sadiq yake karaf suka hada ido, da sauri ta sunkuyar da Kai kasa.
     Kasa-kasa yayi da murya yace, " idan kinajin kunyar idan mutane bani address dinki sai inje insame ki.


Yanzun ma bata bashi amsa ba, kudi ya zaro yan bandir-bandir na dubu Daya ne akallah sun doshi dubu hamsin ko sama da haka, Mika Mata yayi Yana washe baki.
      
      Tashi tayi daga guri tayi hanyar fita da sauri ya bita, ta glass yake kokarin riko Mata hijabi aykuwa da sauri Sadiq ya  karasa ya fusge hijab, cikin tsananin b'acin rai ya fara yiwa Alh masifa, " Kai tsohon najadu ka wuce daga gurin Nan kar ranka ya b'aci, ka girma bakasan ka girma ba, Kai bakasan duk Abinda kayi 'yar wani ba sai anyi da taka ba " ganin yanda idanun Sadiq suka kada sukayi jawur yasa sum sum sum Alhaji ya taka motar sa yayi gaba da gudun gaske.
 
   Mutane har sun Fara taruwa ana kallansa wasu har sun Fara yiwa Alhaji tofin Allah tsine, ko kallan inda farida baiyi ba ya juya ya cigaba da harkar gabansa, guntun tsaki yaja afili ya furta dole ki daina barar Nan dole ne, ya Karasa magana Yana kaiwa bango duka.

       Abokin Aikinsa Ibrahim ne ya karaso gurinsa yace, " Easy my man, " Wai me yake faruwa da Kai ne, meye hadinka da gal din cen lokacin Daya naga duk sai tada jijiyar wuya kake yi, ko dai kana ciki ne karfa kamun shigar sauri Dan kwana kusa nake San kawai na sa Kai "

      Wani haushin ibrahim ne ya kamashi cikin fad'a ya ture hannun sa yace, " Dan Allah ka saurara mun bana San damuwa "
     Ganin fuskar Sadiq ba walwala yasa ibrahim wucewa ya cigaba da aikin gaban sa.

    Sadiq wayarsa ya zaro wata number ya Kira, dauka akayi ya Fara magana, " hello Abokina Dan Allah kasa a cigitamun gidan haya me daki biyu "

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                15&16



Daga daya bangaren amsa masa yayi da, " Sadiq lafiya wannan bugowa waya afujajan ko  sallama babu bare asamu gaisuwar kirki? Wai ma abokina me zakayi da gidan haya Kai da ba iyali ba, Kuma ni ko  ta group banga invitation card ba bare ince aure zakayi "

      " Look mustapha banasan yawan tambaya,  inzaka bada cigiya to inbazaka bayar ba insa wani ya cigitamun " cewar Sadiq.
     " To me yayi zafi haka mutumina da alama wani ne ya tab'o ka, nidai bani na kar zomon ba " mustapha ya fad'a.

   Kit Sadiq ya kashe wayar Yana huci.
       Farida na gefe atsaye kamar wacce kwai ya fashewa aciki, ganin yanayin da Sadiq yake ciki ba karamin tsoratata yayi ba, cikin zuciyarta take fadin, " dama Haka yake da masifa wannan mutumin tab wato Allah ya taimake ni Kila da na kula mutumin Nan da har marina sai yayi "
    Gajiya tayi da tsaiwa ta tsallaka kasan gada, Inna na ganin yanayinta ta fahimci wani Abu na damunta, gefen Inna ta samu ta zauna zugum tayi kamar me nazarin wani Abu, Inna ce ta katse Mata tunani da, " farida lafiya na ganki haka ko wani abun ne yake damunki? "
    " Inna kaina ne yake ciwo shiyasa ma na tsallako da wuri " cewar farida.

   "  Sannu Allah ya sawak'e ki duba cikin kayan cen akwai sauran paracetamol ki dauka ki sha " Inna ta fad'a.
    Tashi farida tayi ta hau bude kaya magani ta dauka ta Sha ta koma gefe tayi lamo kamar wata mara lafiya.

       Tun daga ranar Sadiq ya daukewa farida wuta ko tsallakawa yayi gurin Inna zai zauna su gaisa, yayi kamar baisan da farida agurin ba, idan ta gaisheshi ma sama-sama yake amsawa, hakan ba karamin sanyata cikin damuwa yayi ba, ita kadai idan ta zauna zata dinga jinta kamar wata me laifi.
      Damuwarta gaba take karayi musamman hukuncin da yanke akanta Wanda ya sanarwa da mahaifiyar ta washe garin ranar  abunda ya hadata da Alh, kudi masu Dan dama Sadiq ya diba ya kaiwa Inna sannan yace Mata bayasan yaga sun karayin bara ita da farida, duk Abinda suke bukata su tambayeshi zaiyi musu, Inna ba karamin Dadi taji ba saboda ita kanta adole take tura farida yin bara badan San ranta ba, tun daga ranar kuwa babu inda farida take zuwa ta zaune kusa da Inna, Sadiq kuwa idan ya je ya basu kudi masu Dan dama sai ya shafe sati bai koma gurinsu ba, sai ya kintaci kudin hannunsu ya kusa karewa sannan zai koma ya Kara musu wasu yayi tafiyarsa.

    Wannan hukuncin ba karamin tsanani yayiwa farida ba, rashin ganin Sadiq na kwana biyu duk sai ta ji ta ba walwala, wani lokacin idan Inna zata saimusu abinci ko wani kayan bukatar, karaf take tace zaje ta siyo sabosa ta samu tabi ta gurin da zata hangi Sadiq ko daga nesa ne, Inna sai ta hanata zuwa, wannan hukuncin ba karamin jefa zuciyarta cikin damuwa yayi ba.

       Duk ranar da Sadiq ya tsallako ta dinga jin nishadi kenan duk da ba wata kyakykywar gaisuwa ce ke shiga tsakaninsu ba, Inna tun Bata fahimci damuwar farida ba har sai da ta Fara ganewa, inna ce ta sata agaba tana tambayar ta ko akwai wani Abu da take damunta azuciya, ta tambayeta yafi a irga Amma amsar Daya ce zata ce Mata ba komai.

Sadiq da mustapha neman gida suke ba dare ba rana, duk gidajen da suke samu sai daki hurhudu ga dankaren tsada sai Kuma Wanda ya kasance da mutune aciki, wani gida suka samu a unguwar sheka zaman mutum uku ne d'aki biyu ankama d'aki Daya ne ya rage, ba laifi gidan yana da kyau ashekara dubu Ashirin ne.
    Biyan kudin yayi sannan ya wuce kasuwa yayiwa su Inna siyayya ledar tsakar daki yan kwanika da robobin amfani harda su rusho da kwanikan cin abinci, tabar ma guda biyu ya sai musu dasu butoci harda yan tukwane guda biyu, Sadiq yayi musu siyayya dai-dai misali irin ta me karamin karfi, dama akace gayawa me zuciya biki ba me dukiya ba.

Almajirai yasa suka wanke dakin suka shinfid'a ledar dakin sannan suka shiga da sauran kayan amfaninsu, key yasa ya rufe dakin bashi ya bar gidan ba sai bayan magriba.
     Sadiq bai karasa gurin su Inna ba sai kusan Tara na dare, lokacin da yaje sun kwanta har sunyi bacci, tsayawa yayi cikin tausayawa Yana kare musu kallo, gani yayi suna bacci lakadan hankali kwance kamar masu bacci a cikin daki, rasa yanda zai tashe su yayi ya yanke shawarar  zuwa safiya sai ya samesu da maganar.

   Washegari da safe, Sadiq ne ya karaso gurinsu Inna da sallama ya karasa gurinsu, Inna da mamaki ta kalleshi suka gaisa, saboda bai Saba zuwar musu da sassafe irin haka ba.

    Zama yayi sannan ya Kira Inna, " mama " amsawa Inna tayi cikin kulawa saboda yanda taji ya ambaci sunanta ga dukkan alama akwai magana Mai muhimmanci da take tattare dashi.
     " Dama wani Dan shishshigi ne nayi muku" Sadiq ya fad'a.

   Da mamaki Inna tace, " shishshigi Kuma? "
 "Inasan ku daina zama agurin nan saboda bai dace daku ba" inji Sadiq.
       Girgiza Kai Inna tayi tanajin wani yanayi azuciyarta tace, " Sadiq kenan, kasan komai kamawa yakeyi muma ba  asan ranmu muke wannan rayuwar ba Haka Allah ya kaddara mana, idan munada raban barin gurin Nan sai kaga wataran Allah ya hore Mana munbar gurin Nan "
" lokacin yayi mama, inasan ku tashi mutafi akwai wani Dan guri Dana Samar muku Ina ganin yafi Nan gurin dacewa da ku " Sadiq ya fad'a.
   Ba Inna ba farida ma zaro idanu tayi cikin mamaki Inna tace, " mahalli ka Samar Mana Sadiq "
   " Eh inasan ku tashi yanxu mu karasa "cewar Sadiq.

    Inna fashewa tayi da kukan farinciki sai tayi me isarta sannan ta Fara jerowa Sadiq addu'o'i ba adadi, farida ma godiya ta fara jero Masa tana karajin wani Abu azuciyarta data kasa fasaltashi.
     Kayansu suka dauka suka Sadiq ya tsaida Dan sahu suka tafi Kai tsaye unguwar sheka, suna shiga Sadiq yasa key ya bude kofar Inna na shiga dakin kanta ne ta fara juyawa ganin ko amafarki Bata tab'a zatan zata Kara samun mahalli irin wannan ba, kuka Inna ta fashe dashi me tsuma zuciya farida ma hawaye ne yake zuba a idanunta, Sadiq ji yayi idanunsa sun ciko da kwallah ya kaud kansa gefe yana karajin wani iri azuciyarsa. 
      Inna ce ta tsugunna har kasa tana yi Masa godiya, dakatar da ita yayi hannu biyu yasa ta dagota Yana Bata Baki.
     Duk kayan bukatar da ya siyo musu duk yayi musu bayaninsu, wata babbar roba ya bude ya nuna musu kayan abincin dake ciki, Inna ji tayi kamar yasata a aljanna farincikinta abayyane yake, bakinta yaki rufuwa Ina ka saka Ina ka ajiye haka Inna ta dinga yi da Sadiq sai da suka Dan tab'a Hira sannan Sadiq ya dau dari biyar ya bawa Inna yace ta rike a hannunta, karamar wayar hannu ya mik'awa Inna yace ta rike idan da abunda suke bukata kafin ya zo gurinsa sai ta Gaya Masa.
    Rasa bakin magana Inna tayi sai hawaye ne kawai da yake zuba daga idanunta.

Bayan kwana biyu su Inna har sun Fara sabawa da mutanen gidan, zaman su suke gwanin sha'awa saboda Inna taja girmanta suku a da yake yaran Mata ne duk cikin ba wacce Tai haihuwa hudu, shiyasa suka dauketa uwa farida ma tana girmamasu shiyasa zamansu yake gwanin birgewa.
     Lokaci-lokaci Sadiq yakan leka su ya Kai musu Dan abun alheri.
   Lokaci daya farida da Inna suka canja fatarsu ta murje, tsantsar kyau da hasken fatarsu ya Kara fitowa, idan bakaga gurin da suke zaune ba zakayi tunanin daga wani gidan babban me kudin suka fito, Inna aykawa tayi makota tana sanar musu tana wankau na yara da manya.

   Ba laifi jifa-jifa tana dan samu acewarta zaman Haka bashida Dadi, acewarta ko wani abun zata ragewa Sadiq na dawainiyar da yake musu.

    Sai zama yayi zama ake gane halin kowa, zaman Inna da matan gidansu Basu da matsala sai dai makocinsu Wanda dakinsa yake kallan na Inna shine ya Fara bullowa da farida wani hali, Sam Inna batasan wainar da ake toyawa ba.
    Idan Yana gida idanunsa na Kan farida, ta window yake zama Yana kare Mata kallo daga baya sai ya tsiri, sai yaga farida ta shiga band'aki sai Shima ya sab'i buta ya tafi band'aki want lokacin ko magana bayayi sai ya nemi tura kofa, tun farida Bata lura ba harta Fara hankalta dashi.
    Idan wanke-wanke Farida take yi, sai ya dauko brush ya fito Shima yaje bakin rariya yanayi Yana satar kallanta suna had'a ido zai Fara yi Mata murmushi Yana kifta Mata ido, har wankin matarsa yake debowa Dan ya zauna wanki musamman idan yaga farida na wasu ayyukan awaje. 

    Akwai ranar da Inna taje Yar kasuwar sheka siyayya har ta Kai magriba, ranar shikuma matarsa ta tafi biki, mlm d'ahiru na fitowa daga dakinsu fakar ido yayi Kai tsaye dakin Inna ya afka, farida na kwance tana game awaya sai ganin mutum tayi, ihu ta ke niyyar yi.

Duburburcewa yayi cikin rashin gaskiya yace, " farida dama gurin Inna nazo aran kudi ko bata Nan ne? " Ya karasa fad'a Yana karewa farida kallo.

       Watsa Masa harara tayi sannan tace, " Bata Nan "
   " Idan bazaki damu ba inasan muyi wata magana dake, ina fatan Zaki fahimceni ki bani hadin Kai " cewar mlm d'ahiru.

      " Ko ka fita ko inyi maka ihun kwarto, " farida ta fad'a cikin tsawa.
    Ba shiri mlm d'ahiru ya fice aguje.

    Fitarsa tayi daidai da fitowar dayar matar gidan tasu me suna fa'iza.
       Fa'iza gwalo idanu tayi tana wassafa abubuwa azuciyarta, afili ta furta, " lallai namiji munafiki ne wallahi bari mariya ta dawo kamar agabanta akayi.

_Yanzu wasan ya fara_🤣

_Ku karbeshi da hakuri banyi editing ba, Ina godiya da yanda kukaita cigiyata jiya Allah yabar so da kauna, inayinku irin sosai din Nan._🥰

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

              17-18


Mlm d'ahiru gabansa ne ya dinga faduwa Yana Addu'ar Allah sa fa'iza Bata ganshi ba, zama yayi gefen gado ta santar window Yana lek'e, sallamar mariya yaji da sauri ya haye saman gado yana Jan numfashi sama-sama, da sallama mariya ta shiga dakinta ga mamaki taga mlm d'ahiru kwance Yana Jan numfashi, cikin firgici ta Fara tambayar sa, " mlm lafiya me yake damunka? "
    Dan d'ago da Kai yayi kadan Yana kasa da murya yace, " kaina ne yake ciwo ga jikina duk wani irin tsuko Yana yi min " to kasha magani ne? " Mariya ta tambaya.
      " Eh Nasha d'azu kinsan saukin ne Sai ahankali " to Allah Kara afuwa " cewar mariya.

   Fa'iza da take alwala tanajin abunda mlm d'ahiru yake gayawa mariya rike Baki tayi tana mamakin makirci irin na namiji, daureta yayi da jijiyoyin jikinta ta yanda ba yanda zatayi ta iya gayawa wani abunda ta gani game da shi.
   Farida tana Nan zaune tana al'ajabin baragurbin hali irin na mlm d'ahiru, cikin zuciyarta tana tunanin tayanda zata gayawa Inna tunda abun nasa ya Fara wuce gona da iri, sallamar Inna taji da murna ta tari mahaifiyar ta kayan hannun Inna ta karba sannan suka zauna Inna tana nuna Mata kayayyakin da ta siyo a kasuwa.
         Suna Nan zaune bayan sallar Isha'i wani yaro ne ya shigo tsakar gidan Yana sallama, Inna ce ta fita ta amsa Masa sannan yace, " Wai ance ana sallama" Inna tace, " sallama Kuma? Ba Nan bane jeka cen dakin ko ka lekan wancen dakin " tana gamawq ta koma d'aki.

    Bayan fitar yaron ya Kara dawowa yace, " Wai ance sallama ake da farida " mamaki ne ya kama Inna sannan tacewa yaron " kace tana zuwa " farida zumburar Baki tayi tace " Inna nifa wallahi bansan me yin sallamar Nan kawai Zaki ce ina zuwa gaskiya ni ba inda zani "
    " To dama ay basai kin sanshi ba farida bakyau wulakanta Dan Adam ballanta mutumin da yace Yana sanka, Kinga ba Dadi ko da hikima sai kiyi Masa har ku rabu lafiya " cewar Inna.

    Hijabi farida ta dauka ta fita kofar gida, atsaye ta samu matashin saurayin cikin manyan kaya sai baza kamshi yake yi sallama tayi daga haka taja baki ta tsuke, murmushi yayi sannan yace, " barkanki da fitowa sarauniya Mata ina fatan kin wuni lafiya?" Lafiya klau " farida ta bashi amsa atakaice cikin tsuke fuska.
     " To da farko dai sunana khaleed nida iyayena muna zaune akasan layin Nan na yaba da hankalinki matuka shine nace Bari na miko kokon barata ko zan samu karbuwa " inji khaleed.
      Kanta na kasa Bata ce Masa uffan ba har ya gama magana, jin taki bashi amsa yace, " gimbiyar Mata ko bataji abinda na fada bane, inafatan dai banyi laifi ba " ummm bakayi ba" fatifa ta bashi amsa.
    " Masha Allah, Amma har yanxu Baki bani amsa ba " cewar khaleed.
     Adaidaita sahu ne yayi Parking daga Dan gabansu, kallan wajen farida tayi karaf wa idanta zai gano Mata sai Sadiq, gabanta ne ya yanke ya Fadi ta shiga zullumi, Bata dauke idanta ba kuwa suka hada ido tuni jikinta yayi sanyi, Sadiq ko ta kanta baibi ba ya wuce shige ciki gurin Inna, gaba daya farida Bata cikin nutsuwarta, khaleed ne ya katse ta yace, " my dear kinyi shiru ko inbaki lokaci ne?" Farida Dadi taji har cikin ranta tayi zuruf tace, " eh ka bani lokaci nayi nazari " sallama khaleed yayi Mata sama-sama farida ta sallameshi ta shige gida tana tunanin irin hukuncin da Sadiq zai yanke Mata.

Lokacin da tashiga d'akin Sadiq na zaune suna Hira da Inna, Da sallama ta shiga gefe ta zauna ta gaida Sadiq ga mamakinta cikin sakin fuska ya amsa Mata gaisuwarta sannan yace, " Kuma Baki turo sirikin nawa ya gaisheni ba? " Dan duburburcewa farida tayi cikin jin kunya tace, " ay ya tafi " Inna murmushi tayi tace, "aykuwa Baki kyauta ba da kince ga yayanki ku gaisa lokacin da zai shigo " shiru farida tayi tana mamakin Sadiq duk atunaninta bazasu kwashe ta Dadi ba, ganin kallan da ya watsa Mata lokacin da zai shiga Amma yanxu kamar bashi ba, uniform din makaranta islamiyya ya kawo wa farida sannan yace ta shirya washegari da azahar zai raka ta makaranta, godiya sukayi Masa me yawa da Addu'ar Allah ya Kara budi, Yar Hira suka taba da su Inna bai Dade ba yayi Musu sallama ya tafi.

   SADIQ na fitowa wasu matasa yasamu yake tambayar su inda Sadiq yake da yake ya Saba dasu saboda yawan zuwan da yakeyi yau da gobe, tiryen-tiryen suka kwatanta Masa layinsu khaleed da gidansu.
     Sallama yasa akayi da khaleed bayan ya fito suka gaisa sannan Sadiq yace, " Abokina nasan baka sanni ba ko?" Jin jina Kai khaleed yayi sannan yace, " tabbas bansanka ba waye Kai?"
     " Ni yayan farida ne" cewar Sadiq.
     Dan russunawa khaleed yayi cikin jin kunya yace, " ashe Yaya ne "
      Afakaice Sadiq ya watsawa khaleed harara cikin dakiya yace, " eh nine, dama nazo na Dan Gaya maka wata magana "
    " To inaji Yaya" inji khaleed.
    " Farida yarinya ce shekarunta basu wuce Sha biyar zuwa Sha shida ba, Dan Allah inasan ka dakata da zuwa gurinta saboda karatu take yi ka Bari sai zuwa Nan da wani lokaci, umarni nake baka karka Kara zuwa gurinta " cewar Sadiq.
   " Amma Yaya....." Katse shi Sadiq yayi, " ina ganin mutumcin ka ne shiyasa nazo har kofar gidanku, Dan haka ka kiyaye " Sadiq na gama magana yayi gaba yabar khaleed atsaye bude da Baki.
 
   Sadiq na tafiya zuciyarsa na Masa suya Yana mamakin Yana yake jin haushin duk wani Wanda ya rabu da farida, washe gari kuwa Sadiq ne ya Kai farida makaranta nurul Huda islamiyya, uniform din lemon green ne ba karamin kyau sukayiwa farida ba, anyi Mata interview ba laifi tana da kokari ta amsa tambaya bakwai cikin goma, aji uku aka sata Wanda suke izu Ashirin da biyar, acikin makarantar Sadiq ya fansar Mata da kur'ani da sauran litattafan da ake bukata.

Farida ta maida hankali sosai tana karatunta, abunda ya mamaki tun ranar da khaleed yaxo bai kara dawowa ba, Haka zalik tana yawan samari na zuwa Amma daga me zuwa Daya sai me zuwa biyu wani saurayi me suna shuriem shine ma yayi zuwa uku daga Nan bai Kara zuwa ba, abin tun baya damunta ita da Inna har ya Fara damunta, hakan ne yasa Inna take ta yi Mata addu'a Allah ya kawo Mata nagari.
    Abinda su Inna basu ba Sadiq yasa ana Masa gadin farida, duk saurayin da yaje gurinta zasu binciko Mai unguwar da yake shikuma Sadiq yaje yace masa shi yayan farida ne Kuma karatu zatayi, idan Yana Musu bayani Kuma jadadda Musu yake da babbar murya, ahaka har su Inna suka shafe wata shida acikin gidan.

     Mlm d'ahiru ganin waccen kurar ta lafa yasa yayin lamb'o yake neman hanyar da zai bullowa da farida da ita wacce asirinsa bazai tonu ba.

Wata ranar laraba Inna ce ta tashi da ciwon kirji sama-sama magani farida ta siyo mata a chemist ba laifi zuwan wani Dan lokaci ciwon ya lafa, ganin jikin Inna yayi sauki farida ta Musu girki ta wuce islamiyya, da yamma bayan ta dawo tana shiga d'akin ta samu Inna akwance tana Dan jin jiki.
     Da sauri ta karasa gurin ta tana fadin, " Inna lafiya ko jikin ne ya dawo?" Daga Mata Kai Inna tayi tana tafe kirji, da sauri farida ta dauki waya Sadiq ta Kira ta sanar masa, ko minti talatin ba'ayi ba sai gashi ya karaso, ganin yanayin da Inna take ciki ya bashi tsoro, farida fuskarta har tayi ja saboda kuka, me adaidaitan Daya kawo Sadiq shi suka shiga ya kaisu asibiti, agajin gaggawa aka bawa Inna, magunguna ne aka Bata sai allurai, olcer ta tayar Mata da alama ta Dade ajikinta, zuwan bayan sallar Isha'i jikin Inna yayi kyau, gida suka dawo Sadiq ya wuce siyan sauran magungunan da babu aciki.
      Tun shigowar su Inna idanun mlm d'ahiru na kansu shi atunaninsa unguwa suka tafi, bayan sun zauna ruwan wanka farida ta dorawa inna da yay zafi ta sirka ta Kai Mata, wanka Inna ta tashi ta shiga aykuwa tana shiga mlm d'ahiru yayi wuf ta shige d'akin su farida, kasancewar matarsa Bata Nan itada fa'iza sunje suna kanwar mariya ta haihu.

   Farida na ganin mlm d'ahiru taja da baya tana Shirin kurma ihu, da karfi ya fisgota ya makure yasa hannu ya toshe Mata Baki, kokawa suka farayi farida na Kai Masa duka da yakushi, kokarin zura Mata hannu ariga yake Wanda yayi daidai da dawowar Sadiq daga siyan magani, sakin ledar yayi ganin irin halin da farida take ciki da abinda mlm d'ahiru yake Shirin aykatawa.
    Wani bagaggon naushi ya kaiwa mlm d'ahiru ta baya aykuwa ya rufeshi da duka da kutufo ta ko ina, mlm d'ahiru so yake ya gudu Amma ina Sadiq yayi Masa mugun ruko, Inna ce ta kawo Kai cikin dakin jin kokawa yasa ta saurin d'aga labule, salati ta rafka tana salallami ganin Sadiq na dukan mlm d'ahiru, ita mamaki ne ma ya kamata me yakai mlm d'ahiru cikin dakinta mutumin da ko hada ido da ita bayayi saboda girmamawar da yake Mata.

    Su mariya ne sukayi sallama cikin gidan turus sukayi suna kallan abinda ke faruwa, Sam basu gane wa ake duka ba, fa'iza ce tace, "Inna me yake faruwa ne yau me zamu gani haka?"
    Ajiyar zuciya Inna ta sauke tace, " nima abunda ya daure mun Kai musamman Dana ga mlm d'ahiru suna fad'a da Sadiq " mariya karaf tayi tace "wane mlm d'ahirun Inna? " Inna na Shirin magana sukaji an warb'o mlm d'ahiru tsakar gida Yana maida numfashi da kyar.

    Cikin huci Sadiq ya Fara magana, " Inna inasan ku hada kayan sawarku yanxu ba sai anjima ba zan maidaku cen gidanmu gurin umma " Yana rufe Baki ya Kara komawa Kan mlm d'ahiru zai cigaba da duka Inna ta rike Mai hannu tana dakatar dashi.

   Su mariya salati suke suna karawa, cikin takaici mariya tace, " mlm Wai me yakaika d'akin Inna, badai mugun halin naka ka dawo dashi ba?.

_Nasan kunsha kura-kurai banyi editing ba, kuyi sorry_🤣

[8/30, 7:48 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*
