*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   19-20




Tsumu mlm d'ahiru yayi ya sunkuyar da Kai kasa kamar Wanda ruwa ya cinye shi sai raba idanu yake kamar Dan yawan sallah, cikin bakin ciki mariya ta Fara magana, " wallahi mlm kayi asarar hali Allah wadaran wannan halin naka, ashe dama ka cigaba da munafurtata wato bayan sulhun da akayi Mana ashe baka fasa 'yan lek'e-lek'enka ba, zama nida Kai ya kare wallahi gidanmu zan tafi, ko kaine autan maza na barka har abada" mariya na gama magana ta shiga daki ta Fara hada kayan ta ajaka.
          Cikin magiya mlm d'ahiru ya Fara magana, " Dan Allah mariya ki taimaka karki tafi ki barni ki taimaki rayuwata idan Kika tafi bansan yanda zanyi da rayuwata ba, ki saurareni mariya kinsan duk maccen data fita ba da izinin mijinta ba akan wuta take tafiya mariya aljannarki na karkashin........"

Wani wawan mari Sadiq ya sakarwa mlm d'ahiru ya katseshi cikin zafin zuciya, " rufemana Baki muafikin banza munafikin wofi, kasan da Allah ka shiga dakin wasu gurin yarinya kake kokarin cin zarafinta, da girmanka da komai tashi muje wallahi sai yan vigilante sun hukunta ka, bazaka Sha abanza da wofi ba "
    Magiya mlm d'ahiru ya farayi wannan Karan kamar me Shirin yin kuka cikin firgici yake rok'on Sadiq, " Dan Allah kayi hakuri karka fitar Dani mutumcina ya zube a idan yan unguwa, wallahi nayi nadama bazan Kara ba wannan ma sharin shaidan ne......"
    Naushi Sadiq yakaiwa mlm d'ahiru abaki sannan yace, " kaima ay babban shaidan ne annamimi wuce muje " kiiiiiiii Sadiq ya farajan mlm d'ahiru zuwa soro,  Yana  fita dashi mutane suka zubo musu ido suna kallo, wani shagon yan vigilante dake cen tsallaken wata kwata ya mikashi Ya shaida Musu duk abinda ya samu mlm d'ahiru yake kokarin aykatawa, tun agaban Sadiq suka Fara sarrafa mlm d'ahiru, sai zunduma ihu yake Yana neman agaji.
      Sadiq na fitowa gida ya dawo inbanda huci ba abinda yakeyi, mariya ce ta fito da Yar Jakarta ahannu ta sharar kwallah, ko sallama batayiwa su Inna ba ta fice cikin takaici da bakin ciki.
      Inna gaba daya jikinta yayi sanyi kanta ya kulle ta rasa abinda yake faruwa itadai tasan ta tafi wanka tabar farida zauna afalo Amma ta fito ta tarar da wannan hatsaniyar, Wai ma meye ya hada su gashi mlm d'ahiru ya kasa katabus sai bawa Sadiq hakuri yakeyi, ajiyar zuciya ta sauke afili ta furta, " Allah ya kyauta "

     Sadiq ne ya karasa cikin gidan daga tsakar gidan ya tsaya yace, " inna ku Sanya kayan sawarku cikin jaka Bari na Kira abun hawa sai mu wuce, saboda hankalina bai gama kwanciya da gidan Nan ba" Yana gama maganar ya fice bai jira cewar Inna ba.

        Farida da Inna shiryawa sukayi tsab suka Sanya hijabansu, Yar ledar kayan su suka ajiye agefe, tunda dama kayan nasu basufi kala hurhudu ba suna jiran Sadiq.

     Bayan lokaci kadan Sadiq ne ya shigo cikin gidan ya sanar Musu da zuwan adaidaita sahu, ba Musu suka dauko ledar kayansu suna yiwa fa'iza sallama, abin duniya tuni ya ishi fa'iza afili tacewa Inna, " yanxu Inna Kuma tafiya zakuyi ku barni ni kadai agidan Nan kamar mayya, oh yau ninaga ta kaina  wallahi mlm d'ahiru ka zame Mana  masifa, muna zaman mu cikin mutumci Amma rana daya ka tarwatsa mu aykuwa Bari baban Fatima yaxo wallahi nima bazan zauna agidan Nan ni kadai ba "
       Su Inna basu jira karshen xancen fa'iza ba suka sa kai suka shige adaidaita sahu, fa'iza har kofar gida ta rakasu tana d'aga Musu hannu idannunta fal hawaye.

      Sadiq shi ya dinga kwatantawa me Dan sahu har kofar gida aka kaisu, zuwansu kofar gidan shi yayi daidai da faduwa gabansa, sai yanxu umma ta fad'o Masa da irin dambarwar da zasu Sha da ita, cikin sanyin jiki ya wuce gaba su Inna suna biye dashi abaya.

    Da sallama suka shiga cikin gidan umma na d'aki tana jin radio, amsawa tayi cikin sakin fuska ganin Sadiq ne tafe  tace, " Auta Wai ina ka shiga tun d'azu nake cigiyarka wayarka ma na kirata akashe.. " sororo umma tayi ganin wad'anda suke biye da Sadiq, Yar dariyar yak'e take sannan tayi Musu maraba lale.
       Su Inna nashiga d'akin kasa suka zauna cikin hadin bakin suka Fara gaida umma, fuska asake ta amsa sannan tace, " haba Dan Allah ku tashi ku hau sama meye na zama a k'asa, Dan Allah ku zauna Kan kujera ties din Nan sanyi gareshi "
     Girgiza Kai Inna tayi sannan tace, " a'a hjiya Nan ma ya isa mungode "
    " Sadiq debo Musu ruwa Mana " cewar umma.
       Sadiq Dadi yaji ganin yanda umma ta sakarwa su Inna fuska, da karfin gwiwarsa ya tashi ya deb'o Musu ruwa acikin jug.
      Shiru ne ya biyo baya Dan numfasawa umma tayi sannan tace, " Sadiq bangane fuskokin su ba Kuma naji bakamun bayani ba "
      D'an Sosa Kai Sadiq yayi sannan yace, " amm dama umma bayin Allah Nan ne danake baki labarinsu da nace Miki suna bukatar taimako, kinsan kwanaki nace Miki basuda mahallin zama akasan gada suke kwana, to daga baya Kuma sun samu mahalli anbasu hayar d'aki, sai kudinsu na wata shida ya kare basu samu damar biyan na watannin gaba ba, to shi ne me gidan daga baya ya tashi duk mutanen gidan, ya basu notice duk sauran mutanen gidan sun tashi sai su kadai ne basu da kudin da zasu nemi wani gidan kuma yanxu inda suke zaune akarkashin gada gwamnati tasa ana kama mutane, saboda haka ne me gidan nasu yace idan suka Kai gobe sai dai su Debi kansu da kayansu a kofar gida, shi ne nace su zo su D'an zauna anan da kwana biyu, da farko ma ita mama taki amincewa tace karta takura mana, nikuma nasan umma bakida damuwa shine nace suxo mu taho tare, inyaso zuwa gobe sai aje akwaso Musu kayansu kafin Allah yasa ita mama ta samu sana'ar da zata iya kama Musu gidan da zasu koma "

        Ba farida ba hatta Inna ido ta zubawa Sadiq tana mamakin maganganun da taji ya jerowa mahaifiyar sa.
     Ajiyar zuciya umma ta sauke sannan tace, " duk naji bayaninka Sadiq Kuma na gamsu, Amma inasan zanyi muku wasu tambayoyi Kuma inasan ku bani amsa  tsakaninku da Allah, kinsan duniya ba yarda Kuma mutum ya zama abin tsoro, kinganni nan zuciya ta daya zan zauna daku ku rike gaskiya da amana, kar muzo sai mun saki jiki daku ku cucemu ku gudu ku barmu, yanxu dai dare yayi mu barwa zuwa safiya  idan Allah ya kaimu"
       Sadiq Dadi yaji kamar Wanda akayiwa babbar kyauta yasan ko ba komai umma zata yadda su zauna daga baya Kuma zai Mata wata dabarar har ta amince da zamansu.


Inna juyawa tayi tana kallan umma tace, " mungode kwarai da gaske hajiya Allah saka da alheri Allah Kara girma, insha Allah Zaki samemu masu amana Kuma ashirye nake na amsa Miki duk tambayoyin da zakiyi Mana, mungode mungode hajiya Allah ya biya bukata "
      Farida ma godiya ta shigo jero wa umma, cikin sakin fuska umma tace, " 'yan Mata ke Kuma meye sunanki? "
     Murmushi farida tayi tace, " suna na farida "
      " Masha Allah suna me Dadi " inji umma.

     Sadiq sai yanxu hankalinsa ya kwanta, tashi yayi ya wuce d'akinsa Yana shiga fadawa yayi Kan katifa Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya, afili ya furta " Alhamdulillah, Allah ya Kara Dora mu akanki umma "
    Umma taliya ta dafa Musu dama tana da sauran Miya, ta zubo musu ci sukayi suka Sha ruwa inna ta Sha magungunan ta, zaune suke agefe kujera jugun-jugun.
        Umma ce ta shiga cikin d'akin Sadiq, akwance ta sameshi  gefe ta samu ta tsaya tace, " Sadiq ka kawo mun mutane ban sansu ba bansan asalinsu ba ina tsoron yanda rayuwa ta lalace, har yanxu xuciyata rawa take akansu zuwa gobe da safe zan tambayesu inji asalinsu, to wai Sadiq su basu da kowa agarin Nan ne? Anya mutanen kirki ne kuwa, yanxu ace ko kaza baka da ita da zakace wannan Dan uwanka ne, Wannan fa abun dubawa ne, Allah sa dai ba cikin shegen ta tayo ba iyayenta suka ji haushi suka korota, amma ace basuda dangi ko kadan "
       " Mama insha Allah ba abinda zai faru sai alheri, mu dinga kyautatawa junanmu zato umma baiwar Allah Nan gani nake ba zata aykata abinda kike fad'a ba, idan Allah ya kaimu sai ki tambayeta Dan muji asalinsu da tushensu " Sadiq ya fad'a kasan zuciyarsa Sam baiji dadin maganganun umma.
        Juyawa umma tayi ta fita cikin zuciyarta dai Bata gama yarda dasu Inna ba.

      Dakin ta shiga su Inna na zaune inda ta barsu tuni sun gama cin abinci, umma ce ta Kira farida tace, " farida shigo d'aki ga katifa Nan zo ki dauka ki shinfid'a muku afalo "
       Ba Musu farida ta shiga ta dauko katifa ta shinfid'a Musu, net umma ta miko musu sannan tayi Musu sai da safe ta shiga daki.

    Washe gari da safe tunda bayan sallar asuba Inna da farida basu koma ba sai 'yan share-share sukeyi, kafin wani lokaci tuni sun gyare gidan tsab, da mamaki umma ta fito tana kallan gidan gaida ta sukayi sannan  suka koma gefe suka zauna.
    Cikin jin dadin abinda su Inna sukayi umma ta Fara magana, " yanxu tun bayan tashin ku Baki koma ba Haka kuka yita aiki ayda kun Bari ankarya anci abinci "
     Murmushi Inna tayi tace, " ay aikin bame yawa bane "
    " Ay duk da haka dai " cewar umma.

   Farida da umma tare suka hada kayan breakfast bayan sun karya suna zaune suna Dan Hira umma tace, " to yakamata muji tarihinku da asalinku maman farida, amma ina fatan dai Baki mun mummunar fahimta ba, kinsan duniyarce tsoron tsoro akeyi " umma ta karasa maganar tana Dan murmushi.

       Inna ce ta danyi murmushi tace,  " haba hajiya babu komai ni banga laifinki ba, mutane ne suke koya maka hankali Kuma yanzu rayuwar ta zama yanda ta zama, to nidai sunana *HALIMA* amma amfi kirana da *SADIYA* ni asalina ba  'Yar kasar Nan bace, ni da iyayena yan asalin kasar *SUDAN* ne.

[9/1, 3:50 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

😭 *TSANTSAR BUTULCI*😭

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   21&22



A lokaci Daya Umma da Sadiq suka kalli juna da yanayin mamaki afuskokinsu jin cewar Inna tace ita Yar kasar Sudan ce, Amma ba abin mamaki bane idan akayi duba da yanayin kyawun surarta, ita kanta Inna ta lura da yanayin da su Umma suka shiga jinjina Kai tayi idanunta tuni suka sauya kala zuwa ja Tunowa da rayuwarta ta baya, ajiyar zuciya ta sauke ta cigaba da cewa.
      " _Mahaifina sunansa Abdallah Muhammad mahaifiya ta sunanta Fatima, dukkaninsu yan kasar Sudan ne, mu biyu ne agurin iyayena nida kanwata Khadija, mun taso cikin gata da wadata mahaifina me kudin gaske ne agarinmu shida yan uwansa_"
     " _Mahaifina su goma ne agurin mahaifinsu, su biyar uwa daya uba Daya sai 'yan ubansu biyar, family mahaifina babban family ne saboda tun tasowarsu auran zumunci sukeyi ajunansu basa Bari wani daga cikin su ya auro bare, Haka zalika basa Bari su aurawa wani bare 'yarsu, intakaice muku labari ma lokacin da kakanmu Muhammad Yana Raye duk ababban gidan suke zaune 'yayansu da jikoki, bayan rasuwar sa ne da Aka Musu rabon gado wasu daga cikin su suka fita suka sayi mahallansu suka ware su da iyalansu acikinsu kuwa harda mahaifina, su uku ne suka fita sauran bakwai din suna zaune waje Daya, kafin sauran 'yan uwan Babana su amince da zaman su mahaifina awajen gida Saida aka kai ruwa Rana da kyar babban wansu ya amince._"
     _Munyi karatun mu tundaga primary har zuwa secondary daga Haka Bamu cigaba ba kasancewar akidar dangin mahaifina ne ba'a cigaba da karatu sai ayiwa Yarinya aure da Wanda suka daidaita dashi acikin dangi, wannan akidar a iya Kan Mata kawai ta tsayawa na family mu, saboda mazan cikinmu har kasashen waje suke fita karatu su karanci duk fannin da suke bukata, tun ina karama Allah yasamun kwazon karatu da kaunarsa Amma Haka na hakura saboda nasan ba abune da zai taba yuwu ba._
         _Afannin ilimin addini munayin karatu me Dan zurfi saboda bama rufa shekara goma Sha takwas Bamu sauke alkur'ani ba, Kai kafin ma asamu makarantar Boko sai munyi nisa a makarantar islamiyya tukunna, hakan ne yasa idan kika shiga family mu zakiga muna gudanar da rayuwarmu gwanin ban sha'awa._

" _Dafarko na tashi da akidar San karatun Boko Amma ahankali mahaifiyata ta dinga nusar Dani akidun danginsu, harma ta bani labarin rayuwarta da yanda itama ta tashi da San ta karanci fannin lafiya, Amma ba yanda ta iya tana yin candy da wata biyu aka daura Mata aure da mahaifina, Haka ta hakura ta fawwalawa Allah lamuranta._
        _Wata ranar juma'a da yamma nida kanwata Khadija mun shirya mun tafi siyayar kayan kwalliya awani babban mall dake cikin gari me suna *_ASLAM STORE,*_ _babban store ne daya amsa sunansa duk wani abun bukata babu abinda zakaje nema baka samu ba, muna tafe muna Yar hirar mu nida Khadija arashin sani na bamgaje wani bawan Allah harsai da kayan hannunsa suka Fadi, da sauri na sunkuya zan kwashe Masa kayansa hakan ne yayi dai-dai da sunkuyawar shi shima Yana shin kwashewa, da kallo ya bini dashi har na gama kwashe masa kayan sa na Mika Masa baisan inayi ba, harsai sai na maimata Masa sau biyu sannan ya karba, juyawa mukayi zamu tafi yace, " me b'arin Kaya kina ganin kin gama biyana ne?_ " 
     _Da mamaki na juyo Ina kallansa nace, " ay na kwashe maka to me yayi saura?"_
        " _Kalli yanda Kika yaga mun leda ko hakuri Baki bani ba idan nace sai kin biyani fa?"_ ya fad'a cikin kwaikwaiyar muryata.
      _nida Khadija dariya mukayi banbashi amsa ba har ya karaso wajenmu sannan yace, " me 'barin kaya ya sunanki ne ? " Kauda Kaina nayi gefe Khadija tayi farat tace, " sunanta halima, Kuma wallahi sunan daka sa Mata ya hau da ita saboda ko agida karka ga yanda take yiwa Ammi barna"_
   _Harara na zabgawa Khadija ko ajikinta kamar wacce ta Dade da sanin sa Haka suka dinga Hira dashi harda musayar number waya, Ina gefe Ina kallansu har sukayi sallama mun juya zamu tafi Khadija tace Masa, " ka Mana wayo kai bamuji sunanka ba?" Murmushi yayi yace " sunana malam" nida Khadija muka kalli juna bamuce komai ba muka juya muka tafi gurin biyan kudi, karasowa yayi gurinmu anayin total ya zaro kudin ya biya Mana sai alokacin na Kara magana, itama kuma godiya ce nayi masa daga Haka mukayi sallama muka wuce gida.

      _Da daddare muna dakin mu nida Khadija sai ga bakuwar lamba ta shigo Khadija ce ta dauka da sallama, bayan sun gaisa ahirar su na fahimci Wanda ya kirata awaya, sun danyi Hira me tsayi sannan ta mik'omun waya, nokewa nayi nace bazan karb'a ba sai da kyar na amince na karba muka gaisa sai alokacin yake sanar Dani sunansa Hashim Ibrahim lamido, tun daga ranar kusan kullin sai Hashim Ibrahim Lamido ya kiramu awaya Amma mafi yawan hirar nidashi muke yi tun ina nokewa har na saki jiki dashi._
   _Wata shakuwa ce take Kara shiga tsakaninmu dashi kwatsam wata Rana Hashim ya furta mun kalmar so, memakon inji farinciki wani tunani na fad'o mun bakin ciki ya rufeni, Khadija na samu da maganar ita kanta ba karamin girgiza ta maganar tayi ba, wannan maganar da nake muku duk tsawon lokacin da mukayi muna Hira da Hashim babu Wanda ya sani daga Mahaifina har mahaifiyata, ana cikin wannan halin ne Hashim yace mun yanasan ya dinga zuwa Hira gurina, tas na bashi labarin akidar family mu na auran zumunci Amma dukda Haka Bai daina kirana ba kuma Nima ban daina biyemai muna soyayyarmu ba._
     _Zazzafar soyayyar ce ta shiga tsakaninmu harta kaiga idona ya rufe Sam akan akidar family mu, hatta Khadija da take gayan inrabu da Hashim saboda karsu Ammi su gane haushinta nakeji, hakan ne yasa ta kyaleni ta zuba mun ido inacin Karena babu babbaka._
       _Takurani Hashim yayi akan sai yaxo ya nemi izinin Mahaifina, nikuwa tuni idanuna sun rufe ko dar banji ba Haka naje na samu Mahaifina na sanar Masa, b'acin rai karara na gani a idon Mahaifina aykuwa ta inda ya shiga ba tanan yake fita ba, fad'a yake mun ba kakkautawa maganar da tafi d'aga mun hankali ta kuma daskarar dani agurin da ya sanar Dani cewar, aure ma za'a hadani da yaya maheer d'an yayansa idan ya dawo daga karatu Kuma baifi su sa wata shida ba, ayanda ta gayamun Yaya maheer baifi saura wata Daya ya dawo ba._

   _Ranar nayi kuka kamar Raina zai fita, tun daga ranar na koma shiru-shiru Dani ko abinci banaci idan yunwa ta dameni sai dai insha ruwan tea, haka kuma na kasa sanarwa da Hashim duk abinda yake faruwa, gaba Daya gidanmu yayi mun zafi gani nake kamar kowa ya tsaneni, gajiya nayi da halin da nake ciki Rana tsaka na hada kayana acikin jaka, nacewa Ammi zani babban gida inkwana biyu ko Mahaifina bansanarwa ba, sanin halin da nake ciki ne yasa Ammi ta kyaleni aganinta ko na Dan samu rangwamen damuwata._

_Sati na Daya da zuwa na babban gida Yaya maheer ya dawo jin labarin auranmu yasa yaketa wani shishshigemun Yana nannan dani, nikuwa ko ajiki na idan zanyi waya da Hashim sai na shige d'aki na kulle sannan nake wayata hankali kwance, ko intafi cen wajen garden inyi zama na Ina kallan shukoki Ina hirata cikin jindadi, wata ranar talata ne muka wayi gari da mummun tashin hankali da bakin ciki, bakin labari ne ya riskemu daga gurin Mahaifina, adaren ranar b'arayi sun shiga gidanmu da bindugu sunyi wa Mahaifina fashi da makami, abun haushin da takaici su wajen Rai bakwai sunyiwa Khadija fyade, lokacin da Mahaifina yake fad'a tuni Khadija na asibiti cikin mawuyacin hali har batasan ma waye akanta ba, lokacin da mukaje asibiti naga irin halin da take ciki kuka nake wiwi saboda duk me tausayi yaga halin da Khadija ta shiga bazai so Kara ganinta ba, kwanan ta Daya a asibiti rai yayi halinsa, badan kar nayi karya ba sai ince ko su Ammi basu kaini jin mutuwar kanwata Khadija ba, har akayi sadakar uku bana cikin hayyacina Kuma ban yarda Khadija mutuwa tayi ba, sai da Mahaifina ya dinga mun nasihohi sannan nadan dawo cikin nutsuwata, gaba Daya family mu sunji mutuwar Khadija saboda Yarinya ce me barkwanci gashi Bata da fushi, ta wannan silar ne hashim yasan gidanmu Nina kwatanta Masa yaxo yayi gaisuwa, sosai Shima Hashim yaji mutuwar Khadija saboda akwaita da shiga rai Kuma sunyi saboda da ita sosai._
     _Mahaifina ya Kai report Amma babu wani labari da ya biyo baya, karshe ma zargi aka dinga wad'anda sukayi fashin tuni sun haure kasar waje, haka ne yasa Mahaifina ya barwa Allah komai, bayan Arba'in din Khadija ne aka tsaida Mana da ranar aure nida Yaya maheer, tundaga ranar nayi sallama da farinciki na daina ci na daina Sha sai kuka, Ammi tun tana watsi Dani har abun ya Fara damunta ganin irin ramar da nayi duk nayi zuru-zuru kamar wacce ta shekara tana jinya, shima Hashim Dana sanar dashi ba karamin tashin hankali ne ya kamashi ba._

    _Wata Rana muna zaune afalo nida su Ammi Mahaifina ne ya kare mun kallo ganin duk yanda na fige na lalace, tambaya ta ya shiga yi aykuwa kamar Wanda ya bude famfo haka na barke da kuke inata zubar da hawaye._

[9/1, 4:10 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   23&24


_Idanu Mahaifina ya zubamun Yana mamaki, sai da naci kuka na nakoshi sannan ya tsare ni da tambaya, " Halimatu me yake damunki ?" Sunkuyar da kaina kasa nayi nace, " Abba bana sanshi " mamaki ne karara afuskar Mahaifina yace, " waye bakya so?" Da sauri na bashi amsa, "Yaya maheer " adan firgice Mahaifina ya kalleni Yana kauda Kai gefe sannan yace, " to wa kike so?" har tuntuben harshe nake nace, " Hashim" waye kuma hashim" gaba Daya suka tambayeni lokaci daya, "wannan na kwanakin Dana baka labari nace yace zaizo....." Marin da Mahaifina ya sakarmin ne yasa sauran maganar makalewa abakina, cikin tsananin fad'a ya Fara magana, "shashashar banza da wofi ni Zaki hada da yan uwana, wallahi kinyi kadan bazaki jawo wani bare ki kawo cikinmu ba, tun kafin ahaifeki ake auren zumunci adanginmu akanki bazamu fasa ba, tashi ki bani guri sakarya kawai. " Haka na tashi na shiga dakina na fashe da kuka, tun daga lokacin na daina fitowa Koda falo banida aiki sai kuka baci ba Sha, sai dai Ammi ta shigo ta rarrasheni ta Dan gayan maganganu masu sanyaya zuciya, ciwon kirji ne ya sarke ni idan ya tashi numfashi na kamar zai dauke ni kadai nake jinyata adaki ba Wanda na gayawa,  wata Rana ciwo ya ciwoni ko tafiya banayi da rarrafe na fito falo ina karasowa tsakiyar falo na Fad'i yaraf, Ammi ce ta sauko daga saman bene, ganin yanayin da nake ciki yasa ta karasowa da gudu tana kwalawa Abbana kira._
      _A mota ya sani aka wuce Dani asibiti likitoci suka rufu akaina, bayan wani lokaci wani likita ne ya fito yace yanasan ganin Mahaifina a office dinsa, Abbana shida babban yayan su ne suka shiga gurin likita kasancewar tun lokacin da aka kaini asibiti yasanarwa da 'yan uwa sa, Dr ne ya zame glass din idansa Yana goge gumi yace, " Alhamdulillah mun Bata treatment yanda ya kamata Amma Alh ya akai kuka Bari ciwon zuciya yake San kama 'yarku da kananan shekarunta, Yarinyar tana cikin damuwa sosai Saida muka Sha wahala kafin ta dawo dai-dai, abin farincikin dai da ciwon Bai kamata sosai ba Amma ya kamata akiyaye abunda zai b'ata Mata rai"_
     _Hankulansu ba karamin tashi yayi ba da jin abinda Dr ya fad'a, jikinsu asanyaye Haka suka fito daga gurinsa, bayan sun zauna babban yayan su Abbana Baba Hussain ne yake tambayar Abba, " dama akwai wani Abu da yake damun Yarinyar Nan ne Bamu sani ba?" Cikin yanayin damuwa Abba ya zayyanawa baba Hussain duk abinda yake faruwa tsakanin da Hashim da yanda mukayi dashi, jinjina lamarin yayi sannan yacewa Mahaifina, " babu komai mudai fatanmu Allah ya Bata lafiya sai musan abinyi, kayi kokari ka Nemo number yaron shi Hashim din zanyi Masa magana " d'ago Kai Mahaifina yayi yace, " haba Yaya wallahi ba abinda za'a canja game da lamarin aurenta da maheer, da ciwo ba ciwo idan lokacin mutuwarta yayi saita mutu " mubar wannan maganar zuwa wani lokaci itadai Allah ya Bata lafiya " inji baba hussain._
       _Kulawa ta musamman likitoci suka dinga bani cikin abinda bai wuce sati guda ba na warware suka sallameni, sai dai duk na rame na lalace gida muka koma nakoma  Yar gidan jiya wato tunanin yanda zanyi rayuwa batare da Hashim ba._
         _Wata Rana muna zaune baba Hussain ya Kira Abba yasanar dashi cewar akwai meeting adaren ranar Yana bukatar mu taho tare, hakan kuwa akayi da yamma gaba dayanmu muka hallara ababban gida jikina na bani akwai wani muhimmun Abu da za'a gabatar da har aka taramu gaba daya, bayan magriba aka zauna meeting kowa da kowa ya hallara agurin Yara da manya mazanmu da matanmu, baba Hussain ne ya Fara magana, " Assalamu alaikum, dukkan yabo da godiya sun tabbata ga ubangijin talikai salati da aminci su tabbata ga Annabi Muhammad sallallahu alaihi wasallam, nasan zakuyi mamaki kwarai da jin wannan meeting da aka sanar na gaggawa, mak'asudin taron ba wani Abu bane sai akan maganar auren Halima da maheer, halima inasan inji daga bakinki Kuma duk abinda Kika fad'a dashi zanyi hukunci, karki ji nauyin ko kunyar idanun jama'a ki cuci kanki shin kinsan son maheer ko bakyasansa? "_
     _Gaba Daya falon ido aka sakomun yan kuskus na tashi kasa-kasa tuni nayi tsilli-tsilli Ina raba idanu, hada ido mukayi da Abba ya sakarmin harara tuni na sauke kaina kasa, baba Hussain Kara mai-maita mun tambayar nayi, azuciyata kamar na zabi maheer Amma tuno irin soyayyar da muke da Hashim yasa nace, " ni Hashim nake so" salati Mahaifina rafka aykuwa tuni ya rufeni da duka da kyar baba harun ya karbeni, fad'a suke Yi Masa sosai sannan suka bawa maheer hakuri da cewar zasu zabar Masa Wata acikin sauran yaran, Mahaifina ne ya Fara magana, " Yaya kun Riga kun yanke hukunci banida ta cewa Amma ni wannan auren ba hannuwa aciki taje gata gashi Nan duk abinda ya biyo baya ba ruwa na" fad'a sosai yayyan Abba sukayi Masa sannan yadan sauko yace ya amince da aure Allah sa Albarka Amma kwandalarsa bazai fitarba da sunan zai saimun kayan daki, baba Hussain ne yace ya amince shi zai dauki ragamar komai da Haka akayi addu'a aka tashi daga taron, family sai tsegumi na akeyi kowa da abinda yake fad'a, masu zagina nayi masu cewa na birge nayi, saboda akwai wad'anda da yawa wannan al'adar take ci Musu tuwo akwarya, murna suke suna fadin watakila ta sanadina suma su Sami 'yancin auren wad'anda suke so._

_Sai bayan mun koma gida Ammi ta rufeni da fad'a kamar zata cinyeni, tunda nake bantaba ganin b'acin ran Ammi irin na ranar ba, sai alokacin nakejin Sam ban kyauta ba Amma da yake soyayya ta rufe mun idanu tuni na basar na cigaba da sabgata, Abba ma fushi ya dauka Dani daga gaisuwa ba abinda yake Kara hadani da shi Ammi ma da farko ta dau fushi Dani daga baya ne ta sauko muke Dan Hira da ita._
      Ajiyar zuciya Inna ta sauke sannan tace hajiya aynaga gwagwrmayar rayuwa, cikin San jin karashen labarin Sadiq yace, " mama Dan Allah karasa Mana inji so nake kafin na makara inji karashen labarin Nan " murmushi Inna tayi tace, " wannan labari bazai kammalu ba yanxu Sadiq ka tafi gurin aikinka kawai karka makara "

   " Wallahi Inna naji labarin ne akwai daukan darasi aciki " umma ce tace, " ka tafi gurin aikinka idan ka dawo da daddare sai ta cigaba da Bamu labarin, nasan ma labarin da daddare sai yafi Dadi "
       Ba karamin Dadi Sadiq ya ji ba tuni ya je ya Fara Shirin tafiya aiki ba jimawa yayi Musu sallama ya tafi.
      Wunin ranar umma dasu  farida tare suka dinga aikin gida,Dan wunin ranar umma tayi dasu harta Dan Saba dasu yanda ta lura Sam basida duhun kai gasu da San aiki.

Ayanzu umma ta fahimci Inna macece me hakuri ga kawaici Bata da San abun duniya ko kadan, yanayin halayyarsu yasa ta D'an saki zuciyarta dasu, ta Dan daina d'ari-d'ari dasu, bayan sallar magriba su Inna da umma suna zauna suna fira Sadiq ya dawo, da sallama ya shiga gidan Fallon umma ya shiga, farinciki ne ya kamashi ganin umma zaune dasu Inna suna Hira gwanin birgewa, guri ya samu ya zauna sukayi Masa sannu da zuwa, gaida su Umma yayi Farida shima ta gaisheshi, umma ce ta umarci farida data Kai  Masa abinci dakinsa, ba Musu ta tashi taje ta kaimasa ta dawo ta zauna, Sadiq ne ya farayiwa Inna magana, " mama yau bansamu lokaci ba naso naje da mota na kwaso Kaya Amma na dauki Hutu gobe insha Allah sai naje na kwaso su gaba daya "
        " Babu komai Sadiq kabi ahankali idan ka samu lokaci Sai kaje ka debosu " cewar Inna.
         " Eh ay na dauki permission gobe zan je da wuri, yauwa mama acigaba da labarin Ina fatan dai ba'a wuce ni ba " Sadiq ya fad'a.
     Murmushi Inna tayi tace, " a'a sai ka huta tukunna ko fa abinci baka ci ba "
      Farida tayi farat tace, " kyaleshi Inna so yake olcer ta kamashi "
      Umma tace wa Farida, " wallahi fa kodan itafa olcer Nan ance Wata ma ba yunwa ce take kawota ba, amma ka wuce kaje kaci abinci Sadiq idan ka gama sai kaxo acigaba da Bamu labarin, Dan Nima na k'agu naji cigaban labarin  "
      Dan tura Baki Sadiq yayi kamar Wata mace yace, "Nidai umma sai dai inzo inci inaci ana Bamu labarin "
      Farida dariya tayi ganin yanda yayi ba karamin birgeta yayi ba.
   Umma ce tace, " to Auta wato abunne ya motsa kenan, kaji kunya agaban kanwarka zakace zakayi Wata shagwaba yanxu ay ka girma tunda ga Farida Nan."
      Dariya sukayi gaba daya Sadiq ya wuce d'aki ya dauko Abincin da Farida ta kaimasa, dawowa yayi ya zauna sannan ya Fara cin abinci, ci yake Amma kamar Wanda baya San cin abincin Yana taunar ahankali.
     Farida cikin zuciyarta tace, " ji mutum cin abinci yake kamar me cin Kashi, mutum komai nasa sai yayi iyayi" 
     Kamar Wanda Farida ta fad'a asaitin kunnensa ya d'ago da Kai ya Watso Mata manyan idanunsa ldanunsa, wani shock taji ba shiri ta dauke kanta gefe kamar batasan yanayi ba.
    Umma ce tace, " mufa Nan duk saurarenki mukeyi maman Farida"

    Gyara zamanta Inna tayi ta cigaba da cewa.
     " _Baba Hussain shi ne ya Kira Abba yace Masa akarbo Masa number Hashim yanasan ganinsa, Abba shima Ammi ya turo ta karbi number Hashim agurina ranar ji nayi kamar wacce aka daukewa duk Wata damuwa azuciyata, Kai ji nayi kamar wacce akace ansa ranata da hashim._"

" _Umarni baba Hussain yabawa hashim akan yanasan ganinsa ababban gida, Haka kuma yasa aka Fara binciko masa halayen Hashim da Kuma iyayensa, ranar da na sanarwa da Hashim iyayena sun amince da aurenmu ba karamin farincikin ya shiga ba daga yanayin muryarsa zaka gane Yana cikin nishadi, ranar da baba Hussain ya sawa Hashim da yaje gaida iyayena sun yaba kwarai da hankanlinsa sun Kuma Kara jadadda Masa cewar zasu bashi aurena sai dai sunce sai sunyi tsatstsauran bincike akansa, kafin ya turo magabatansa ayi maganar aure, tundaga ranar su baba Hussain suka dukufa wajen binciken waye Hashim da iyayensa, nikuma tundaga ranar kamar wacce aka daukewa buhunhunan matsaloli naji duk Wata damuwa ta ta gushe, soyayyar mu muke sha nida hashim._
      " _Matsalar dana Kara fuskanta itace su baba Hussain sun gano Hashim ba Dan kasar Sudan bane, sun gano cewar Hashim kasuwanci ne yake kawoshi Sudan ya koma kasarsa ta haihuwa, wannan dalilin ne yasa su baba Hussain yace Allah ya kashesu bazasu bashi aure na ba, Hashim Haka ya tsugunna Yana rok'on iyayena Amma ba Wanda ya saurarenshi, Hashim ya maida babban gida kamar shagon kasuwanci sa kullin Yana kofar gidan azaune Daya ga wani ya fito daga cikin iyayena zai bisu ya dinga magiya, ganin Yana takura Musu yasa suka sa me gadin gidan yake korar Hashim kwata-kwata suka hanashi zuwa._

   " _Nikuma abangarena Abba ya karbe wayata acewarsa idan ya barni da waya kamar yace mu cigaba da soyayya da hashim._"

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  25&26



" _Wannan hukuncin da aka yanke akaina shine yasa ni komawa Yar gidan jiya, Nan da Nan ciwona ya Fara kokarin dawowa Ammi na lura da yananyin da nake ciki, ita ta samu Abba ta  sanar dashi cikin halin ko'inkula yace Allah ya bani lafiya, sannu sannu Ina cikin ciwo na har yaci karfina, Wata Rana Ammi taji shirun nawa yayi yawa tana zuwa d'akina ta sameni magashiyan Ina Aman jini, cikin tashin hankali ta sanarwa da Abba ba shiri aka kaini asibiti wannan Karan Saida na shafe kusan Wata guda Ina jinya a asibiti, ba karamin fad'a Dr yayiwa su Abba ba acewarsa meyasa zasu Bari adinga samun damuwa azuciyata gashi har ciwona yayi tsanani, su baba Hussain ne suka sauko daga hukuncin da suka yanke akaina, suka amince akan zasu aura mun Hashim, jin wannan labarin yasa na warke garau dani hakan ne yasa aka sallameni daga asibiti muka koma gida na cigaba da harkokina._"
   "  _A bangaren guda ashe shima Hashim na kwance Yana jinya wani abokinsa da suke gida daya harya yanke shawarar mayarda Hashim garinsu, kwatsam suka samu labarin neman da baba Hussain yake Masa, shima mik'ewa yayi garau kamar bashi ne kwance ba, sai dai duk Wanda ya ganshi zai fahimci ya rame duk ya lalace sai tsayi daya Kara, dama shi ba jikin kirki ba._"
     " _Albishir din aurena baba Hussain yayi Masa sannan yace Yana bukatar address dinsa wato address din mahaifarsa inda iyayensa suke zaune saboda zaisa ayi masa bincike, hakan kuwa akayi cikin abinda baifi Wata guda ba aka gama yiwa baba Hussain bincike dangin Hashim kuma Alhamdulillah ba'asamu Wata matsala ba, Kuma Hashim ya sanarwa da mahaifansa har suka zo neman aurena, Kuma aka tsaida aure aurenmu Wata uku._"
    " _Saidai har zuwa lokacin Abba Bai bani wayata ba Kuma Bai wani sakarmin fuska ba, akwana atashi har bikina ya mazo da Hashim Wanda alokacin ne aka hada maheer da madina, Rana Bata karya saidai uwar diya taji kunya, Wata ranar juma'a ne aka daura Aurenmu bikin Daya Tara dubban manyan mutane yan uwa da abokan arziki, anan cikin garin Sudan Hashim ya kama Mana gida me daki biyu Dan madaidaici, muna zaune abun mu gwanin ban sha'awa_"

      Numfasawa Inna tayi tace, " ko zamu Bari zuwa safiya "
       Kamar hadin Baki gaba daya suka ce, " a'a ki cigaba"
    " To shikenan, Amma ay yakamata ku tambaye ni inane asalin Hashim da tushensa"
      Umma tace, " to ay mu 'yan saurarene Dan Haka mun Baki wuk'a da nama "
         Inna mik'e kafarta tayi saboda tsamin da tayi Mata sannan ta cigaba da magana.

    " _Kamar yanda nace muku Hashim ba D'an kasar Sudan bane Dan Nigeria ne, awani gari me suna Adamawa bafulatani ne gaba da baya mahaifinsa sunansa Ibrahim lamido, matan sa uku Yanada 'yaya Ashirin da Daya uwargidan sa goggo habi tana da yara goma sai mahaifiyar hashim goggo fatsim d'anta daya hashim ta haifi Yara uku duk sun mutu sai ta ukun goggo Abu itama yaranta goma, duk cikin y'ayan baba lamido maza ne yaransa Mata biyu, Hashim shine D'an baba lamido na bakwai shi kadai ya zage yayi karatun Boko har zuwa secondary, sauran 'yan uwansa duk sunfi maida hankali a fannin noma da kiwo, shima Kuma Hashim ya samu kwarin gwiwa ne daga gurin mahaifiyar sa, kasancewar ta mace ce me San ilimi, Hashim na fuskantar matsaloli iri-iri daga gurin 'yan uwansa, basasan cigabansa ko kadan, sannun ahankali bayan ya gama secondary wani kawunsa kanin mahaifiyar sa ya nemi alfarma gun baba lamido akan zasu dinga fita Sudan da Hashim Kai Kaya suna Saro wasu suna siyarwa saboda shi bashida d'a namiji yaransa duk Mata ne, da kyar Mahaifinsa ya yarda aykuwa hakan ne yasa su goggo habi suka Kara hadewa goggo fatsim Kai, cikin ikon Allah su Hashim suke kasuwanci Allah na Kara sa Musu Albarka, shekarar su uku da Fara kasuwanci kawunsa ya kwanta jinya Bai Dade ba Rai yayi halinsa, dukiyar kawunsa gaba Daya ya tattara ya maidawa matar kawun nasa daga baya akaimusu rabon gado_"

     " _Ganin yanda Hashim ya ke gudanar da kasuwancinsa cikin gaskiya da amana yasa Mahaifinsa ya d'ank'a Masa duk dukiyarsa domin shima ya cigaba da kulamai da ita, da farko 'yan uwansa sunyoo caaa suka ce bazasu yarda ba sanin halin Mahaifinsu yasa suka fidda mafita daya na cewar ahada Hashim da babban yayan su wato Yaya Ali, hakan akayi sun Fara kasuwanci kamar gaske Yaya Ali ya Fara fito da wadansu miyagun halayensa, wani lokaci ma da Hashim yaje gida Hutu waya yayo Yana kuka Wai b'arayi sun fashe shagon sun kwashe duk kudin ciki, Mahaifinsu Sam Bai yadda ba hakan ne yasa ya ce sai ya biyashi kudinsa, gudun rigima ne yasa Hashim ya Debi kusan fin rabin jarinsa ya biya Mahaifinsu dasu, da kyar yashawo Kan Mahaifinsu akan cigaba da kasuwancin ya amince Amma da cewar Yaya Ali bazai Kara bin Hashim da sunan kasuwanci ba_"
      " _Hashim na cigaba da business dinsa Harka ta Kara bud'a Musu, sai dai bayan shekara ko wata shida ya shirya yazo yagaida iyayensa ya koma, Wata Yar uwarsa me suna wasila itace ta kwallafa ranta akan Hashim ita bilhakki sanshi take, 'yar kanin babansa ce me suna Inna raliya, tuni Inna raliya farincikin ya kamata acewarta wasila zata koma Sudan da rayuwa Hutu zai ratsa ta ko suma sa dandali abin arziki, duk abinda akeyi Hashim Bai sani ba sai wani zuwa da yayi mahaifinsa yake sanar Masa, atake ya sanarwa da mahaifinsa cewar bayasan ta shi ya samu matar aure, wannan labarin ba karamin b'atawa Inna raliya rai yayi ba, Bata gama tsinkewa da lamarin ba sai da ta ji labarin sa ranar aurenmu danma tana tsoron baba lamido saboda mutum ne da baya jurar raini, hakan ne yasa ko cikin gidan suke shakkar aikata wani abun Koda Zasuyi saidai suyi ab'oye, ahaka har auranmu ya gabato ba karamin rudewa sukayi ba ganin irin dukiya da Hashim ya narka mun ba,  saboda duk Wata al'ada da akeyi agarinmu ta aure sai da Hashim yayi mun, hatta iyayena sai da suka jinjina Masa da irin kokarin da yayi bayan ankai kaya lokacin aure nayi aka daura Mana aure, mutum bakwai ne sukaxo daurin aurenmu daga bangaren Hashim saboda yanayin nisan kasarmu, mun tare da kwana uku duk suka juyo kasarsu._"


" Wannan shine takaitaccen tarihin Hashim wato Mahaifina farida da irin rayuwar Daya taso acikinta" cewar Inna cikin yanayin sanyin jiki, idanunta tuni suka Fara zubda kwallah.
   Gaba Daya sai jikinsu yayi sanyi, Sadiq ne yace, " sannu mama kiyi hakuri ga dukkan alamu naga wannan labarin kamar ya fama Miki wani ciwo dake zuciyarki, mu Bari zuwa safiya inyaso sai ki cigaba " ya karasa magana Yana mikowa Inna ruwa me sanyi.
     Karba tayi tasha sannan Sadiq ya fice ya tafi d'akin sa, umma kasa magana tayi sai daga baya ta tashi ta shiga d'aki su Umma suka yi shinfid'a suka kwanta.

   Washegari da safe ma Haka su Inna suka gyara ko ina suka share ko ina, bayan sunyi breakfast Inna ta d'orada labari ta daga inda ta tsaya.

" _Bayan bikinmu da Wata shida ne muka shirya nida Hashim mukaje garinsu, naga karramawa wajen mahaifi da mahaifiyar sa, munyi Musu tsaraba Sha Tara ta arziki Ammi ma ta bani Kaya masu yawan gaske na Kai Musu, satina Daya agidan su na fahimci yanayin zaman gidansu, mahaifiyar hashim tana kaunata sosai tanaji dani ko aiki Bata barina nayi me yawa, satinmu shida muka tarkato muka dawo Sudan da tsarabar Adamawa me yawan gaske, iyayena sun ji Dadi ganin irin abun arziki da aka had'omun da irin labarin da nake basu na karramawar da sukai mun, Abba ma ya ya D'an Fara sakkowa ba laifi muna Dan zama da shi muyi Hira sab'anin da,   da gaisuwa ce kawai take hadani dashi._"

      " _Shekarar mu Daya da rabi da aure mahaifin Hashim ya kwanta ciwon ajali, ko sati biyu baiyi ba Allah yayi Masa rasuwa ba karamin tashin hankali Hashim ya shiga ba, awashe gari muka shirya muka tafi Nigeria, tuni anyi Masa sutura anbinne shi, duk Wata d'awainiya ta zaman makoki Hashim ne yakeyi har akayi sadakar bakwai, muna zaune agidan su Hashim har sai da akayi sadakar arba'in sannan muka koma sudan, wani Abu Daya faranta raina yanda naga iyayena gaba dayansu sunzo gaisuwar mahaifin Hashim harda abun sadaka suka bayar kwanansu Daya suka koma._"
     
  *MAFARIN MATSALA*
    " _Bayan dawowar mu  Sudan da Wata uku 'yan uwansa suka bukaci Hashim yaje suna San ganin sa cikin gaggawa, kwana biyu dayin wayarsu ya shirya ya tafi satinsa biyu ya dawo yaxo mun da maganar da ta d'agamun hankali cewar zamu koma cen Nigeria da zama, Sam bai gayamun dalilin komawar mu ba Haka naje cikin fargaba na sanarwa da iyayena, ba karamin Kai ruwa Rana akayi ba kafin Mahaifina ya aminci Amma ba karamin b'acin Rai ya shiga ba Dan yayi fushi dani me tsanani, ahaka nayi sallama dasu ina kuka Ammi ma tana kuka muka tsallako k'asa Nigeria, kafin muje tuni angyara Mana wani d'aki muna zuwa acen muka sauka_"
 
    Inna ta d'ago da kanta cikin hawaye tace, " nasha bakar wuya agidansu mahaifinki farida, rayuwar daka ga muna yi ta karkashin gada me saukin ce Sadiq, tafimun kwanciyar hankali akan wacce nayi a Adamawa" ta karasa fad'a hawaye na Kara wanke fuskarta.
    Ajiyar zuciya umma ta sauke cikin tausayawa tace, " ki daina kuka sadiya Allah shine gatan bawa, komai na rayuwa me wucewa ne Kuma wannan jarrabawa ce ubangiji yake yi muku"
    
    Inna ta share hawaye tace, "Haka ne" sannan ta cigaba.
   " _Bayan mun koma Adamawa ne aka Fara maganar raba gadon su Hashim, iya dukiyar Mahaifinsu ya dauko ya d'ank'a Musu suka ce Sam basu yarda sudai sunsan duk dukiyar ba da take hannun hashim ta Mahaifinsu ne, wannan rikicin ne ya Kai su Hashim har kotu, da yake sunfishi yawa Haka suka hade Masa Kai sukayi kule-kukensu har Alkali ya wajabtawa Hashim ya bayar da kudin gurinsa gaba Daya arabasu cikin gado, munaji muna gani Haka Hashim ya kwashe duka kudin hannunsa ko tsinke Bai rage ba ya dauka ya basu akayi rabon gadon, wannan rabon gadon ba karamin annoba ya zame Mana ba.........._✍🏻


*Nayi muku typing da yawa gobe da kyar zaku samu updated*😆

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   27&28


" _Bayan da Hashim ya basu duka dukiyar hannunsa sai ya buk'aci suma da su maido duk gonakin baba lamido wad'anda suke nomawa, Haka sukayi tsalle suka dire sukace Sam Babu sauran gonar baba ahannunsu acewar su wai kafin yarasu duk ya karbe dukiyarsa ko tsinke baibar Musu ba, ba yanda Hashim ya iya haka zuba Musu ido, Hashim ya bawa 'Yan uwansa kudi duk atunaninsa za'ayi rabon gado ne cikin 'yan kwanakin Amma abun mamaki har aka shafe Wata uku ba maganar gado babu alamunsu, kamar yanda na Gaya muku gidan su Hashim babban gida ne me cike da iyalai da yawa, farkon dawowa ta yawancin matan gidan sun nunamun kulawa ashe duk badan Allah bane saboda abun hannuna ne, 'yan kudaden gurina dasu muke komai na bukatar yau da kullin, har girki ma dama nawa nakeyi ni kadai duk wasu abubuwa daya danganci matar gida to nawa nakeyi ni kadai abina, idan na girka abinci zan kaiwa goggo fatsim nata inkuma wanke-wanke zanyi shima inhad'o da nata, saboda wanki ma sai dawowa ta Nigeria na koya har nakeyi wani lokacin nayi wani lokacin Hashim ne yake mana, sannu ahankali akace zara Bata barin dami zuwa wani lokaci duk kudaden hannuna ya kare, sarka da D'an kunnena nace zan siyar Hashim ya hanani acewarsa Bai kamata na Fara saida kadarorina ba,  muna haka ne har abinci yafara yi Mana wahala wani lokacin ma goggo fatsim ce ta ke bamu nata Wanda ake dafa wa na hadaka gaba Daya, abangare daya Kuma ga wariyar da facalolina suke nunamun ko kadan basa Hira Dani idan wucewa nazo yi zan je gurin goggo ko zan shiga  band'aki saidai inji sun sheke mun da dariya, kullin rayuwar d'aki nakeyi idan kuma na ji zaman ya isheni sai intashi inshiga gurin goggo mudanyi Hira inkuma Hashim na gida banida damuwa, banida ikon aiken yaro acikin gidan Ina Kiran yaro uwarsa zata Fara tsigaleni tana mun gorin haihuwa suma Yara dama babu me zuwa aikena, ganin irin abinda iyayensu suke mun yasa suma suka faramun dariya idan na fito Ina yan aikace-aikace na, sai nayi kamar insanarwa da Hashim sai kawai inhakura, ganin matsalar da muke ciki na Kara yin tsamari yasa Hashim yiwa 'yan uwansa maganar kudin gado Wanda alokacin mun kusa shekara da dawowa  Nigeria, wannan maganar da Hashim yayi ba karamin tada tarzoma tayi ba, Haka suka sashi agaba suna yi Masa sababi wai ay bazasu cinye Masa kudi ba har suna ce Masa dududu ma idan ankasa kason nashi nawa kudin nasa yake, wannan Karan baiyi Musu shiru ba Haka ya fito ya nuna Musu Sam shi hakkinsa yake so, ganin suna niyyar su danne Masa hakkinsa yasa ya nuna Musu cewar zai iya kararsu akotu, zuwa yayi ya sameni da maganar da farko na nuna Masa ya kyalesu Amma yaki sannan yace mun zai nemi kudin da shiga kotu Dan yayi kararsu akan hakkin sa_"
         " _Takurawar da nake ciki memakon tayi baya sai gaba takeyi, ada babu Wanda yake shiga sabgata saidai idan naje wucewa ta gurinsu Amma da suka Kara tsananta mun har d'aki suke zuwa su sameni, idan wanki nayi ban isa na shanya ba Ina komawa d'aki kafin na fito zan samu anwurgomun shi k'asa anyi futu-futu dashi, daga baya ma salo abun nasu ya canja har yanyanka mun sutura sukeyi, idan kayan wanke-wanke na fita dasu zanyi Ina giftawa daga gurin zan nemi wani abun agurin inrasa, girki dama adaki nakeyi saboda indai zanyi girki awaje muddin zan gifta daga gurin sai dai inji Karar tukunya anwurgata kasa, da nayi wani abun bisa kuskure shikenan zasu rufeni da fad'a suna mun habaici, akwai ranar da har sharrin maita suka mun saboda Wata Yarinya acikin yaransu ta jefeni da dutse na Mata magana sai ta zageni, nikuma zuciya ta debeni  na mareta, Ta kuwa zuba da gudu tana ihu tana tumami karshe har kusan dukana sukayi Dan mahaifiyar Yarinyar har marina tayi tace ta ramawa 'yarta_

" _Ranar nayi kuka ad'aki kamar me lokaci Daya naji auren hashim ya fice mun akai, Ina cikin kuka ya shigo ya sameni tambayar duniya yamun naki kulashi sai da ya ga na tsagaida da kukan sannan ya tambayeni cikin lallashi, labarin abunda ya hadamu nayi tun ban rufe Baki ba ya fita afusace yaje ya samu Yarinyar ya zaneta, aykuwa zuwa yamma Riki ya b'alle atsakin 'yan uwan biyu, dayake duk bakinsu Daya ba Wanda ya bani gaskiya, wannan fadan da sukayi shi ya Kara jefani cikin matsala da fuskantar wulakanci, lura sukayi da inajin tsoron k'adangare Dan Wata Rana Daya shiga karkashin gadona sai adakin goggo fatsim na kwana, da gayya suke koromun k'adangare d'aki nikuma haka zan fice da gudu cikin tashin hankali sai Hashim ya dawo yake korar munshi, tukin mota Hashim ya samu wani mutum ne ya sassayi motoci yake bayarwa ana kawo Masa balance duk karshen sati, shiyasa ma damuwa ta karu saboda zaman kadaicin da nakeyi_"
        " _Abu na gaba daya Fara damuna rashin haihuwa ta da farko bana damuwa ko kadan Amma yau da gobe irin habaicin da suke mun yasa abun nima ya Fara damuna, ko kadan basa kirana da sunana saidai su ce, " ke juya" tun bana amsawa har abun yazame mun jiki_"
 
   " _Alhamdulillah rayuwar mu ta Fara komawa irin ta baya saboda Hashim mutum ne me wadata iyali matukar Yanada kudi ahannu, bak'in ciki karara ne ya bayyana afuskar 'yan uwan Hashim da matansu Amma ba yanda suka iya Haka suka zuba Mana ido, matsala ta gaba ta wani kanin su Hashim ne me suna nasiru Wanda yake neman kudi a Lagos, duk bayan wasu 'yan watanni Yana zuwa ganin gida, wannan Karan daya dawo ne yafara takuramun Yana shiga sabgata, Koda hijabi na fita zuwa band'aki ya dinga bina da kallo, tun baya mun magana har ya Fara gaisheni, sama-sama nake amsawa saboda banasan abunda zai dinga shiga tsakanina dashi tunda na lura ba mutumin kirki bane, aron kwano ya farayi agurina ko Kofi acewar sa nafi sauran matan gidan tsafta, wani lokacin sai Ina zaune adaki zanji ya Fad'o mun d'aki sai yace mun aron Kofi yaxoyi, Wata Rana na fito daga wanka daga ni sai daurin kirji Ina shafa Mai ya Fad'o mun d'aki, aranar ne nayi Masa rashin mutumci nayi Masa tatas da yake bashi da kunya bud'ar bakinsa sai cewa yayi shifa Sona yake inbashi hadin Kai, agabansa na dau waya na Fara gayawa Hashim da sauri ya fice, ko awa daya bamuyi da waya da Hashim ba sai gashi ya dawo, bayani na Kara yi Masa tun daga farko har karshen fita yayi yaje ya kirashi ya Fara tambayar sa k'iri-k'iri ya karyata ni hasalima sai cewa yayi ninake binsa, goggonsa kuwa harda karawa tace wai kwanaki ma har saida tamun tatas tace zata gayawa Hashim sannan na saurara, aykuwa tuni zance ya Fara zaga gari Yar Sudan na neman haikewa nasiru haka nazama mujiya acikinsu, muna shiga d'aki Hashim ya rufeni da fad'a saboda kishi ya rufe masa ido, aykawa tuni zuciya ta ciwo ni Kuma nace nagaji da zaman auren dashi na Fara had'a kayana, ganin da gaske nake yasa Hashim sakkowa ya Fara lallashina da kyar na yarda na hakura na zauna, Wata ran na fita zanje gaida goggo fatsim muka hadu da nasiru shida innarsa aykuwa suka tsareni suna gayamun maganganu marasa dadi, karshe nasiru yacemun tunda na nemi tona Masa asiru sai ya kullamun sharrin da sai na Dade ban kunce shi ba, ban biye ta tasu ba nasa Kai na wuce abuna_"

     " _Allah me iko shekarar mu biyu da komawa Adamawa kwatsam na samu ciki nikaina bansan dashi ba sai da ya kusa Wata biyu, shima Kuma asibiti mukaje saboda yawan zazzabin dare da nakeyi sai Kuma amai da ciwon Kai, lokacin da aka sanar damu ba karamin murna mukayi ba mukaja bakinmu mukayi shiru ko goggo Bata sani ba, ahankali ciki na ya Fara tasawa sai Kuma Allah yasakamun laulayi me tsananin gaske, cikina Yana da Wata uku Wata Rana natafi band'aki naji facalolina su kande suna gulmata akan kunnena naji suna cewa da alama ciki gareni har ya fara girma, Inna raliya na gefe ta yi dariya sannan tace taci alwashin saita zubar dashi, cikin zuciyata nake mamakin irin kiyayyar da suke nuna mun, washe gari kuwa na shiga wanka har na fito zan gifta ta gurinsu Inna raliya naji suna kus-kus suna ganina suka kwashe da dariya, Inna raliya na watsamun kallo tana cewa mutanen Sudan masu cikin gayu ta Kuma kwashewa da dariya, wucewa nayi abuna ko ajikina saboda na Saba da jin irin maganganun Nan nasu_"


" _Bayan kwana biyu Wata rana da daddare muna zaune muna Hira nida hashim nace Masa sha'awar mangwaro nakeji, fita yayi yaje ya siyomun bayan fitarsa gilmawar wani Abu nagani kamar k'adangare gudun karna bawa kaina tsoro yasa na share, motsi nadanji abayana na waiga Amma ba komai zuwa wani lokaci naji abu kamar ya hau kan d'ankwalina da sauri na fisgeshi na Yar Ina dube-dube, kamar tafiya nakeji ajikina tuni na tashi Ina yan tsalle-tsalle ji nayi gaba Daya jikina kamar Abu namun yawo, nice har tsakar gida Ina karkad'e-karkad'e sukuwa matan gidanmu suna daga dakinsu suna lekena, sallama Hashim yayi yaga halin da nake ciki da sauri ya kamani ya duddubani Yana tambayata, cikin tashin hankali nake ce Masa k'adangare ne yake bina haskani ya shigayi ba komai ganin har zuwa lokacin ban yarda da abinda ya fad'a ba yasa ya rike ni tsam ajikinsa, muna shiga d'aki kuwa kamar wacce aka budewa ido haka naga k'adangaru birjik suna akasa suna kwance wasunsu suna budemun Baki, Kara na kwalla da karfi na fisge daga hannun Hashim na fita da gudu nikaina bansan inda na nufa ba, shima Hashim rufamun baya yayi Amma Ina nariga da nayi Masa nisa saboda gudu nake ba'a hayyacina ba, bansan inda na nufa ba sai farkawa nayi naganni kwance agadon asibiti, ashe me mashin ne ya bigeni Kuma ya gudu cikin jikina kuwa tuni ya zube, satina guda aka sallameni daga asibiti muna komawa gida fir naki yarda na koma d'akina sai dakin goggo fatsim na koma da zama dama itace ta dinga jinyata a asibiti, Wata na guda Hashim ya nemi na koma d'akina kamar gaske muka tafi da niyyar inkoma cen Amma muna zuwa a idona na Kuma ganin wadannan k'adangarun, kankame Hashim nai kamar zan sume Ina nuna Masa Sam ba abinda ya gani hakan ne yasa ya fahimci da alama sihiri ne akamun, cen d'akin goggo fatsim ya maidani sannan ya dukufa da nemar mun magani, cikin ikon Allah akayi dace na warke garau har na koma d'akina, na Kuma dukufa wajenyin azkar ba dare ba rana_"
       " _Ana cikin Haka ne Alhajin da yasaiwa su Hashim motoci yayi karayar arziki, aykuwa ya buk'aci da su dawo Masa da motocinsa zai hada ya Saida su, ba karamin tashin hankali muka shiga ba aykuwa tuni muka koma Yar gidan jiya, alokacin ne shima Hashim ya dawo musu da maganar gadonsu, da farko sun ki yarda araba Amma da sukaga da gaske kotu zai kaisu Kuma kotun addinin musulinci tuni suka amince aka raba aka bawa kowa hakkinsa, Dan ma wasu kadarorin na baba lamido basu fito dasu ba, Hashim Bai bi ta kansu ba ya kyalesu da Allah, millions hudu da rabi-rabi suka raba, gida Dan madaidaici Hashim ya saimana me daki uku da kitchen da band'aki na million Daya da rabi, D'an gyare-gyare yayi Mana sannan muka kwashe kayanmu nida Inna muka koma cen, motar hannu ya siya me Dan kyau ya cigaba da sana'ar sa saboda Allah yasa Masa Albarka aciki, abun gwanin sha'awa ba mu da damuwa nida Inna har 'yar kiba nayi hasken fatata ya Kara dawowa, shi kanshi Hashim yayi kyau kamar bashi ba_"
     " _Wata rana muna kwance cikin dare wasu b'arayi suka shigo Mana Daya daga cikinsu harda bindinga, muna baccin suka tashemu suka ce Hashim ya basu mukillin motar sa da sauran kudin   da suke gurinsa, da farko yayi niyyar hanasu Amma ganin zasu illatashi yasa ya mik'a musu komai da suka bukata, mamaki muka dingayi ta yanda akayi sukasan da kudi agurin Hashim, duk wannan abun da ya faru damu babu mutum Daya da yaxo yayi Mana jaje daga cikin 'yan uwan Hashim, Haka abokan arziki suka dinga zuwa Mana jaje suna Allah ya kiyaye gaba_"
 " _Alokacin har iyayena nace zan sanarwa Dan su taimakawa Hashim Amma fir yak'i_"
        " _Dillalai  Hashim ya  Nemo yace acigita masu siya zai Saida gidansa ya kama jari na Wata sana'ar, Yaya Ali ne yayi farat ya ce zai siya lokacin da suka ji cigiyar, aykuwa dillalai suka mai kudi million Daya da dubu dari uku da hamsin, atake Yaya Ali ya bada kudi sannan ya bawa Hashim sati biyu akan suyi su nemi wani gidan su tashi zaiyi kiwon kaji_"

Ajiyar zuciya Inna ta sauke tana goge hawayen fuskarta tacewa su Umma, " Hajiya ban taba ganin 'yan uwa masu mugun hali irin na Hashim ba, har yanxu acikin azuciyata Ina zargin da sa hannunsu wajen yin wannan fashin Dana baku labari "
    Umma ce tace, "sadiya labarinki ya matukar tab'amun zuciya cigaba munajinki ya akayi bayan kunsaida gidanku?"
  
     Inna tace.
" _Bayan Hashim ya Saida gida sai muka nemi hayar gida ashekara dubu hamsin muke biya, gidan me kyau ne gwanin sha'awa sauran kudin Kuma sai ya karbi hayar shago ajikin gidan da muke ya bude provision yake Saida kayan kanti Kuma sai Allah ya albarkaci lamarin, kwatsam Wata Rana bayan sallar Isha'i Hashim ya shigo gida cin abinci mukaji ana ihu Yana cewa wuta wuta jama'a kukawo agaji, da gudu Hashim ya Suri bokiti cike da ruwa Yana fita da niyyar Kai taimako, da mamaki ya sandare ganin shagonsa ne yake ci da wuta, wani saurayi ne yake bashi labarin cewar bayan shigarsa gida, wasu samari ne guda biyu sukaxo da fetir ajarka suka watsa suka kunna ashana kafin akamasu tuni sun d'ane mashin sun gudu_"

Inna goge kwallar idanta tayi tace, " _hajiya naga rayuwa iya rayuwa_" 

_Idan kinji dadin shafin Nan kiyomun baiti daya daga cikin wakar M SHARIFF, HAMISU BRIKER ko WAKAR ZEE BABY ta comments sai najiku, saboda wannan shafin nakune masoya😉._💃🏼💃🏼💃🏼💃🏼

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   29&30



Cikin tausayawa umma tace, "lallai sadiya Kinga jararrawar rayuwa, to ya kukayi da wannan iftila'in na gobarar shago? "
   Jingina da bango Inna tayi sannan ta cigaba da labarin.

     " _Alokacin da mukayi wannan gobarar munga tashin hankali domin har d'aki na ta Fara tab'awa, labulayena kusan duka ne suka kone, Amma abun farinciki da babu Wanda wutar ta tab'a, Hashim ya shiga tsananin tashin hankali danma mutum ne shi me tawakkali da mik'awa Allah lamuransa, haka Hashim ya dinga buga-buga duk Dan ya nemar Mana abunda zamu ci shine aykin dako shine diban yashi da bulo, kowanne sai ya Fara sai abu ya tsaya cak  aiki yaki samuwa, lokaci daya Hashim duk ya rame yayi wani irin baki inkangashi duk ya zama abun tausayi, dabara ce ta Fad'o mun Wata Rana muna zaune muna Hira nake ce Masa me zai Hana nida shi mu nemi koyarwa Koda amakarantar kudi ne ko Yaya ma rufa asirin juna tunda Hashim ya hanani Saida d'ankunne na da sarkokina, Bai Musa mun ba ya karbi takarduna ya fita tun Daga ranar Abu Daya yake baya Nan baya cen Yana fafutukar nema Mana aiki, cikin ikon Allah yasa mukayi dace muka samu, shi awata islamiyya ya samu (musababbin kiranshi malam kenan, tun bana fad'a har sunan nima yabi bakina) nikuma Wata private school, ni suna biyana dubu uku shikuma dubu biyu ake biyansu, da yake makarantar ba nisa da gida tare muke zuwa mu dawo tare, ahankali na Fara yin kayan Yara Ina kaiwa Allah ya taimakeni Ina ciniki sosai Ina samu  muna Kara rufawa juna asiri, kwasam mummunan labari ya iso Mana cewar fad'a ya barke a Sudan anata kashe-kashen mutane, hankali atashe na Kira su Ammi tabbas sun shaidamun da gaske haka ne Dan sunce fadan har Yana neman ya gagari mutane wasu ma har sun Fara barin kasarsu saboda kashe-kashe da kone-konen gidajen da akeyi, Amma ganin yanda hankali na ya tashi yasa suka kwantar min da hankali suna Kara nuna fadan ba wani me tsamari bane, tundaga ranar kusan kullin sai na kirasu idan bankira ba zasu kirani har muka shafe Wata biyu ahaka, tun bayan wani lokaci idan na Kira laying su Ammi bana samu har lambar su, har su baba Hussain da duk wani Wanda nasan zan samu wayarsa daga cikin yan uwanmu, Nan da Nan hankalina ya tashi bacci ma bana iya yi, danma Hashim shida goggo suna tausa ta suna Kara kwantar mun da hankali, ganin tafiya ta Mika kusan Wata guda bana samun wayarsu yasa na tada hankali na nace dole sai na je sudan Nagano halin da ake aciki, Hashim ne ya hanani acewarsa ana tsaka da fadan baikanata na shiga ba, wannan tsanani damuwar ce tasa har saida ciwon zuciya ta ya nemi ya tashi, Allah ne dai ya rufa asiri ciwon nawa baiyi tsanani ba, anshari satika masu tsayi sannan muka samu labarin fadan Sudan yayi sauki kura ta lafa, aykuwa alokacin na tada balli nace dole sai na tafi Sudan, Hashim nunamun yayi bashida kudi inyi hakuri zuwa Dan wani lokaci, tuni nace zan siyar da sark'ok'i ta da D'an kunnayena da farko Hashim ya hanani awannan lokacin da nake k'ishiryar ganin iyayena sam ban saurare shi ba, biyu na d'iba daga cikin sark'ok'ina na ajiye sauran guda d'ayar data rage, da wuri na shirya naje kasuwa na saida su ina dawowa gida kamar wacce ta dawo da ciwo haka zazzabin me tsanani ya rufeni, kafin asuba tuni na galabaita amai nake kwarawa kamar zan amayar da hanjin cikina, da duku-duku muka tashi mukaje asibiti alokacin kudin zuwa asibitin ma sai acikin kudin sark'a muka dauka saboda lokacin ba'ayi albashi ba, albishir likita yayiwa Hashim na cewar inada ciki ba Wata biyu da sati biyu, murna agurinmu Taki musaltuwa haka ma Inna, kwana na d'aya a asibitin suka sallameni muka koma gida"_

" _Kulawa ta musamman Hashim yake bani har kunya wani abun yake bani tunda tare muke da goggo fatsim ita kanta tana bani kulawa kamar ita ta haifeni, bayan naji sauk'i na warware na tada maganar tafiya Sudan aykuwa Hashim yace Sam baisan Zancen ba, aykuwa nima nace Allah ya kasheni bazai hanani tafiya ba, cikin fushi yace mun ko zan tafi sai na haifar Masa d'a ko y'arsa tunda ya hanani naki ji, wannan Karan ya kaini bango matuka nace Masa indai cikin jikina ne zai hanani tafiya muje acireshi na hakura da haihuwar, ban rufe Baki ba ya wanke ni da mari cikin tsananin b'acin rai na rike kunci na Ina kuka sannan nace Masa, lallai ka cika namiji nagode kwarai wato sakayyar da zakamun kenan saboda na nuna inasan inje inga halin da iyayena suke ciki, saboda ba iyayenka bane Kai kafiya sanka wato dayake Kai kana cikin y'an uwa da danginka shine zaka hanani tafiya, to shikenan tunda hakane wallahi tallahi auren ma na daina na hakura da Kai gaba daya, ka duba irin cin kashin da y'an uwanka sukaimun Amma duk nacin ye saboda Ina sanka, Hashim idan har kana Kan bakarka to wallahi na hakura da auren, na karasa maganar Ina kuka me tsuma zuciya, kayana na Fara sawa acikin akwati saboda yanda nakeji zuciya ta alokacin bank'i ko cikin dare ne nafita na bar gidan Hashim ba, ganin da gaske nake yasa jikinsa karayin sanyi Yana tsaye agefen kofa na zuge akwatina na dauki hijabi na da karamar jakata nayo waje abina ko goggo banyiwa sallama ba, da gudu Hashim ya biyoni ya rike jakata Yana kokarin dawo Dani gida, cikin b'acin Rai na nuna Masa fir ba inda zani da kyar yasamu ya shawo kaina shima cewa yayi acikin satin zan shirya intafi Sudan, muna shiga gida goggo ta zaunar damu ta farayiwa Hashim fad'a sannan ta sasantamu, sati na zagayowa na gama duk wasu shirye-shiryena alokacin ciki jikina watansa hudu cif har ya Dan Fara turowa, sallama nayiwa goggo harda tsarabarta ta bani me yawan gaske nayi Mata godiya sannan muka fito nida Hashim, Sam baiso tafiyata ni kadai ba saidai matsala ta rashin kudi tunda kudin gurina bazasu ishemu mu biyu ba, mun dad'e nida Hashim sannan mukayi sallama ji yake kamar ya biyoni mu tafi"_

" _Saukata kasar mu ta Sudan ba karamin mamaki ne ya kamani ba ganin yanayin garin duk ya sauya mun, afili na furta ikon Allah shekara uku da barin kasarnan shine har ya canja haka Allah me yanda yaso, ban Kuma tsinkewa da lamarin ba Saida naga gine-gine arurrushe wasu guraren ma sunyi bak'i wuluk alamar wuta ta tashi agurin, yanayin garin ne kadai zai bayyana maka bashida isashiyar lafiya, tsoro ne ya Fara kamani ganin tunda na Fara shigowa unguwar mu babu mutum Daya da na gani Wanda na sani, wadansu mutanen ma yanayinsu baiyi kama da y'an Sudan ba, gaba na ne ya Fara dukan uku-uku ganin mun shigo unguwarmu amma Banda makeken fili ba abinda na gani, wani masallacin da yake kallan gida mu na gani shine ya bani tabbacin nazo dai-dai kofar gidanmu, Amma abun mamaki babu komai agurin sai kango da sauran k'asa agurin, jikina har karkarwa yake zuciyata kamar zata fito tunanin babban gida ne ya fad'o, sauri nake har bana sanin inda na ke jefa kafata domin inkarasa cen dan inji halin da ake ciki, ina karasaw babban gida abunda ya Kara firgitani da razanani ganin rusashen ginin da nayi Wanda da alama kafin ya rushe wuta ce taci fiye da tunanin me tunani, jiri ne yake niyyar dauka ta hankali na yayi kololuwar tashi, na karanta innalillahi wa'inna ilahirraji'una yafi a irga,  juyace ta cigaba da d'bata wani irin duhu naga ya giftamun daga Nan ban Kara sanin inda nake ba sai farkawa nayi na ganni ad'akin Wata Mata kwance akan gadonta, yunkurin tashi nayi ta taimaka mun tana mun sannu da mamaki nake tambayar ta itadin wace Kuma a Ina nake, murmushi matar tamun sannan tace, " ni sunana Fatima d'azu na fita cefane bayan na dawo na ganki kwance a k'asa, da farko naji tsoro nace ko anyi hakanne Dan hadin Baki sai da lura kamar kedin bakuwa ce tunda naganki harda akwati, shi ne nakira babban yarona ya taimaka mun muka kawoki gida " kallan tsaf nayi Mata saboda matar Bata yi kama da y'an Sudan ba ahankali na Fara Tambayar ta, eh ni bakuwa ce daga Nigeria nake tafe naxo babban gida ne kuma abun mamaki sai nasamu gurin ba kowa duk anrushe gidan na karasa maganar Ina rushewa da kuka, lallashina matar ta yi sannan tace, " tabbas kinyi tafiyayyiya kinsha hanya Amma nayi mamakin da naji kina tambayar wani gida agarin Nan, nasan fad'an Sudan din Nan babu inda Bai zaga ba nima Nan da Kika ganni satina biyu da zuwana kasarnan aiki ne ya kawo mijina Kinga nima bakuwa ce Amma fa yanda naji labari duk mutanen kasar Nan hijira sukayi wasu Kuma ankashe su, Amma Bari idan mijina ya dawo sai ku tattauna shi zaiyi Miki Karin bayani tunda ya fini dadewa agarin Nan" ajiyar zuciya na sauke ko amsa ban iya Bata ba na runtse idona hawaye na bin gefen fuskata ta, tashi nayi da kyar ta taimaka mun nayo alwala nayi sallah wayata na duba abun mamaki 40 Miss call, Hashim ne na danna zan kirashi wani Kiran nasa ya Kara shigowa, dauka nayi muryata na rawa ban iya magana ba naji kuka ya kubcemun na fashe da kuka me tsuma zuciya, hankalin Hashim ba karamin tashi yayi ba cikin firgici yake tambaya ta meyake faruwa, sai da nayi me isata sannan na zayyane Masa komai ba karamin tashin hankali shima Hashim ya shiga ba, matar Dana sauka agidanta yace inbawa wayar ba Musu na mik'a Mata bansan me yace Mata ba har suka yi sallama, karbar wayar nayi na ya dinga lallashina Yana kwantar mun da hankali"_

" _Abinci me rai da lafiyata kawo mun ko kadan bai birgeni ba danma ta takura ni ne na iya loma biyu, bayan magriba muna zaune maigidanta ya dawo bayan mun gaisa yake mun ya hanya saboda tuni ta sanar dashi halin da ake ciki, bayan ya kamalla komai ya zauna yake tambayata, tun daga saukata sudan har farkawata acikin gidansa na bashi labari, jinjina kansa yayi sannan yace, " tabbas na gane gidan da kike nema dan har ma muna Dan gaisawa dasu Amma fa tun farkon zuwana garin nan kusan shekara d'aya kenan, maganar gaskiya hajiya mutanen gidan Nan sunyi hijira sun koma Wata kasar ko ince Miki mutanen garin nan gaba Daya, wasu Kuma sun mutu bazan iya bambance Miki wad'anda suka mutu ko suka rayuwa ba, Amma kaf cikin garin Nan bance yanxu dai akwai Wanda yake dad'addene ko haifaffen Sudan acikin garin ba, Wata Daya da suka wuce ne mutane suka Fara shigowa garin Nan wasu Baki ne wasu Kuma wad'anda suka tafi ne Amma bana ce Miki gasu ba" tashin hankali Wanda ba'a sa Masa Rana shiru nayi Ina nazari ji nake kamar mafarki ne, godiya nayi Musu sannan nace zan tafi fir suka hanani sukace na Bari zuwa safiya, adaddafe na Kai safiya sannan namusu sallama na tafi, sai da na shafe wuni guda cur Ina neman iyayena Amma ko me kama dasu bantaba gani ba, yamma ce tayi na rasa gurin kwana Dan dole na dawo gidan mutanen Nan nakara rok'on alfarmar kwana agidan, sai dana shafe sati biyu Ina nemansu Haka shima me gidan ya taimaka mun matuka Amma ba amo ba labari, ahaka na yanke shawarar na tarkato na dawo Nigeria cikin k'unan zuciya da kewar iyayena, alokacin Dana dawo nayi kuka nayi kuka kamar Raina zai fita harsai da ciwona ya tashi D'an sauran kudin Dana dawo dasu sune akayita jigilar magani cikin ikon Allah jikina yayi sauki na warware, Amma kana ganina kasan Ina cikin tashin hankali har cikina ya tsufa idan kaganshi zakayi tunanin baifi Wata shida ba saboda kankantarsa"_

Goge hawayen idanta Inna tayi ta kalli umma tace, " har yau ban cire rai da ganin su Ammi ba, rabona da sasu a idona tun ranar ta zan taho Nigeria tun inada kuruciyata, Amma Kuma tafiya ta mik'a nidasu Ammi sai a darussalam Ashe kowa da tashi irin k'addarar arayuwa, Allah ya raba ganawarmu dasu Ammi bansani ba sunaraye basa Raye Allah ne Masa ni" kukan da Inna takeyi shi ya ci karfinta ta kasa karasa maganar da takeyi.

 Sadiq goge kwallar idansa yayi yace, " kiyi hakuri mama kowa da irin tashi k'addarar, insha Allah Zaku gana me rai baya fidda tsammani Allah ya hada fuskokinsu cikin farin ciki da sallama"
    Ba karamin Dadi Inna taji da maganar Sadiq ba,umma ma Yar nasiha ta Kara da yi Mata, ta Kuma lallasheta da maganganu masu Dadi, sannan hankalin Inna ya Dan kwanta, saboda labarin da Inna take Musu Yana fama Mata da tsohon ciwon da ke damunta.


_Nagode kwarai da Addu'ar ku kanma ya warware ya daina ciwo Allah barzumunci._

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                    31&32



Inna Kara zamanta tayi ta cigaba da cewa.
      " _Tunda na dawo daga Sudan duk wani farin ciki na ya kau banida aiki sai tunani ahaka har cikina ya tsufa Wata Rana muna zaune ciwo ya Dan Fara tabani ashe nakuda ce nakeyi ahankali har  ta Fara zafi, arude muka tafi asibitin gwamnati kafin muje tuni jinina ya hau na Fara jijjiga, cikin gaggawa likitoci suka dukufa akaina kaitsaye d'akin tiyata aka wuce Dani, cikin sa'a anyi aiki da nasara Amma yaron baixo da Rai ba, duk Wanda yaga jaririn Nan bazai tab'a tunanin yakai wata Tara ba saboda k'ank"antarsa, satina guda a Asibiti aka sallamoni muka tawo gida, cikina Bai dau lokaci ba ya warke na warware garau dani kamar bani ba, sai dai duk na fige na lalace Amma jinina ya sauka nayi kalau abina, koyarwa ta na cigaba da zuwa Dan zuwa lokacin na fauwalawa Allah lamurana sai Addu'a da nakewa su Ammi ba dare ba Rana, maganar Nan da nake muku duk abubuwan da suke faruwa damu yan Hashim basu taba zuwa da sunan suyi Mana jaje ba, Kai ko ahanya muka had'u babu wacce ta ke kulani daga cikin facalolina, har gwara shi idan ya fita insun ga dama suna yi Masa magana Amma inbasu ga dama ba kowa wucewarsa yake, wata Rana muna tafiya mun dawo daga makaranta muna tafe muna Hira lawandi D'an gidan Yaya kamilu ya taho Yana tamole da kwallonsa Yana ganinmu Bai fasa ba har sai da ya bugamun ita agefen fuska tuni na Fara hab'o jini na zuba ta hancina, karkuyi tunanin yaro ne domin ashekaru inajin zaiyi Ashirin da biyu alokacin, ko kallanmu baiyi ba bare ya Bamu hakuri ganin Haka ne ran mlm yayi mummunan b'aci ya karasa gurin da yake ball din ya dank'o shi ya shiga wanka Masa Mari, ba yanda banyi ba Dan ganin ya kyaleshi Amma sai da yayi Masa duka shima kansa lawandin kokarin ramawa yake Amma karfinsu ba d'aya ba, mutane ne suka Fara taruwa agurin ganin Haka yasa na dinga rok'on mlm da kyar ya saki wuyansa muka wuce gida, fargabata daya abunda zaije yadawo saboda nasan y'an uwansa basuda mutumci ba kuma su da imani, sannan Kuma sarkin yawa akace yafi sarkin karfi, muna zuwa gida ko minti biyar bamuyi ba sai ga zugar 'yayan yayyansa Nan sunfi su Ashirin kowanne su day mugayen makamai wani makocinmu ne yaxo da gudu ya sanar damu sannan yace maza-maza mu rufe gida idan ba Haka ba kisan Kai ma zasu iyayi, da farko mlm ya yi gaddama Amma da goggo tasa Baki shine ya amince, Haka muka rufe gidan muka shige d'akuna muka rufe munajinsu suka dinga buga kofa kamar zasu b'alle ta, gajiya sukayi Dan kansu sannan suka tafi, tun daga ranar muka Fara wasan mage da b'era dasu ganin abun nasu na wuce k'a'ida yasa mlm ya Kai report gun police, sai da akayi Mana tsakani dasu sannan muka samu kwanciyar hankali"_
      " _Mun kusa shekara da faruwar wannan lamarin wata Rana mun dawo daga makaranta, mukaxo muka tarar da babban tashin hankalin da Bamu tab'a gani ba, zuwa mukayi muka tarar da anyiwa goggo fatsim yankan rago atsakar gida Kuma da alama tun bayan fitarmu aka aykata Mata wannan zalincin, sai awannan lokacin ne 'yan uwan mlm da kishiyon goggo sukaxo suna koke-koke idan ka gansu saika dauka zuciyarsu Daya suke wannan kukan munafircin, report muka Kai gun police Kuma cikin ikon Allah Aka kama wad'anda suka yi wannan kisan Amma saidai  wani abun tsoro da mamaki da antambaye su Wanda yasasu aykatawa sai su yanke jiki su Fadi asume, tun police na ganin kamar abun nasu harda iskanci har abun ya Fara basu tsoro duk me tunani idan ya lura da yanayin sumansu da silar suman nasu zai gane aikin sihiri ne, saboda za'a zauna dasu ayi magana Kuma sun yarda sun amince su sukayi kisan Kan, Amma da antambaye su waye ya aikesu Sai su yanke jiki su Fadi, ranar da aka kaisu kotu ana shiga dasu suka zabura suna ihun Zaki zai kamasu kafin wani lokaci tuni sun tubure da hauka kamar wad'anda suka shekara da hauka, Dan dole aka sallami wannan karar sukuma akayi gidan mahaukata dasu, kafin akaisu har sun Fara yagar Naman jikinsu da hakoransu, duk da andaure su Saida suka jiwa wad'anda suka kaisu ciwo"_

" _Muna ji muna gani maganar kisan goggo yasha ruwa sai Addu'a da Mike binta da ita, bayan rasuwar goggo ba dadewa Allah ya taimakeni na Kuma samun wani cikin sai dai shi bansha wuyar laulayi ba, Amma Daya girma nauyinsa ba karamin damuna yayi ba cikin ikon Allah har ya isa haihuwa na haifo yarana yan biyu kyawawa mace da namiji, mace tunda taxo ba lafiya aka haifeta ana gobe suna ta rasu, dukda akwai Hassan dinta hakan Bai hanani jin rashinta azuciyata ba,sannu ahankali  Hassan ya Fara wayo har ya isa yaye, alokacin mlm tuni ya Dade da Fara sana'ar  Saida nama Kuma Allah yasa Masa Albarka acikin kasuwancin shiyasa ma mukayi fes abunmu bamuda damuwar komai idan kaga Hassan kamar Dan larabawa kowa sha'awarsa yake yaron bulbul dashi ya taso da farin jini, Ina yayeshi na kuma samun wani cikin bayan wata Tara na haifo diyata kyakykywa ranar suna Yarinya taci fatima, watan Fatima goma na samu cikin farida yarana kowa Sha,awarsu yake yi musamman idan muka fito unguwa, idan ka ganni kamar banice na haifosu ba saidai yanayin kammani da muke dasu, Amma duk cikinsu farida tafi kama dani"_

     " _Wata rana mlm ya dawo daga siyo nama bayan ya shigo gida ba dadewa, mukaji matasa sunyo zanga-zanga suna ihu Wai basu yarda ba, ga mamakin mu sai muka ji sun tsaya akofar gidanmu wani makocin mu ya dakatar dasu cikin lallami Yana tambayar su, budar bakin wani daga cikin su sai cewa yayi ay mlm ne ya kashe Musu dan uwa tunda sunga gawarsa akan rumfar namansa, gaba nane ya Fadi mlm har zai fita na hanashi fita dabara makocin yayi Musu yace zai je ya gani shi duk atunaninsa karya ne tunda ko kaza mlm bai tab'a kashewa ba, suna zuwa yaga abunda ya tsoratashi gawar wani matashi ce akan rumfar mlm ankashe ga wuk'ar Naman mlm Nan soke ajikin gawar, shawara ya bawa matasan yace suje suyi report police station suna tafiya yazo Yana tambayar mlm ya akayi Haka ta faru, iya abinda yasani ya gayawa makocinta kuma ya gamsu duba da ganin kyawawan halayen mlm, shawara Daya ya kawowa mlm ta cewar ya gudu inba haka ba me fiddashi sai Allah tunda case din kisa ne, hakan kuwa akayi kafin yan sanda su zo duk wadansu abubuwanmu masu amfani munsa ajaka ko kayan kirki Bamu dauka ba, mukabi ta bayan gari muka yanka ta cikin daji, mlm ne ya dauki Hassan da jakarmu nikuma na Goya Fatima farida na kafad'a ta, idan na gaji sai inbawa mlm yadauketa ya bani jakar, ahaka har dare ya riskemu Bamu Isa wani garin ba, da kyar muka karasa wata ruga muka nemi alfarmar masauki ganin mu agalabaice yasa suka bamu guri da abinci, cikin dare kukan Hasan mukaji Yana ihu Yana cewa Nan ne, muna haskawa mukaga ashe maciji ne yasareshi duk wasu abubuwa daya kamata ayi Masa na agajin gaggawa anyi Amma da yake ba rayayye bane asuba nayi ya rasu, nayi kuka kamar zanbi Hassan haka inaji Ina gani akaje aka binne Hassan, washegari muka Kara daukan hanya sai da mukayi tafiya me nisa muka Isa titi Tasha muka tambaya aka nuna Mana mukaje muka hau motar Kano, lokacin da mukaxo Kano ba Wanda  muka sani driven dayake na kirki ne shi ya kaini gidansa gurin matarsa shi Kuma mlm yake kwana atasha, satinmu biyu drive Nan ya samo Mana d'aki a wani gida mu biyu ne muda wata tsohuwa, zamanmu muke muda ita cikin kwanciyar hankali saboda macece me kirki nikuma na dauketa kamar mahaifiya ta, jin Ina Kiran wannan tsohuwar da Inna yasa su Fatima suke kirana nima da Inna alokacin ba yanda banyi ba Dan su daina Amma tuni ya shiga bakinsu"_
   
    Inna ta karasa fad'a tana kallan su Umma, umma ce tace, " kamar kinsan tambayar da take Raina kenan naganki da sauran kuruciyarki Amma ana ce Miki Inna"

    Sai alokacin farida tayi magana tun lokacin da Inna ta Fara bada labarin tace, " to Inna Ina labarin tsohuwar ni bantab'a ganinta ba?"

Murmushi Inna tayi tace, " yaro man kaza, wannan maganar da nake Miki tun bakida wayo"

Sadiq ne yace, " to mama ya zamanku ya kare awannan gidan Wanda kuke tare da 'yar tsohuwa?"
     Inna ta cigaba da cewa.

    " _Inna bata da matsala ko kadan tana kyawawan halayen gata mace ce me faran-faran da mutane, bayan dawowar mu Kano mlm da 'yan kudadensa a hannu Amma yan d'awainiyar da yakeyi ta yau da kullin kudin hannunsa suka kare tunda acikinsu ya kama Mana haya, ya siya Mana kayan Abinci da D'an abinda ba'a rasa ba, aikin kwandasta ya nema agurin wannan driver Daya saukemu agidansa, kuma cikin ikon Allah ya d'auke shi suke aikinsu Kuma ba laifi muna samun na rufin asiri, shekararmu awannan gidan me gidan ya tashe mu acewarsa babban d'ansa ne zaiyi aure kuma acikin gidanmu zai zauna da matarsa, wannan ne silar rabuwar mu da inna, neman gida muka shiga yi cikin abinda baifi wata biyu ba muka samu wani karamin gida me d'aki biyu acikin unguwa uku wajen filin kashu, dawowar mu unguwa uku mlm sai ya sake sana'a ya koma yin gadi agidan wani mutum da yake hotoro, wannan aikin ba karamin karbar mlm yayi ba domin Yana samun albashi me tsoka Haka ne yasa rayuwar mu tayi fes muka koma cikin walwala da jin Dadi yaranmu aka Sanya su makarantu na kudi idan ka ganmu gwanin sha'awa, shigowar ciwon 'kenda shine yayi sanadiyar rasuwar Fatima, tare sukayi da farida Amma cikin ikon Allah ita farida sai ta warke Amma ita dayake ba me tsawoncin kwana bace sai Allah ya d'auke kayarsa, Haka muka cigaba da rayuwa Kuma bankara samun wani cikin ba, hankalinmu gaba Daya ya kwanta sai dai lokaci-lokaci Ina yawan tunanin su Ammi idan abun ya juyomun harsai mlm ya sani agaba Yana lallashi na."_

" _Bayan shekara goma lokacin farida nada shekara goma Sha biyu, alokacin ne Allah ya saukarwa mlm da ciwon k'oda tun Yana jurewa Yana iya zuwa gidan da yake gadi har abun yaci karfinsa, sark'a ta da na Dade Ina ajiyarta ita na fito da ita na saida muka shiga zaryar asibiti, da farko jikin yayi kyau Amma daga baya sai abun ya dawo da tsanani, tun muna samun abunda zamuje asibiti har ya kama sai naje gidan radio na roki taimako ataimaka Mana, to kunsan dai gidan radio yanda yake bani kadai bace me bukata akwai mabukata da yawa, gidan  Alh da yake gadi naje na yi Masa karin bayanin halin da ake ciki duk da Yana da labarin hakan, kudi ya deb'o ya bani nayi Masa godiya dasu muka cigaba da yawan asibiti, da kudin asibiti ya Kara k'ak'are Mana na koma gidan Alh Amma sai akace baya Nan, tun Daga ranar kullin naje sai dai ace baya Nan daga Haka har na hakura na daina zuwa, kayan d'akina nashiga siyarwa da abun yayi gaba har gadona na siyar duk gurin neman magana, Haka na zama kamar mahaukaciya bana Nan bana cen tafi-tafi har abinci ya Fara neman yi Mana wahala ga lokacin jikin mlm yayi tsanani ko tashi baya iyayi, tun daga lokacin na Fara sarewa da mlm duba da yanayin da ya kasance aciki, haka na kasance ko yaushe cikin kuka banida aiki sai kuka idan farida taga Ina kuka itama rumgumeni take tayita kuka, tashin hankali na d'aya kar mlm ya mutu ya barmu cikin wannan rayuwar da banida gaba banida baya banida dangin uwa bare na uba, banida dangin mijin da zasu tallafi ni da farida su nuna Mata gata, Allah ne ya taimkeni alokacin ciwona Bai tashi ba Amma na tabbata jinina ya hau yafi a irga"_

   Share hawaye Inna tayi tana jin mutuwar mlm ta dawo Mata sabuwa da tsantsar kaunarsa, sannan ta cigaba da cewa.

       " _Mlm sai da yashafe shekara hudu cikin wannan jinyar Saida ya koma abun tausayi duk me imani idan yaga mlm sai ya tausaya Masa ko ya fashe Masa da kuka hatta magana ma wahala take masa, acikin wannan halin ne Allah ya karbi rayuwar mlm, nashiga tashin hankali matuk'a Amma sai na mik'awa Allah lamurana, tashinmu akayi daga gidan da muke haya bamuda gurin da zamu zauna, dole tasa muka koma k'ark'ashin gada da zama, acen ne Allah ya had'a mu da Sadiq yaron kirki koni na haifi Sadiq iyakacin abinda zai Mana kenan, wannan shine labarinmu atakaice."_

Inna ta karasa magana tana kallan umma, ajiyar zuciya umma ta sauke tace, " sadiya lallai kun ga rayuwa da abubuwan data kunsa na tausaya muku Kuma na d'auki darasi aciki, Amma na Miki alkawarin zamu zauna daku har karshen rayuwar mu, zan d'aukeki tamkar kanwar da muka fito ciki d'aya haka farida zan d'auketa kamar 'yar cikina Allah ya Bamu hakurin zama da juna kunsan zama zo mu zauna zo mu sab'a ne"

Umma ji tayi kamar ta zuba Ruwa a k'asa ta Sha saboda farin ciki, godiya takewa umma harda hawayenta na jin Dadi.

     Farida na d'ago Kai suka had'a ido da Sadiq ya watsa Mata wani kallo yana kashe Mata ido daya, fakar idon su Umma tayi itama ta galla Masa harara had'e da murgud'a Masa baki, mamakinta ne ya kamashi jinjina Kai yayi alamar zamu had"u dake ne.

      _Hmmmmm Ina ganin wani Abu wani abu baya ganina, wato Sadiq ta Haka salon naku ya fara.🤣_

_Dan Allah ku Sanya Aminiyata cikin Addu'a Bata da lafiya, tana bukatar Add'uar ku, wannan shafin naki ne my one and only Aminiya (Mmn Ihsan)  Allah ya Baki lafiya Dan matsayi da isar Annabi Muhammad sallallahu alaihi wasallam._😞👏🏼
