*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  33&34



Ita dinma karkata kanta gefe tayi tana D'an murmushi, umma cewa Sadiq tayi, " yakamata ka je ka deb'owa maman Farida kayansu ko? inyaso kafin ka dawo sai mu gyara d'akin cen asa Musu kayan aciki sai suyi zamansu aciki?"
      " Eh Bari na tashi na shirya insha Allah yanxu zanje inkwaso musu" cewar Sadiq.
   Yana gama maganar ya tashi ya fice, Inna batayi k'asa agwiwa ba ta cigaba da zabgowa umma godiya da Addu'a, tashi sukayi umma ta nuna Musu wani d'aki dake kusa Dana umma, d'akin babu komai sai D'an shirgin umma da basu taka Kara sun karya ba, kwashe su Umma tayi Inna da farida suka share d'akin sannan suka wanke shi.
      D'akin guda Daya ne sai dai babba ne yana da kyau da girma dai-dai gwargwado, kafin wani lokaci Sadiq ya gama shiri ya fito tsab cikin kananan Kaya, jar Riga ceta T-shirt sai bakin wandan jeans gashin Kansa sai kyalli yake, yana fitowa kamshin turarensa ya gwauraye gidan, farida na d'aki taji kamshin turarensa lumshe ido tayi tana Kara shak'ar kamshin turaren Wanda ba karamin Dadi yayi Mata ba, tana Nan zaune taji shigowarsa d'aga kanta tayi karaf kuwa suka had'a ido lokaci Daya gabanta ya yanke ya fad'i, murmushi ya  sakar Mata sannan yayiwa su Umma sallama ya fice daga gidan, Kai tsaye gidansu Inna na da ya wuce da adaidaita sahu.

    Tun bayan fitar Sadiq Farida ta kasa sukuni ita kadai take jin wani irin nishad'i da farinciki, tana lumshe ido hoton Sadiq ne yake Kara dawo Mata ita kadai take murmushi batare da ta sani ba, bayan wani lokaci Sadiq ne ya dawo da kayan su Inna tarbarsa sukayi ya dinga shigo da kayan suna karba suna shigar dasu cikin d'aki.
     Tuni d'akin su Inna yayi fes ya fito gwanin sha'awa, bakin Inna har kunne saboda murna da farin ciki, tun Daga ranar kullin Inna da Farida su suke komai nacikin gidan daga shara wanke-wanke da 'yan aikace-aikace, umma ba karamin dadin zama take ji dasu farida ba saboda basuda San jiki ko kadan gashi suna Bata girma yanda ya kamata, Inna idan zatayi wanki har kayan umma take had'awa dasu abinda umma take yi acikin gidan Nan girki ne kawai shima da farida nayi umma ta hutar da ita acewarta zaman Haka bashida Dadi yakamata itama ta Dan dinga motsa jikinta, duk Wanda ya shiga gidan Nan sai zaman su Inna ya birgesu da umma tun suna D'an d'ari-d'ari da umma har suka sake jiki da ita, sannu ahankali ciwon k'irjin Inna yayi sauki ta warke garau da ita, Sadiq ne ya yankowa Farida form din makarantar kulafa'urrashad islamiyya dake hausawa sabon titi, uniform ya yanko mata aka dinka Mata ranar wata asabar da kanshi ya kaita makarantar interview akayi Mata sannan aka kaita ajin daya dace da ita, sannu ahankali farida take ta karatunta ta maida hankali yanda ya kamata, Bata dad'e da Fara zuwa islamiyya ba umma tasa Sadiq ya kaita hausawa model secondary school, SS1 yasa aka sakata tuni farida ta zama Yar makaranta gaba da baya, wato ga ta islamiyya ga boko.
     Su Inna sun canja jikinsu ya mirje sunyi fes abinsu yanzu basuda wata damuwa hankalinsu ba karamin kwanciya yayi ba, hasken fatarsu ya Kara fitowa Farida har wani ja takeyi ga wata Yar k'iba da suka Kara yi ita da Inna, 'yanmatan cin Farida sai Kara fitowa yake musamman yanxu da umma ta d'ind'inka Mata sutura iri-iri, ta maida hankalinta sosai gurin karatu Kuma tana ganewa sosai sannan kuma da daddare idan Sadiq ya dawo daga gurin aiki Yana Kara yi Mata bita.
     Wata irin shak'uwa ce me tsananin gaske ta shiga tsakanin Farida da Sadiq, farida ta matuk'ar yadda da cewar tana kaunar Sadiq har cikin zuciyarta, Wanda ta lura suna da banbanci da Sadiq saboda ta lura kamar babu wannan atare dashi hakan ne yasa ta Fara kokarin yak'ar zuciyarta Amma Ina san Sadiq ya Mata mugun kamu.

   Daga bangaren Sadiq ma hakan take sai dai da yake shi namiji ne atunaninsa tayi yarinta Daya furta Mata kalmar so ta wani fannin kuma Yana gudun karta Raina shi (🙄🙄🙄 _Ka zauna kallan Ruwa......_) shiyasa ya sawa ransa cewar bazai furtawa farida kalmar soyayya ba sai ta kammala secondary school, Amma ta bayan fage Sadiq ya kafa ya tsare duk Wanda yaga Yana kokarin shigowa rayuwar farida Yana iya kacin kokarin ganin ya yanke alakarsu.

Malamin makarantar su farida tun ranar da aka kaita ya d'ora idansa akanta yaji ta kwanta Masa har cikin zuciyarsa, sannu ahankali ya Fara kokarin isar da sakonsa gurin farida sai dai Sam nuna wa take yi kamar Bata fahimci abinda yake nufi ba, gudun kar raini ya shiga tsakaninsa da sauran d'alibansa yasa yaja bakinsa ya tsuke Amma yaci aniyar gidansu zai tunkara Kai tsaye ko yafi samun abinda yake bukata.
    Sannu ahankali rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya har farida ta shiga SS3 Wanda alokacin tuni sun Fara shirye-shiryen yin candy, awannan lokacin tana da shekara goma Sha takwas aduniya ga Wanda baisan farida ba zaiyi tunanin tayi shekara Ashirin duba da yanda jikinta ya bud'e ta Kara zama babbar budurwa, awannan lokacin ba namiji ba hatta Mace idan taga Farida baza tayi Mata kallo Daya ta d'auke idanta ba, samari har tsoron tunkararta suke Yi da zummar furta soyayya, malam jabir shine malamin su farida wata Rana shiryowa yayi yaje yasa akayi sallama da ita, ko kadan batayi mamakin ganinsa ba saboda ta dad'e tana fuskantar yanayinsa, abinda ta Saba sanarwa da sauran maza shi dinma Haka ta sanar Masa wato sai tayi candy zata Fara kula samari, badan yaso ba Haka mlm jabir ya juya ya tafi kuma ko afuska farida ba Wanda ta gayawa a islamiyyarsu saboda tasan iskancin d'alibai Sarai sata gaba zasuyi su dinga yi Mata tsiya, dama tuni wasu daga cikin k'awayenta sun Fara harbo jirgin mlm jabir, hakan da farida tayi ba karamin dad'i yayiwa mlm jabir ba saboda mutum ne shi da bayasan raini.

      Alh sabi'u Abdullahi zango kanin mahaifine ga Sadiq Wanda yake zaune agarin jahun, tsohon soja ne Yanada 'yaya hudu maza uku mace Daya me suna hafsat, ziyara ya kawowa su Umma shida iyalansa cikin girmamawa su Umma suka tarbesu karamawa suka yi Musu aka shiga hirar yaushe gamo, cikin hirar da su Umma suke yi ne yake sanar Mata da cewar yaji  labarin za'a Fara d'aukan training din sojoji Yana bukatar takardun Sadiq, umma tayi murna sosai waya ta d'auka ta Kira sanar Masa halin da ake ciki da murna ya dawo gida Karin bayani kanin mahaifin nasa yayi Masa sannan ya ce Masa duk abinda ake ciki zaiyi Masa karin bayani.

    Tunda farida ta dawo daga makarantar islamiyya taga kallan da hafsat takewa Sadiq gaba Daya taji ta tsani Yarinyar ko gaisuwar arxiki basuyi da ita ba, afakaice Farida take watsawa hafsa harara ita kuwa batasan ma tanayi ba, sai Kara kashe murya take tana Kara shishshigewa Sadiq, shidinma haushi ta Fara bashi ganin irin yanda takeyi agaban iyayensu ko kunya Bata ji cikin zuciyarsa guntun tsaki yaja Yana Fad'in, " wannan idan har wayewa ce ay batayi ba, sai wani bro bro kike kina Kara nani'kewa mutane" tashi Sadiq yayi cikin dabara karaf hafsa tace, " Bro Ina zuwa Kuma muna hira?" Yak'en dole yayi sannan yace, "Nan baya zan fita" aykuwa da sauri ta mik'e tsaye tana Fad'in, "nima zani Bro na gano garin naku" wani kululun bak'in ciki ne yakama shi Amma sai ya basar yace, "to ba komai muje".
    Hannunsa  hafsa ta kama ta rike suna fitowa kuwa karaf a idon Farida, ji tayi kamar ta shak'e hafsa yi tayi kamar Bata gansu ba ta cigaba da harkar gabanta, har sunxo fita hafsa tace, " Bro dama waccen gal din agidan Nan take Nina d'auka 'yar makotanku ce ta shigo" guntun tsaki yaja k'asa-k'asa yace, " Agidan Nan take" iyakacin abinda Farida taji kenan aykuwa ta cika tayi fam bak'in ciki ne ya Kara kamata.

    Suna fita hafsa ta Kara jefo Masa tambaya, "Bro wace ita ni bansanta a family dinmu ba?" Adakile ya bata amsa, " eh ba lallai ki santa ba Amma itace Yarinyar da zan aura" ras gaban hafsa ya dinga fad'uwa tuni taji tsanar Farida ya d'arsu azuciyarta, idanunta ne suka k'ank'ance saboda takaici Bata Kuma magana ba, Haka suka cigaba da tafiya ba Wanda yake kula D'an uwansa, ganin tafiyar ta gundureta yasa tace Masa ta gaji su koma gida, hakan kuwa akayi gida suka wuce suna shiga ta tarar da Farida na zaune tanayi yiwa kanta kalba manya guda takwas gashin kanta duk ya bazo ya rufe Mata fuska, batasan ma sun shigo ba Saida taji kamshin turaren Sadiq sannan ta d'ago da kanta, hannu tasa ta kwashe gashin kanta tayi gefe dashi, wata uwar harara hafsa ta watsa Mata sannan taja dogon tsaki ta shige ciki, D'an kwali tasa ta d'aure kanta tana mamakin abinda hafsa tayi mata, d'akinsu itama ta shiga taje ta kwanta tana karajin haushin su gaba daya har Sadiq din.

    Sai bayan magriba sukayiwa su Umma sallama sannan suka tafi duk yanda hafsa taso Kara ganin farida abun ya gagara, ko da sukayiwa Inna sallama zasu tafi tana kwance ko kallan gurin batayi ba, ganin yanayin da hafsa take ciki yasa suka tambayeta bud'ar bakinta sai cewa tayi kanta ne yake ciwo, daga Haka ba Wanda ta Kara kulawa har sukaje gida.

Gabanin su farida zasu Fara exams lokacin ne kanin baban Sadiq ya sanar Masa da cewa sati me zuwa zasu tafi training tunda dama shi ya shige Masa gaba ya aywatar Masa da komai da akeyi, gaba Dayansu sunyi murna kwarai da jin wannan labarin kafin lokacin kuwa tuni duk wasu shirye-shirye da suka kamata ya Riga da ya kammala su, lokacin da zai tafi yaji kewarsu matuk'a musamman farida idan ya tuno da yanayin zamantakewar su, Addu'a kam yasha ta har ya gode Allah sannan yayi Musu sallama ya tafi.
     Tunda Sadiq ya tafi farida duk taji gidan yayi Mata wani iri ta koma wata shiru da ita, su Umma har suna Mata tsiyar Wai rashin abokin karatunta ne yasata koma miskila, exams suka farayi farida ta na maida hankali yanda ya kamata, Sadiq duk sanda zaiyo waya haka zaiyi ta jaddada Mata cewar tayi karatu sosai zaiyi mata surprise akwai babbar kyautar da zaiyi mata, gargad'i na biyu da yake Mata shine ba ruwanta da kula kowa ta maida hankali akan karatunta inta ci exams zai nemar mata makaranta ta cigaba da karatu, jin dadin haka ne yasa ta Kara dagewa yanda ya kamata ahaka har suka kamalla suka yi candy.

      Duk wani Abu da akewa d'aliba nayin anko da sauran party da akeyi yayin candy ba Wanda umma batayiwa farida ba, idan baka sani ba zakayi zatan umma ita ta haifo farida saboda irin d'awainiyar da takeyi da ita.

       Bayan wani lokaci, exams dinsu farida ta fito saidai ba'asamu yanda akeso ba, ganin yanda farida ta damu kar Sadiq yaji yayi Mata fad'a yasa umma ta Fara sanar dashi, shi dinma baice komai ba saboda yasan farida tana da maida hankali da karatu, lallashinta yayi sannan yace ta kwantar da hankalinta zai biya Mata ta sake wata a private school, har yake shaida Mata cewar kafin lokacin ma sun fito  wato sun gama training din da sukeyi.

    Daga b'angaren Sadiq Kuma awannan lokacin ne yaci alwashin Yana dawowa gida zai zayyanewa farida duk abinda yake cikin zuciyarsa, saboda yagaji da dakwon soyayyarta na tsawon shekaru.
        Akwana atashi kwanaki na tafiya har su Sadiq suka kamalla training dinsu ranar da zai dawo gida farida jinta takeyi kamar sallah, lokacin da farida tayi tozali da sadiq duk sai taji kunyarsa take ji, gaba Daya taga ya canja ya Kara zama wani had'ad'd'an Gaye, suna had'a ido ya sakar Mata murmushi tuni taji wani kunyarsa ta Kara kamata sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tayi tana wasa da hannunta, tana sanyin Masa sannu da zuwa Amma nauyi dajin kunyarsa duk sun dabaibaita, gaisawa sukayi dasu umma da Inna ahankali Farida tace masa, " Ina wuni ya Sadiq ya hanya" kallan tsaf ya kare Mata Yana mamakin sauyin farida ta zama kamar wata bakuwa, amsa Mata yayi da, " lafiya kalau farida, me yake faruwa naga kin zama so silent?" Sai alokacin ne ta Kara fahimta dai-dai da muryarsa ta Kara canjawa, gaba Daya ta manta tana gabansu Inna ta kafe Sadiq da ido tana kallansa, hannunsa 🤏🏼yasa awajen fuskar ya D'an murza sannan ta dawo daga duniyar tunanin da takeyi, akunyace ta sunkuyar da kanta, murmushin gefen baki yayi sannan yace, " kallan Nan yayi yawa fa karki gama gane kyawun fuska ta tun budurwata Bata gani ba"
       Azabure farida ta d'ago tana kallan Sadiq  tuni idanunta suka ciko da kwallah, suna had'a ido ya kashe Mata ido daya😉 Yana d'aga Mata gira🤨 umma da Inna tashi sukayi suka fito daga d'akin suna Hira Dan dukkansu sun Fara fuskantar wani Abu daga gare su.

   D'akin ne yayi tsit farida kirjinta na bugun goma-goma, Sadiq ne yace, "FARIDA" har tsakiyar kanta taji Kiran da yayi mata ahankali ta amsa, " na'am" idanunsa na kanta yace, " d'ago ki kalleni" shiru ne ya biyo baya saboda gani take bazata iya abinda ya umarceta ba, karara maimaita Mata yayi, "d'ago ki kalleni" wannan Karan ma shiru tayi tana Kara Wasa da hannunta, murmushi yayi sannan yace Mata, " ko ki d'ago ki kalleni in fad'a Miki sak'on da yake cikin zuciyata ko Kuma inrike hannunki inkaiki gaban su Umma insanar da ke acen" da sauri farida ta zaro manyan idanunta tana kallansa, gyada Mata Kai yayi sannan yace.............🤭  Ku biyoni Yasin yanxu na gaji🤣

_Wallahi Ina matukar jin dadin comments dinku inayinku irin sosai din Nan Allah ya bar mu tare._

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                35&36


Gyada Mata Kai Sadiq yayi sannan yace, " Fareeder ! Tun ranar da na Fara ganinki arayuwa naji wani abu namun yawo game dake acikin zuciya ta Wanda bantab'a jinsa game da wata 'ya mace ba, alokacin nayi tunanin tausayinku ne yasa nake jin wannan yanayin, daga baya sai na fahimci ba Haka bane saboda nakanji banasan ganin kowanne d'a namiji ya rab'eki Koda ba da niyyar soyayya bane, babban abunda ya Fara d'agamun hankali game da rayuwar da kuke ciki abaya yanda naga Kuna kwanciya atiti batare da wata kulawa ba, Allah shi yake tsare da bawansa Amma alokacin Nan ko isashen baccin dare bana iyayi fargabata kar wani musu mugayen suje cikin dare su keta Muku hak'k'inku amatsayinku na 'ya'ya mata musamman keda kike matashiyar budurwa, macen ma kuma kyakykywa kamarki, ranar da wannan Alh yatab'a zuwa yayi Miki tayin muguwar rayuwar dayake ciki kwana nayi hoton abun Yana dawomun, Ina rumtse idona sai abun ya Kara dawomun sabo, hankalina Bai kwanta ba sai lokacin da kuka koma tsohon gidan Nan da kuka zauna, nayita lallashin zuciya akan 'yan tsurarin samarin da kike yi akoda yaushe Kika sa k'afa Kika fita cikin zullimi da tashin hankali nake, tsorona Kar wani saye zuciyarki, tafiya ta training din Nan ba karamin kokari nayi ba saboda akullin sai zuciyata ta ayyanamun abubuwa masu yawan gaske, wani lokacin har gani nake kamar zan iya rasa ki, fareeder naci alwashin sanar dake sirrin zuciyata Ina fatan bazan samu wata matsala atare dake ba, I LOVE YOU MY HABEEBTY " ya karasa fad'a Yana watsa Mata sexy eyes dinsa alamar inasan ji daga gareki.
      Tunda Sadiq ta Fara magana wani irin farinciki ne ya lullub'e farida, jinta take tamkar wata saurayinyar Mata, acikin zuciyarta take Fad'in, " ashe bani kadai ce na Dade Ina yin dakon soyayya ba, Allah na gode maka da ka Sanya soyayya ta azuciyar bawanka " sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tayi ahankali tace, "unmmm" kallan tsaf Sadiq yayi Mata yace, " ok, kina nufin bakiji me nace ba ko? " Shiru farida tayi sannan tace, " naji" 

    " Ok, inasan ji daga gareki, DID U LOVE ME?" Farida Bata d'ago ta kalleshi ba ta d'aga kanta alamun,eh. Tsareta yayi da ido sannan yace, " Baki da ba ki ne? Ni bakiji me nace Miki ba" duk yanda farida taso Taki furtawa Sadiq tayi Amma  ya kureta harsai da tace itama tana kaunarsa, aykuwa da gudu ta tashi ta shige uwar d'akin umma, da idanu ya bita da kallo Yana jin kwanarta na Kara shiga cikin zuciyarsa.
       Tashi yayi ya d'auki jakar sa Kai tsaye dakinsa ya wuce sai kamshi ne yake tashi, lumshe ido yayi Yana Kara shak'ar kamshin turaren d'akin, toilet ya shiga yayi wanka bayan ya fito yasa kananan kayansa ya kwanta Kan katifarsa, lumshe ido yayi Yana murmushi shi kad'ai Tunowa yayi da kyakykywar fuskarta wacce take Kara sashi annushuwa.
      Bayan wani lokaci umma ce ta aiki farida ta tambayo sadiq abinci idan Yanada bukata akai Masa, Yana zaune da waya ahannu Yana D'an dannawa yaji shigowar farida da sallama ta shiga d'akin tana isar Masa da sak'on umma, cikin farinciki yake amsa Mata, " ki Kai Mana falon umma yanxu zan fito sai muci" ahankali Farida tace, " ni naci nawa" da kallo Sadiq ya bita ya Fara kwaikwaiyar ta, " nikuma dake zanci " dariya farida tayi sannan ta tashi ta fice.

      Farida Bata dad'e da fita ba Sadiq ya biyo bayanta, da farko farida taki yarda suci abinci Saida taga Sadiq Yana niyyar yi Mata d'ura sannan ta zage suke cin abinci.
       Tundaga ranar wata zazzafar soyayya ce ta shiga tsakanin Farida da sadiq tun sunayi aboye har su Umma suka gano halin da suke ciki, daga Inna har umma kowanne su yayi farinciki sai binsu da Addu'a da sukeyi, duk Wanda yaga kulawar da Sadiq yake bawa farida sai ta birgeshi Banda suturu masu tsada da yake sai Mata duk wasu abubuwan kyale-kyale  da ya gani ya burgeshi zai siyo ya kawo Mata, babbar waya ya siya Mata me tsadar gaske gaba Daya farida ta Kara wayewa ta zama babbar Yarinya idan tayi kwalliya ta fita zakayi zatan 'yar gidan wani attajirin ce saboda yanda fatar jikin ta tayi kyau ta murje Hutu ya ratsa ta.

        Shekara ta Kara zagayowa dalibai nata fafutukar zana jarrabawa,  Registration din jamb sadiq yayiwa farida lokaci nayi taje ta zana sannan ya biya Mata kudin neco awata private school, bayan wani lokaci suka zana jarrabawa cikin ikon Allah duk sakamako ya fito duka farida ta samu abinda take so, duk wasu abubuwa da akeyiwa dalibi na neman Admission Sadiq yayi wa farida cikin ikon Allah tasamu gurbin karatu a Northwest University abangaren English Department.

      Umma ce takeyiwa farida nasiha game da Fara zuwanta school, " farida kinsan ita wannan makarantar da Zaki shiga ta had'a mutane da dama akwai na gari akwai Bata gari, kisan irin k'awayen da zakiyi sannan karki sake ki sakarwa malamai fuska tunda akwai masu halin dabbobi acikin, ki kare mutumci ki na 'ya mace, karatu ne ya kaiki ki tsaya kiyi karki biyewa kawayen banza wad'anda ba karatu ne yake kaisu ba, daga karshe Ina Miki Add'uar Allah ya bada nasara"
     Nasihar umma ta ratsa farida ahankali tace, " to insha Allah umma zan yi duk yanda kikace"
      Inna ce ta d'ora da cewa, " farida duk wani Abu da zan fad'a umma ta fade shi, Abu Daya zan ce Miki shine kiji tsoron Allah ki kame mutumcinki Allah ya taimaka ya bada abinda ake nema na alherin"
      Wannan Karan ma farida amsawa mahaifiyar ta tayi.

      Farida ta maida hankalinta sosai gurin karatu ta lura akwai dalibai kala-kala, ta lura ta halayyar yawancinsu, tun ranar data Fara zuwa wasu gayu suka kawo Mata tayin soyayya, ganin ba fuska yasa suka  Kama gabansu.
          Watan farida biyu da Fara zuwa makaranta akayi posting din su Sadiq garin kaduna, kafin tafiyar sadiq Account ya bud'e Mata duk wasu abubuwan bukata idan ya tashi turo Masu da kud'i ta account din Farida yake turowa, hatta kudin makarantar ta da na handouts duk tanan yake turo Mata.

     Saudat Kamal Muhammad itama Daya daga cikin classmates din Farida ce, Yar shahararran me kudi ce, tun ranar data Fara ganin farida taji ta kwanta Mata tana birgeta, ita ta Fara neman kawance da farida, farida ta lura da saudat Yarinya ce me hankali Sam batayi tunanin akwai yaran masu kudi Haka masu saukin Kai ba gashi abun hannunta Bai rufe Mata ido ba, saudat Allah yayita da San mutum kyakykywa duk da ita dinma ba mummuna bace itama da kokari dai-dai gwargwado da maida hankali, Bata da kule-kuken samari gata da faran-faran da mutane, Wanda baisan saudat ba zaiyi tunanin matsayinsu daya da farida saboda rayuwarta me sauki ta d'auketa.
        Ita kadai ce agurin mahaifi da mahaifiyar ta, shiyasa duk wani gatan duniya suke nuna Mata, duk abinda saudat take so to suma suna kaunarsa kuma duk abinda take San siya duk tsadarsa zasu siya mata, daga mahaifinta har mahaifiyar ta duk asalin su yan garin gombe ne zamane ya kawo su garin garin Kano a unguwar Nasaraw GRA, agarin Abuja suka Fara zama daga baya suka dawo Kano. 
        A Koda yaushe farida cikin bawa saudat labarin ya Sadiq take, daga baya har ya zamana suna gaisuwa idan ya Kira farida awaya Haka zasuyita Hira da saudat har itama tayi sabo da sadiq tunda dama ta sanshi ahoto, tunda dadewa saudat tayi-tayi da farida akan taje suje gidansu Amma Taki, itakuwa saudat Tasha zuwa gurinsu umma yafi a irga hartakai sun Saba dasu umma, wata Rana ne itama farida ta shirya saudat ta tura driver yaje har gida ya d'auko ta  yakai farida gidan su saudat, farida kanta ba karamin kullewa yayi ba yanda take fasalta gidansu saudat ya wuce haka,  iyayen saudat mutanen kirkine masu San jama'a Haka shima mahaifinta mutum ne Mai yawan fara'a, lokacin da farida zata taho Sha Tara ta arziki suka had'a mata har taxo gida tana mamakin dukiya irin tasu saudat, haka ta dinga bawa su Umma labarin gidansu saudat da kayan alatun data gani agidan.
         Saudat ta d'auki farida babbar k'awa ko ince Aminiya Bata boye Mata komai game da ita, duk abinda zata siya tare take yi Musu itada farida, sutura kuwa dama kusan kullin iri Daya suke sawa, ko lokacin da Daddyn saudat yaje Dubai sak'o saudat ta bashi ya siyo Musu waya da laptop ita da farida, hakan kuwa akayi farida sai godiya da take yiwa saudat ita kuwa sai dai ta b'ata fuska, saboda Tasha Gaya Mata ta daina yi Mata godiya ita ta d'auketa Yar uwa ne, idan farida ta bawa Sadiq labarin karamcin da saudat take Mata takanas yake Kiran saudat yayi Mata godiya saboda farida ta bawa Sadiq number saudat, wani hutun semester da suka samu lokacin saudat da iyayenta zasu tafi umara tare tasa mahaifinta ya biya Musu suka tafi da farida, satinsu biyu suka dawo tsaraba kala-kala haka farida tayiwa su Umma Amma fin rabi na Sadiq sunfi yawa, kowa yaga kawancen su farida da saudat sai sun birgeshi ba Wanda yakejin kansu.
      Sau tari saudat idan taga irin soyayyar da farida sukeyi da Sadiq abun ba karamin birgeta yakeyi ba, har wani lokacin takan ce Mata, " nidai nagaji da jin labarin ya Sadiq dinan idan naganshi ido da ido zan tambayeshi wacce irin mallaka yayiwa zuciyarki Haka" sai dai farida tayi dariya tace, " hmm Dan bakisan waye ya Sadiq bane".

Wani hutu da sadiq ya dawo gida ne ya shirya yaje ya samu su farida a school bayan sun fito daga lectures ya kirata yake sanar da ita ya karaso makarantar su, lokacin da saudat taga sadiq lallai ta yarda da abinda Farida take Gaya mata, ba karamin birgeta suka yi ba sunyi matukar dacewa da juna.

       SADIQ da farida soyayya sai abinda yayi gaba, lokacin da farida ta shiga level 3 umma ta yiwa dangin mahaifin sadiq maganar aurensa da farida, lokacin da hafsa taji wannan labarin kamar tayi hauka, Amma da yake mahaifiyar ta na tausarta sai abun yaje Mata da sauki.
      
       Tashin farko dangin  mahaifin Sadiq kin amincewa sukayi acewarsu bazasu yarda d'ansu ya auro Musu Yar da basusan asalinta bare tushenta, yayan baban Sadiq me suna Alh karami ne ya rufe umma da fad'a, " Rukayya kinbani mamaki yaushe kikayi sanyi Haka kina tunanin zamu Bari Abubakar yaje ya auro Yarinya da Bata da dangin uba ne, su biyu ne tak aduniya kamar tsuntsaye wato ke me d'a ko to wallahi baku isaba kedashi d'in"
     Umma ce tace," Yaya wallahi mutanen nan ba mutanen banza bane Kuma sun gaya mun duk tarihinsu........." Da sauri ya katseta, " me suka Gaya muku ?" 
       Duk abinda Inna ta gayawa umma shi ta bawa Alh karami labari sannan ta Kara da cewa, "  Yaya Dan Allah karkayi Musu mummunar fahimta wallahi yaran Nan sun dad'e suna kaunar junansu"
      Wata Yar dariya Alh karami yayi yace, " wannan labarin wasan kwaikwaiyon da Kika bani kin d'auka zasuyi tasiri agurina, wad'annan kalaman basu Isa su yaudareni ba, Wallahi muddin banga dangin mahaifin Yarinyar Nan ba Abubakar bazai aureta ba idan Kuma kuka dage akai kuje ke sai kiyi Masa auran Amma ki sani ka za daga cikin danginmu bazaki gani ba Kuma daga ranar Daya aureta mun yafeshi daga cikin zuriyarmu, Zancen banza duk ga Yara Nan acikin family wato basuyi Masa ba harsai yaje ya kwaso Mana wata"

   Jiki asanyaye umma ta taso ta baro gurinsa, Koda ta dawo gida da damuwa fal fuskarta Amma duk yanda Inna taso jin me yake damun umma abun ya faskara, duk abinda yake faruwa Saida umma ta sanarwa da sadiq gaba Daya abin duniya ya dameshi, bayan faruwar maganar da sati Daya Sadiq ya dawo gidan Alh karami yaje duk yanda yaso yashawo Kansa abun ya faskara, magana daya ya Gaya Masa itace yanasan sanin dangin mahaifin farida kuma yanasan yayi tozali dasu.

     Duk yanda suka so boye maganar Saida ta fito tunda sharad'in da Alh karami ya basu kenan, umma ce ta sanar da Inna halin da ake ciki, hankalin Inna ba karamin tashi yayi ba musamman idan ta tuna gurin wad'anda zasuje, ita kanta tasan watan wata Rana dama dole haka zata faru, wani tunani tayi azuciyarta na cewar wannan shine mutumcin farida, sannan ta Kara Tunowa da wasiyyar mlm lokacin da yake ce Mata ta nemi 'yan uwansa idan farida ta samu mijin aure, wannan wasiyyar data tuno ta Kara famo Mata da rashin mlm asanyaye ta furta, " Allah ya gafarta maka mlm, ubangiji ga bayinka Nan Allah ka d'ora mu Akan dangin mahaifin Yarinyar Nan.
      Sati biyu suka tsaida zasu tafi cen garin Adamawa, alokacin su farida sun samu hutun semester, wad'anda zasu tafi daga Alh karami, umma, Inna, Sadiq, farida sai kanin mahaifin Sadiq wato mahaifin hafsa.
       Wata ranar Asabar da duku-duku suka shirya suka dauki hanyar Adamawa, itadai Inna fargabarta Daya irin tarbar dasu Yaya Ali zasuyi Musu, abangaren farida kuwa murna take cikin zuciyarta duk da tasan mugun halin iyayenta amma ko ba komai yau zata ga wani daga cikin dangin mahaifan nata.
      Lokacin da suka shiga cikin kauyen su mlm yanayin gine-gine Nan ne suka canjawa Inna hakan ne yasa Bata gane ba Saida sukayi tambaya sannan aka kwatanta Musu tiryen-tiryen har kofar gidan su mlm, tsarin gidan na Nan yanda yake sai dai D'an canje-canjen da akayi Masa Ankara zamanantar da gidan ba kamar da ba, su Alh karami aka Bari a mota Inna da dasu umma suka wuce cikin gidan, farida ce Kan gaba su Inna na biye da sallama suka shiga cikin gidan, wani D'an dattijo ne ya kafe farida da kallo bayan ya amsa sallamar.
    
     _Juma'@t Khareem_

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                     37&38

_Not Edited_
  
Da mamaki a fuskarsa ya furta, " Yar Sudan kece, ko idanuna ne suke mun gixo?" Ya karasa fad'a Yana nuna farida.
       Inna ce tayi karfin hali tace, " gani Nan". Mutumin waigawa yayi Yana kallan Inna sai alokacin ne ya gano cewar waccen ba 'yar Sudan din bace me kama da itace, mamaki ne k'arara ya Kara bayyana akan fuskarsa yace, " Yar Sudan Ina hashimun yake? Tsawon shekaru Ina kuka shiga " Inna ce tace, " Yaya muttaka ad'an Bamu guri mu zauna" dan dingisawa yayi Yana cilla mak'alalliyar kafarsa kwalawa matarsa Kira yakeyi kafin wani lokaci tuni tsakar gidan ya cika da Yara, yan Mata da samari harda masu rarrafe, kallan su Inna sukeyi kamar sunga bak'in hallita, da yawan cikinsu basusan Inna ba tunda wasu ana goyonsu lokacin data bar gidan wasu kuwa ba'a ma haifesu ba wasu, da yawa daga cikin su kuwa duk batama game fuskokinsu ba, katuwar tabarma aka shinfid'a Musu acikin rumfa, lokacin da Inna ta zauna Tunowa tayi lokacin baya awannan rumfar facalolinta suke zama suna yi mata shagube, uwani ce tsaye tana susar Kai tabi su Inna da kallo tace was Yaya muttak'a, " baban jamilu bak'in birni mukayi ne? Wallahi sai nake ganin kamar da idon sani acikinsu?" Tsaki Yaya muttak'a yayi yace, " ni banasan wannan sakarcin naki wuce ki deb'o Musu ruwa" buguzum-buguzum uwani ta wuce tana tura Kai gaba alamun taji haushi, lokacin da aka kawowa su Inna ruwa sanar da Yaya muttak'a sukayi cewar basu kad'ai suke tafe ba akwai sauran bak'i amota, atake ya tura aka Kai wa su Alh karami tabarma da ruwa, Inna tun zuwanta take mamakin ganin sauyi Daya gurin Yaya muttak'a kamar bashi din data sani ba.

   Gaisawa suka shiga yi bayan sun gama gaisawa Inna ta Fara magana, " Yaya wad'annan bayin Allah daka gani muke tare da su, wannan itace farida munzo ne gurinku domin auren Yar ku ne ya tashi" Inna na rufe Baki  suka ji alamar shigowa cikin gidan, dama tin saukarsu labari ya bazu acikin garin anyi bak'in yan birni, yayyan marigayi malam ne, da fara'a suka shigo sunayiwa su Umma barka da zuwa, turus sukayi suna karewa Inna kallo ba Wanda ya tanka Amma kowannen su da mamaki fal cikin zuciyarsa, Yaya muttak'a ne ta basu umarnin zama sannan yace Musu, " Ina fatan kun shaida wannan fuskar" yakarasa maganar Yana nuna Inna.
     Gaba Daya k'ura Mata ido sukayi zuwa wani lokaci sukace, " tabbas mun shaidata matar hashimu ce to shi Yana Ina?" Inna ce ta karbe da, " 'dan uwanku Allah yayi Masa rasuwa ga dai magajiyarsa Nan Daya Bari sunanta Farida " wannan Karan ma ido suka Kara zubawa farida, gaba Daya salati suka d'auka suna jimamin kamar gaske, bayan sun gama Yaya Ali yace, " to ko kayan rabon gadon mu kuka kawo mana? "

Lokaci Daya Inna dasu umma suka kalli juna cikin zuciyar Inna tace, " lallai Mai hali baya fasa halinsa" D'an gyara zama tayi tace maganar auren Yar ku ne ya kawo mu, waliyyan Angon suna wajen acikin mota " daya Daya cikin ne ta kece wata sakaryar dariya sannan yace, " Yar Sudan wato sai yanxu kikasan munada ranako, tsahon shekara da shekaru kin gujemu sama ko k'asa shine yanxu saboda aurenta yaxo har Zaku xo gurinmu, Amma bazance komai ba har sai naji ta bakin y'an uwana " luwali Kani ne agurin malam shine yace, " Yaya wannan ay bakin ciki ne zakayi Mana kana ganin alamar za'a lashi zuma zaka nemi kayi Mana sagegeduwa, munji mun amince indai aure nee mun bashi duniya da lahira " umma sai yanxu take Kara tabbatar da duk labarin da Inna  ta Bata, tabbas wad'annan mutanen basusan darajar nasu ba, sudai burinsu su Sami kud'i, Yaya muttak'a ne yace, " haba lauwali mubi abin asannu Mana da girmanku Haka Bai kamata ya fito daga bakinku ba" akuwa kamar Wanda ya Watso musu wuta haka suka rufeshi ba masifa ba yanda ya iya haka yaja bakinsa yayi tsit.

     Umma ganin abun nasu tayi bana hankali bane yasa ta Fara magana, "Salamu alaikum, dan Allah ku D'an dakata Mana muyi abinda ya kawo mu, neman aure muka zo ba tashin hankali ba, mu Mata maganar ba ahannunmu take ba akwai wan mahaifin yaron da kaninsa tare mukaxo dasu Zaku iya zuwa gurinsu sai ku tattauna acen " umma nagama magana suka tashi duuuuu kamar awaki sukayi waje, da ido yaya muttak'a ya bisu bayan fitarsu ya Fara bawa su Inna hakuri sannan ya Fara rok'on ta gafara, " Sadiya abaya banida wasu makiya da suka wuce keda mijinki, ke kinsani ba wata irin kiyayyar da ban nuna muku ba, daga baya shiriya taxo mun nayi nadama kwarai Akan abubuwan Dana aikata, naso inhadu da D'an uwana innemeshi gafara Amma hakan Bai Sami ba, ki yafemun sadiya ni kad'ai ne Allah ya sanyomun shiriya saboda Yan uwa sunyi nisa basajin Kira, kina ganin yanda suka tsaneni suke tsangwamata saboda Ina Gaya Musu gaskiya ashe fadar gaskiyar Hashim ce tasa abaya muka tsaneshi bama kaunarsa, nikam nayi nadama ki taimaka ki yafemun sadiya" Yaya muttak'a ya karasa maganar idanunsa na zubda kwallah.
        Gaba daya sai jikinsu Inna yayi sanyi ahankali tace, " na yafe muku Yaya nidama bantab'a rukonku azuciyata ba, Allah ya yafe Mana gaba Daya" murmushin jindadi yayi sannan yacewa farida, " 'yata matso kusa Mana mu gaisa" farida matsawa tayi gurin Yaya muttak'a ya ruko hannunta yace, " yau Kinga tsohon babanki da baki sanshi ba ko?" Murmushi farida tayi tace, " baba Koda bansanku ba Amma Ina ganinku Naga Kuna mun yanayi da Babana musamman wannan na d'azun Daya gama magana" D'an gyara kafarsa me ciwon Yaya muttak'a yayi yace, " ay mun Nan da Kika ganmu duk kusan kamarmu Daya saboda Kama mukeyi da marigayi Lamido, bari na karasa mu gaisa da surukan nawa " tashi yayi da kyar Yana D'an dingisawa da kallan tausayi su Inna suka bishi har ya fice.

   Matan gidan ne suka karaso gurinsu saboda labari ya isar musu Yar Sudan ta zo, abinci da abinsha suka dinga kawo Musu kasancewar ance Musu da wasu Alhazawa suka xo, sai Hira suke yiwa Inna ta yaushe gamo ita Inna abun har mamaki yake bata Wai yau itace dangin Hashim suke yiwa Haka lallai duniya juyi-juyi, ganin sun saki jikinsu da ita itama ta saki jiki dasu tambayar wasu daga cikin y'an uwan Hashim tayi wad'ada Bata gansu ba wasu ace Mata basa Nan wasunsu Kuma sun rasu, Yaya murtala kuma aka ce Mata Yana gidan yari saboda samun shi da akayi da mugayen laifuka.

Gabatar Musu da kansu sukayi su Alh karami da manufar da take tattare dasu, ko kadan dangin mahaifin farida basuja da maganar ba saboda sa Rai da sukeyi da Dan hasafi, shiyasa duk yanda Alh karami yace sai dai suke to, atake agurin Alh karami ya fito da kudi dubu d'ari ya bayar da sunan kudin nagani Ina samu, maganar sa Rana ce suka ce sai farida ta kamalla karatu, idan aka tashi sawa baifi asaka wata biyu zuwa uku ba, kowa yayi na'am da maganar dama tuni Yaya Ali ya karb'e kudin auren farida yayi ram dasu ahannu, sadiq nacen daga gefe Kan kujera Yana jin duk abinda suke tattaunawa, ta text message ya tura wa farida sak'o kamar haka, " _Farin cikina Ina fatan kina cikin koshin lafiya, nasan kina cikin farin ciki da samun dangin mahaifinki cikin k'oshin lafiya, ga albishir din da San sanar Miki Amma sai kin bani tukwici_" tura Mata yayi aykuwa farida najin shigowar sak'o ta Duba, ganin sadiq ne ya turo Mata yasa ta bud'e sak'on cikin zumud'i, bayan ta karanta sak'on murmushi tayi tace, " _inajinka wane irin tukwici  kake da buk'ata? Fad'i kanka tsaye ashirye nake na baka shi my soulmate_" tura Masa sak'on itama tayi tana jin farinciki da annushuwa azuciyarta, sadiq na bud'e sak'on farida yayi Mata reply da, " _Alh ya bayar da kudin aurenki sun Kuma ce kina karasa karatu za'a tsaida Mana ranar Aure, Ina cikin farin ciki Mara musaltuwa saura kiris ya rage inmallaki sarauniyata_" bayan ya tura Mata itama reply ta tura masa, Haka sukayi ta hirarsu kowannen su Yana bayyanawa D'an uwansa farin cikin da yake ciki.

     Bayan sun kammalla neman auren farida Alh karami ne ya umarci su Umma dasu fito Dan su tafi, Yaya muttak'a ne ya hanasu tafiya ba yanda basuyi ba Dan su juya aranar Amma yaki yace su Bari zuwa safiya, ba yanda suka iya Haka suka hakura, 'ya'ya da jikokin gidan su mahaifin farida sai zuwa suke kallan su Inna kwanikan abinci kuwa sai da sukaci har suka ture, agurin kwana ma har abun so yayi yazama da fad'a kowacce na cewa ad'akin ta su Inna zasu kwana, daga karshe dai suka yi kwanciyar su ad'akin matar Yaya muttak'a, washe gari ba yanda Yaya muttak'a baiyi da 'yan uwansa ba akan su bawa Inna kudin auren Amma suka k'ek'ashe k'asa sukayi shiru da maganar, haka su Inna suka said kafa suka baro garin ko hirar kud'in ma basuyi Mata ba sai Yaya muttak'a ne yasanar da ita duk yanda ake ciki, ita dinma ko afuska Bata nuna Musu komai ba Amma cikin zuciyarta tana Kara jaddada mamakinta na son zuciya irin na dangin mlm, Koda ta sanarwa da umma cewa tayi ta kyalesu Allah zai rufa asiri I Auren yazo.

Bayan dawowarsu farida ce ta samu saudat awaya ta sanar da ita halin da ake ciki, ba karamin murna saudat ta Taya farida ba, Haka ta Kira sadiq tana tsokanarsa saboda saudat ta maida sadiq kamar kakanta.


Tun Daga ranar umma ta shiga yiwa farida Dan tanadi itace sayi wannan sayi wancen ita kanta umma sana'ar Saida Mai ta farayi cikin ikon Allah sana'ar ta karbeta Yar ribar da ita take zuwa kasuwa tana yiwa farida Yar siyayyar kayan da ba'a rasa ba, ta bangaren saudat itama ba karamin burin bikin farida taci ba idan kaga yanda take d'okin bikin zakayi tunin itace Amaryar, farida ta kowanne fanni da zama Yar gata gaba da baya sannu ahankali lokaci yake ta karatowa har su farida suka shiga semester su ta karshe wato sun kusa yin graduation, Ango Sadiq ba abinda yasa agaba sai hidimar had'a lefe komai ya gani siya yake Yana kawowa gida, idan kaga kayan da Sadiq ya had'a zakayi tunanin wani hamshak'in me kudin ne ya had'a su, shiyasa akace gayawa me zuciya biki bame dukiya ba.
    Farida karatu take yi tukuru Dan ganin ta kamalla makarantar ta ba tare da wata matsala ba, ita da saudat sun dage sosai kowa yi yakeyi iya karfinsa.
      Duk Abinda akace maka andibar Masa lokaci to da sannu zakaga karshensa, wata ranar talata itace ta kasan ranar dasu Farida zasu zana paper ta karshe, bayan sallar la'asar suka rubuta ta suka fito, rukunin d'alibansa ne kala-kala kowannen su da irin Ankon da suka saka kowa zaganshi Yan uwansa sunzo anata d'aukan hotuna cikin farin ciki, idan ka kalli fuskar d'aliban zaka fahimci kowanne su Yana cikin farin ciki, sadiq sun d'auki hotuna shida farida yafi a irga, hakama saudata 'yan uwanta da iyayenta sunxo ana ta celebration, lokaci Daya wasu daga cikin d'aliban sau hau saman motoci d'auke da manyan speakers kid'a na tashi, wasu daga cikin su har rawa suke takawa, bayan wani lokaci su farida dasu Inna duk suka tarkato suka taho gida.

   🙄🙄🙄 _Bazafa mu manta da sanin Asalin sadiq ba, Dan bazan bashi auren farida ba sai nasan wanene shi Kuma Dan wane gari?_🤪

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   39&40


Abubakar muktar Abdullahi zango shine ainihin cikekken sunan sadiq, mahaifinsa marigayi muktar Abdullahi zango Asalinsu 'yan  garin daura ne acikin wata rugar fulani me suna madaka, su biyar ne agurin iyayensu maza hudu mace Daya, Akwai Alh karami Wanda shine babban yayansu Salisu sai Muktar me binsa, mahaifin sadiq ne na uku  sai Habiba take binsa sannan sabi'u shine Dan Autansu wato mahaifin hafsa.
      Kasuwanci ne ya fito da Mahaifinsu daga garin daura zuwa cikin garin Kano bayan ya auro matarsa me suna Amina wacce take kanwa ce agareshi  ya dawo gaba daya cikin garin Kano da zama a unguwar tudun Maliki, Yana kasuwanci na fata Allah ya  albarkace shi.
     Mahaifin su Sadiq sun sunyi karatunsu daga primary har zuwa secondary sai dai anan suka dakata, yayinda Sabi'u kaninsu shi yake da sha'awar karatun Kuma shine ya cigaba har ya kammala karatunsa ya shiga aikin soja, akwana atashi ba wuya agurin Allah kowannen su yayi k'arfi ahararkar sana'ar Mahaifinsu ta fata, tunda kwarinsa Yana iya zuwa har girma ya kamashi ya zamana su suke gudanar da komai na harkar kasuwancin, sai da aka aurar da duk su muktar daga baya nahaifansu suka rasu, bayan rasuwar Mahaifinsu ma saida suka cigaba juya jarinsu suna raba ribar atsakaninsu, habiba da take gidan mijinta saidai insun raba su tura Mata nata.

      Mahaifin sadiq mlm Muktar ya had'u da mahaifiyar sadiq me suna Aisha anan unguwar zooroad, Wanda asalinta ya kasance ya Nan unguwarce, bayan sun dai-daita kansu ya nemi izinin tura manyansa, hakan kuwa akayi aka tsaida Musu da ranar aure lokacin biki nayi aka shafa fatiha, bayan Auren Aisha da muktar zamansu suke cikin kwanciyar hankali.
    Umma haihuwarta hudu duka Mata, Fatima, zainab, nabila da nafita, daga kansu Bata Kara haihuwa ba Saida ta shafe shekara goma Sha biyar ta samu wani cikin, har ta fidda Rai da haihuwa Allah yayi nashi ikon ta haifo d'anta namiji Wanda yaci suna Abubakar Sadiq daga kanshi Bata Kara haihuwa ba, son duniya su Umma suka d'ora wa Sadiq kasancewar shi kad'ai ne namiji tilo acikin yaransu.
     Fatima da zainab tare akayi Musu aure, bayan wata Tara zainab ta haifo jaririyarta bayan wata biyu da haihuwa zainab Fatima ta rasu awajen haihuwa, mutuwar data girgiza su matuk'a ranar sadakar bakwai Mahaifinsu sadiq shima yace ga garin ku Nan.

    Bayan anyi Addu'ar bakwai dangin mahifinsu Sadiq sukace zasu tafi da duk yaran umma, nabila, nafisa da Sadiq Wanda alokacin baifi shekara biyu ba, umma Sam cewa tayi batasan zance ba, suma Kuma sukace bazasu yarda ba sai sun d'auki  yaran D'an uwansu sun rabasu kowa ya dauki dai-dai, ba karamin gwagwrmaya akasha ba Amma umma Taki yarda daga Haka suka sallama Mata yaran gaba Daya, da farko fushi sukayi da ita har yaran ko tsinke bame zuwa ya gansu bare ya kawo Musu, Haka umma tayita d'awainiya da yaranta ita ce har wankau da sirfe danta rufa Musu Asiri saukinta ma babban yayansu Yana taimaka Mata da D'an kayan abinci, sai bayan ta aurar dasu nabila sannan Kuma dangin mahaifin nasu suka gane sunyi kuskure sannan suka dawo suka tallafi sadiq daduk d'awainiyar sa, musamman Alh sabi'u ( _mahaifin hafsa_) da yake duk yafisu karfi duk wasu harkokinsa na karatun Boko Dana Addini shi yake ja Masa gaba wajen ganin ya gabatar dasu, bayan da Sadiq ya zama saurayi alokacin ya kamalla degree dinsa aiki na wuyar samu hakan ne yasa Alh sabi'u y Samar Masa aiki anan gidan Mai na AA RANO, da wata babbar sallah su nabila sun taho daga garin Zaria sukayi accident duk Yan motar suka k'one kurmus, kasancewar mazajansu wa da k'anine agari Daya suke aure.

        _A CIGABA DA GASHI....._
      
      Ranar dasu Farida suka kamalla degree dinsu ranar ne Allah ya had'ata da wani saurayi mujaheed, mujaheed had'ad'd'an Gaye ne iya haduwa mahaifinsa shine commissioner ilimi, gurin wata kanwarsa ya shigo tayawa murnar kamalla karatunta anan suka had'u yaji saudat ta kwanta Masa arai, alokacin tuni su farida sun jima da tafiya, itama alokacin abinda ya tsaida su driver su yaje ya Sha Mai ya dawo, exchanging phone number sukayi bayan ta koma gida ya kirata suka Fara Hira, tana gama waya da mujaheed ta Kira farida ta sanar Mata tun Daga farkon had'uwarsu har wayar da sukayi, farida ba karamin farinciki tayi ba cikin zolaya tace Mata, " Yarinya lallai ke Y'ar baiwa dama kince bazaki had'a karatu da soyayya ba gashi kin kammala Kuma kinsamu masoyi aranar da Kika gama, Ina tayaki murna kawata Allah Sanya alheri Allah sa ayi damu Amma fa Naga Gayen Nan da zafinshi ya sauka lokaci daya Naga kinyi WUFF da d'an mutane "  dariya saudat tayi tace, " kutsiii kefa 'yar iska ce ni ce ma nayi WUFF dashi wato bama shi ba, d'adin abundai ba akaina aka Fara WUFF da d'an mutane ba" ta Karasa maganar tana y'ar dariya k'asa-k'asa, farida ce tace, " oho na gane inda maganar ki ta dosa kinyi na lokacinki Yarinya sai anjima" farida ta katse wayar tana dariya.

    Bayan wani lokaci ciwon kirjin Inna ne yake D'an tasar Mata lokaci-lokaci, idan ya tashi tana Shan magani zai kwanta wata Rana ne ta tashi da matsanancin ciwon, numfashin ta har d'aukewa yakeyi agigice sukayi asibitin nasarawa, likitoci ne rufe akanta suna Bata agajin gaggawa, Alhamdulillah kwananta biyu a asibitin jikinta yayi sauk'i ya warware aka sallamosu gida, ahaka jikin Inna sai yayi kyau sai Kuma ciwon ya Kara dawo Mata wannan Karan ma ciwon zuciyar tata ne ya Kara tashi lokacin da su Umma suka dauketa suka kaita asibiti ko numfashi batayi, Saida sukaje aka sa Mata oxygen da taimakon Allah sannan numfashinta ya dawo Amma Bata cikin hayyacinta Bata masan waye akanta ba, gwaje-gwaje akayi Mata sannan likitoci suka tabbatar da sai anyi Mata aiki azuciya saboda wadansu irin k'aruruwa (k'ari masu yawa)  ne masu yawa suka fito Mata, Kuma aikin ba'ayinsa a k'asar Nan sai k'asar India, ga yanda lissafin kud'in aikin ya kama 12.5millions ne, ba su Umma ba hatta sadiq da akeyiwa waya asanar dashi halin da ake ciki Saida hankalinsa ya tashi.

Gaba daya tunaninsu ya tsaya akan yanda zasuyi su samu wannan kud'aden ne, filin da sadiq ya saya na dubu d'ari takwas tuni ya sashi akasuwa,umma ma tana da wani tohon filinta tace da sadiq duk ya had'a ya sasu akasuwa idan ansamu masu siya asayar, anyi dace ansamu gaba Daya ansaida dasu kudin filayen 2million, abun duniya duk yabi ya dame su musamman idan suka tuna da yawan mak'udan kud'aden aikin, umma danginta tayi wa bayani aka dinga had'a Y'an
 kud'ad'e daga gurun y'an uwan ta aka had'a dubu dari biyar, abangaren su Alh karami suma sun had'a nasu na dai-dai gwargwado sun bayar da sadakar dubu dari bakwai.
      Idan ka dubi yanayin y'an gidan gaba Daya basa cikin nutsuwarsu kowa cikin zullimi yake, Dan ma jikin na Inna ya Dan yin kyau tunda tana Gane mutane har tana Musu magana, wannan ne ma ya Kara k'arfafa Musu gwiwar Nemo kudin aikin nata, farida duk ta fita hayyacinta komai nasu ta Kai kasuwa ta saida.
      Saudat ce taje Duba pjikin Inna asanyaye ta fito daga d'akin da inna take ta kalli farida tace Mata, " yanzu farida kina nufun Inna tana cikin wannan mugun yanayin? Amma basuyi Mata aiki ba?" Da alama saudat Bata gama sanin me ake ciki ba, farida ce ta jingina da bango tana lumshe idanu cikin damuwa tace, " saudat ba anan k'asar za'ayiwa Inna aiki ba, hasalima kud'in aikin ma da gindin kansa yake zaune, yanxu Haka mun Saida duk wani Abu da mukasan zai kawo Mana kudin, lefe na ma jiya na bawa ya sadiq shawara yakai kasuwa kawai ya Saida tunda bamuda hali, d'azu ma na cewa umma mukai kokenmu gidajen radio ko zamu samu Allah ya taimaka akara had'o Mana wasu, saudat wai yaushe ne zamu tsallake wad'annan jarabobin ne nida inna......." Da sauri saudat ta rufe Mata Baki tana Fad'in, " karki ce komai farida komai kikaga ya samu bawa da sanin ubangiji, yanxu nawa ne sukace kudin aikin?" Ajiyar zuciya farida ta sauke tace, " Haka ne kawata, 12.5 suka ce Kuma a k'asar India ne, ga Nan dinma zaman da mukeyi awannan asibitin Banda kashe k'udi ba abinda akeyi " jinjina kanta kasa saudat tayi tace, " hasbunallahu wani'imal wakeel, babu komai Allah zai rufa asiri, ungo wannan kwa sayi magani sai munyi waya" agurguje saudat ta Mik'awa farida dubu Hamsi tayi gaba ko sallamar kirki basuyi ba.
   Farida na komawa ciki ta mik'awa umma kud'in tanayi mata bayani, godiya umma tayi sosai sannan taje ta sayo musu 'yan abubuwan da ba'a rasa ba da Kuma sauran magungunan da ba'a siya ba na Inna.

Saudat agurguje ta shiga cikin falonsu Mommyn ta na zaune tana Shan fura da nono, gaba daya yanayin saudat ba walwala da hanzari take tambayar mahaifiyar tata, " mommy har yanxu daddy na sama Bai fita ba? " Kallan tsaf mommy tayiwa Farida tace, " Eh Yana sama" tun kafin mommy ta rufe Baki saudat ta kama step din bene ta haye saman da ido mahaifiyar ta tabita da kallo har ta haye.
     Kamar anjefa ta d'akin haka ta shiga da sallama, gefe ta samu ta zauna tayi shiru tana tunanin ta inda zata Fara gabatarwa da mahaifinta bukatarta, jin shirun yayi yawa yasa Daddyn saudat d'agowa Yana janye laptop din da ke gefen cinyarsa ya kalleta yace, "ya akayi ne 'yan matana ko akwai abunda yake damunki ne?" Aykuwa dama kamar jira take ta shiga zayyanowa Daddynta abinda yake faruwa da Inna.
     Dan Jim kad'an yayi bayan ya gama saurare ta yace, " 'yan Mata na kinaji da wannan kawar taki da yawa, but zan bada Rabin kudin aikin mamanta right? " Ya karasa maganar Yana kallan fuskar saudat.
    D'an tsuke fuska tayi ta Kara shagwabe fuska tace, " daddy ni gaskiya duka nakesan ka biya mata mutanen Nan fa basu da hali Kuma kasan farida she is my one and only friend da muka Dade tare tana so na sosai daddy" murmushi Daddyn saudat yayi yace, " Ay ba ita kadai take sanki ba kema na lura kinaji da wannan faridan, shikenan zan biya Mata Haka yayi?" har k'asa saudat ta tsugunna tana godewa mahaifinta bisa karamcin da ya nuna Mata, wata irin soyayyar 'ya da ubace ta Kara ratsa jinin jikinta, da murna ta fice daga d'akin tana Fad'in, " Bari na kira farida na Gaya Mata " da ido Mahaifinta ya bita Yana murmushi ya ciki gaba da aikin da yakeyi a laptop dinsa.
     Saudat na sauka k'asa ta sanarwa da mahaifiyarta duk yanda sukayi, itadin ma cewa tayi zata dinga sa driver ya dinga Kai Musu abinci asibiti kafin su tafi India, sanar da farida tayi duk yanda sukayi da daddynta farida Dan murna harda kuka, adaren ranar kuwa basuyi baccin kirki ba saboda farin ciki.
     Washegari da safe umma da farida sukaje har gida sukayiwa Daddyn saudat godiya, sannan suka Kara godiya da irin karamcin da saudat take nunawa farida, acikin satin ne transfer aikin sadiq ta dawo Kano aranar daya sauka ranar yaje har gida yayiwa mahaifin saudat godiya.
    Tun Daga ranar saudat da mujaheed sai sunje asibitin dubo jikin Inna, ahankali Sadiq da mujaheed suka sama.
    Duk wasu shirye-shiryen tafiya da zasuyi k'asar India Daddyn saudat ne yayi Musu ticket biyu yayi Musu na Inna da umma kasancewar karatowar tafiyarsu farida bautar k'asa (NYSC), kudin filayen da sauran kud'ad'en da aka Tara umma cewa tayi sadiq yasa a Account dinsa daga baya zasu yi amfani.

    Wata ranar Alhamis ranar ne su Umma suka tashi zuwa k'asar Indian Daddyn saudat ne ya had'a su da wani abokinsa mazaunin cen yayi Masa duk bayanin zuwansu da abinda ya kaisu cen din.
     Satin su Inna da tafiya suma su farida suka shiga Shirin tafiya, wannan karan su farida sun rarraba saboda ita farida ankaita Benin ita Kuma saudat tana katsina, duk wasu abubuwan tafiya Sadiq shi yayiwa farida komai, haka ta b'angaren saudat ma kusan komai biyu take saya Musu, lokacin da zata tafi shi ya kaita har tasha ya sata amota Saida motarsu ta tashi sannan ya juyo ya koma gida.
       Cikin ikon Allah anyiwa Inna aiki cikin nasara ansamu yanda ake so sai jiran Shan magani da kwarin jiki da takeyi Amma babu abinda yake Mata ciwo Bata da damuwar komai.
       Da farko kowaccen su bataji d'adin zamanta agarin da aka tuttura ta ba, sai da su farida suka D'an saba sannan suka Fara jin dadin garuruwan da suke ciki, wata k'awa farida tayi me suna lubna itama y'ar Kano ce shiyasa ma kawancen su yaxo daya, kullin suka yi waya da sadiq fad'an da yake Mata ta kama mutumcin kanta, saboda gurine nacin gashin Kai kowa abinda ya ga dama shi yakeyi, kwatsam wata Rana farida ta dawo daga diban ruwa ta samu wayar saudat ta Mata Miss call, kiranta farida tayi awayar da sukayi saudat take ce mata mujaheed zai kirata Dan shima ya shiga garin Benin gurin wani kanin babansa zaixo gurinta su gaisa, aykuwa suna gama waya Kiran mujaheed ya shigo cikin wayar farida, bayan sun gaisa ne take sanar dashi kwatacen gidan da take, ba afi awa guda da wayarsu ba ya karaso gidan hannunsa niki-niki da tsaraba, abinci ta zuba Masa 3spoon kawai yayi yace ya koshi agurguje ya wuce, godiya farida take Masa har motarsa ta b'ace daga gurin, sanarwar da saudat tayi duk abubuwan daya kawo Mata sannan tace Mata akara Mik'a Mata godiya agurinsa.

     Bayan wasu watanni tuni su Inna sun dad'e da dawowa jiki yayi garau ta warke kamar ba ita ba, umma ce ta raka Inna har gida ta yiwa dadyn saudat godiya, lokacin da farida taji dawowar su Inna ba shiri ta had'a Jakarta tazo gida sai da taga yanda Inna ta murmure sannan hankalinta ya Kara kwanciya, kwananta biyu ta had'a kayanta ta koma.

     Akwana atashi ba wuya agurin Allah, su farida har sun kamalla bautar k'asar su kowa ya dawo gida, lokacin da saudat taxo gurin farida da suka ga juna kamar sa fasa gidan da k'ara suna murna sai da sukayi me isarsu sannan suka zauna hirar yaushe gamo, bayan kwana biyu aka sa ranar farida da Sadiq wata biyu Wanda lokacin iyayen mujaheed har sun kawo kud'in Auren saudat ankuma sa Mata Rana wata shida masu zuwa.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  41&42


      Shirye-shirye daga wad'annan b'angarorin sai kankama suke, Inna farin ciki take har murnarta taki boyiwa, lambar Yaya muttak'a ta Kira ta sanar dashi maganar sa rana Haka ya nuna farin cikinsa afili ya shaida Mata zuwansu idan lokacin bikin yayi, umma ba kunya haka ta zage tana gyara farida kamar ita ta haifeta saboda ta d'auki farida kamar diyarta ta cikinta, ganin irin gyaran da umma takewa farida yasa Inna Bata ba ta komai ba game da wasu magungunan gyaran jiki, umma ta hana farida zuwa ko Ina acewarta saboda gyaran jiki Yana bukatar nutsuwa, sai dai idan akwana biyu saudat ta kawo Mata ziyara tayita tsokanarta tana Amarya Amarya.
       Tun ranar da aka tsaida ranar su farida da daddare taga sak'o ya shigo Mata cikin waya wajen k'arfe goma na dare, sak'on ba karamin tashin hankali ya Sanya ta ba ga mamaki Bata tab'a sanin Koda makamanciyar irin wannan lambar ba, tasan dai ita arayuwarta Bata da matsala da kowa Amma Kuma Abu Daya ne ya Kara daga Mata hankali acikin sak'on da yake Fad'in, " _Farida kike kowa Ina me gargad'arki tun wuri tun lokaci Bai kure Miki ba, ki gaggauta barin sadiq domin nawa ne ni kadai ko kadan Bai dace dake ba, wallahi-wallahi muddin Kika yi burus da umarnin da na baki to sai kin Tafka babban kuskure, saboda akan Sadiq Ina iya salwantar da rayuwarki ma gaba Daya saboda ke kaskantacciya ce makaskanciya, dake da takalmin kafata d'aya na d'auke ku har gwara takalmina ma nasan darajarsa saboda yanamun Rana ke kuwa babu wata tsiya da kike tsinanamun, idan kunne yaji jiki ya tsira._"

Sororo farida tayi tana bin wayarta da kallo, ta Kara karanta sak'on yafi sau biyar ganin Yana neman sata damuwa yasa ta watsar da komai tayi alwala tayi sallah sannan tayi kwanciyar ta, washe gari tana tashi daga bacci da wani sak'on ta Kara cikin karo dashi budewa ta yi ta shiga karantawa, " _Tabbas nasan Zaki dauki abun kamar wasan kwallo Amma ba Wasa bane ki d'auke shi da gaske sai zaifi zuwar Miki da sauk'i, Farida kamar yanda kwanakin aurenki suke raguwa haka kwanakin farin cikinki suke Kara karewa, da na yanke hukuncin insa akashe ki Amma sai Naga hakan bazai bani damar musguna Miki ba kamar yanda Kika Dade kina musgunawa rayuwata ba, Amma wallahi-wallahi idan har Kika dage akan sai kin auri sadiq to ki tabbatar da kin nemi ruguza rayuwarki ne tunda k'uruciyarki, ki nemi wani mijin duk tsadarsa zan siya Miki shi indai kud'i suna magani, amma Ina me k'ara tabbatar Miki  da sai na auri sadiq Koda tsiya Koda arziki, daga yanxu ki Fara lissafi yau kwanakin farin cikinki saura kwana hamsin da Tara._ wannan Karan ma farida ta jima azaune tana maimaita sak'on da aka turo Mata, da sauri ta Danna dialing call Amma ina wayar akashe take, aranar baccin da farida takeyi na safe ma Bata samu ta koma ba saboda damuwa, lokaci-lokaci take daukar wayarta tana Kara karanta sak'on tana mamakin me turo Mata da sak'on, wani tunani ne ya Fad'o Mata afili ta furta, "SADIQ, kodai sadiq nada wata budurwar ne bansani ba? Kai Anya sadiq bazai mun Haka ba " wata zuciyar ce ta Kara ce Mata, " to idan ba budurwarsa ba wacece? Kinsan dai babu me yi Miki wannan barazanar sai budurwarsa, to Amma Kuma sadiq ay ba ma'abocin kule-kule bane" Hafsa ce"  d'ayar zuciyar ce ta Bata wannan  amsar, afili ta furta, " tabbas itace, itace take mutuwar kaunar Sadiq na tabbata zata iyayin komai Dan ganin ta mallaki sadiq, Amma zan binciki wayar sadiq ko zanga number Nan aciki, Amma kuwa muddin na samu sadiq da cin amanata sai nayi Masa mummunan hukunci " kwanciya farida tayi gaba Daya wunin ranar tayishi babu wani sukuni da walwala har saida Inna ta lura da halin da take ciki Amma Inna Bata tanka Mata ba atunaninta ko fargabar aure ce farida ta farayi, bayan sadiq ya dawo gida wayarsa ta ara ta sa wannan number aciki Amma babu irinta aciki duk iya bincikenta da zatayi ta babu ita babu alamarta, kanta ne ya Kara kullewa damuwa ta Kara yi Mata yawa, kullin da irin sak'on da ake Kara turo Mata na razanarwa da firgitarwa ana Kuma Kara lissafa Mata ranakun aurenta, farida rasa Wanda zata sanarwa tayi tana tunanin karta d'aga hankalin su Inna, wayar saudat ta Kira ta sanar Mata duk halin da take ciki, da yamma saudat ta karaso gidansu farida tare suka had'u suna jajantawa junansu saudat ta Kara k'arfafawa farida gwiwarta, hakan ne yasa farida ta Dan ji sauk'i da damuwar da take ciki.

Saura sati uku bikin su sadiq, farida tana zaune suna Hira atsakar gida ita dasu umma wayarta ce ta Fara ringing tana d'aukauta taga number saudat da murmushi ta d'auka tace, " beb how fa" daga dayan b'angaren muryar Mommyn saudat ce ta katse ta da cewar, " farida ba kawar taki bace ita ce dai tacemun na kiraki, Allah ya jarabci kawarki farida muna cikin tashin hankali " cikin tashin hankali farida tace, " mommy me yasamu saudat din Dan Allah ki Gaya mum mommy me yake faruwa da ita? " Jin mommy na shashshek'ar kuka yasa hankalin farida ya Kara tashi jikinta har rawa yake, da sauri ta sa takalminta ta janyo hijabinta dake Kan igiya ko kayan jikinta Bata tsaya canjawa ba, cikin tashin hankali tayiwa su Inna sallama ko k'wak'wk'waran bayani batayi Musu ba Kai tsaye ta tari napep tayi gidansu saudat.
        Duk Wanda yaga farida zai tabbatar da irin tashin hankalin da take ciki, tana zuwa ko gaisuwar kirki basuyi me gadin gidan ba saboda yasan farida hakan ne yasa kanta tsaye ta wuce cikin gidan, lokacin da ta shiga falon gidan ba kowa  sallama take ta kwalawa shiru, ganin shirun yayi yawa yasa ta wuce part d'in saudat da sauri ta tura kofar d'akin bedroom d'inta, akwance cikin bargo ta samu saudat mommy na gefenta tana goge hawaye, saudat na ganin farida ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tana zubda hawaye, asanyaye farida ta zauna tana tambayar mommy, " mommy me yake faruwa ne, ina cikin damuwa me yake faruwa da saudat ne? " Kukan da mommy take rikewane ya kubce Mata ta fashe da kuka me tsuma zuciya, farida ita d'inma ji tayi hawaye na zubo Mata daga idanta, karfin hali tayi cikin kuka tace, " Dan Allah saudat ku fiddani daga duhu bansan me yake faruwa dake ba Naga sai kuka kukeyi?" Mommy ce ta goge hawayen idanta tace, " farida wasu azzalumai ne suka haikewa kawarki jiya da daddare taje unguwa da yake bada driver ta fita ba, da mota suka biyota tace fuskarsu duk arufe take su biyar duk Saida sukayi amfani da ita, sai cikin dare suka Yar da ita agefen hanya da kyar ta iya d'aukan waya ta Kira ni mukaje muka daukota,  farida bansan me rayuwar Nan ta koma ba, yanxu kina ganin sun kyauta Mana kenan? " Tun kafin mommy ta gama magana farida ta rushe da wani matsanancin kuka me ratsa zuciya, sai da tayi me isarta sannan tace, " Sannu kinji saudat Allah ya saka Miki, mommy Kuma baza ta gane inda suka kaita ba? " Ajiyar zuciya mommy ta  sauke tace, " Nima na tambayeta Amma tace mun suna cirota daga mota suka hura Mata wani abu Bata Kara farkawa sai abakin titi ta ganta acikin motarta, Amma Daddynta tuni ya mik'awa police report? " Goge kwallar idanta tayi sannan tace, " insha Allah za'a  kamosu, kiyi shiru kawata ki daina kuka" mommy tashi tayi ta basu guri sannan saudat tace, " farida kowa da irin tashi k'addarar, Amma mutanen sun zalinceni"  matsawa kusa da ita farida tayi tanata lallashinta daga baya ta Mata sallama ta koma gida.
  
      Farida na zuwa gida ta zayyanawa su Inna duk halin da ake ciki, sunyi bak'in ciki matuk'a sunyi jimami washe gari kuwa suka shirya farida ta Musu jagora sukaje sukayiwa su mommy jaje, Haka sadiq ma ba karamin jimami ya taya saudat ba awaya ya kirata Yayi Mata Sannu sannan ya jajinta Mata.
     Tun daga ranar kullin farida sai taje gidansu saudat ta dubo ta, hardai saudat taji sauk'i ta warware.
     Saura kwana goma bikin su  farida, umma har ta farayiwa farida gyaran jiki tuni ta Kara canjawa fatar jikinta ta murje har wani yellow-yellow take yi, shigowar saudat suka gani da gudu ta fad'a Kan farida tana kuka Saida tayi me isarta sannan farida ta rarrasheta, ta rik'e hannunta suka shiga d'aki agefen katifa suka zauna farida ta Fara tambayar saudat, " me yake faruwa kawata Naga kin shigo cikin damuwa ?" Da hawaye saudat ta Fara yi Mata magana, " mujaheed ne farida, mujaheed ya gujeni ya fasa aurena, farida mujaheed ya ce nida shi har abada saboda daddy na ya sanar da mahaifin sa abunda ya faru dani, shikenan na rasa me aurena haka zan yi rayuwata da bak'in tabo kina gani aurena saura wata hudu da 'yan kwanaki shikenan farida " saudat ta fad'a jikin farida tana kuka, cikin tausayawa farida take bubbuga bayanta sannan tace, " Babu Wanda zai ganki da bakin tabo kawata tunda bake Kika Kai kanki ba Allah ne ya k'addara Miki hakan zai faru dake......" Katseta saudat tayi da, " haba farida karki yaudareni Mana ki gayamun maganar da kema kinsan ba Haka bane kina gani ko wani na aura  zai gani da k'ima da mutumci ne?" Murmushi farida tayi tace zai ganki Mana saudat, Zai ganki da k'ima saboda shi Yana da banbanci da sauran maza na tabbata bazai wulakanta ki ba saboda shi din bame wulakanci bane " sororo saudat ta kalli farida tace, " wai me kike fad'a ne farida dawa kike? Keda nace Miki mujaheed ya fasa aurena Kuma kike mun wani sokiburutsu" murmushi farida ta karayi tace, " ba mujaheed nake nufi ba saudat Ina nufin SADIQ Zaki aura" azabure saudat ta mik'e tsaye cikin mamaki tace, " kina cikin hankalinki kuwa farida me kike cewa ne?" Abinda kikaji akunnenki shi nake fad'a" farida ta Bata amsa tana Jan hannunta ta zaunar da ita.
    Da k'arfi ta fizge hannunta daga na farida tace, " gaskiya kin samu damuwa farida Bari kiga ni na wuce" tashi saudat tayi da niyyyar fita aykuwa farida ta kamota cikin tsukewar fuska farida ta Fara magana, " saudat idan Kika mun haka kin nunamun ni ba komai bace agurinki Ashe nida ke bazamu had'u mu rufawa kanmu asiri ba, ko kina ganin sadiq D'an talakawa ne Bai Kai matsayin da Zaki aureshi ba? " Sunkuyar da kai k'asa saudat tayi sannan tace, " ko kad'an ni ban Raina arxikin sadiq ba, sai dai bazan iya auren mijinki ba  farida, me kike tunanin mutane zasu ce?" Farida zata bawa saudat amsa sak'o ya shigo wayarta tana dubawa wannan number da ake turo Mata dai sak'o da itace, dafe Kai farida tayi bayan ta gama karanta sak'on, saudat ce ta tambaye ta, " me yake faruwa ne Farida? " Cikin damuwa tace, " wallahi number Nan ce dai da ake turo mun da sak'o Kinga wannan sak'on fa abinda akace mun, ni wallahi na Fara tsorata da wannan lamarin, farida ta karasa maganar tana mik'awa saudat wayar, ahankali saudat ta Fara bin sak'on tana karantawa.
     " _hahahahahahaha Amarsu ta Ango kenan shirye-shirye sai karatowa suke, Ina yi Miki jimami fa Kar wannan gyaran jiki da akewa Ango ya zamana wasu garadan zakiyi wa, shiyasa tun tuni nake Baki shawara kink'i d'auka, hmmmm da Sannu Zaki fahimci abinda nake nuna Miki alokacin da zakiyi Amarcinki a inda Baki yi tsammani ba, Asha gyaran jiki lafiya._" ajiyar zuciyarta saudat ta sauke tace, " to wai kawata wane mataki Kika d'auka akan wannan y'ar iskar me turo Miki da wannan sak'on?" Asanyaye farida tace, " wane mataki zan d'auka kawata, ke kilama irin kurarin Nan ne takeyi, ninafi zargin wata cousin sis dinsa hafsa Dan itace take sanshi kamar ta mutu, ke mubar wannan maganar mu koma waccen, kinji ni abinda na yanke Kuma idan sadiq yazo zan sameshi inyi Masa bayani, Kuma su Umma ma haka kema inasan ki sanarwa dasu mommy" kallan tsaf saudat tayi wa farida tace, " to uwata naji, ni tashi kimun rakiya zan tafi" farida kofar gida ta raka saudat driver na cikin mota Yana jiranta tana shiga yaja mota suka wuce.


   Farida na komawa cikin gida ta sanarwa da su Umma bukatarta, da farko umma kin amincewa tayi Amma data ga inna ta dage da maganar yasa itama ta yarda da maganar, Ina ce ta cigaba da cewa, " hajiya kinga Yarinyar Nan ta taimaka Mana idan muka juya Mata baya Kinga kamar mun nuna Mata TSANTSAR BUTULCI ne, ita da iyayenta mutanen kwarai ne bamuda abinda zamu biya su" umma tace, " eh haka ne maganarki shikenan Allah zab'a abinda yafi alheri, Amma sai sadiq din yazo munji ta bakinsa" hakan kuwa sukayi da sadiq yazo umma ce da kanta ta sanar dashi abinda farida taxo Musu dashi, tana gama gaya Masa ya mik'e zai bar gurun, dawo dashi umma tayi ta zaunar tace, " Kai bana San shashancin banza, bakaji abinda nace maka bane Naga ka Mik'a Kai gaba zaka tafi? " Gyara zama yayi yace, " umma ni maganar ce Sam ban d'auketa da muhimmanci ba wannan ay shirmene ita farida tana cikin hankalinta kuwa? " Inna ce ta shigo cikin d'akin sannan ta farayiwa Sadiq magana, " Sadiq Yarinyar Nan sunyi Mana karamci Dan Allah ka taimaka ka aureta tunda yaron da zata aura ya gujeta, na tabbata bazaka wulakanta ta ba tunda Kai kasan duk abunda yake faruwa, Bai kamata ace mun gagara rufa Musu Asiri ba " Inna ta karasa maganar tana tsugunna nawa har k'asa,da sauri sadiq ya d'ago Inna sannan yace, " haba mama Dan Allah karki k'ara banaso na Amince zan aureta shikenan" gaba Daya suka amsa da," Allah yayi maka Albarka sadiq ya baka abinda kake nema na alherin" Ameen ya rabbi" sadiq ya basu amsa, Yana gama magana yasa Kai ya fice daga d'akin.
   Tun Daga ranar sadiq ya dauki fushi da farida, ita kanta tasan dalilin fushinsa shiyasa ta shareshi Dan idan ta cika bibiyarsa tana bashi hakuri sai yafi d'aukan abun da zafi, farida shiryawa tayi taje ta samu mommy tayi Musu bayanin duk hukuncin da suka yanke, ba karamin dad'i taji ba daddy na dawowa ta sanar Masa halin da ake ciki.
      
     _Rana Bata Karya....._

Duk wasu abubuwa da mahaifi zaiyiwa y'ar sa daddy yayiwa farida da saudat, hatta da kayan kitchen cewa sukayi Kar asiyawa farida Koda ne cokali su zasuyi Musu komai, dama batun lefe ma daddy tuni ya bada kud'i aka had'o wa saudat kaya na gani na fad'a, duk wad'ansu party da ake yi mahaifin saudat ne ya dauki nauyin yinsa Amma babu Wanda sadiq ya hallata acewarsa bashi da wannan ra'ayin, Haka akaci biki aka more kowanne gidaje na cikin shagali da farin ciki, daga cikin dangin mahifin farida Yaya muttak'a ne kawai yaje har ya bawa Inna y'ar gudunmawa ta biki, sosai Inna taji Dadi koba komai ace ga waliyyin Amarya wan babanta, daga b'angaren Sadiq kuwa hafsa saboda bak'in ciki ji tayi kamar tayi hauka, tana gani y'an uwan ta duk suka taho biki ko rakiya get batayi Musu ba, gidan su Umma ya cika ya tumbatsa da al'umma anatashan shagali, Inna ce cikin zuciyarta tace, " oh wai yau ni zan aurar da y'ata ta cikina, Allah sarki su Ammi da kunanan farida da k'ink'ara ganin gata".
       Ranar Lahadi Aka Daura Auran Sadiq dasu farida, na farida aka Fara daurawa da sadiq sannan aka daura na saudat da sadiq, wani katafaren gida Daddy ya bawa su sadiq a unguwar tudun yola, gidan me part biyu ne yace su zauna aciki, farida aka Fara kaiwa cikin gidan sannan aka je aka dauko saudat ita ma aka kaita sashenta, bayan wani lokaci  Abokan sadiq ne sukayi Masa rakiya daga bakin kofa ya dakatar dasu ya karb'i ledojin hannunsu, ya wuce ciki, acewarsa shi bayasan wannan rakiyar da Abokan Ango sukeyi aje ayita surutai marasa fasali, shiyasa ma bayan ankawo amare ya turo wani abokin sa sulaiman yace duk ya sallami kawayen da suka zauna.
      Farida na zaune ita kadai tana karewa d'akin kallo taji shigowar sak'o wayarta, da sauri ta dauka ta bud'e gabanta ne ya yanke ya fad'i lokacin data Fara karanta sak'on, " _Amarya Amarya Amarya kin tuna maganarmu dake, to adaren Nan wasan zai fara ina ganin fa kamar zai bada amarshi"_ farida Bata tab'a jin tsoron wannan sak'on da ake turo Mata ba sai wannan lokacin, tana Nan zaune taji shigowar sadiq, sauri tayi zata rufe kanta da mayafi ya katse ta da, " Haba 'yan Mata nifa bazaki mun wannan alayen naku na amare ba, kunaso Kuna kaiwa kasuwa" murmushi farida tayi ta D'an tura Baki gaba tace, " ba wani Nan wallahi kawai dai ka fad'a ne" zama yayi kusa da ita ya sak'ala hannunsa a kad'arta  yace, " Give me five" ya karasa magana Yana mik'e Mata hannu✋🏻 akunyace farida ta mik'e hannunta suka tafs, zai Fara magana tace, " wai kuwa kaje gurin saudat?" Guntun tsaki yaja yace, " banjeba sai me? " A'a ba komai Amma dai kasan itama matarka ce kuma ubangiji zai tuhumeka akan hak'k'inta" D'an jim yayi sannan yace muje kimun rakiya" hararar Wasa tayi Masa tace, " cab inraka ka Ina? kaidai kaje kawai inrufe kofata Saida safe" tsuke fuska sadiq yayi yace, " wallahi zakisa na fasa zuwa ma" a'a kayi hakuri jeka dawo da Wasa nake maka" ledar saudat sadiq ya d'auka ya tafi sashenta tana zaune agefen gado ya shiga da sallama, amsawa tayi k'asa-k'asa tambayar ta yayi ya gajiyar biki ta bashi amsa, ledar hannunsa ya mik'a Mata sannan ya tambayeta ko akwai abinda take bukata, tace masa "  bakomai"
    Sashen farida ya koma Yana kiranta, shiru yaji atunaninsa ko ta shiga toilet ya zauna gefen gado Yana jiranta, shiru yaji ba amsa ba Motsin ruwa tura kofar toilet yayi wayam Bata ciki, Yana fitowa yaji karar get din gidansa Anja da karfi, da sauri ya fito ya nufi kofar abakin get din ya ga wayarta akasa da sark'ar wuyanta, hankali tashe ya tura kofar ya fita wata bak'ar mota ya hango ta tashi da gudu ta fita daga layin, saudat na daga jikin window tana kallan duk Abinda ke faruwa ta fashe da dariyar farin ciki har tana fad'awa Kan gado, watar Yar karamar waya ta dauko ta buga wata number ana d'auka tace, " Aikinku Yana kyau me duniya" Bata jima ba ta kashe wayar ta fad'a Kan gado ta cigaba da k'yak'ya dariya, cikin nishad'i ta furta, " Yarinya dama na gaya Miki adaren Nan za'a Fara wasan".

   _Team farida Kuna ina🤣 sai ku tafi Nemo Amaryar sadiq._


_Alhamdulillah am +1 kuyani sawa kaina Albarka, kwanaki na karewa Allah ya sa mu cika da imani Aameen._

😭 *TSANTSAR BUTULCI*😭

                _AmeeraAdam60_
