*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   43&44



Da gudun gaske sadiq ya rufawa motar baya Yana gudun Yana yekuwar neman taimakon mutane Amma shiru da yake unguwar bame yawan jama'a bace, wata kwana da zaibi yayi dai-dai da shigowar wata mota bisa tsautsayi tayi ciki da Sadiq, mutumin da ke cikin motar shida wani agefen sa cikin tashin hankali sukayi parking din motarsu da hanzari suka fito, cikin firgici suka je gurun da sadiq yake yashe cikin jini, da farko babbar rigar jikinsa suka Fara kokarin cire Masa jini yake fita ta gefen kansa sai kafarsa da itama take zubda jini, cikin karfin hali yake kokarin tashi, mutumin yace Masa, " Sannu samari kaji tsaya mu taimaka maka Naga kana kokarin tashi....." Da karaji Sadiq ya buga Musu tsawa yace, " ku kyaleni sun sace mun mata, kubarni naje na daukota karsuyi Mata lahani farida tana bukatar taimako na" sororo sukayi suna kallansa da mamaki dayan yace, " suwaye suka sace maka Mata?" Ko kallan me tambayar sadiq baiyi ba ya yunkura ya tashi ya nufi hanya da kyar Yana dingisawa jinin jikinsa Bai fasa zuba ba, ido suka xuba Masa har yayi Musu nisa motarsu suka shiga suka tafi.
        Sadiq fita yayi titi Yana kalle-kalle kamar wani sabon kamu, duk Wanda yaga yaxo wucewa da sauri zai bishi ya dinga ce Masa, " Dan Allah mlm kaga mutanen da suka sacemun farida, Dan Allah kazo ka tayani nemanta, acikin wata bak'ar mota suka tafi" yawan cin mutane sai dai su kalleshi su jinjina Kai suyi gaba, wasu Kuma saidai suce Masa, " ikon Allah duniya ba tsoron Allah dubi mutum kamar me hankali Amma Kan da sauk'i, Allah ya baka lafiya kaji" ganin mutane nayi Masa kallan Mara hankali yasa wani bak'in ciki ya Kara turnukeshi, Tunowa ya yi da farida cikin zuciyarsa yake fargabar halin da take ciki lokaci D'aya hankanlinsa ya K'ara tashi ransa ya K'ara b'aci, tafiya kawai yakeyi batare da yasan inda yake jefa kafufuwansa ba Haka zalika Bai fasa tambayar mutane ba, tafiya yake har kafa ta Fara daukewa idan ba kallan tsaf kayiwa Sadiq ba zakayi tunanin me hauka ne sabon kamu.
        Lokacin da sadiq ya tafi sashen saudat, Yana fita ba jimawa taji shigowar mutum lokacin ta fito daga toilet, da mamaki ta Fara magana azatonta sadiq ne, " Kai me nake gani ya sadiq badai har ka dawo ba...." Ganin mutanen dake tsaye abakin kofa ne yasa sauran maganar ta makalewa amakogaro, cikin tsoro ta Fara tambayar su, " Dan Allah kudin su waye?" Daya daga cikinsu ne yace, " ba abin tashin hankali bane tambaya ce zamuyi Miki kina bamu amsa shikenan Amma muddin Kika nemi b'uya ko guduwa ko ihu to zamuje mu kashe mijinki da Kuma Aminiyarki" jikin farida na rawa ta rab'o daga jikin toilet tace, " Dan Allah karkuyi Musu komai na Amince zan baku amsa kowacce iri ce " dayan na matsowa kusa da ita ya shammaceta ya fesa Mata Hoda afuska tuni ya bawa sauran umarni suka karaso suka d'auki farida da take Shirin sulalewa kasa, kamar k'iftawar ido Haka suka fice daga b'angaren farida, lokacin da sukaje fita daga get din gidan ne sukaji ga kamar sadiq ya gansu shiyasa suka tafi da gudu Wanda ya d'auki farida wayarta na hannunsa cikin rashin sani ya yarda ita kusan lokaci daya itama sark'ar jikinta ta Fad'o, da gudun gaske suka figi motar suka fice daga unguwar alokacin tuni Farida ta dad'e da fita daga hayyacinta.
       Bayan saudat ta gama k'yak'yata dariyar ta tasowa tayi ta Kara leken Sadiq, lekawarta yayi dai-dai da ficewar da sadiq yayi da gudu yabi motar, cikin fargaba saudat ta d'auki wayarta ta Kira ogansu me suna me duniya Yana d'auka ta Fara magana, " me duniya Naga mijina ya fito ya biyo ku, banasan kubar wata shaida da za'a iya gano inda kuke, kasan halina Sarai zan iya nemarwa kaina mafita kukuma ku kwana aciki, kunsan ko waye Mahaifina akaina zai iya salwantar da duk dukiyarsa, banasan ku tsaya cikin garin Nan inasan kuyi nesa da gari kutafi cikin wani dajin, zanyi Muku transfer kud'i ku yi duk y'an buk'atun ku dasu, sannan banasan ko kadan ku Bari Yarinyar Nan ta gane fuskar d'aya daga cikinku, nasan wacece farida tana da kaifin kwakwalwa da saurin fahimtar Abu, bance kuyi Mata komai ba idan har Bata nemi tserewa ba Amma idan ta nemi ta tona Muku asiri to kuyi Mata dukan mutuwa ku kaita cikin teku ku Yar, Amman ku tabbatar da kome zakuyi sai kun sanar Dani, ko kuma karku Yar da ita ku bani zuwa Nan da anjima zan Kara yin shawarar yanda zakuyi da ita, Ina fatan kun fahimta? " Saida me duniya yagama sauraren saudat sannan yace, " Angama Ranki ya dad'e sai abinda Kika ce " kashe wayar saudat tayi, sai kuma sadiq ya Fad'o Mata tunani ta shiga yi afili ta furta, " to Ina Kuma ya tafi na d'auka zai dawo cikin gida domin insamu damar gabatar Masa da wata kissar Amma Bari na Kira mommy " number mommy ta Kira Saida ta kusa tsinkewa sannan mommy ta d'auka, " saudat ya akayi Kika kirani bayan d'azun Nan aka kaiki d'akinki Ina mijinnaki? " Tas duk Abinda ya faru saudat tabawa mommy labari, salati mommy tayi sannan tarufe saudat da fad'a, " u are very stupid, me Kika aikata haka saudat?  Nace miki komai cikin ruwan sanyi zamu aywatar dashi, what is wrong with u? Akanki aka Fara soyayya ne, na Gaya miki da ki barmun komai a hannuna, kin cika gaggawa saudat nifa na Miki alkawarin da kafarta zatabar gidan Nan zanxu bakya tunanin za'a iya zarginki akan b'atanta? Wai ma meyasa bakiyi shawara Dani ba ? " D'an tura Baki gaba saudat tayi cikin shagwabe tace, " mommy kinsan dai nayi hakuri for how long da zan zuba ido Ina ganin yanda suke nunawa junansu soyayya, mommy d'akin ta fa ya Fara shiga Daya shigo Nan ko minti biyar baiyi ba ya fice, yanxu mommy sai inkyale shi yaje ya kwana ad'akin wannan sakaryar Yarinyar, tsawon shekara nawa Ina kallan wannan kayan haushin nasu, gaskiya mommy ki k'yale kawai ninasan yanda zan b'ullo Musu babu Wanda zauzargeni " dogon tsaki mommy taja ta bugawa saudat tsawa sannan tace, " ta yaro kyau take Bata karko, idan su basu zargeki ba kina tunanin idan maganar taje waje hukuma bazasu zargeki ba, karki manta mijinki fa ma'aikacin tsaro ne na tabbata zaiyi duk iya kokarin sa ganin ya sanar da Abokan Aikinsa Dan ganin sun gano inda matarsa take, ki barni dasu ki zuba ido sannan inasan ki yi gaggawar kiransu kice su dawo da ita cikin dabara yanda babu Wanda zai gansu bare ya bada sheda akansu, Banda ma abinki bakya ganin yanda nake mulka mahaifinki har akwai abinda xai dameki Sadiq dinme? Duk atafin hannuna suke ki barni dasu gaba daya har wannan kinibabbiyar uwar tasa, sai kin juyasu kamar yanda ake juya waina a tanda, kinji me nace kiyi maza kice su dawo da ita ko akofar gida ne su wurgar da ita su tafi ki bani lokaci da kanki Zaki kirani kice mun aiki ya fara ci, danma dai boka yace mun aiki bazai Fara cinsu ba sai kin aureshi da tuni wani zancen ake ba wannan ba, ke sai anjima naji daddynki zai shigo kinsan shi da tambayar k'wak'waf yanxu yace hirar me mukeyi" Mommy na gama magana ta katse wayar, wani uban tsalle saudat ta buga akan gado sannan tace, haba mommy idan nasa aka dawo da ita adaren Nan ay zata ci bulus ma kenan,  wani murmushi ta saki tace, " farida yanxu Zaki ga aynihin kala ta" ta K'ara fashewa da dariya sannan ta fara tana tunanin ta inda zata Fara bullowa lamarin yanda babu Wanda zaiyi tunani ko zarginta.

     Sadiq tafiya yake kamar Wanda ya tabu Yana tafe yana ambatar sunan farida, wucewa yayi ta wajen wani mutum dake Duba motarsa da alama matsala ta samu sadiq ne ya Fara tambayar sa kamar yanda yake yiwa sauran mutane, har ya gama maganar mutumin sakin Baki yayi yana kallan Sadiq cikin mamaki mutumin yace, " Sadiq wai me yake damunka na ganka jina-jina su waye sukayi maka haka, Ina zaka da tsohon Daren Nan, wai ma ba yau ka tare ba Ina matar taka su waye suka tafi da ita, ni din ne baka gane ba baban su zakari ne fa ? " Lokaci Daya mutumin yake jerowa sadiq wad'annan tambayoyin, kamar wani sakarai sadiq ya juya cen Yana nuna Masa sannan yace, " sun d'auketa Ina shiga b'angaren saudat na dawo Naga sun tafi da ita" D'an lek'a inda sadiq ya nuna wa baban su zakari yayi ko zai hangi abunda sadiq yake nuna Masa lokaci daya Kuma tsoro ya kamashi, da hanzari yacewa sadiq, " to zo mu tafi dama mota ta ce ta dau zafi na tsaya na zuba ruwa, muje Kar su dawo su had'a damu" ya karasa fad'a Yana janyo sadiq, cikin jin haushi sadiq ya fisge yace kayi tafiyar ka ba inda zani sai sun dawo mun da ita" babansu zakari ganin da yayiwa Sadiq kamar ba ahayyacin sa yake ba yace Masa, " to muje ni zan rakaka sai mu karb'o ta Amma zan Kai sak'o gida sai mu wuce" sadiq kamar karamin yaro haka ya wuce ya shiga cikin mota babansu zakari Yana mamakin abunda ya faru da sadiq awannan Daren.
     Kai tsaye gida ya wuce da sadiq 'yan biki tuni kowa yayi bacci, babansu zakari ya dad'e Yana bugawa sannan akayi magana jin yace da sadiq suke yasa suka bud'e kofa, haske su Umma tayi da fitila gabanta ne yayi mummunar fad'uwa ganin fararen kayan sadiq kace-kace da jini, da gudu ta janyo sadiq jikinta tana tambayar sa me yake faruwa, jin maganganun mutane yasa duk y'an biki suka tashi, cikin gidan sadiq ya wuce a tsakar gida ya zauna ya rafka uban tagumi,  gaba Daya taruwa akansa sukayi Inna ce ta karaso ta dafa shi cikin tashin hankali tace, " sadiq waye yayi maka Haka me yake faruwa ne ? Ya akayi ka fito cikin Daren Nan?" Sadiq na kallan Inna wani tausayinta ne ya Kara Kama shi, umma ce tace da sauran matan su tafi d'aki zatayi magana da sadiq, suna shiga d'aki umma ta ruko hannun sa tace, " gayamun Sadiq me yake faruwa? " Sai alokacin ne sadiq ya samu ya fashe da wani irin kuka me ratsa zuciya,   idanu suka zuba Masa Inna da umma babu Wanda ya Kara ce Masa uffan sai dai gaba daya jikinsu ya gama sanyi, cikin kuka sadiq ya Fara magana, " sun sace mun ita umma sun dauketa shikenan bansan ya zanyi ba, daga fita ta d'aga d'akin ko minti biyar banyi ba Ina dawowa....." Kuka ne yaci karfin sadiq ya kasa karasa maganar da zai gayawa su Inna.
      Jikin Inna har rawa yake tace, " Bamu gane ba safiq wai wa aka sace?"  Kwallar idansa ya share sannan yace, " mama wasu ne suka sacemun farida" yuuuuuuuuuu Inna taji maganar ta d'aki dodon kunnenta, tuni ta Fara jin wani irin jiri da sauri ta dafa bango tana Fad'in innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un, umma ce tace, " aka sace farida Kuma su waye?" Sadiq bai Bata amsa ba ya cigaba da kuka kamar mace, Inna ce ta fashe da kuka me cin zuciya cikin kuka tace, " Allah na gode maka da irin jarrabar da kake mun, yanzu shikenan banida kowa aduniya farida ke kadai nake gani inji Dad'i kema yau babu ke, Allah duk Wanda ya d'auke mun Yarinyar Nan Allah ka tona asirinsu, Allah ka shiga lamarin ta Allah ka Duba marainiyarka" ta karasa magana tana Kara fashewa da kuka, ummace ta fara lallashin Inna sannan ta koma Kan Sadiq, yanda suka ga Rana Haka suka ga dare, zuwa safiya labarin b'atan farida tuni ya cika unguwa, duk wasu guraren cigiya Sadiq ya Kai gidan radio da television har ta yanar gixo anwatsa hotunan farida, umma ce tacewa sadiq, " to Ina saudat tasan halin da ake ciki ne? " Girgiza kansa yayi yace, " Bata sani ba saboda daga d'akin ta na fito D'an na Mata sallamar sai da safe" aykuwa ka Kira is sanar mata Dan kuwa na tabbata Bata sani ba da tuni ta karaso gidan Nan " umma ta fad'a.
       Sadiq ne ya kafe umma da kallo sannan yace, " umma Ina mamakin ya za'ayi adauke farida ba'a dauki saudat ba, nasan dai kowa yasan farida ba y'ar kowa bace a garin Nan sab'anin saudat? " Hmmmm ka rabu da mugu wallahi ba banza ba akwai wata akasa ba anganka akatan gida ba za'a yi zatan Kai wani shararran me kud'i ne shiyasa.........." Umma Bata karasa magana ba sukaji shigowar saudat da gudu cikin kuka ta fad'a jikin umma tana kuka me ratsa zuciyar duk Wanda yake gurin, cikin kuka take Fad'in, " umma an sacemun farida umma su waye suka Mana Haka me farida ta tsare musu, me yasa za'ayi Mana haka?  umma kalli Ina zaune bayan nayi sallar asuba na dau wayata ta wani group Naga hoton farida anturo wai ana nemanta ansace ta adaren amarcinta " hawaye saudat ta goge ta cigaba da cewa, " da farko karyatawa nayi Ina zuwa sashenta Naga ba kowa Dana fito get Kinga wayarta acen na d'aukota, umma Allah ya sakawa farida " duk mutanen gurin sai da suka tausayawa halin da saudat ta shiga, innace ta janyo saudat jikinta tana goge hawayen idanta tace, " Addu'a kawai zamuyi saudat kuka bashida magani Amma kawarki sai wani ikon Allah" wani sabon kukan saudat ta fashe dashi da kyar aka lallasheta daga baya tace zataje gida gurin Mommy su dawo tare.

         Me duniya da yaransa tafiya sukayi me nisan gaske sai da suka shiga cikin daji sannan wani shiga cikin wani surkuki  lungu daga nesa baza tab'a tunanin D'an Adam zaiyi rayuwa agurin ba, wani gida ne madaidaici suka bud'e get suka shiga, kamar gawa Haka suka d'auko Farida suka kaita Kan gado suka kwantar, daya daga cikin su ne ya D'an lek'a d'akin Yana waigen Yan uwansa, me duniya ne ya buga Mai tsawa yace, " Kai dallah me yasa ka fiya harkar jagwal ne? " D'an Sosa keya yayi yace, " me gida wancen kayan ne" sai kuma yayi shiru, harara me duniya ya watsa Masa yace, " banasan akuyanci bakaji me kayan abinda tace ba tace ajiyeta Kar ayi Mata komai sai da umarninta, Kai kasan yawan kud'in da zata Bamu kuwa? Dallah ware daga gurin" ba kunya ya juya wuce sumi-sumi.
        Suna Nan zaune afalo suna ya zuke-zuke su saudat ta Kara kiransu awaya ban ogan nasu ya d'auka tace, " me duniya nasake shawara, idan Yarinyar Nan ta farko ku Bata abinci me rai da lafiya Kuma Bata guri tayi wanka inasan Nan da gobe wani daga cikin yaranka yaje ya siyo  English wears na rashin mutumci Wanda kukan karuwai ke sawa, inasan ku nuna Mata bunduga ku kuma yi Mata barazanar kashe Inna da umma da Kuma nida da Sadiq, kuce zaku kyaleta tatafi amma sai ta sa waddan kayan, duk yanda zakuyi ku tursasata intaki ku bugawa shegiya kwaya Tasha ta bugu,ku tabbatar da kin dauketa hotuna harda video inasan wani daga cikinku ya dinga romancing dinta a video karku Bari fuskar shi ta fito, idan kunyi Haka ku turo min, abu na gaba inasan ku nutsu zan turo Muku da number sadiq ku kirashi azuwan ku y'an kidnappers ne, kuce Kuna bukatar kudade masu yawa, kubarmun sauran aikin ni zan karasa kudai ku tabbatar Bata ga fuskarku ba, Kuma idan za'a kawo Muku kudin ku tabbatar da kunyi duk yanda zakuyi ba'a kamaku ba, kuce million Ashirin kuke so nasan zasu nemi ragi sai tsaya a 15million, shine kud'in Aikinku but u have to be careful " saudat na gama magana me duniya ya Bata amsa sannan ta kashe wayar ta kwanta cikin farin ciki, dogon tsaki taja ta janyo fulo ta rungume tace, " y'ar wahala makirci yanxu Kika fara gani "

Saudat na zuwa gida ta sanarwa daddy b'atan farida ya jimanta matuk'a, atake ya Kira police ya sanar musu halin da ake ciki saboda duk Bai San plan din saudat bane, d'aki mommy da saudat suka shiga sai da suka gama kitse-kitsensu sannan suka fito sukayiwa Daddy sallama acewar mommy zasuje yiwa su Inna jaje, gabanin zasu karasa gidan farida ta Kira me duniya ta ce Masa ya Kira wayar sadiq Daga lokacin zuwa minti talatin, hakan kuwa akayi su mommy na zaune bayan tayi jaje wata makociyar su Umma tace, " Kuma wai ko ba Yan garkuwa ne suka d'auke Yarinyar Nan ba? " Mommy tayi karaf tace," wallahi Nima azuciyata Sai da na ayyana Haka Amma ay har yanxu shiru basuyi waya ba ko?" Ta karasa maganar tana kallan su Inna cikin sigar tambaya, suna cikin haka suka ga sadiq ya fito tsakar gida Yana waya, " eh eh, ni ne mijinta ne, Ina faridan? Dan Allah karku cutar da ita? " Daga daya b'angaren akace, " bazamu yi Mata komai ba illa kud'i da muke bukata zaka bada 20m sannan ka fansheta" sadiq har rawar murya yake yace, " eh naji zan bayar Ina take bani naji muryarta" mik'awa farida wayar akayi cikin kuka tace, " ya sadiq Dan Allah kazo ka tafi dani " da sauri suka fizge wayar sannan sukace, " inafatan hakan yayi maka " runtse ido sadiq yayi yanajin wani d'aci azuciyarsa cikin b'acin Rai yace, " hakan yayi" kit yaji sun kashe wayar, dafe kansa yayi umma ce ta tambaye shi, asanyaye yayi Musu bayani, Inna ba abin da take sai hawaye, umma ce tace, " sadiq million Ashirin fa ba dubu Ashirin bace, meyasa baka nemi ragi ba ay ciniki akeyi dasu Kuma su yanxu abun lallabawa ne ko me zasu ce maka karka nuna zafin rai, yanxu ka kirasu cikin masalaha ku dai-daita.
      Ba Musu Sadiq ya Kira su sukayita ciniki daga karshe dai suka tsaya a 15millions, bukatar karajin muryar farida yayi Sam suka hanashi,  mommy ce tace zata je gida ta sanarwa da daddy duk halin da ake ciki, abinda Daddy yace zasuji, saudat Kuma tace sadiq ya fito suje suyi report agun police yanda za'a kamasu cikin sauk'i, sadiq ba karamin dadi yake ji ba yanda yaga saudat duk ta damu, maganar da tayi yanxu ce tasa shi karajin Dadi, tare suka fita Saida suka Fara zuwa station sukayi report sannan mommy da saudat suka wuce gida, suna shiga cikin falon gida suka fashe da dariya mommy tace, " lallai saudat bakiyar akasa ba, wannan wasan kwaikwaiyon naki ya tafi yanda akeso bari na hau sama in sauko mu tafi gurin boka Dan tsitu na cikin kogon dutse.

_Wollah comments dinku ya sani nishad'i kwarai inayinku irin sosai din Nan, lobiyu  6isters_❤️❤️❤️❤️❤️

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

       

        *BAN HAKURI GAREKU MASOYA*

_Assalamu alaikum barkanku da wannan lokaci, Dan Allah kumun Afuwa kwana biyun Nan bazaku samu cigabansa ba, saboda bana zama sosai ko zaku samu cigabansa sai zuwa nan da ranar talata, nagode kwarai da nuna kulawarku agareni Allah ya bar so da kauna Ina tare daku akoda yaushe._👏🏼👏🏼👏🏼
[9/23, 7:58 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  45&46



    _Not Edited_

      Hannu saudat ta mik'awa mommynta suka tafa sannan saudat tace, "  mommy ayda ke zaki b'ata Mana shiri da kikace adawo da ita yanxu kina ganin wazai zargemu, Kuma abinda Baki sani ba na shirya Mata wani mugun kulli Wanda Bata tab'a fidda kanta ba, mommy Ina fatan mutumin Nan Yana aiki kamar yankan wuk'a?" Mommy ce ta watsawa saudat harara tace, " idan Kika bani haushi zan fasa zuwa tunda har kina tantama akan aikin boka, wato ni banzama shaida ba akan abinda nakewa mahaifinki ko? To ki zuba ido ki gani takalminki sai yafi wannan kodaddiyar Yarinyar daraja agurin mijinku " adan tasawace saudat tace, " stop mommy banaso karki Kara cewa mijinmu mijina dai, mommy kinsan yanda nake mutuwar San sadiq kuwa, wallahi mommy ko kisa ne zan iyayi indai zan mallakeshi ni kad'ai" murmushi mommy tayi tace, " kibi asannu saudat ba'a irin wannan aikin da gaggawa, boka Dan tsitu ya wuce duk yanda kike tunaninsa  Bari na hau sama na sauko" mommy ta karasa magana tana hayewa sama, saudat guri tasamu ta zauna ta ciro wayarta tana bud'e data dinta, wad'ansu irin matsiyatan hotunan farida ta gani wasu videos ta gani guda uku, da sauri ta kunna su ta Fara gani, farida ce sanye da mini skirt sai Yar karamar half vest da alamar Bata cikin hayyacinta tana zaune kan kujera ta jingina kanta jikin kujera, wasu kuma ita da wasu samari ne ma maza wadanda aka boye fuskarsu Amma suna rungume da farida, wasu hotunan kuma hannunta rike da kwalaben giya sai wani video da aka kunna wak'a farida na tsaye wani Gaye na rike da hannunta dayan hannunsa na dai-dai Kan k'ugunta Yana janta suna rawa Amma babu fuskar meyin rawar, ga dukkan alamu farida Bata cikin hayyacinta ko kadan saboda yawancin hotunta idanunta duk alumshe suke sai yanda mazan suka da ita.
       
      Saudat tun bata karasa kallo ba ta daka wani ubban tsalle ta kwalla Kara tana Fad'in, " yess soooooo" daddy ne ya katse ta da mamaki yace, " 'yan Mata na wannan tsallen duk na murnar anga Aminiyar taki ne? " Da sauri ta kashe wayarta ta ajiye agefe tace, " wallahi daddy Bari jiya ko bacci banyi ba saboda fargaba,  daddy nasan mommy ta Gaya maka kudin da mutanen suka buk'ata ko? " Dafa ta daddy yayi yace, " ta gayamun Kuma zan biya saboda Yarinyar Nan farida nada hankali ki Duba yanda ta rufa Mana asiri har ta sadaukar da soyayyar mijinku tace ya aureki, tayi Mana halacci Allah ya kubuto da ita ya Kuma tona asirinsu".
     Daga saudat har mommy ba Wanda ya amsa sai mommy ce tayi farat tace, " hmmmm Bari Alh ay mutane sun b'aci da zalincin, Allah dai ya Kara tsare mu Baki d'aya" gaba Daya suka amsa da Ameen.
       Gaba Daya suka fice daga gidan har su saudat gudun Kar dady ya kawo Musu tsaiko yasa mommy batayi maganar zataje unguwa ba, D'an zungurar saudat mommy tayi tuni saudat ta dau haske saidai ba Haka tqso ba acikin zuciyarta.
     Kai tsaye gidan su farida suka wuce, su mommy ne suka sanar da zuwan daddy, umma ce ta fita Saida suka gaisa sannan ya ce Mata ya na San ganin sadiq suna cikin maganar sadiq ya fito har k'asa ya tsugunna ya gaida daddy sannan yacewa sadiq ya Kira Yan kidnappers akan ya tambayesu inda za'a Kai Musu kudin.
     Ba Musu sadiq ya Kira su Yana tambayar su, sanar musu gurun sukayi  sannan suka jadadda Musu cewar muddin suka je da jami'an tsaro sai sun kashe farida, duk Abinda suke fad'a akan kunnena umma da daddy, waya daddy ya Kira yasanarwa da police da gurun da aka ce akai kud'in gurun.
          Daddy d'aukan sadiq yayi amota suka tafi lokaci-lokaci yake Kara Kiran su Yana tambayar su suna yi Masa kwatacen gurun da suke.

    Tafiya sukayi me nisa sannan suka karasa cikin dajin yayinda police ke binsu aboye, dakatar dasu me duniya yayi yace su tsaya ajikin wani dutse su bar gurin sannan sukace suje bakin wani kogi dake gefen hannun hagunsu su dauki farida acen, ba Musu sadiq ya tafi inda suka ce Masa Yana tafe yana dube-dube cen nesa ya hango farida kwance an daure hannu da kafarta, sai ido da Baki da suka rufe Mata, da gudun gaske sadiq ya tafi yaje ya dauko Farida ya fisge abinda suka rufe Mata Baki da ido sai ya kunce Mata hannu da kafarta, farida kallan sadiq tayi cikin kuka tace, " ya sadiq" sai Kuma ta fad'a jikinsa ta fashe matsanancin kuka, har cikin zuciyarsa yake jin kukanta, D'an bubbuga bayanta yayi sannan ya rike hannunta suka wuce jikinta duk ba karfi, tqfiyarma suna tafe tana had'a hanya, har suka karasa gurin motar da daddy yake azaune.
 
     Da sauri suka shiga motar saboda ganin gawar mutum hudu da suka akwance cikin jini anharbesu da bindiga gefe ga jakar kud'in da sadiq ya ajiye Musu nan, Wanda yayi dai-dai da zuwan motar police din gurun cikin takaici suka kwashe gawarwakin suka sa amota suka wuce gaba Daya.

Suna zuwa gida ba abinda yan uwa sukeyi sai murna da farin ciki, farida na ganin inna ta rungumeta tana zubda kwallar farin ciki, tana sakinta jikin umma ta fada ta rungumeta tana jin dadin ganin duk mutanen gurin na farin ciki da dawowarta, juya idanu takeyi da alamar akwai abunda take  nema tana waigawa cen gefe ta hango saudat da take mata murmushi,da gudun gaske ta tafi ta fad'a jikin saudat tana fadin, " yar uwa nayi missing dinki wallahi kinga abunda ya faru ko? Dan Allah ki kara mik'a mun godiya gurin daddy Allah saka da alheri" murmushi saudat tayi me dauke da mnufofi iri-iri wanda ba kowa ne zai fahimci ma'anarsa ba sannan tace, " nayi kewarki sosai tawan wallahi daga jiya zuwa yau na shiga wani irin hali mara musaltuwa, mungodewa Allah dai da ba abinda ya sameki" ta karasa maganar tana rungume farida, zuciya daya farida itama ta kara rungume saudat, sun jima ahaka sannan suka saki juna, suna nan tsaye ba kunya sadiq ya zo yaja hannun farida suka wuce d'akin umma, akunyace farida ta bishi da farko taso ta fisge hannunta ya rik'e ta gam, suna shiga d'aki ya zaunar da ita bakin gadon umma, ido ya zuba mata yana kallo sunkuyar da kanta farida tayi tana wasa da 'yan yatsun hannunta, " _*FARIDA*_" sadiq ya ambata, d'agowa tayi da ido tana kallansa sannan ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa, "  Babu dai abinda mutanen nen sukayi miki ko? " Sadiq ya fad'a, gyad'a masa kai tayi sannan yace dafa kafadarta yace, " ina tunanin ruwa yayi zafi muje kiyi wanka kid'an huta zuwa magriba sai mu wuce gida" yunkurawa zatayi sadiw ya taimaka mata ta tashi tare suka fito waje abokan wasansa sai zolayar sa sukeyi, ruwa aka sirkawa farida ta shiga wanka.

     Lokacin da sadiq yaja hannun farida suka wuce d'akin umma, saudat wani k'ululun b'akin ciki taji ya taso mata wani yawu me d'aci ta had'iya tuni idanunta suka canja launi, gudun kar mutane suyi saurin ganota yasa ta tafi cikin mota ta zauna, duk abunda yake faruwa akan idanun mommy tuni itama taji ranta ya b'aci ganin yanda sadiq yake ta nannan da farida amma ko bi ta kan saudat baiyi ba.


Kiran mommy saudat take yi awaya ba jimawa mommy ta fito ta bud'e motar ta shiga, mommy na shiga motar saudat ta fashe da kuka tana fad'awa jikin mommy cikin damuwa mommy take d'an waigawa gudun kar wani ya hango halin da suke ciki, dafa saudat tayi sannan tace, " haba my baby meye zaki dinga b'ata ranki abanza da wofi? Inace dan kinga yaja yarinyar cen d'aki ne,  kisha kuruminki suyi na yau ne kawai na yau dinma anan gidan, ki share hawayenki kinsan banasan b'acin ranki " wani sabon kukan saudat ta fashe dashi cikin kukan tace, " mommy sai yaushe ne wai za'a dauki matakin daya dace ne nagaji wallahi, mommy ki wuce kawai muje gurin bokan nan yanxu....." Da sauri mommy ta katse ta, " ke me yake damunki baki da hankali ne, kinsan nisan kauyen kuwa? Ga kishiyarki ta dawo kina ganin za'a barta ta kara kwana ne agida? Ki dawo hankalinki kuma ki daina nuna damuwarki agaban mutane har agane ki, nice da wannan yak'in gobe zan buga sammako naje gurin boka d'an tsitu, ke kuma inasan kiyi duk yanda zakiyi ki hana sadiq kwana adakin wannan tsinanniyar yarinyar, saboda nasan muddin ya kwana adakinta magana ta riga da ta k'are kin tuna maganar boka?" Mommy ta k'arasa maganar tana kallan saudat, gyad'a mata kai saudat tayi sannan suka k'arasa kitse-kitsen su mommy ta fito tayiwa su umma sallama sannan ta wuce gida,itama saudat kai katsaye gida ta wuce tana zuwa ta kira me gadin makotansu ta bashi kudi yaje yayi musu cefane me yawan gaske, farar shinkafa ta dafa ta markad'a kayan miya sannan ta d'ora miya, waya ta kira mommy tace tana san akawo musu 'yan aiki mutum biyu, suna gama waya mommy ta kira wata kawarta ta sanar mata suna da buk'atar yar aiki.


     A cen bangaren su sadiq bayan farida tayi wanka alwala tayi sannan ta gabatar da sallah ruwan aka kawo mata tasha sannan ta ci abinci kwanciyarta keda wuya bacci yayi awan gaba da ita ba ita ta tashi va sai gabannin magriba, alwala ta gabatar ba jimawa aka kira fara kiraye-kirayen sallar magriba,bayan sunyi sallah farida ta kalli sadiq tace, " dear tun tashina banga saudat ko ta tafi gida ne? " D'an kuda kai gefe yayi yqce, " wallahi bansan inda take ba kila dai ta wuce gida itada mommy " far farida tayi da ido tace, " dan kiramun ita dan Allah inji tana ina" hannu sadiq yasa a aljihu ya kira saudat, lokacin da sadiq yake kiran saudat ta gama girki tana kwance sai sak'a da warwara take, ganin kiransa ba karamin dadi taji ha, murya cen k'asa ta dauki wayar kamar mara lafiya tace, " hello " daga b'angaren sadiq yace, " hello saudat ina kika je ne?" Kara k'asa da murya saudat tayi tace, " wallahi gida na dawo na d'ora girki nasan ba lallai ku dawo da wuri ba, ga jikin farida ya kamata ya kara samun hutu bata dora girki ba, kuma ina gamawa ciki na ya fara ciwo" d'an kad'an sadiq yayi sannan yace, " sannu Allah kara afuwa kisha Magani, ga farida nan zaku yi magana, ya karasa fad'a yana mik'awa farida wayar, amma cikin zuciyarsa ba karamin dad'i taji ba musamman yanda yaga tana bawa farida kulawa, kamar yanda saudat ta gayawa sadiq haka ta fad'awa farida sannu suka k'ara yiwa saudat sannan sadiq yace wa saudat gasunan zuwa, ba haka farida ta so ba saidai ba yanda ta iya saboda kowa bai nuna goyan bayansa agareta ba, anata tunanin ta zata zauna agida ta kwana biyu amma fir kowa yace tabi mijinta su tafi.
      Sai bayan sallar isha'i sannan suka shirya suka tafi suna hanya saudat ta kira sadiq cikin wahalalliyar murya tana shaida masa ciwon cikin da yake damunta, hankali tashe suka k'arasa gida saboda yanda suka ji muryarta, suna shiga sashenta suka wuce tana kwance cikin bargo da sauri farida taje ta d'an bude bargon tana fad'in, " yar uwa cikin ne yayi tsanani haka?" D'an lumshe ido saudat tayi tace, " eh wallahi murd'amun yake amma yanxu naji ya d'an lafa da sauki" to Allah kara sauk'i" cewar sadiq. 
       " Ko zamu je asibiti ne" sadiq ya fad'a. " A'a naji ya d'an lafa" saudat tace. "Ok bari mu k'arasa"  cewar sadiq.
     " A'a ni zan tsaya da saudat har zuwa jikin nata yayi sauk'i" farida tace, badan yaso ba haka ya d'aga kafad'a ya wuce part dinsa, kaya ya sake sannan ya fice daga gidan, da manyan ledoji biyu ya shigo ya mik'awa farida na saudat yayi ita kuma farida ya wuce mata da nata part dinta.
      Suna nan zaune farida dq saudat lokaci-lokaci farida ke yiwa saudat sannu, a yatsine saudat take amsawa da " yauwa" zuwa wani lokacin saudat tace, " washhhh Allah cikina" sai dai farida tace mata, " sannu ki dinga Addu'a saudat" bata iya magana sai dai saudat ta hau gyad'a kai tana cije baki, suna nan zaune har dare ya fara tsalawa gyangyad'i farida take yi saudat ta d'ago da kanta ya ganta ta sunne kai tana murmushi cikin zuciyarta tace, " duk rawar kan nan na Ango ga amaryarsa ta b'ige da gyangyad'i" tana cikin wannan tunanin taji sadiq ya turo k'ofa ya shigo, da sauri ta koma tayi luf cikin bargo motsin kofar ne ya farkar da farida sannu ya karayi wa saudat sannan yace farida ta wuce su tafi, d'an kallan saudat farida tayi ganin yanda take cije baki yasa tacewa sadiq, " dear har yanxu tanajin jikin mu bari zuwa anjima na gani bai kamata abarta ita kad'ai ba" jinjina kai yayi yace, " hakane amma naga kamar kin fara bacci ne amma ba komai Allah kara afuwa " farida ta amsa da, " Aameen".
    Ficewa sadiq yayi saudat ta koma ta kwanta tana " wash wash" suna nan zaune basuyi aune ba kimanin karfe 1:30 am bacci yayi awan gaba dasu gaba daya, fitsari ne ya tashi farida taga saudat tana baccinta lakadan ganin haka yasa farida tayi tunanin saudat ko ta samu cikinta yayi sauki ne har ta samu bacci, part dinta ta wuce kai tsaye tana tura bedroom din sadiq ta samu kwance rigingine kamar me tunani yanajin sallamarta da sauri ya tashi zaune, mayafin jikinta ta cire ta shiga toilet tare da dauro alwala, dadduma zata shimfid'a sadiq yayi karaf yace, " haba habeebty ki bari mu gabatar da nafilar tare mana tun kafin farida tayi magana sadiq ya sauko ya wuce toilet ya d'auro alwala, gaba ya wuce ya jagorance su suka gabatar da sallah nafila, hannu ya d'oran farida yayi mata Addu'a sannan ya wuce ya dauko musu ledar daya ajiye acikin firji, kaji ne sai su hollandia da fresh milk kala-kala aciki da kanshi ya dinga bata tun tana jin nauyi harta zage take karba sai da suka ci sukayi kat sannan suka sha fresh milk, hira suka cigaba dayi sadiq na tambayarta yanayin gurin da aka kaita ko zata iya ganewa, wani abun ya xolayeta wani abun suyi dariya wani kuma su jajanta, da haka har hirar ta su ta fara sauya salo a million na fice daga d'akin na banko musu k'ofa.🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️

    _Team farida where are u?_

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.* 

               47&48

😡 _Na kusa awa ina edinting ina tashi Ayaman ya gogemun ku karanta da hakuri akwai kurakure da yawa aciki._


         Kimanin karfe 4:00 am na asuba firgigit saudat ta mik'e azabure kamar wqcce aka tasa, wuri-wuri ta hau yi tana neman farida, wani tunani ne ya fad'o mata azabure tayi part din farida lokacin da saudat ta shiga bangaren farida tana kwance tana bacci sadiq ya fito daga toilet kenan kamar anjeho ta yaga saudat ta shigo cikin dakin da mamaki yabi ta da kallo, ita kanta saudat sai da ta shigo dakin ta rasa gane me ya kawota ko kuma idan ya tambayeta me zata ce masa, aykuwa kamar ta shiga zuciyarsa taji yace, " saudat lafiya da tsohon daren nan" dan sosa kai saudat tayi tace, " wani mummunan mafarki nayi wai mutanen nan sun kara zuwa zasu tafi da farida shine ina farkawa na taho dan inga halin da take ciki" subhanallah insha Allah babu abinda zai kara faruwa, ina fatan kinyi addu'a ko? Kinsan haka manzan rahama ya koyar damu, farida bacci take yi muje na rakaki b'angarenki" dan jim saudat tayi sannan tace, " ni bansan ma lokacin da farida ta dawo ba ta dad'e da bacci ne" saudat ta fad'a da sigar bugar ciki dan taji ko wani abu ya shiga tsakanin farida da sadiq.
         " Eh " sadiq ya bata amsa atakaice, ganin ba fuska yasa saudat ta juya ta wuce sashenta cikin wasi-wasi azuciyar tana Addua'ar kar wani abun ya shiga tsakaninsu.
       Bayan sallah Asuba sadiq yaje masallaci ya dawo ta part din saudat ya fara biyawa ya tasheta sallah sannan ya koma na farida, lokacin da ya fara tashinta jikinsa zam da zazzabi dakyar ta yunkura ta tashi ya taimaka mata ta shiga toilet, alwala ta dauro ta gabatar da sallah adaddafe tayi addu'o'i takoma ta kwanta saboda baccin da yake kanta.
       Sadiq da kansa ya wuce ya had'a musu breakfast ya jera musu akan dianning, kafin ya kammala tuni gari ya fara wayewa bayan ya gama tea ya had'owa farida akofi sannan ya taho mata da magani tashinta yayi ta shiga tayi brush sannan ta dawo ta tasha tea ta sha magani sannan ta koma ta kwanta, shima ta gefe ya koma ya kwanta bacci yayi awon gaba dasu.
        
        Saudat tunda ta koma sashe ta ta kasa zaune ta kasa tsaye, sai zirga-zirga take tana sak'a da warwara har bacci har lokacin da taji alamun shigowar sadiq shine tayi lamo kamar me bacci, shima atunaninsa bacci take haka yayita tashinta sai zuwa wani lokaci sannan ta tashi tana d'an murza ido alamun me bacci.
       Bayan fitar sadiq key ta sawa kofar da sauri ta d'auko wayarta ta kira mommy bugu biyu mommy ta d'auka, " hello saudat me yake faruwa naga kiranki yanxu" had'iyar yawu saudat tayi sannan ta zayyanewa mommy duk abinda ya faru, mommy ce ta fara magana, " kinyi kuskure saudat ya akayi kika bari bacci yayi awan gaba dake ki saurareni da kyau inasan ki sa idanki akan farida da kyau ki gani idan kinga wasu alamomi ki bugo ki sanar mun, yanxu ma daga wanka na fito zan shirya na tafi gurun boka d'an tsitu idan naje zan kira ki sai kimun karin bayani inhar kinga wani canji atattare da ita, yanxu zan k'arasa shiryawa banasan na makara " agaggauce mommy tayiwa saudat sallama ta cigaba da shiryawa ita kuma saudat ta fad'a kan gado, sai awannan lokacin ta d'an ji nutsuwa azuciyarta.

       Kimanin karfe goma na safe wayar sadiq ce ta fara ringing, farida ce ta fara tashi a hankali ta fara bubbuga bayan sadiq tana tqshinsa, dan yamutsa fuska yayi da hannu ta nuna masa wayarsa sannan ya kai hannu ya d'auka da sallama, umma ce ta fara magana, " sadiq kuntashi lafiya ya su farida?" D'an shafa kai sadiq yayi sannan yace, " lafiya kalau umma ya jama'ar gidan? Umma ta bashi amsa da, " lafiya su klau yanxu  ma suke shiri zasu xo nan gurunku daga nan duk zasu wuce su kama gabansu" da sauri sadiq yace sun taho ne? " A'a basu taho ba sundai gama shiri amma yanxu zasu fito, shine nace bari na gaya maka" umma tace.
        " To shikenan Allah ya kawo su lafiya" sadiq ya fad'a.
     Bayan ya gama waya da umma yake sanarwa da farida zuwa bak'i, yunkurawa tayi ta shiga toilet tayi wanka lokacin da ta fito baya dakin, sosai takejin kwarin jikinta ba kamar tashin da tayi da asuba ba, gaban mirror taje tazauna tana shafe jikinta da mai, motsin kofa taji ta cikin mirror ta hango saudat da murmushi afuskarta ta juyo tana yiwa saudat magana, " Amarya ya jikin? Tun dare bansamu da dawo naga jikinki ba dukda ya Sadiq yace mun  da sauk'i amma naso na kara lek'awa bacci ya sha munkai" tun shigowar Saudat take jin Farida da kallo ganin da tayiwa farida gaban mudubi ne yasa gabanta yayi mummunan fad'uwa amma sai ta maze ta k'irk'iro murmushin dole sannan tace, " A'a kinji ki dazolaya wane ni akirani da amarya, ay amarci na gurunku to antashi lafiya ya amarcin? " Ta kara sa fad'a tana karantar yanayin da farida zata shiga, ko kadan Farida batayi mamaki da jin haka daga Saudat ba,murmushi Farida tayi tace, " kedai Saudat bakya rabo da zolaye, to ance miki ni haka nake ba wani amarci, ke bari na maza na shirya naji yayi waya da umma wai bak'i na nan tafe, kema ya kamata ki shirya dan tun d'azu naji tace zasu karaso" d'an yamutsa fuska saudat tayi tace, " to me zasu xo suyi mana da sassafe haka? " Murmushi farida tayi tace, " kinsan mafi yawa a al'adance anayin haka wad'anda basuga d'akin amarya ba sai washe gari zasu shiryo suje su gani daga nan su wuce gida" dan guntun tsaki Saudat taja sannan tace, " ay sai suyi ta zuwa " ta karasa maganar tana zama agefen gadon Farida tana yi tana karewa kan gadon kallo. 
      Wayar farida ce dake kan stool ta fara ringing tasowa Farida tayi da sauri taje zata d'auka wayar ta katse, tunda ta tashi saudat ke k'are mata kallo ko zata fahimci wani abu daga gurin Farida amma ganin farida na tafiya garau yasa taji wani sanyi azuciyarta, tashi tayi tana fadin, " kai Farida bari nakarasa ciki na shirya kar suxo ban shiryaba kinyimun wayo,". D'an waiwayowa farida tayi tace, " wayo kuma na nawa 'yanmata tunda kaya ya rage kawai insaka, dan tani ko yanxu su xo" afakaice saudat ta watsa mata harara sannan tace, " to bazama su zo yanxun ba sai na gama kintsawa ni kinga tafiya" ta karasa maganar tana jan kofar d'akin farida.
      Saudat na shiga sashenta da sauri da danna lock ta kira mommy ta sanar mata da duk abinda ta gani da yanayin data ga farida, ita dinma mommy cikin farinciki take sanarwa da Saudat cewar tana gaban boka d'an tsitu, ganin wayar na kara tsayi yasa boka ya bugawa mommy tsawa har ita kanta Saudat saida taji da sauri mommy ta katse wayar ko sallama basuyi ba.
        Ahankali mommy ta sunkuyar da kai k'asa tace, " inna ta huci zuciyar sarkin bokayen duniya tuba nake da alama da b'ata ran d'an tsitu " idanu d'an tsitu ya zurawa mommy wadanda suke jawur gasu manya-manya masu abun tsoro, babu kaya ajinkinsa daga shi sai wani ganye daya d'aura ak'ugunsa, jikinsa duk gashi haka ma fuskarsa da kansa, idan kayi masa kallo d'aya zaka d'auka shima aljanin ne, wad'ansu irin rubutu ne ajikinsa banda shi babu wanda zai gane abunda aka rubuta, rubutune ba fasali da jini akayi da wad'ansu irin bak'in abu ajiki, lokaci d'aya ya kece da wata banzar dariya sannan ya cewa mommy, " Banasan doguwar hira acikin kogon dutse na inba haka ba fushi zai sauka akanki keda yar taki, ki kiyaye abunda zai kara sa na miki magana" gum mommy tayi tana raba idanu sannan tace, " inna ta taimaki d'an tsitu dama tambayarta nayi ko wani abu ta faru tsakanin mijinta da kishiyarta domin insanar maka......." Wata mahaukaciyar tsawa d'an tsitu ya bugawa mommy tuni hantar cikinta ya fara kad'awa cikin tsawa yace, " har ni zaki taimakawa dangane da aikin da zanyi? Kina nufin kinfini sanin me yake gaba na ne, tabbas idan bakiyi wasa ba sai mummunan hukunci ya tabbata agareki kafin ki fita daga kogon dutsen nan " jikin na rawa mommy ta durk'usa har kasa tana rok'on d'an tsitsu sannan tace, " boka dama naji kace idan munasan aikinmu ya tafi yanda muke so sai  ya kasance mijinta bai kusanci kishiyarta ba, to shine na tambayeta kumw tace mun ba abinda ya faru dan tace bata ga wani canji daga gurin kishiyarta ta ba" mommy ta k'arasa fad'a tana k'ara zubewa gaban bota d'an tsitu.
        Turb'une fuska yayi lokaci daya ya kece da wata mahaukaciyar dariya sannan ta kara turb'une fuska kamar bashi ne yayi dariya ba yace, " duba mun wannan " wani k'ok'o ya dauko dake gefensa cike yake da jini, mommy na lek'a wa ta hango farida aciki tana zaune gefen gado daga ita sai towel da alama waya takey, rike baki mommy tayi tana mamaki sannan d'an tsitu yace, " ki barmun komai a hannuna zan bincika ingani idan ma wani abu ya faru zan sani" boka na karasa maganar ya d'auko wani guru d'an gajere ya tsoma shi acikin kwaryar nan me jini, ya fito dashi sannan ya sa ahannunsa ya dunk'uleshi bayan wasu 'yan sakanni yana bud'e gannunsa sai ga d'akin saudat ya bayyana atafin hannunsa daga lokacin da farida ta farka har zuwa shigarta sashenta da lokacin da ta shiga toilet zatayi alwala, tana turo kofa zata fito yaga hannun nasa yayi duhu dund'um ba abinda suke gani mommy sandarewa tayi a zaune tana mamakin shaid'anci irin na boka d'an tsitu.
       Ransa ne ya fara b'aci ganin yayi iya yinsa dan ganin ya samu yaga abunda yake faruwa sam abun yaci tura, har wani ruwa me yauk'i ya d'ebo ya yarfawa hannun nasa amma duk abanza, kallan mommy yayi yace, " da alama yarinyar nan hatsabibiyace duk iyayina nakasa gano abunda nake da buk'ata amma zamu bullo mata ta wata hanyar bari ma ki gani " yana rufe baki ya d'aga kansa ta mik'a hannunsa sama yana fad'in innaaaaaa innaaa innaaaa, har sau uku sai ga wata jakar fata ta fad'o hannunsa me nauyi, zazzageta yayi ya d'ebi wani wani gari dake cikin wani k'aton dodon kod'i sai ya d'auko wani jaririn kunkuru atake ya murd'e masa wuya ya cire kan, jini na fara zuba ya tara wata katuwar kwalba sai da jinin me yawa ya zuba aciki sannan ya rufe kwalbar ya jijjiga sau uku sannan ya bud'e ya d'ebi jinin dake cikin kwaryar ya zuba ya had'a da wannan garin na cikin dodon kod'i sai yad'ebo wasu layu masu yawan gaske jikinsu duk antsireshi da allurai ya zubasu aciki dunkule hannunsa yayi ya buga akan gangar jikin kunkurun take ya dagargaje ya debi tsokar naman ya zuba acikin wannan kwalbar ya rufe yana gama rufewa ya jijjiga ta sau uku sannan ya bud'e ya bud'e ya fara magana da muryarsa me amsa amo da rashin dadin sauraro, " anyi nasara dama ni na sani dole ma nayi nasara tunda ko yaushe nine me nasara, nasara na gareki innaaaaaa " sai kuma ya bushe da mahaukaciyar dariya ya cigaba da cewa, " Faridaaaaaaa munyi nasara kanku keda Sadiqqqqqq Saudat zaki shinfid'a milkinki yanda kike so sai yanda kika ce acikin gidanki" yana rufe baki ya fara wasu surutai ba babu me gane abunda yake fad'a, yana gama maganar ya tofar da yawunsa acikin wani bak'in kasko lokaci guda ya kama da wuta, sake hasko farida yayi acikin kwaryar nan me jini ya dinga kad'a hayakin kaskon kan kwaryar, alokacin farida na gaban mudubi tana kwalliya gashin kanta duk ya bazu abayanta, yana gama kora mata hayakin ya kara kecewa da dariya sai da yayi me isarsa sannan ya juyo gurin mommy ya mik'a mata, wannan katuwar kwalbar da yayi had'e-had'e acikin yace, " Aiki na ya kamallu saura na 'yarki wannan kawalbar ta tabbatar da ta ajiyeta acikin d'akin farida a inda tasan bazata fashe ba" wani gari ya d'auko had'e da wani wuri guda d'aya yace, " wannan kuma ta ajiyeshi acen sashenta duk lokacin da take san magana dani ta cilla shi sama sau daya ta kira sunan inna sau uku zan turo Aljani d'an gudul ta zayyane masa komai kamar ta gayamun ne, garin maganin ta ringa girki dashi tana bawa mijinta, wannan ruwan turaren duk lokacin da mijinta zai kusanceta ta tabbatar da ta shafashi ajikinta, sai abu na karshe ta hakura da yin sallah ta kwana arba'in, kinsan aiki nabana bayani kai da kanka zaja gani a k'asa " mommy godiya take kamar ta kwanta katse ta yayi da, " ajiye kudin ki wuce kinsan banasan yawan godiya, tab'ewa ta tabbata ga wanda ya zo gurin d'an tsitu da iyalansa, tsinuwa ta wanzu akanki fita da baya-baya, karki manta yau bake ba sallah har sai gobe war haka, innaaaaaaaa ki bamu nasara aduk inda muke, kuma dama dole muyi nasara tunda mu masu nasarane" haka boka ya dinga surutai har mommy ta fice daga gurin bayan ta ajiye masa damin kudi masu yawan gaske.

           Farida na gaban mudubi tana kwalliya taji wani abu yammm yana bin jikinta duk inda kamar tashiyar gizgizo, karkad'e jikinta tayi ganin ba abinda ya fad'o ta cigaba da kwalliyarta, tana kallan madubi taga kamar giftawar mutum da sauri ta tashi ta waiga amma babu ba abinda ta gani, zama tayi ta cigaba da kwalliyarta tana shafa lipstick lokaci d'aya taji jikinta yayi wani sanyi daga baya kuma taji gumi na keto mata, da mamaki ta tashi daga gaban mirror din ta je da d'auko kayanta ta saka kitchen ta nufa kai tsaye tafiya take amma gaba daya bata jin ta take wani iri, taje wucewa ta dainning ta hango sadiq zaune a falo da waya ahannu yana dannawa, kamshin turarenta ne ya sa shi kallo gurin da take, shar ya ganta kamar wata sarauniya tashi yayi yaje ya ruk'o hannunta yace, " kinga yanda kikayi kyau kuwa? yarinyar nan kinasan ki rikirani ko" ya karasa maganar yana jan hancin farida, dan turo baki gaba tayi tace, " ni ba wani rikitawa hoda ce ba kawai sai lipstick dana shafa kawai dai tsokana ta kake yi" Sadiq na shirin bawa Farida amsa yaji kansa ya wani sara masa harsai da ya dafe kan cikin sauri lokaci daya yaji juya na neman d'ibansa, runtse idanu yayi ganin yanayin da ya shiga yasa Farida tayi saurin rikeshi tana tambayarsa, " lafiya ya Sadiq me yake faruwa ne? " Lokaci d'aya yaji komai ya warware masa kamar ba shi ba bud'e idansa yayi ahankali yace, " ba komai dama kaina ne ya d'an sara mun " yana gama maganar ya saki Farida ya koma kan kujera ya zauna, Farida Dianning ta wuce ganin komai ashirye tsab tayi murmushi ta waigo tana tambayar sadiq, " Ya Sadiq tun yaushe ka had'a kayan breakfast din nan gaskiya ka shammace ni " tambayar da Farida tayi ce yaji ta b'ata masa rai adak'ile yace, " tun d'azu" Farida bata damu da yanda ya bata amsa ba saboda taji yace kansa na sara masa, tana gama magana ta wuce sashen Saudat tana fadin, " to bari na kira Saudat muyi breakfast kar muji sallamar bak'i " da harara Sadiq ya bita harta wuce sannan ya cigaba da danna wayarsa, Farida na shiga sashen Saudat ta ganta sanye da doguwar riga tayi kyau dai-dai gwargwado, Saudat gabanta ne yayi mummunan fad'uwa ganin irin kyan da Farida tayi, sanye take da bakin leshi ba ratsin gold dinkin riga da skirt sunyi das ajikinta ta kafa d'aurin d'ankwalinta tayi kyau sosai, Farida ce ta fara magana, " wooooo Amaryar mu gaskiya kinyi kyau yasin zaki rikita ya sadiq karfa kisa na fara kishi, wannan irin zuk'o kyau haka " Saudat gani take kamar Farida gatse take mata ita kuwa zuciyarta d'aya take gaya mata haka d'an yake tayi sannan tace, " haba dai na kaiki ne uwar gida sarautar mata ay da anganki kece amarya tunda kece kika sha amarci tuni ango ya dad'e da kashe boss " Saudat ta Fad'a cikin bugun ciki, Farida murmushi tayi sannan tace mata, " dallacen waya ce miki ni haka nake to bari kiji muna gama breakfast zan koro miki mijinki danni kam da saura na karku gogemun hadda" murmushi Saudat  tayi dama ta fad'i haka ne dan taji me Farida zats ce tasan dai yanda suke da Farida ba abinda zata iya boye mata katse mata tunanin Farida tayi da, " Amarsu muje Ya Sadiq na falo fa yana jiranmu" ba musu Saudat ta wuce suka k'arasa cen gurin da Sadiq yake, tahowarsu Sadiq ya ji suna k'arasowa Saudat yaga tayi masa masifa kyau da murmushi afuskarta ta gaidashi aykuwa kamar jira yake ya amsa yana tambayarta, " ya kika ya ciwon cikin naki " ita Saudat ma sam ta manta da tayi wani Karyar ciwon cikin sai lokacin ta tuna, cikin wani salo ta bashi amsa, " da sauki ya Sadiq ya warware " Farida ce tace, " oh kinganni ko intambayeki ya jiki? Wallahi na sha'afa ya cikin naki? " Murmushi Saudat tayi tace, " da sauki Tawan " to Allah kara sauki " Farida ta bata amsa, daga haka tashi sukayi suka wuce dianning cin abinci, tunda suka zauna Sadiq ya kafe Saudat da kallo lokaci-lokaci Saudat take d'ago da kanta suna had'a ido zai sakar mata  murmushi, tun Farida bata lura ba har ta fara Lura wani abu taji ya tsaya mata ak'ahon zuci da kyar ta hadiyi wani yawu me d'aci, wata zuciyar ce ta ce mata, " me yayi zafi zaki b'ata ranki akan d'an wannan abun inace itama matarsa ce, keda ya kwana agurinki bata nuna damuwar ta ba sai ke saboda yana kallanta yana murmushi, farida yaushe kika koyi san kanki da yawa haka " wannan ne yasa Farida ta sake ta kauda komai tq cigaba da cin abincinta cikin nishad'i har suka suka gamawa.
         Saudat tunda ta lura da irin kallan da Sadiq yake mata wani irin dad'i ta dinga ji yana ratsa ta cikin zuciyarta twce, " yanxu ma ka fara da ba kanacen gurin wannan kod'add'iyar yarinyar nan ba, muje zuwa ajuri zuwa rafi wata ran tulun zai fashe " 

             Farida ce ta fara tashi daga ta k'oshi falo ta koma ta zauna ta fara game da wayarta, ganin ta basu guri yasa Saudat ta cigaba da cin abincin kamar bataso shikuwa gogan naku sai kallanta yake yanajin tana kuma birfeshi, ganin haka yasa saudat ta k'irk'iro tarin karya kamar wacce ta kware yi take tun k'arfinta da sauri Sadiq ya mik'a mata ruwa amma tak"i karba ta cigaba da tari tanayi tana dafe k'irji, ganin haka yasa Sadiq tasowa yaje ya rik'eta yana mata sannu, Farida ce itama ta taso cikin sanyin murya take mata sannu, sai da ta d'an jima ahaka sannan ta daina ruwa Farida ta mik'a mata sai alokacin ta karb'a ta sha tana lumshe ido, tallab'o hab'arta Sadiq yayi yace, " Sannu, ba abinda yake damunki ko? " D'aga masa kai tayi sannan ya wuce falo ya zauna, Faridan ma kamo hannunta tayi suka koma falo suka zauna daga nan babu me cewa kowa uffan, suna nan zaune sukaji a nocking Sadiq ne yaje ya bud'e kofar bak'in da  umma tace zasu zo ne, tun daga bakin get suka fara yiwa sadiq tsiya har aka shigo falon, da sakin fuska Su Farida suka tarbesu har Saudat mutane sai sha'awarsu sukeyi da yanda suka ga kansu a had'e abinsai ya birgesu, Nan da nan farida ta hau kiciniyar dora girki kafin kace kwabo tuni ta tuk'a musu tuwan semovita miyar kubewa danya, bajewa sukayi ana ta hira ganin hayaniyar tayi yawa yasa Sadiq komawa cen Sashensa.

       Kamar yanda boka ya umarci mommy ta fita da baya-baya har sai da ta fice daga cikin kogon dutsen sannan ta juya dai-dai ta cigaba da tafiya cikin wannan surk'uk'in dajin ba gida gaba ba gida baya, sai da tayi tafiya me nisa sannan ta fito titi gurin da driver yake zaune  amota tuni yayi bacci sai da mommy ta d'an bubbuga glass din motar sannan ya tashi yana dube-dube, bude mata yayi ta ciki sannan ta shiga, atake ya tada motar ya cillata kan titi suka d'auki hanya.
         Sai da sukayi tafiya me nisan gaske sannan suka karaso gida, zuwan su gida suka tarar da har ankawo mata masu aiki 'yan mata su biyu gaba dayansu bazasu wuce shekara goma sha tara zuwa Ashirin ba, sama ta hau ta kira Saudat take ce mata yanxu zata turo Driver da 'yan aiki sannan zata had'o su da kayan aikin da boka yayi musu, murna gurin saudat kamar tayi me haka ta dinga zumud'in jiran zuwan 'yan aikin.

       Mommy nad'e kwalbar tayi cikin wani zaninta ta sashi cikin wata 'yar jaka sannan ta sauko kasa gurun yaran da aka kawo mata, kallan tsab tayi musu sannan ta fara tambayarsu, " meye sunayen ku? " Me d'an girman jikin ce ta fara magana, " ni sunana Asabe " sai 'yar tsamurmurar itama tace, " nikuma lami  " sai da mommy ta gama jinsu sannan ta kalli dillaliyar data kawo su tace, " duk garinsu daya ko daban-daban suke?" Dillaliya har rawar murya take tace, " a'a hajiya kowacce garinsu daban " numfasawa tayi sannan tace, " yayi kyau ga wannan sak'o ne ku kaiwa saudat kuce Daddynta ne yace akawo mata, yanxu zansa driver ya kaiku banasan naji wani complain daga gareku, kuma banasan munafurci kuji kuk'i ji ku gani kuk'i gani, zaku iya tafiya yanxu muje nayiwa driver bayani " mommy ta karasa maganar ta mik'ewa da sauri suma suka bi bayanta, sai da mommy ta gama yiwa driver bayani sannan ta ciro kudin dillaliya ta mik'a mata suka shiga mota ita kuma ta juya gida.

    Lokacin da su Asabe suka k'arasa tuni bak'insu Farida sun dad'e da tafiya kai tsaye suna zuwa sashen Saudat suka tambaya, Sadiq daya bud'e gida shi ya raka su har bangarenta suna shiga ya fito ya koma nashi, dama tun kafin zuwan su mommy ta gama yiwa saudat bayanin komai ta waya, suna shiga bayan sun gaidata abunda ta fara tambaya shine sak'on da aka basu, jiki na rawa suka mik'a mata ta karba ta wuce bedroom dinta tasashi cikin drower sannan ta fito gurunsu, sunayen ta tambaya suka bata amsa lami ta nuna tace ta zauna a b'angarenta sannan tacewa Asabe ta wuce suje sashen Farida, lokacin da suka shiga tana kwance k'asan carpet tana chartting shigowar su Saudat ne tasa tatashi zaune, bayanin Asabe tayi mata sannan ta cewa asabe Farida ita ce uwar d'akinta, agurguje ta tayi ta wuce part dinta tana saurin taje taga sak'on da mommy ta aiko mata dashi na gurun boka d'an tsitu.

      Sai da ta danna key akofarta sannan ta zauna ta zaro shi daya bayan daya take dubawa kwalbar ta d'aga tana juyawa ganin abubuwa iri-iri tayi aciki sannan ta d'auko wurin da zata dinga kiran aljani d'an gudul sai garin maganin da zata dinga zubawa Sadiq a Abinci, karkashin gadonta ta leka sannan tayi wani murmushi, cikin yar jakar ta maida kwalbar sannan ta turata cen karshen gado yanda babu wani mahaluki da zai iya gano ko mene agurun, wurin ta d'auka ta kaishi cikin drower mudubinta acikin wani d'an mitsitsin kit ta sashi sai ta jefa cikin kayan sark'ok'inta da d'an kunnayenta ta maidashi ta rufe, garin maganin ne ta dinga juyashi ta rasa ya zatayi ta girkawa Sadiq Abinci tunda taga Farida ce tayi kuma ak'ai'da sai yayi kwana bakwai zai dawo d'akinta amma yanda take ji cikin zuciyarta ko awa bakwai bazata iyayi ba Sadiq bai dawo part dinta ba, wayar mommy ta kira take neman shawara tuni mommy tace mata idan bazatayi girki ba to ta dafa masa tea ta kai masa inyaso sai ta zuba garin d'an kad'an tunda boka yace ako menene zata iya zuba masa indai zaici to magana ta kare, uban tsalle Saudat ta buga cikin murna taje ta boye shi tana Allah-Allah dare yayi ta dafa masa tea ta kai masa.
        Hakan kuwa Akayi farida da Sadiq na zaune a main falour dinsu kowa yayi shiru bame kula d'an uwansa, abinda ya faru kuwa tun magriba Farida ta fesa wankanta ta zauna jiran sadiq da niyyar idan ya shigo zata yi masa rakiya zuwa b'angaren Saudat tana taroshi tun daga bakin k'ofa ya tureta, mamaki abun ya bata sosai amma ganin daga waje yake yasa tayi tunanin ko wani ne ya b'ata masa rai bayan ya zauna taje tana tambayarsa ko akwai abinda yake buk'ata, adakile yace mata, " babu" duk abinda tace masa agajarce yake bata amsa ganin haka yasa ta hakura ta kyaleshi, atunaninta ko yana cikin b'acin rai ne shiyasa ta koma gefe itama ta d'auki wayarta take 'yan danne-dannenta.
         Saudat sanye take cikin english wear riga da wando kayan sun kamata sosai, ganin yanayin da ta gansu ba karamin dad'i taji ba, da sallama ta shiga falon hannunta d'auke da karamin kofin tea wanda yasha garin maganin boka, amsa mata sukayi da murmushi ta cewa Farida, " tawan kuna nan ashe " ita dinma murmushi tayi mata tace, " wallahi muna nan ayda naji kishiru nace ko kin kwanta ne" d'an yamutsa fuska Saudat tayi tace, " a'a inacen iba dafa wa ya sadiq tea nace ko zai buk'ata yanxu " ta k'arasa maganar tana nufar gurin da Sadiq yake.
       Tunda Saudat ta shigo d'akin kamshin turarenta ya bugi Sadiq har lumshe ido yayi, gani yayi tayi mugun yi masa kyau, yanasan yayi mata magana baisan dame zai fara ba, tunda ya zuba mata ido ya kasa koda kiftawa har ta k'araso gurunsa katse shi tayi da,  " ya Sadiq ga coffee nace bari na kawo maka " da sauri ya karb'a cikin murmushi yace, " tnx dear may Allah bless u" wani irin dadi Saudat taji sannan tace, " never mind ok, " ta k'arasa maganar tana kashe masa ido d'aya, suman zaune Sadiq yayi yanajin kaunar Saudat na k'ara shiga cikin jikinsa, juyawa Saudat tayi zata tafi da sauri ya rik'o hannunta yana fad'in, " ina zuwa? " Da gayya Saudat ta saki jiki ta fad'o kan Sadiq tana fadin, " wash, ya Sadiq zaka k'arasani " kara riketa yayi tsam yace, " haba dai ko ke ki k'arasani ba, zauna ki tayani hira man " ba musu ta zauna agefensa yana shan coffee suna hira daga karshe ma laptop dinsa dake gefe Saudat ta janyo kunna musu yayi suka fara kallo, gaba daya ma sun manta da wata Farida dake zaune akan kujera.

        Farida sakin baki da hanci tayi tana mamakin yanda taga Sadiq ya sakarwa Saudat lokaci daya, kanta ne ya k'ara kullewa ganin ko kunyarta basu ji ba suke nunawa junansu soyayya, cikin zuciyarta take tambayar kanta, ko dama cen Sadiq yanasan Saudat ne? To idan baya santa taya za'ayi har irin wannan wasan ya fara shiga tsakaninsu, wani tunani tayi tace to ko ya huce ne shiyasa yanxu naga ya sake haka, bari naje muyi hirar tare, farida na gama tunanin ta tashi ta k'arasa gurunsu cikin murmushi tace, " ya Sadiq wane film kuke kallo ne haka?" Banza yayi kamar baiji ba, zama tayi itama ad'ayen gefen nasa tace, " ya Sadiq wane film kuke kallo ne?" Wani haushinta ne ya turnik'e shi azafafe yace, " ina ruwanki da film din da muke kallone, wai ma meye damuwarki da film din da muke kallo " atake idanun Farida suka ciko da kwallah cikin sanyin jiki tace, " kayi hakuri ya sadiq idan wani laifin nayi maka " dogon tsaki yaja ya mik'e tsaye yayi gaba ya cewa Saudat, " dear zo mu wuce idan kuma zaki zauna anan shikenan " yana gama maganar yayi part dinsa.
        Saudat ji take kamar ta zuba ruwa ak'asa ta sha saboda farin ciki, amma saboda makirci sai ta kalli Farida da yanayi mara dad'i tace, " Tawan me yake faruwa ne? Ko kinyiwa Ya sadiq wani laifin ne?" Aykuwa farida kamar dama jira take ta Rushewa da Saudat da kuka cikin kukan tace, " Bansani ba tawan bansan me nayi masa ba, da rana lafiya kalau muka rabu dashi amma duk ya sauyawa idan wani laifin nayi masa aysai ya fad'a mun, ko laifi nayi masa ya Sadiq baya kyarata bare hantara bansan me nayi masa ba" ta karasa fad'a tana rushewa da matsanancin kuka, rungumeta Saudat tayi tana bbbuga bayanta zuciyarta wasai, saida taji Farida ta tsagaita da kuka tace mata, " tawan bari naje na lallab'o ko zai sauko ya hakura kinji"  jijjiga kai Farida tayi tace, " ki kyaleshi Tawan nasan waye Ya Sadiq ....." Katseta Saudat tayi, " a'a ki bari zan jarraba na gani " Farida bata kara ce mata komai ba har Saudat ta tashi ta dauko kofin coffee din data kawowa  Sadiq tayi part dinsa tana tafiya itama Farida tayi Part dinta kwanciya tayi akan gado tana tunanin ko akwai abunda tayiwa Sadiq har ya d'auki wannan fushin da ita.
 
      Lokacin da Saudat ta shiga sashen sadiq yana kwance kan gadonsa ya lumshe idansa kamar me bacci, amma idansa biyu cikin zuciyarsa yake jin kamar ya aikata wani abu ba dai-dai ba, Shigowar Saudat ce ta katse masa tunanin da yakeyi da murmushi ta tarbeta, cikin kissa tace, " ya Sadiq me Farida tayi maka ne?" Tsuke fuska yayi ko sunanta bayasan ji yace, " idan kindamu ki je ki tambayota mana, idan kinsan maganar ta ce zata kawo kema ki tashi ki tafi saboda banasan duk wani abu daya shafeta" wayyo dadi Saudat ji tayi kamar ta tashi ta taka saboda murna, murmushi tayi tace, " tuba nake ranka ya dad'e, ga coffee dinka na biyo ka dashi naga ka barshi afalo" karba Sadiq yayi suna hira yana sha har ya shanye tass, wata sabuwar soyayyarta yaji na kara shigarsa da wani irin yanayi da yaji yana shiga game da Saudat tuni hirar tasu ta fara canja salo.
      Da gayya Saudat ta fara tsokanar sa ta fice aguje shi dinma biyota yayi aguje Saudat na ganin haka da gudu tayi Sashen Farida, lokacin tana zaune kan dadduma tayi Sallah bacci har ya Fara d'aukanta taji anturo kofa da k'arfi kanta ankara tuni Saudat ta shigo tana wata dariya me had'e da ihu cikin wannan yanayin take yiwa Sadiq magana, " Dan Allah ya Sadiq kayi hakuri ka kyaleni wallahi yau ban shirya ba " tana karasa fa d'a ta fad'a kan gadon Farida tana janyo blanket dinta ita Adole ta b'uya aykuwa yana shigowa da gudu shima ya bita yana Fad'in, " naki wasa ne yarinya ke kika taro match ay " ya sa  hannu yana janyo ta, Farida dake zaune bak'in ciki ya gama cikata banda hawaye ba abinda takeyi, Saudat na daga kan gado tana hango duk halin da Farida take ciki aykuwa da sauri ta farayiwa Sadiq cakwulkuli banda dariya shida ita ba abinda sukeyi, shammatarsa tayi ta diro da gudu daga kan gadon tayi part dinta, shidinma hakan yayi vangaren Saudat.
        Bayan sun zauna sun huta toilet din Saudat Sadiq ya shiga, da farko taso ta bishi amma tayi wani tunani ta fasa, yana shiga taje ta d'auko turaren boka ta boyeshi bayan ya fito ita ma ta shiga ta watsa ruwa daga cen ta shafo turarenta sannan ta fito, umartarta yayi da tayo alwala zasu gabatar da nafila, gudun karya zargeta yasa to koma toilet din tayi wasa da ruwanta ta fito, Sadiq ne yajasu Sallar bayan sun idar ya gabatar da Addu'o'i sannan zance yasha ban-ban.🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️🧗🏼‍♀️

      Da Asuba da kyar ya tashi saudat ta tashi ta gyara kanta amma fir tak'i sai ma kuka da ta fashe dashi, shi kuma sai wani nannan yake da ita, wani tunani Saudat tayi aykuwa ta k'ara narke murya tace, " ya Sadiq dan Allah nidai ka kiramun Farida ta taimaka mun " ba musu kuwa yayi sashenta tana kwance idar da sallah ta ba dad'ewa tana jan carbi taji shigowarsa, asanyaye tace, " ya Sadiq ina kwana " bai amsa mata sai ma umarni da ya bata agadarance,  " ke ki tashi kije ki gyara Saudat bata jin dad'i " Rass gaban Farida ya fad'i wani irin zafi taji akirjinta musamman tayi tsammanin abunda take zargina........., Katseta yayi cikin tsawa, " nace kije ki taimaka mata ko " tuni farida ra fara hawaye  sannan tace, " to " bai bata amsa ba yayi gaba abinsa, ita dinma fitar tayi b'angaren Saudat ta shiga, warwas ta samu Saudat akan gado sai wani cije baki take tana wash Sadiq ba gefe yana mata sannu, Farida hadiye wani yawu tayi me d'aci ta kama hannun Saudat tace, " muje toilet din" kama hannun da farida tayi tana d'ago Saudat ta wani kwallah k'ara, Sadiq cikin tsawa ya farayiwa Farida magana, " Kiyi mata ahankali mana bakya ganin yanda take ne, dallah ki lallab'ata nawa Saudat din take "

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*
      
                  49&50


          Farida lokaci daya ta kafe Sadiq da ido tana masa kallan mamaki cikin zuciyarta tace, " yau ni ya sadiq yake dizgawa agaban wata? Wai yaushe suka D'inke da Saudat ne bansani ba? Me nayi maka ne ya Sadiq da kake mun haka....." Katse ta Sadiq yayi cikin fad'a, " matsa-matsa idan bazaki taimaka mata ba, kin wani kafeni idanu kamar zaki cinye ni" ya karasa fad'a yana zagayowa ta b'angaren da Farida take tsaye, cikin sanyin murya kamar wacce zatayi kuka tace, " kayi hakuri ya Sadiq bari na rakata " hannu ta kara sawa ta tallab'o Saudat ita kuwa sai yatsine fuska take tana wani cije baki, ahaka har ta samu Saudat ta sauko daga kan gadon tana tafe tana dingisa k'afa har ta kaita bakin toilet, Saudat na shiga toilet din Farida ta juya da niyyar ta koma part dinta azafafe Sadiq yace mata, " ina kuma zaki tafi wannan gadon wa kike tunanin zai gyara ko shima sai nace ki gyara " wani takaici ne ya rufe Farida "wannan cin fuskar har ina dama ya Sadiq zai iya yi mata irin wannan wulakanci" duk cikin xuciyarta take wannan tunanin, afili ta ce masa, " to " da sanyin jiki ta k'arasa bakin gadon Saudat tanayi tana hawaye, tana cikin gyarawa taji Saudat na kwalawa Sadiq kira, Farida runtse ido tayi tana karajin wani zafi azuciyarta sannu ahankali ta fara karanta innalillahi wa'inna'ilahirraji'um.
      Jiki na rawa Sadiq ya tura toilet din yana tambayar Saudat me take buk'ata, Agurguje Farida tayi ta k'arasa gyaran gadon ta fice daga sashen Saudat tana zubda kwallah, tana shiga bedroom dinta ta fad'a kan gado ta fashe da kuka me cin zuciya, sai da tayi me isarta sannan ta fad'a toilet tayi wanka tana fitowa mai ta shafa ta koma kan gado bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.
        Farida ba ita ta tashi ba wajen karfe tara na safe agurguje ta fita dan ta nemar musu abunda zasu karya, tana shiga falo taga su Asabe sun gyara ko ina gaida sukayi Asabe tace, " hajiya zan iya shiga ingyara cikin d'akin?" Murmushi farida tayi mata tace, " a'a zan dinga gyara bedroom, falo kawai zaki dinga gyaramun da sauran abubuwan da ba'a rasa ba, wai kuwa kunci abinci?" Ta bata amsa da, " a'a hajiya bamuci ba, ba'a nuna mana kitchen ba kuma bansan me kike da buk'ata" ok, bari naje ina zuwa, nidai banda girki zaki dinga mun ni zan dinga girkina bari na k'arasa " cewar Farida, agurguje take aikin gaba daya ta manta da su Asabe sai da ta fito falo ta gansu, kafin wani lokaci tuni ta gama duk abinda zata yi musu, Asabe ta kwalawa kira da sauri taje ta zube agabanta Abincinsu ta bata sannan ta kira lami ta sata ta jera mata su akan dianing, daki ta wuce taje ta fesa wanka ta fito doguwar rigar atamfa tasa ba karamin kyau tayi ba, falo ta koma ta zauna tana jiran shigowar su Saudat ta kusa minti goma Azaune sannan taji shigowarsu, da murmushi take kallansu har suka iso falon, cikin sakin fuska tace, " Amarsu ta ango ay yanxu nake zancenku araina, har ina shirin kiranku ince naji shiru ko Amarcin ne baku gama ci ba? " Ba kunya saudat dake mak'ale da Sadiq tace, " haba tawan wane irin Amarci har safiya, bayan tun d'azu na koroshi gurinki yak'i " wani abu ne ya d'an soki Farida azuciya take fad'in, " wato har gurina tace ya taho yak'i lallai ya Sadiq ya dauka da zafi " amma afili sai tayi murmushi tace, " cab a'a na yafe nan da cen din ay duk d'aya ne " Saudat tayi tunanin Farida zataji haushi amma sai taga ko ajinki, Farida ce ta cewa Sadiq, " ya Sadiq ina kwana? " Ko kallan wajen da take baiyi ba, Maimaitawa Farida ta k'arayi amma abanza, Saudat ce tace, " Ya Sadiq tana gaishe ka fa ka amsa mana " kallan Saudat yayi cikin so da kauna yace, " kinmanta nace miki banasan duk wani abu daga gurinta amma idan kince na amsa shikenan sai na amsa din " wani sanyin dad'i Saudat taji afili kuma tace, " a'a Ba'ayi haka ba ka amsa mana, wai ko wani abun Farida tayi maka ne?" Watsawa farida harara Sadiq yayi sannan yace, " ba abinda tayi mun kawai dai natsane ta ne, banasanta banasan ganinta " kusan lokaci d'aya hawaye ya zubo daga idanun Farida da karasa maganar Sadiq, kafeshi tayi da kallo kunnuwanta nayi mata amsa kuwwa jin maganar tayi tana k'ara amo acikin kunnenta, da ace wani ne ya fad'a zatayi mugun k'aryata maganar da taji amma abun ma mamaki ya bata  cikin k'arfin hali tace, " Ya Sadiq kasan me kake fad'a kuwa? Ni ce baka san gani? Da bakinka fa kake cewa ka tsaneni, nice fa faridanka " harara ya k'ara watsa mata cikin jin haushinta yace, " karki kuma cewa ke farida ta ce, inba haka ba ranki sai yayi mummunan b'aci sai me dan nace na tsaneki na fad'a natsaneki natsaneki " hawaye bibbiyu ne ke zuba a idanun Farida cikin kuka tace, " ya Sadiq kana cikin hayyacinka kuwa......." Wawan marin daya zabga mata ne yasa sauran maganar tata makalewa, wannan karan mamaki had'e da tsoron Sadiq ne ya kamata, rik'e kumatu tayi ta ma kasa yi masa magana, Saudat dake tsaye har murmushin gefen baki tayi saboda farin ciki amma sai ta wayance cikin kissa tace," Ya Sadiq meye haka dan Allah me yayi zafi haka harda mari? " Komawa tayi b'angaren Farida ta janyota ta rungumeta tana lallashinta, sai awannan lokacin Farida ta samu ta rushe da wani irin kuka me tsuma zuciyar me sauraro, shi kansa Sadiq sai yaji ba dadi amma yana kallanta yaji ransa ya k'ara b'aci, janye Saudat yayi yace, " muje muyi breakfast dear ki rabuda wannan wawuyar yarinyar" ba kunya Saudat tabi sadiq sai da ya fara zaunar da Saudat akujerarta sannan shima ya zauna, ganin haka ba k'aramin b'atawa Farida rai yayi ba da gudu ta wuce part dinta tana shiga d'akinta ta fad'a kan gado tana kuma fashewa da wani sabon  kukan.
         Sai da tayi me isarta  ta tashi ta koma falo lokacin da ta shiga tuni sun gama cin abincinsu, ruwan tea kawai ta iya sha ta wuce zata koma part dinta, Asabe ta gani yanayinta duk ya canja asanyaye tace, " hajiya me za'ayi yanxu? " Girgiza mata kai Farida tayi murya adashe tace, " ki gyara falo kiyi wanke-wanke " daga haka bata kara ce mata komai ba ta wuce d'akinta, tana gama shan tea ta kwanta kan gado ta kwanta tana tunanin rayuwa, afili ta fara magana ita kadai saboda abunda Sadiq yayi mata ya matuk'ar girgiza ta, " dama haka soyayya take juyewa kiyayyah ko dama haka mazan suke, me nayi maka ya Sadiq me yasa ka canja mun? " Wata zuciyarce tace, " me zai hana kije ki sameshi kuyi magana keda shi ko akwai laifin da kikayi masa " wannan shawarar ta d'auka amma ta ce bari ta bari sai zuwa dare lokacin tasan ya huce, da wannan shawarar Farida taji d'an dama-dama har damuwarta ta ragu.

       Kimanin karfe tara na dare Farida ce tayi kwalliyarta cikin kananan kaya riga da wando ba karamin kyau tayi ba sai zuba kamshi take, part din Sadiq ta nufa tana tafe kamshi na tashi kofar falon sa ta tura da alama yana cikin bedroom, shiga tayi kanta tsaye da sallama, fitowarsa kenan daga toilet da murmushi ta tareshi, yana ganin k'arasowar Farida ya kauce ya wuce ta gefenta yaje gefen gado ya zauna, ita dinma binsa tayi ta tsugunna wajen k'afafuwansa tace, " Ya sadiq dan Allah idan wani abun nayi maka kayi hakuri ka yafe mun, ya sadiq bansan ka da haka ba idan wani abun nayi maka ka gayamun? " Tunda ta fara magana yake kallanta ba k'aramin kyau tayi masa ba amma gaba daya sai ya ringa jin haushinta amma daga baya sai kuma yaji kamar baya kyautata mata, jin yayi shiru ne yasa tace, " dan Allah karka ce baka hakura ba ya sadiq, wallahi kana wahalar da zuciya wannan fushin da kakeyi dani" adak'ile yace mata, " ba abinda kika mun kuma ki tashi ki fice karki k'ara shigowa part din nan idan bani na kiraki ba, oya get out " ya k'arasa maganar yana nunawa Farida k'ofa.
        Tashi Farida tayi kamar wacce kwai ya fashewa aciki tana tafe tana kwallah, taje fita taci karo da Saudat ta sako wasu tsinannun kayan bacci tana ganin Farida tace, " tawan lafiya naganki kin fito haka?" Farida shanye hawayen tayi tana sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tace, " ba komai" tab'e baki Saudat tayi tace, " to inkin fita kija mana k'ofar pls dan inajin nida fitowa sai zuwa safiya " bata jira jin ta bakin farida ba ta tura kofar bedroom din Sadiq tana fadin, " baaaabbbbyyyy " galala Farida tabi Saudat da idanu karo na farko da taji saudat ta fara bata haushi, jiki ba kwari ta koma d'akinta da tunani iri-ir, nitsawa tayi cikin kogin tunani, tana mamakin yaushe soyayya me k'arfi ta shiga tsakanin Saudat da Sadiq haka wata zuciyar tace  mata kika sani ko dama sun dad'e suna soyayyah aboye kece baki sani ba afili ta furta, "  dama akwai ranar da Ya Sadiq zai iya juyamun baya, amma tunda yasan baya sona me yasa ya yaudareni da soyayyarsa, me yasa ya koyamun kaunarsa me yasa zaimun haka me yasa ya Sadiq?" Ta k'arasa maganar tana k'ara fashewa da kuka me sauti, sai da jima tana kuka sannan ta shiga toilet ta dauro alwala tayi sallah ta mik'a buk'atunta gurun ubangiji, zazzabi ne ya rufeta ta tashi ta koma kan gado ta janyo bargo ta rufa, kusan kwana tayi da zazzabi ajikinta sai gabannin asuba ta samu ya sauka.

        Da safe Farida bata samu ta farka da wuri ba sai wajen sha daya saboda ramakon baccin da tayi, tana fitowa su lami suka zube kasa suna gaisheta cikin sakin fuska take amsawa, tambayarsu tayi ko sun karya Asabe ce ta bata amsa sannan tace, " ay  muna gama cin abinci su hajiya suka zo suka tambayi nasu bayan sun duba cen babu sai mukace baki fito ba tukunna shine suka fita, sun d'auka har da nasu muka girka" d'an jim Farida tayi sannan tace, " ok, yau na makara ne ban tashi da wuri ba, amma bari nayi maza na kammala lunch kafin su dawo " kitchen Farida ta shiga ta na mamakin gurin da su Saudat suka tafi, amma gudun karta sawa ranta damuwa yasa ta kauda tunanin ta cigaba da aikinta, sai da ta kammala sannan ta yi nata breakfast din tuni yunwa har ta fara cinta.
           Bayan ta gama cin abincin zama tayi tana game awayarta tana nan zaune taji shigowar su Sadiq da wasu ledoji manya ya ruk'o ahannunsa suna tafe suna hirar masoya har shigo cikin falon tun daga get Farida take jiyo dariyarsu, sannu da zuwa Farida tayi musu ciki-ciki ya amsa Saudat ta amsa mata da murmushi ta na mik'a mata tata ledar, karb'a tayi ta yi musu godiya, akan idanta suka wuce suka barta zaune afalo, ita dinma part dinta ta wuce saboda haushi ko ledar da aka bata ba ta bude ba ta wurgata kan gado ta koma gefe tana zuba uban tagumi.

        Tun daga ranar Farida bata k'ara koda tunanin zuwa gurin Sadiq ba musamman idan ta tuna da gargad'insa sai dai ta zauna afalo idan ya fito ta gaisheshi watan ya amsa wataran yayi banza da ita, lamarin Saudat da Sadiq ta zubawa sarautar Allah ido tana mamakin ko kunyarta basaji yanda suke nunawa junansu soyayya, idan tasa baki sadiq ya gwaleta ganin baya buk'atar ta acikin lamarinsu yasa take jan bakinta ta tsuke, idan abun yayi mata ciwo sai dai ta shiga d'aki taci kukan ta ta mo re ta kuma mik'a kukanta gurin ubangiji, cikin 'yan kwanaki k'alilan duk tabi ta rame ta lalace ko abinci bataci na kirki, duk wanda ya ganta sai ya fahimci irin ramar da tayi duk tayi k'ashin wuya saboda damuwa wuni take ta kwana da ciwon kai, Farida haka ta dinga rayuwar kunci da rashin 'yanci har suka shafe wata biyu ahaka, wata rana Farida na zaune afalo tana jiran shigowar su Sadiq falon, bayan sun shigo Farida gaida Sadiq yayi tayiwa Saudat barka da safiya, sama-sama Saudat ke bata amsa tamabayar Sadiq tayi tanasan zuwa taga su umma tunda tun bayan dawowarta bata k'ara zuwa gida ba, adakile yace sai ta dawo, murna gurin Farida bata misaltuwa agurguje ta shirya ta fito tana fito wa Sadiq ya zaro kud'in mota ya mik'awa Saudata ita ta bawa farida, sallama tayi musu ta wuce gida cikin zumud'i.

     Siyayayya kala-kala tayiwa su umma da yake ta taho da sauran kud'inta, Da sallama tashiga gidan, umma ce ta taso da sauri ta rungumo Farida tana mata sannun  zuwa cikin farin ciki, akunyace Farida ta gaida su suka shiga tambayarta ya me gidan da kuma kishiyarta, ce musu take duk lafiya klau sunce agaishe ku, cikin jin dad'i suka shiga hira tsarabarsu Farida ta mik'a musu sukayi ta godiya da sa albarka.
        Lokacin da rana ta fara d'agawa girki umma take ta kiciniyar dorawa miya ta fara d'orawa tafasowar farko da zatayi Farida taji warin miyar ya hautsina mata ciki da gudu ta tafi bakin rariya tana kwara amai, amai take sosai kamar zata amayar da abin cikinta sai da ta gama ta koma ta zauna su inna suke mata sannu cikin zuciyarsu wani farin ciki ne ya lullub'esu gaba d'aya, warin miyar ne yake kara damun Farida tacewa umma, " dan Allah umma arufe kitchen din nan wallahi miyar nan warinta ba dad'i" murmushin manya umma tayi tace, " da miya zanyi amma me kike san ci na dafa miki? " Jim kadan Farida tayi sannan tacewa umma, " dan wake naso da manja " 

   _Farida kodai-kodai_🙊
