*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                51&52


Umma cikin jin dad'i ta tashi ta hau yiwa farida d'an wake kafin wani lokaci tuni ta gama, me yawan gaske ta Zubawa farida zagewa tayi tana ci cikin jin dad'i kamar wacce ta samu nama, sai da ta kusa cinyewa sannan ta ture kwanon gefe, adaddafe ta wanke hannu ta d'auro alwala ta gabatar da sallar azahar throw pillows din kujerar umma ta janyo akan daddumar ta b'ingire sai bacci, inna da umma gaba d'aya farin ciki ne ya mamaye zuciyarsu saboda tun zuwan farida suka fahimci wani abu daga gareta, ta jima tana bacci sai da ta shafe kusan awa biyu tana bacci, sai da aka idar da sallar la'asar sannan ta farka, alwala ta gabatar sannan ta zauna suka dasa sabuwar hira dasu umma, cikin hirar ne umma take tambayar Farida cikin damuwa, " Farida ina fatan babu wata damuwa acikin gidan naku saboda naga kin rame duk kin zabge inda wani abu ki gayamun?" Murmushi farida tayi tacewa umma, " lah umma gani kikayi na rame? Ba abinda yake damuna kwanaki ne dai kawai na d'anyi zazzabi amma ba wata matsala wallahi " jinjina kai umma tayi tace, " nina d'auka ma gaba daya zakuxo har Saudat din da shi Sadiq ko ya koma aiki ne? " Da farko d'an rasa amsar da zata bawa umma tayi dabara ce ta fad'o mata tayi sauri tace, " Eh Ya Sadiq tun sati biyu daya wuce ya koma aiki amma Saudat ita da tare zamu zo ta tashi da zazzabi kinsan zazzabin nan da cin k'arfin mutum amma ay tace mun zata zo inta warware" d'ari bisa d'ari su umma sun yarda da maganar Farida Inna ce tace, " ayyah amma duk cikinku ba wanda ya fad'a ma ayda kota waya mun dubaku, amma dai shi sadiq lafiyarsa kalau ko? " Ganin sun yarda da maganarta yasa cikin jindad'i tace, " eh shi lafiyarsa klau nidinma na warware Saudat ce kawai ta tashi dashi yau " gaba daya su Inna suka ce, " To Allah kara afuwa " Farida ta amsa da, " Aameen ".

      Wajen karfe biyar na yamma Farida tayiwa su Inna sallama ta tafi haka suka cikata da daddawa kuka busashiyar kubewa, gyadar miya da dai sauran kayam miya kowanne suka bata sai sun had'a dana Saudat, tun ahanya Farida ta kira Asabe ta sata nik'a mata kayan miya da yake ta bata wata 'yar k'aramar wayar tecno dinta, lokacin data k'arasa tuni Asabe tayi mata duk abinda ta umarceta saboda Asabe akwai biyayya ga hankali shiyasa Farida ta d'auketa tamkar kanwarta duk wani abu da tasan zai b'ata ran Farida tana guje masa shiyasa Lami take jin dama itace tayi dacen uwar d'aki ba kamar Saudat da ko maganar arziki bata shiga tsakaninsu da Lami ba, shiyasa idan ta faki idon Saudat take gudowa b'angaren Farida dan tafi samun walwala acen.
        Lokacin da farida ta koma gida ba kowa afalon jakarta kawai ta ajiye da mayafi ta shiga kitchen tana aiki, sha'awar tuwon shinkafa taji da miyar karkashi ita da asabe suke girkin sai bayan magriba suka kammala, part dinta ta wuce taje watsa ruwa sannan ta d'auro alwala tayi sallah magriba ta jima akan dadduma sannan ta fito sanye da doguwar rigar bacci mara nauyi, kamar yanda ta saba ta jera komai akan dianing plate ta d'auka ta zubo nata ta koma kan kujera tanaci tana kallo saboda koda ta Zauna cin abincin Sadiq tashi yake yace sai ta gama ci ta tashi zai zauna, ganin haka yasa ta janye jikinta zata girka musu abincin ta jera musu amma sai ta d'auki plate ta zuba nata,  motsin shigowarsu taji da alama suma fita unguwa sukayi ciki-ciki Sadiq yayi sallama ganin Farida zaune a falon Saudat kuwa ko kallan k'urar ta batayi ba asanyaye tace, " sannunku da zuwa " ba wanda ya kulata gaba d'aya mamaki suke bata acikin su wa zatayi zatan zai iya yi mata haka Saudat ko Ya Sadiq cikin zuciyarta take fad'in, " anya kunyi mun adalci kuwa, Saudat dama akwai ranar da zaku iya juyamun baya " kwallace taji na neman zubo mata, had'iyeta tayi sannan taci gaba da cin abincinta.
           Tashi tayi ta d'auki jakarta sannan ta wuce part dinta tsarabar Saudat ta tuna da ita kai tsaye part din ta ta nufa, kofar falonta ta bud'e taga bata nan lami ta gani ta zata fita daga falon, tambayarta tayi ina Saudat tace mata tana cikin bedroom, tana bata amsa ta fice, zama Farida tayi kan kujera tana jiran fitowar Saudat, tana nan zaune bayan wani lokaci taji Saudat na waya kamar haka, "  mommy nifa banasan samun ciki yanxu kuma kinsan dai Sadiq sai yanda nayi dashi bazai wani nemi haihuwa ba tunda yanxu komai yakeyi ak'arkashin ikona yakeyi, wallahi masu aikin gidan nan sunfi farida daraja agurinsa, ko aiki fa sai da nace ya koma sannan ya amince wallahi ni yanxu babban damuwata mahaifiyarsa karkiga yanda yake jin maganarta na tabbata ma da tasan zaman da Farida take acikin gidan nan ta d'auki mataki akansa, to shikenan umma hakan za'ayi yauwa mommy na shiyasa nake k'ara sanki wallahi, to d'an kashe mommy ana kirana wata wayar ta shigo zan kiraki anjima " Farida dake zaune daskarewa tayi saboda mamaki k'irjinta har bugawa yakeyi saboda tashin hankali, afili ta furta, " innalillahi wa'innailahirraji'un " hawaye ne ya fara zubowa daga idanta Farida jikinta har karkarawa yake saboda tsoro da tashin hankali, tana nan zaune taji fitowar Saudat tana tafe tana waya, " nifa dad'i na dakai jaraba haba K-boy yanxu sati ukun da kayi shine kake cewa kayi hakuri? Gaskiya nikam yau bazan fito ba dan bamu dad'e da dawowa gida nida mijina ba kai nifa manage kawai nakeyi dakai amma mijina yafika komai wallahi, kabari zan nemar mana mafita amma yau dai kayi hakuri.........." Fitowar da tayi taga Farida nazaune tana hawaye ne yasa ta katse wayar batare da ta k'arasa ba, cikin mamaki tace, " Ke me ya kawo ki falona? Hala kinji wayar da nayi yanxu ne?  "
         D'agowa Farida tayi cikin kwallah tace, " Saudat yaushe kika canja yaushe kika sake hali? Ni ce fa Farida me nayi miki kika rabani da mijina waya canja minke, bansanki da mugun hali ba Saudat " wata dariyar rainin hankali Saudat tayi har tana rik'e ciki sai da tayi me isarta sannan ta tsuke fuska ta cewa Farida, " tambayata kike yaushe na canja? To ay dama cen kece baki fahimci ko ni wacece ba to yau zan fayyace miki duk abinda baki sani ba watakila zai miki rana kila ki fahimci matsayinki acikin gidan nan "  Farida zama tayi kamar mutum mutumi haka  ta koma banda hawaye ba abinda take yi, Saudat ce ta cigaba da magana, " Farida tun ranar dana fara ganinki naji na tsaneki saboda kyawunki aganin ke ba 'yar kowan kowa ba amma kike da irin kyawun da koda mace ta kalleki sai ta k'ara kallanki hakan ne yasa na kulla k'awance dake domin insan ta yanda zan bullo miki dan inlalata kyawun naki, nayi k'okarin ganin na turo miki samarin banza 'yan yaudara amma duk babu wanda yayi nasara akanki saboda kinada kamewa da rik'e mutumcinki, dana fuskanci haka sai nima na b'oye tawa alak'ar da nake nazo miki ta irin sigarki, na bullo miki ta b'angaren malamanmu na makaranta saboda malamai biyu na had'a baki dasu su nemeki idan kika k'i amincewa suyi ta kawo miki matsala har ki yarda ki amince tunda nasan ke 'yar talakawa ce, abinda ya kawon cikas Dean  yana mutuwar kaunarki kuma da aure yake sanki gudun tonuwar asirinsu yasa suka bijirewa buk'ata saboda dama samari na ne mun dad'e muna harka dasu, kwatsam sai na fahimci soyayyarku keda yayanki da farko na d'auki abun shirme saboda ina ganin talaka bai isheni kallo ba bare nabi ta kansa ba, sai da kika nunamun hotonsa naga kyawunsa lokaci d'aya naji ya kwanta azuciyata atake kuma naji na kara jin tsanarki azuciyata, wutar soyayyar Sadiq haka ta cigaba da ruruwa araina har lokacin da ya dawo yazo school gurinki nayi arba dashi ido da ido lokacin ne naji kamar na kasheki dan insamu inmallaki Sadiq ni kadai, duk lokacin da naji kuna hira da sadiq inajin bak'in ciki musamman irin kulawar da yake nuna miki, haka na kauda kaina ina tunanin irin hukuncin da zan yanke akanki, na shiga jikinki ne Farida saboda koda na yi miki wani mummunan Abu babu wanda zai yi tunanin ni ce ko ya zargeni, ina cikin yanke hukuncin kashe ki sai wani tsohon saurayi na me suna MD guy ya ga hotonki awayata yace mun yana sanki amma fa shi bada aure yake sanki ba so yake ki zama yarinyarsa kuna fita k'asashen waje, ba k'aramin dad'i naji ba saboda nasamu damar da zaku rabu keda Sadiq, amma sai na hanashi bayyana gareki saboda nasan sarai bazaki amince ba sai nace masa yabani dama akwai plan din da nake shiryawa, da ya d'auka yawo nake masa da hankali har ya yaje gidanku idan baki mantaba wani ya tab'a turo muku da mak'udan kud'ade gidanku kuka maida yaron ganin zai b'ata rawarsa da tsalle yasa ya dawo dai gurina yana neman sanin plan din da nakeyi akanki, fayyace masa soyayyar Sadiq da alak'ar da ke tsakaninku, ba k'aramin dad'i  MD guy yaji ba shiyasa ya ke k'ara taimaka mun da wasu shawarwarin, ba karamin tashin hankali yaji ba da aurenku ya matso da Sadiq yaji ba haka ya hura mun wuta sai da nasa boka ya rufe bakinsa, kinsan meyasa ban amince na had'aku da MD guy ba tuntuni? " Saudat ta k'arasa fad'a tana kallan Farida da ta jik'e fuskarta da hawaye, ido Farida ta bita dashi Saudat bata damu ba ta cigaba da yiwa Farida magana.


 
      " Saboda boka yace mun bazan tab'a auren Sadiq ba indai ba'a daura muku aure ba, hakan ne yasa na nasa aka rufe bakin MD guy bai k'ara mun maganarki ba, nasan zaki ce akan biyan buk'atata nake kashe miki wad'annan mak'udan kud'in ko? " Murmushi Saudat tayi tana kallan Farida sannan tace, " 'yan mata gwara ki daina wannan hawayen naki tun lokacin hawayen baizo miki ba tunda yanxu zamu fara wasan" ta k'arasa maganar tana kashewa Farida ido d'aya sannan ta cigaba da magana, " mafi yawan manyan kud'aden da nake kashe miki ba kud'in daddy bane MD guy ne yake bayarda dasu acewarsa yana jiran ranar da zai fanshe kudinsa ne, idan ma na karb'a agurun daddy ina sanin ta yanda nake maida masa kud'insa dabarata anan dan karki tuhumeni gurun da nake samun kud'in inrasa abunda zan ce miki, kuma nasan idan nayi k'arya da daddy wataran asirina zai tonu tunda nasan wata kyautar dole keda iyayenki kuce zaku je yiwa daddy godiya kinga kuwa idan baisan maganar ba zai tuhumeni inda nake samun mak'udan kudi duk da daddy bayasan b'acin raina amma nasan akan wannan zamu iya samun matsala dashi, ke Abinda baki sani ba kudin aikin mahaifiyarki da zuwanta India har sukaje suka dawo MD guy ne ya biya azahiri ne daddy ya biya amma a bad'ini MD ne, abinda baki sani ba har umarar da muka tab'a zuwa dasu daddy da ke duk da Kudin MD ne sannu ahankali nake maidawa daddy kudinsa cikin dabara yanda bazai ganoni ko ya fahimce ni ba "  kyalkyalewa Saudat tayi da wata shu'umar dariya har tana fad'awa kan kujera, Farida bata san sanda k'afafuwanta sukayi sanyi ba rijib ta fad'a kujera ta baya gaba d'aya jikinta rawa yakeyi tsoron Saudat ne ya cikata wannan karan babu hawaye afuskar ta sai ido da take bin Saudat dashi.


    Gyara zamanta Saudat tayi ta d'ora kafa d'aya kan d'aya ta cewa Farida, " Kinsan me yasa na asirce Sadiq nake juyashi kamar waina? Saboda Alkawari nayiwa MD koda kin Auri sadiq zan bashi ke amatsayin cikakkiyar budurwa, kuma nasan muddin na bar Sadiq  ayanda yake masifar sanki babu abinda zai hana faruwar komai atsakaninku, Tabbas kin taimaka mun na auri mijinki saboda rasa budurci da nayi kuma naci amarci na ba kunya bare wulak'ancin d'a namiji saboda nasan da ban dan haka da nasha gori fiye da tunani na kila ma ya rabu dani, nayi harka da samari iri-iri sai dai babu wanda ya tab'a sanina amatsayin mace sai dai muna romancing da abubuwan da ba'a rasa ba muna cikin haka har k'addarar fyade ta fad'amun wanda yazo mun alokacin da kaina ya kulle na rasa hanyar wargaza aurenki, shiyasa  na gaya miki duk abinda ya faru dani har kikamun tayin auren Sadiq, yarinya ma kima daina tunanin zakiyi rayuwar aure me tsayi keda Sadiq saboda MD na gabda zuwa ya karb'i ajiyarsa, abinda yasa har kikaga kin shafe watanni agidan nan to ya d'anyi rashin lafiya ne yana india, na tabbata yana warwarewa zai duro kasar nan kuma America zai wuce dake "


     _Lallai mugu baida kama_🤣

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  53&54



           Cikin fad'uwar gaba Farida ta d'ago da idanta tana kallan Saudat, bakinta gaba d'aya yayi nauyi tama rasa bakin magana banda hawaye ba abinda take yi, gaba d'aya tsoron Saudat ne ya kamata kallan ta take kamar sabuwar hallita, gaba d'aya Saudat mamaki take bata, kallan tsaf Saudat ta k'arewa Farida sannan tace, " Ke Farida tun muna mu biyu nida ke kiyi saurin zuwa d'akin Sadiq ki nemi takardar saki tun banyi miki ta k'arfi da yaji ba, kuma kinsan me nasan duk wanda zakije ki fad'awa saidai ki fad'a masa amma ba duka bane zasu yarda ke ko sun yarda yardar tasu ta banza ce, tunda lokacin tafiyarku nayi sai dai aneme ki arasa MD guy yayi gaba dake, tashi ki fice mun daga d'aki idan ba haka ba kuma ranki yayi mugun b'aci" Saudat ta k'arasa maganar tana nunawa Farida hanya.
       Kamar wacce bata da laka ajiki haka Farida ta yunkura ta tashi da kyar tana tafiya tana waigen Saudat da kallan mamaki har ta k'arasa part dinta, tana shiga zama tayi bakin gado ta zauna ta rafka uban tagumi, rasa abunyi tayi saboda yanda taji zuciyarta take mata zafi da k'una, jin maganganun Saudat tayi kamar amafarki sai yanxu ta gano dalilin da yasa Sadiq yake hantararta da kyararta ashe duk Aikin asirine, toilet ta wuce Kai tsaye alwala ta d'auro ta shinfid'a dadduma tana sallah cikin sujjada tana Kai kukanta ga ubangiji, ta shafe lokaci me tsayi tana jero Addu'o'i da neman d'auki gurun ubangiji, tana idarwa Kan gado ta koma ta kwanta tana tunanin rayuwar su ta baya ita da saudat, gaba daya ganin komai take kamar amafarki wai yanxu Saudat tata ita ce ta b'ullo Mata da wannan bakon halin nata? Gyara kwanciyar ta tayi afili ta furta, " lallai nayi wawta da tun farko na Baki yarda d'ari bisa d'ari Saudat, bansan zaki cutar Dani Haka ba nayi Miki hallaci Amma Kika nuna mun *TSANTSAR BUTULCI* irin na D'an Adam na zauna dake zuciya d'aya Ashe ke da mugun nufi kike zaune Dani, Anya kinyi mun Adalci Saudat kin raba ni da farin ciki na kin rabani da Ya Sadiq, yanxu kina neman ki rabani da kowa ma gaba d'aya kin ci Amana ta Saudat, idan na cire ya Sadiq kece mutum ta farko da na d'auki dukkan yarda ta na d'ank'a mata wacce ko mahaifiyata batasan wasu al'amuran nawa kamar yanda Kika sani ba, me yasa Saudat me nayi Miki me na tsare Miki me yasa Kika shiga rayuwata Dan ki cutar Dani? " Sai alokacin ne farida ta samu ta fashe da kuka me cin zuciya, ta jima ahaka tana yi sai da tayi me isarta sannan ta shiga toilet ta wanke fuskarta ta koma Kan gado ta kwanta, zazzabi ne ya rufeta da ciwon Kai me zafi waya ta d'auka ta Kira Asabe tace Mata ta had'o Mata tea, cikin lokaci k'ank'ani Asabe tayi nocking Farida ta Bata iznin shiga, ganin yanayin da farida take ciki ne ya tabbatar wa da Asabe ba lafiya take ba, sai rawar d'ari take jikinta zafi ram kamar wuta, ahankali Asabe ta ajiye wa Farida kofin tea din har ta juya zata fita ta dawo take tambayar farida, " Aunty bakya jin dad'i ne ?" D'aga mata kai farida tayi bakinta na karkawa, dawowa Asabe tayi tace, " Sannu Aunty Allah Baki lafiya, me kike da buk'ata yanxu? " Girgiza mata kai tayi cikin rawar d'ari ta cewa Asabe, " ba abinda nake da buk'ata " dan Jim Asabe tayi duk yanayinta ya sauya cikin fuskar damuwa tace, " To shikenan Aunty idan akwai abinda kike buk'ata sai kimun magana " d'aga Mata Kai Farida tayi sannan Asabe ta wuce.
 
     Ruwan tea din ta Sha sannan ta d'auko Paracetamol Tasha ta ja bargo ta kwanta, ta jima a haka sai zuwa wani lokaci bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

     Washegari ma haka ta tashi da zazzabi sai dai da sauk'i Bai Kai na Daren ba, hakan ne yasa ko girki ta kasa d'ora Musu sai su Asabe ta sa suka girka breakfast, da safen ma tea kawai ta iya sha saboda yanda takejin tashin zuciya ko kamshin abinci a dole take shak'ar sa idan ta fita falo Shiyasa ma take kauracewa fita wajen, cikin abinda baifi sati guda ba gaba Daya damuwa ta sa Farida agaba duk ta rame ta lalace ba abinda take iya sha sai tea sai kunun tsamiya, duk Wanda yasan farida ada yaganta ayanxu dole ya nemi sanin halin da take ciki, duk ta rame ta yi wani irin fari ta d'ashe sai Kashin wuya da tayi, kullin ta zauna maganganu Saudat ne suke Mata yawo har lokacin ganin abun take kamar almara, tun Daga ranar ta K'ara janye jikinta daga Saudat Sallama ce kawai take had'asu itama saidai Farida tayi, sai Kuma idan Saudat taso tsokaranarta zata takalota da fad'a ita kuma Farida lura da Haka sai ta fita sabgar ta  Koda ta tsokanota sai tayi kamar bataji ta ba, wani lokacin idan Farida  taje d'aukan wani abun a kitchen suka had'u ta dinga Mata gori tana fad'an maganganu San ranta, idan abun yayiwa Farida ciwo sai dai ta shiga d'aki tayi kuka San ranta ta goge hawaye, ko ta d'auro alwala tayi nafilfilu tayi ta Kai kukanta gurun ubangiji.
      Wata ranar weekend Saudat da Sadiq na zaune afalo suna hira, Farida ranar sha'awar wainar fulawa ta tashi da ita tana fitowa falon Saudat ta watsa Mata harara tana jan tsaki, Sadiq kawai ta gaida shi dinma Bai amsa ba, kitchen ta wuce tana kwaba waina ta Fara soyawa tana gabda k'arasawa saura ta karshe saudat kawai ta wuce kitchen din ko kallan Farida batayi ba ta ta sa spoon ta d'auke ta cikin kaskon ta d'ora ta akan sauran ta farantin tayo waje abinta, kujera ta koma ta zauna ta Dora kafa d'aya Kan d'aya tana kwalawa Lami Kira da sauri ta k'araso ta durk'usa gaban Saudat, mik'a Mata kafa tayi alamar ta mammatsa Mata Lami na ganin Haka ta gane me Saudat take nufi zama tayi tana matsawa Saudat k'afa.
    Farida dake kitchen kululun takaici ne ya kamata falon ta shigo fuska atsuke ta farayiwa Saudat magana, " Saudat me yasa Zaki d'auke mun abinci alhalin bake Kika girka ba? " Dariya Saudat tasa sannan tace,  " Abinci? Au tambaya ta kikeyi me yasa na d'auke Miki abinci? Abincin na ubanki ne ko na uwarki, dangin talauci ubanki ne ya kawo Miki ki gayamun cokali nawa uwar taki ta tab'a kawo Miki ne wato kin samu sake ko, anasamun me kyau anaci to ita nai niyyar ci tunda ba mataccen tsohonki ne ya tashi daga kushewa ya kawo ba, wayasani ma ko Y'ar kwararo ce har nake zancen me dattin hula" da sauri Sadiq ya juyo jin kalmar Saudat suna had'a ido saudat tace Masa, " meye kake kallo na karya nayi ba 'yar kwararon bace.....?" Bata K'arasa magana ba Farida ta sauke Mata zafafan mari guda biyu, cikin tsananin b'acin rai Farida tace, " ke karamar Mara kunya ki iya bakinki Mahaifana ba sa'anin yinki bane, ni ce dai-dai dake naji niba 'yar kowan kowa bace Amma kuma duk Abinda mahaifinki yayimun ayba rok'onku nayi ba, bazan zagi iyayenki ba saboda suna da k'ima da mutumci a idona Kuma ni nasan mutumci da girman iyaye, Ina K'ara Gaya Miki ki iya bakinki idan kuma Kika K'ara mummunar magana akan iyayena saina fasa Miki shi " Farida na kaiwa Nan ta ja uban tsaki sannan ta wuce kitchen tana k'ara dama wani kullin wainar.
       Wasu manyan ashar Saudat keta kundumawa azatanta zata ga Sadiq ya d'auki mataki akan Farida sai taga yama janye kansa cen gefe yana Danna waya, azafafe ta juya ta balbale Sadiq da fad'a, " au kana gani ta mareni babu abunda zaka iyayi ko, bazaka d'auki mataki akanta ba kana gani wajen Mari biyu tamun " kallan tsaf Sadiq ya yiwa Saudat cikin zuciyarsa haushinta yaji akan maganganun data fad'awa Farida, shiyasa yace Mata, " To me kike so nayi Mata kefa Kika tsokano Kuma kike zagin iyaye ta Kinga saidai kiyi hakuri kawai " Yana karasa maganar ya tashi ya fice daga gidanma gaba daya.
      Da ido Saudat ta bi sadiq tana mamakin kalamansa afili ta furta, " lallai ka samu kanka Sadiq inanan zaka dawo ka sameni kaima zakamun bayani " tashi tayi taje kofar kitchen din tana cewa Farida, " ke Kuma matsiyaciya wannan Marin da Kika mun Baki ci bulus ba ki saurari abinda zai biyo baya " da Karfi Farida ta juyo tace Mata, " Saidai inga Alheri aniyarki ta biki " Bata Kuma magana ba ta dawo falo ta d'auki wainar fulawa ta zauna tanaci tana lumshe ido saboda dad'i.
     Farida na gama soyawa itama falon ta dawo ta zauna babu me cewa kowa uffan sai harara da Saudat take watsawa Farida lokaci-lokaci, suna Nan zaune wayar Saudat ta Fara ringing da murmushi afuskarta ta d'auka, " mommy na kin wuni lafiya? " Daga cen b'angaren mommy ta amsa, " lafiya klau Ina bakin get dinku naje gidan hajiya Daso dake cen kasan ku nace yau dai Bari na biyo mu gaisa " wani uban tsalle Saudat ta buga ko kashe wayar batayi ba ta nufi hanyar get ta bud'e kofar, mommy na fitowa suka rungume juna suka shigo cikin harabar gidan, ya mutsa fuska mommy tayi tace, " daughter yanxu duk Wanda yaxo haka kuke zuwa bud'e kofa babu get man ? " Murmushi Saudat tayi tace, " eh dama Asatin Nan Sadiq yace yasa anemo Masa me gadi to ankawo Masa wani mutum gaskiya yayi tsufa shine yace anemo matashi " tab'e Baki mommy tayi tace, " yadai fi Kam Amma ace duk bak'on da kikayi ke Zaki xo ki bud'e kofa Ina ma wai yaran Dana turo Muku? " Cikin sigar shagwab'a saudat tace, " mommy nifa ba Wanda na tab'a bud'e kofa sai ke Kuma yaran suna Nan ba laifi Lami nada hankali saidai Asabe ce Naga kamar munafaka ce sai tayita nan-nan da waccen matsiyaciyar " murmushi mommy tayi sannan tace, " kyalesu cen su k'arata su suka sani " ta K'arasa fad'a lokacin da Saudat ta tura kofar falon.

       Da sallama mommy ta shiga cikin falon Farida na ganin mommy da fara'a afuskar ta tashi ta tarbo mommy ta na Mata oyoyo, itama mommy rungumo Faridan tayi tana Mata barka da gida, zama mommy tayi Kan kujera har k'asa Farida ta tsugunna ta gaida ta, cikin sakin fuska mommy ta amsa tana tambayar ta ya megidan, da kanta ta tashi ta d'ebowa Mommy drinks ta kawo Mata tana tambayar ta, " ya daddy"  murmushi mommy tayi tace, " Daddynku na gida baima San na biyo ba Nima unguwa naje nace Bari nazo na ga yarana " Itama Faridan dad'i taji ganin yanda iyayen Saudat suka d'auketa y'a, Saudat ce taja uban tsaki tace, " malama sai ki tashi ki Bamu guri nida mahaifiyata za muyi Hira " Farida Bata kula Saudat ba ta mik'e tsaye tana tambayar mommy, " mommy me za'a dafa Miki? " Rik'e Baki mommy tayi tace, " rufani ki sayani ko awa guda bazan K'ara agidan Nan ba kawai kuma sai ku xubomun abinci inhau ci idan mijinku ya shigo me zance? " Farida tace, " haba mommy to meye Dan kinci abinci yafa fita Kuma ba lallai ya shigo yanxu ba......." Saudat ce ta katseta cikin tsawa, " dallah uwar shishshigi nace ki Bamu guri ko? " Tsuke fuska mommy tayi ta watsawa Saudat d'akuwa sannan ta Fara Mata fad'a, " ke meyake damunki ne wannan wane irin sakarcin banza da wofi ne, kina hauka ne me yake faruwa acikin gidan naku ne, Farida dama irin wannan zaman kukeyi ne Bamu sani ba Amma Sadiq din ya zuba Muku idanu, wani abun ne ya had'aku ne? " Murmushi farida tayi sannan tace, " a'a mommy ba abinda ya had'amu kawai dai rashin fahimta ne muka samu ni tuni ya wuce agurina " harara mommy ta wurgawa Saudat tace," ki shiga hankalinki Saudat banasan fitina da tashin hankali Kar na sake na k'arajin makamancin irin wannan, oya ki Bata hakuri......." Da sauri Saudat ta katse mommy da, " What" ta fad'a tana kallan mommy, kifta Mata ido mommy tayi ba Musu Saudat ta watsawa Farida harara tace, " Kiyi hakuri " Farida ta amsa, " ba komai ya wuce " farida ta fad'a tana wucewa hanyar kitchen da sauri mommy ta dakatar da ita da cewar bazata tsaya cin abinci ba, hakan ne yasa farida ta wuce part d'in ta ta basu guri, farida na wucewa mommy ta tab'o Saudat tace, " ke me yake faruwa ne atsakaninku?" Saudat turo Baki gaba tayi tace, " muje d'aki mommy kiji abinda ya faru ".

        Ba musu mommy tabi bayan Saudat suka wuce part dinta, afalo suka zauna mommy ta k'agu taji me yake faruwa cikin zak'uwa tace, " ke Ina tambayar ki Baki ce mun komai ba? " Tab'e Baki Saudat tayi ta zayyanewa mommy duk Abinda ya faru tsakaninta da farida tun Daga ranar da Farida tajita har zuwa fad'ansu da Marin da Farida tayi Mata.
     Guntun tsaki mommy taja tace, " Saudat kinso ki b'ata rawarki da tsalle Amma Baki makara ba, Kuma daga yau karki K'ara nuna Mata Koda kallan banza ne agaban mutane ke ko agaban Sadiq ne karki Kuma nuna Mata saboda idan kina Haka kowa laifinki zai gani Amma idan bakyayi ko ta fad'a babu Wanda zai yarda tunda kowa yasan irin zaman da kukayi abaya, karki Bari zafin kishi yaci galaba akanki duk abunda zakuyi ku dinga yinsa iya keda ita Amma muddin Kuna gaban wani nuna kinfita kulawa da kanta fiye da ita" murmushi Saudat tayi tace, " kumafa maganarki na hanya mommy Amma d'azu naji haushi da kika sani Bata hakuri " murmushin manya mommy tayi tace, " Hmmmm ta yaro kyau take Bata k'arko, wannan karya dameki da Sannu Zaki fahimci abinda nake nufi " jinjina Kai Saudat tayi tace, " shikenan mommy, ay komai y kusa zuwa karshe tun MD ya kusa dawowa".
     Da mamaki mommy tace, " waye MD kuma? " Sai alokacin Saudat ta tuna mommy batasan plan dinta na MD ba, cikin basar da maganar tace, " wani ne, mommy ya Daddy? " Mommy kallan nutsuwa tayi wa Saudat da damuwa afuskar ta tace, " Saudat kishiyarki Naga ta canja Ina zargin wani Abu daga gareta Anya ba ciki take dashi ba "  yamutsa fuska Saudat tayi tace, " Ciki Kuma mommy bangane ba?" Ciki dai na haihuwa nake nufi yanayinta gaba d'aya ya juya zuwa na me ciki ya kikayi sake Haka ta faru? " Mamaki ne k'arara afuskar Saudat tace, " haba dai mommy kece dai Kika ga Haka Amma Farida Bata da ciki, to tayaya ma hakan zai faru bayan kullin muna tare da Sadiq itafa ko kwana Bata da shi Kuma kullin apart din Sadiq nake kwana bare ince ko Yana tafiya gunta cikin dare " jinjina Kai mommy tayi tace, " Amma Naga duk ta d'ashe ta rame yanayinta ma kamar me laulayi " mommy ta fad'a tana kallan Saudat, " a'a ba wani ciki tension ne yayi Mata yawa Kika ga tayi wannan Ramar Kuma Kinga dama Fara ce dole kiga Haka , tunda sau tari zan ji ta tana kuka idan abun yayi Mata yawa " mommy ta amsa cikin wasi-wa si " to kidai K'ara da ido akanta duk motsinta ki dinga sanar min dashi " Saudat ta amsa da, " To"  wasu kullin magani mommy ta ciro daga jagarta ta mik'awa saudat sannan tace Mata.
      " Wannan da kike ganinsa sunanshi "kinfi uwa Izza" jiya na karb'o Miki agun boka, ki turara da garwashi Kuma ki d'iga wannan turaren ki dinga ambatar sunansa da yake kiranta dashi shima Kuma ki kirashi sau uku, inkin gama sai ki shiga band'aki ki karasa turaren ki turare fuskarki kije gurin sa ke da kanki zakiga canji, dama kin Fara mun complain ya Fara canja Miki alamun aiki ya Fara sanyi to ki gwada wannan Zaki ban labari" murmushi Saudat tayi ta karb'a tana rungume mommy, d'aki ta takai su ta dawo gurin Mommy.

Mommy  Bata jima ba ta fito part d'in Farida ta lek'a tayi Mata sallama, suna fitowa Falo Sukaci karo da Sadiq ya shigo, har k'asa ya tsugunna ya gaida mommy sannan yayi Mata sallama, har kofa Farida ta rakata sannan tayi Mata sallama ta juya cikin gida, Saudat ce ta raka mommy har bakin motarta dake wajen get sai da taga tafiyar mommy ta dawo cikin gida.

    Bayan tafiyar mommy Saudat da sauri ta koma d'aki ta ciro garin maganin da ta kawo Mata, gawayi ta ciro adrower ta irin na cikin kwallin Nan gas taje ta kunna ta sashi Nan da Nan ya kama tana shiga d'aki ta zuba garin maganin aciki ta d'isa wani turare ja da yake cikin wata y'ar kwalba, wani hayak'i ne Mara d'adin kamshi yake tashi cikin sauri ta Fara ambatar sunan Sadiq Dana umma sai da tayi Haka sa uku-uku sannan ta shiga band'aki da baya-baya tanayi tana ambatar sunan Sadiq da umma tana kammalawa ta turare fuskarta da sauran hayak'in ta gogi turaren shima ta shafa afuska kai tsaye tayi part d'in Sadiq.
      Yana zaune Yana Duba wasu takarsu a laptop dinsa ta shiga, tana shiga suna had'a ido ya kafe Saudat da ido kamar zai cinyeta cikin kissa tace, " Dear aiki kake ne? " Murmushi yayi yace, " ehto takarda aka turo Mana zamuje Lagos aikin shekara d'aya zuwa biyu " murnar Saudat Taki boyiwa saboda yanda taji d'adin maganar sa ko shi sai haka afili ta furta, " Masha Allah dear yaushe ne zaka tafi " hararar Wasa ya watsa Mata yace, " au ni kadai zan ma tafi ba tare zamu wuce ba? " Wani d'ad'in ta k'araji tace, " to shikenan sai mu tafin ay Naga ba'amun tayi ba " hancinta yaja yace, " to ki ma Fara had'a kayanki dan jibi Monday zamu wuce " tsallen murna tayi ta rungume Sadiq.
        Hakan ce ta Faru Sadiq da Saudat tun safe suka gama shiri suka fice Kai tsaye Airport suka wuce jirginsu ya d'aga zuwa Lagos, Banda Lami babu Wanda yasan da tafiyarsu acikin gidan, ita dinma Saudat ce ta ce Mata ta koma gurin Asabe da zama, ita Kuma Lami atunaninta Farida ma ta sani duk da sunsan irin zaman da Farida takeyi acikin gidan Amma batayi tunanin har zasuyi tafiya Bata sani ba.
        Bayan kwana biyu da tafiyar su Sadiq Farida ce take mamakin rashin ganin da Bata yiwa Sadiq  haka ma Saudat, wayarsa take ta Kira tun safe Amma no answer sai da ta shafe kwana biyu tana kiransa Bai d'auka ba sai ta yanke shawarar Kiran umma da sunan su gaisa ko zata samu k'arin bayani agurunta, bayan sun gaisa umma take cewa Farida, " Ya Sadiq din da Saudat, naji kwana biyu ma Bai kirani mun gaisa ba inajin Aiki ne yayi Masa yawa ko?" murmushin karfin hali tayi tace, " lafiyarsu kalau, eh ya Sadiq Kam baya zama aiki ya sasu gaba " to shikenan Kya gaishe su muma sati me zuwa zamu leko ku" cikin murna Farida tace, " To Allah ya kaimu umma sai anjima " bayan sunyi sallama farida take mamakin inda Sadiq ya shiga wato ita kanta umma batasan inda yayi ba kenan, afili ta furta, " Allah ka kawo Mana d'auki ".
      Sai da Su Sadiq suka cika kwana goma da tafiya kullin sai Farida ta Kira no answer, a daren ranar ne ta samu ya d'auka da daddare muryarta har rawa takeyi da sallama ta Fara magana, dogon tsaki taji ya ja sannan tace, " lafiya kike damuna tun rannan ?" cikin karfin hali ta ce, " Ya sadiq Ina ka shiga yau kwana da kwanaki kullin na Kira bana samunka? " Guntun tsaki yayi yace, " yaronki ne ni da Zaki tuhumeni inda nake ko haifata kikayi? " Hawaye ne yake zuba afuskar Farida tace, " umm-umm ban haifeka ba Amma umma ma tacemun inta Kira ba'a d'auka " dakata malama banga damar d'auka ba ko ana dole ne? " Cikin tsoro Farida ta cire wayar daga kunnenta tana kallan number da suke magana sannan ta K'ara maida wayar kunne cikin mamaki tace, " Ya Sadiq umma fa nace maka " cikin tsawa yace, " Umaman uwarki ce ko tawa bazan d'auka ba mttsssssswwwww " sai da ja dogon tsaki ya katse wayar.
     Ido Farida ta zurawa wayar tana mamakin kalaman Sadiq afili ta furta, lallai Saudat kin cika shu'uma Amma babu abinda yafi k'arfin Addu'a " tashi tayi ta shiga toilet ta wanke fuskarta ta d'auro alwala tayi sallah ta dad'e tana Addu'o'i sannan ta sallame.
        Bayan wasu kwanaki Farida ce ta tashi da tsananin ciwon ciki da kirji sosai, ganin ciwon nata na K'ara zafi yasa ta Kira Asabe tace Mata ta Kira Mata umma, aykuwa tana Kira ta sanar dasu halin da Farida take ciki, cikin lokaci kalilan umma ta k'araso Kai tsaye Nana Khadija Special hospital aka wuce da ita, Nan da Nan likitoci sukayi caa akanta suna Bata agajin gaggawa bayan wani lokaci bacci ta D'an samu sannan Dr ya nemi gsnin umma Yana Mata bayani, " Sannu hajiya " umma ta amsa da, " yauwa Sannu likita Ina fatan dai babu wata matsala ko " murmushi yayi yace, " kwantar da hankalin ki babu wata matsala Amma Ina me gidanta? " Na kirashi Bai d'auka ba kamun bayani likita duk d'ayane " cewar umma.
     " Yarinyar ku tana da cikin wata hudu da sati biyu, sannan jininta ya hau gaskiya sosai,  damuwa tayi Mata yawa me yasa ? Akwai tambayoyin da nakesan yiwa mijinta ne shiyasa kikaji na tambaye shi " ajiyar zuciya umma ta sauke tace, " wayarsa ce ba'a samu Amma insha Allah zuwa yamma nasan zan sameshi, likita Ina fatan dai hawan jinin be yi tsanani ba? " Glass din idan sa ya gyara yace, " to Alhamdulillah da sauk'i zamu ce Amma gaskiya Bata buk'atar tashin hankali awannan condition din da take ciki, adinga kiyaye duk Abinda zai haifar Mata da damuwa Kuma ki dinga sa ido akan cimarta tunda ba ita kadai bace " 
    " Zamuyi bakin kokarin mu likita mungode " cewar umma.
      Umma tunda ta fita daga office din Dr take Kiran Sadiq shiru Bai d'auka ba har dare, gaba d'aya ran umma  ya gama b'aci kamar da Wasa ta K'ara Kiran number sa taji an d'auka cikin fad'a ta Fara magana, " Sadiq ka rainani ko,  tsawon kwana nawa Ina Kiran ka baka d'auka?" Daga daya b'angaren aka ce, " wai wacece daga Kiran waya zata dinga yiwa mutane jaraba " mamaki ne ya kama umma tace, " Mahaifiyar me wayarce kedin wacece ?"  Dogon tsaki Saudat taja tace, " to sai me Dan kina mahaifiyar me wayar Zaki damu mutane, Saudat ce ko da magana? " Kuuuuuuuuu umma taji wata kuwa adodon kunnenta da mamaki ta ce, " Saudat fa kece kike gayan magana Haka? " Murgud'a Baki Saudat tayi kamar umma na ganinta tace, " idan na Gaya Miki magana zunubi na d'auka ko sab'o, nifa banasan shegen fi'ilin tsiya " Farida na gefe najin duk Abinda ke faruwa tsakanin umma da Saudat ajiyar zuciya umma ta sauke tana Shirin magana taji Sadiq ya karb'i wayar fuska ba walwala sannan tace, " Sadiq dama Haka matarka take  bansani ba laifin ta bane Kai ka Bata fuska, mu aje wannan agefe muna asibiti tun safe da Farida ba lafiya Ina ka shiga na kiraka kazo ba'a d'auka ba? " Sadiq K'ara tsuke fuska yayi yace," Allah ya sawak'e bana kusa ne" to maza kaxo likita nasan ganinka yace da jawabin da zai maka kuma yanata fad'a yace anbar mace me ciki na fama da hawan jini me tsanani sannan yana fad'an  rashin awon da Bata Fara da wuri ba....." Katseta yayi cikin sauri, " Wace me cikin? " Sororo Umma tayi tana mamaki cikin ranta tace, " me yake faruwa da yaran Nan ne Yana nufin baisan tana da ciki ba?" Afili ta ce Masa, " Farida Mana gata muna asibiti har sun K'ara Mata ruwa ma " da mamaki ayanayin Sadiq yace, " ita tace Muku tana da ciki ko likitan? " Umma haushi maganar ta Bata cikin fad'a tace, " Kai banasan shashancin banza duk Abinda kakeyi ka Bari kazo yanxu Ina jiranka " ba ko d'ar Sadiq yace, " gaskiya Umma ni bazan samu damar zuwa ba saboda ni bana garin ma na koma Lagos da aiki zan turo da kud'i kuyi duk Abinda ya dace " ko ta bakin umma Bai tsaya ji ba ya katse wayarsa, bayan wasu mintuna Sadiq ne ya K'ara kiran Umma Bai jira abinda zatace ba yace, " Idan ansallameta sai ku wuce gida da ita" Yana gamawa ta katse wayarsa.

 Umma da mamaki ya kasheta azaune bin wayar tayi da kallo, gudun Kar Farida ta fahimci wani Abu yasa Umma ta wayance da maganar.

          Farida dake kwance Akan gado maganar Umma ce ta tsaya Mata cikin xuciyarta tace, " nidin ce me ciki, ciki fa to ya akayi bansani ba, Anya kuwa likitan Nan ba canjen result yayi ba, ta yaya za'ace ciki har wata hudu awannan shafaffen cikin nawa ?" Sai Kuma ta tuno da yanayin da taga umma ta shiga tabbas tasan Sadiq ba ahayyacin sa yake ba Amma tasan umma Sam bazata fahimci Haka ba sai ahankali, ganin Umma batasan ta gane damuwar da take ciki yasa itama ta nunawa Umma kamar batasan me yake faruwa ba.
         Suna Nan zaune sukaji sallamar Inna ta shigo d'auke da food flasks zama tayi gefen gadon suka gaisa da umma take tambayar ta me jiki, bayan wani lokaci umma fita tayi ta basu guri fitar Umma keda wuya Inna tacewa Farida, " ya k'arfin jikin naki " Farida na kallan mahaifiyar taji kwallah ta ciko Mata ido hawaye ya Fara zuba ahankali tace, " da sauki Inna " Inna har cikin ranta tasan akwai abinda yake damun Farida kuma da alama abun ya dad'e Yana cinta azuciya, saboda irin Ramar da tayi ta lalace baza'ace na laulayi kawai bane Amma afili sai tace Mata, " kukan na meye Farida kefa ba Yarinya bace ko Dan kinganni ne yanxu? To ni idan kina Haka bazaki K'ara gani na ba harsai an sallameki " Goge hawayen fuskarta Farida tayi sannan tace, " na daina kukan Inna " murmushi Inna tayi tace Mata, " yauwa koke fa Farida na ya jikin naki? " Gyara  kwanciyar ta Farida tayi tace, " da sauki sosai Inna aynama warware " to Allah ya K'ara afuwa ya Baki lafiya" cewar Inna, cikin jin dad'i Farida ta amsa da, " Aameen ya rabbi " Yar Hira suka tab'a daga karshe Inna tayiwa Farida sallama zata fito Taki shigowar Umma, bayannta shigo y'ar Hira sukayi da umma sannan Inna tace Musu zata wuce gida xuwa safiya, har bakin get din hospital din Umma ta raka Inna sannan ta dawo gurin Farida, Abincin da Inna ta kawo umma ta zuba Mata baifi cokali biyu tayi ba tace Mata ta koshi, Umma na bawa Farida kulawa tamkar y'ar cikinta data haifeta da taji Farida ta motsa zata tambayeta ko akwai abinda take buk'ata, ganin irin kulawar da Umma take bawa Farida yasa Inna Sam ba nuna tata kulawar ga Farida, Inna na matuk'ar yiwa Umma ka ra akan Farida ko hirar ta idan ba umma ce ta taso da maganar gidan su Farida ba Inna batayi, itama Kuma tana jin d'adin irin karar da Inna take Mata shiyasa ta Kara rik'e Farida kamar y'ar cikinta, indai ba fad'a Umma tayi ba babu Wanda zaice Farida ba yarta bace.
           Bayan da Sadiq ya gama waya da umma cikin sanyin murya ya cewa Saudat, " Dear maganganun da Kika gayawa Umma basu kamata sunyi tsauri " wata uwar harara ta gasa Masa ciki tsawa tace, " Wacece aka ce maka tana da cikin? " Gabansa ne ya Fad'i cikin shakkarta yace, " Wai Farida " wani narkeken ashar Saudat ta kunduma sannan tace Masa, " au Wai ne ma waye yayi Mata cikin? " Dan duburburcewa Sadiq yayi sannan yace, " ehyyy to Nima dai Abinda bansani ba kenan " hararar ta Kara watsa Masa tace, " Kai kalleni Nan karka Raina min hankali kana nufin ba cikinka bane? " Shiru Sadiq yayi ya kasa cewa uffan cikin tsawa tace Masa, " ba magana nake maka ba zakamun shiru, wancen shegen cikin na uban waye? " Gaba d'aya Sadiq ya gama tsorata da yanayin Saudat ga wani shakkarta da yake ji, cikin rawar murya yace, " to nidai bansan cikin waye ba tunda nidai nasan ba abinda yake had'ani da ita" sai alokacin Saudat ta D'an ji dama-dama Amma Kuma Bata saki fuskarta ba cikin tsuke fuska tace, " kana nufin baka tab'a kusantarta ba kenan?" D'an Jim kadan yayi sannan yace, " Sau d'aya ne Kuma tun washegarin tarewar mu ne " duka Saudat ta kaiwa bango cikin tsananin b'acin rai ta Fara yiwa Sadiq fad'a, " Ashe dama munafurtata kake bansani ba Allah ya Toni asirinka wato lallab'awa kake kaje d'akinta bansani ba ko duk irin kashedin da nayi maka ya tashi abanza kenan " kamar karamin yaro Haka Sadiq ya dinga bawa Saudat hakuri Yana Mata magiya wani tunani ne ya Fad'owa Saudat ta kalli Sadiq shek'ek'e tace, " to ni wannan cikin na farida ma Ina zargin ba naka bane Taya za'a ce sau d'aya Kuma har tasamu ciki, Kai tunfa muna School da Farida take biye-biyen office din malamai, abinda baka sani ba lokacin da mukaje bautar k'asa sau biyu tacemun ta zubar da ciki karka ga irin fad'an da nayi Mata akan wannan mugun halayyar tata ba " wani irin b'acin rai ne ya ziyarci Sadiq lokaci daya idanunsa suka kad'a sukayi ja cikin huci ya Fara yiwa Saudat magana, " me yasa tuntuni Baki gayamun ba meyasa Kika rufemun wannan maganar? " Saudat dad'i taji musamman da taga lokaci d'aya Sadiq yayi na'am da maganar ta, kasa tayi da murya tace, " a'a gani nake bazaka amince ba Amma nasan akwai lokacin da asirinta zai Toni, inasan kabi komai asannu dear karkayi garaje " Bai Bata amsa ba ya jingina da jikin gado Yana lumshe idanu ya nutsa cikin tunani, cikin zuciyarsa Kuma ya kasa gasgata maganar da Saudat ta fad'a domin yasan wacece Farida da irin tarbiyyar ta, sai dai kuma duk maganar da Saudat zata fad'a Yana gagara Musa Mata ita, ya jima ahaka Yana nazari gaba d'aya kansa ya kulle ya rasa wacce magana zai d'auka Amma azahiri Yana tsoron musawa Saudat sai dai Kuma yaki yarda da cewar Farida na aikata fasikanci, ganin yanayin da Sadiq ya shigo ne yasa Saudat zame jikinta ta koma dayan d'akin basu jikinta har rawa yake tana Kiran number mommy, dai da ta Kira kusan Sau biyar no answer wani Kiran ne ya shigo wayarta dogon tsaki tayi fuska ba walwala ta d'auki wayar, " hello ya akayi " daga cen b'angaren yace, " baby na wallahi Ina missing din ki Zaki xo ne ko na karaso Nan gidan naki, tunda kikazo garin Nan fa Sau biyu kawai  muka had'u, Kuma kinsan fa saboda ke nazo garin Nan " fuska ba walwala Saudat tace, " Kai jarababbe ni karka isheni yau bana cikin walwala inada matsala Kuma karka sake kazo mun gida mijina Yana Nan " murmushi Yakson yayi yace, " cool down baby, yanxu akwai damuwar da ni bazan iya sauke Miki ita ba....... " Wani bak'in ciki ne ya rufe Saudat ba shiri ta katse wayar tana cigaba da neman number mommy.

       Sai da Kiran ya kusa katsewa sannan mommy ta d'auka tunkan ta Fara magana Saudat tace, " mommy Wai Ina Kika shiga ne Ina ta kiranki " sorry wallahi Ina gurin Daddyn ki muna Hira yanxu ma shigowa nayi d'aukan Abu Naga shigowar kiranki, Amma lafiya kike kirana yanxu goma ta wuce fa " cewar mommy.
        " Mommy inafa lafiya kiranshi wannan shegiyar uwar tasa tayi Wai Farida na da ciki " inji Saudat, ko mamaki Mommy batayi ba sai ma murmushi da tayi sannan tace, " rannan me nace Miki Saudat kikayi mun gaddama me na Gaya Miki ? Nace Miki Naga kamar ciki gareta kin ce mun ba Haka ba yanxu me Kika yanke? " Idanun Saudat ne suka ciko da kwallah tace, " mommy Kiran da nayi Miki kenan inji Ina mafita da na yanke shawarar turawa Sadiq hotunan Nan na da nasa aka d'aukar mun Amma ta wani layin, mommy hakan yayi? " Jim mommy tayi tace, " eh hakan ma yayi ki Fara tura wad'annan makaman kafi nikuma naje gurin boka D'an tsitu, au na manta ki kirashi Mana ba ya baki wani wuri ba yace Idan kina da buk'atar magana dashi " dafe Kai Saudat tayi tace, " wallahi mommy suna Kano na manta ban taho dasu ba".
        Guntun tsaki mommy tayi tace, " ay Kinga sakacin naki da adaren nan Zaki sanar dashi duk Abinda yake faruwa sai muji daga gareshi " 
        " To mommy yanxu yaushe Zaki je" inji Saudat.
     " Gaskiya gobe bazan samu damar zuwa ba saboda gobe inada bakuwa hjy Hadiza zata zo " cewar mommy.
             " Haba mommy yanxu zuwan hjy Hadiza yafi tawa buk'atar " Saudat ta fad'a.
 " Ke banasan shashanci da ke kike aykena ki Bari zuwa wani satin sai inje Kinga Koda ba zuwan hjy Hadiza zuwa bazai yuwu gobe ba tunda baya aiki ranar juma'a " mommy ta ce.
      Asanyaye Saudat tace, " to Allah ya kaimu Asabar din " mommy ta amsa da, " Aameen" daga Nan sukayi sallama Saudat ta Dade atsaye tana saka da warwara, wani tunani tayi Wanda yasata jindad'i har afili murmushin ta ya bayyana.

        Washegari da safe bayan Sadiq ya tafi aiki Sabon layi Saudat ta siya da sabuwar waya, laptop dinta ta d'auko tayi copy din wasu hotuna da videos na Farida Wanda ta sa su me duniya suka turo Mata, murmushin jin dad'i tayi afili ta furta, " Idan kunsan wata bakusan wata ba " number Sadiq tasa awayar atake ta bud'e WhatsApp hoton Farida tasa a profile din Wanda take zaune Kan cinyar wani ta D'an sunkuyar da kanta k'asa shi Kuma ya juyar da kansa ta gefe yana kallanta sai dai fuskarsa Bata fito ba, ga Wanda baisan ta yanda aka d'auki hoton ba zaiyi zatan kallan soyyaya ne sukeyi, akan username Kuna tasa MD Guy " slm" tayi Masa taga baya active kashe wayar tayi gaba d'aya tana murmushin mugunta cen  cikin kayanta ta mayar da wayar ta boye, kwanciya tayi tana tunanin ta yanda zata b'ullowa da MD Guy tabbas tasan bazasu kwashe ta dad'i dashi ba muddin ya ji labarin Farida nada ciki, wata zuciyar ce tace Mata, " to ke kin tabbatar da cikin na gaske ne ko Kuma sunyi Haka ne dan ya koma Kano? " D'ayar zuciyar tace Mata, " Amma Kuma idan so suke yaje Kano ay ba ta Haka zasu b'ullo Masa ba, Kuma kinsan umma bazatayi Masa karyar ciki ba " afili Kuma ta furta, " To ko shi ne yasa Farida keyin D'an kayan kwadayi yau wainar fulawa gobe kunun tsamiya jibi D'an wake, Kuma dai ayanda Sadiq yake tsorona bazai mun karya ba tunda yace sau d'aya wani Abu ya shiga tsakaninsu, to Amma ba komai zansan yanda zan b'ullowa lamarin " kwanciya tayi harta Fara bacci taji shigowar Kira awayarta  bakuwar number ce hakan ne yasa taki d'aukan wayar har ta tsinke, wani Kiran ne ya K'ara shigowa wannan Karan ma Bata d'auka ba saboda tsarinta ne Bata cika d'aukan number da Bata sani ba, sai da aka Kira sau hudu ana biyar ne tayi guntun tsaki ta d'auka, " Hello," daga d'aya b'angaren aka amsa, " manyan k'asa kina hutawa" ya mutsa fuska tayi kamar tasan muryar me maganar tace, " who is on the line?" Ajiyar zuciya taji ansauke sannan akace, " wow u mean u forgot the sound of my voice, right? " Haushi ta faraji a gaggauce tace, " pls da wa nake magana if not I will..............." Da sauri ya katse ta, " u will do what? Madam MD Guy ne wato har kin manta Dani ko kin manta da duniyar da nake, while na shigo k'asar shekaran jiya so nace Bari na huta tukanna kafin na tab'o ki, ya pretty hope tana lafiya? " Murmushin yake Saudat tayi tace, " lafiyarta klau Wai Kan nufin kana Abuja da gaske? " Ina Abuja wallahi da yau naso yi Miki saukar bazata cikin Kano sai nace Bari na Fara Gaya Miki gobe fa zanzo Kuma da pretty zan wuce sai munyi kamar 2weeks da ita a Abuja sai mu wuce America Amma inajin sai munje England munyi like 1month acen " cikin Saudat ne ya kad'a Amma sai ta wayance ta ce Masa, " Kai fa ka cika zumud'i why not ka Bari ma k'ara warware daga rashin lafiya Amma dan masifa daga dawowar ka zaka ishi mutane, Kuma Haka kawai sai inbaka ita gobe-gobe ka tafi da ita ko ?".

       Tsuke fuska MD Guy yayi yace, " ke Saudat na gaji da wayo da hankali da kike mun yau shekara nawa ana Abu d'aya kullin sai dai kice mun yau ne gobe ne, wannan Karan wallahi-wallahi babu Wanda zai hanani tafiya da ita ta k'arfi da yaji, kinsan halina kinsan waye ni banida kyau musamman akan abinda nake so " jikin Saudat ne yayi sanyi tabbas tasan halin MD zai iyayin komai akan Farida musamman yanda taga ya mutu akan kaunarta, cikin masalaha tace Masa, " haba MD daga zuwanka har zamu Fara fad'a da kai, duk ba wannan ba nayi maka alkawarin Rana ita yau zaka tafi da Farida so nake na sa a shirya ta cikin hikima Kuma infitar da ita cikin hikima ya da babu Wanda zai fahimci abinda ake ciki kaga shikenan Farida ta zama mallakinka har Abada " wannan maganar ba karamin faranta ran MD tayi ba Amma Kuma gani yake 1wk yayi Masa yawa cikin damuwa yace, " but 1wk is too....... " Katse shi tayi, " is too what na, me kake ci na baka na zuba alkawari nayi maka fa idan Kuma na Sab'a duk Abinda kayi niyya kayi shi akaina daz oll " dariyar jin dad'i MD yayi sannan yace, " kina da kyautar dubu d'ari biyar kinsan Hoda tana gara Mana Harka cinikinta muke yi ak'asashen waje kamar ruwa " cikin jindad'i Saudat tace, " shiyasa nakesan Harka da Kai MD Ina godiya ".
     " Karki damu fiye da Haka ma Zaki samu indai akan Farida ne, yane kina gida in b'ullo ne? " Cewar MD.
     " Bangane ka b'ullo ba MD baka da mutumci wai ka manta inada aure ne? " Inji Saudat.
" Yooo Auren me yanxu ni ba matar auren Zaki bani ba? Kawai dai Dan munyi nisa Abuja da Kano ne da yanxu zan faso wallahi " MD ya fad'a.
             " Hmmm to ni yanxu bana Kano ma mijina ya dawo aiki Lagos Kuma ko kusa muke ni bazanyi wannan k'arantar ba kasan ni idan nabar Abu na barshi kenan so mind ur business ni na wuce ajinka " Saudat ta fad'a tana murmushi k'asa-k'asa.
      " Wato Saudat kina Nan yanda nasanki ko, kice ya Baki ticket din cin duniyar ki da tsinke kina Kano ma ya aka Kaya bare Kuma kin faso Kano " cewar MD, guntun tsaki taja tace Kai banasan yawan magana inada abunyi " Bata jira cewarsa ba ta kashe wayarta tana tunanin ta yanda zata b'ullowa lamarin Farida da MD.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   57&58

      _Not Edited_

         Zuwa yamma wayar ta d'auko ta kunna data ta bud'e ta shiga WhatsApp reply din Sadiq ne ya shigo cikin wayar ba adadi tambaya ce yakeyi ta ko ina, babu sanda hankanlinsa ya tashi sai da yaga hoton dake Kan profile din number da akayi Masa magana, tana cikin karanta tambayoyin sa taji kiransa ya shigo cikin wayar, rejecting tayi ta Masa reply ta WhatsApp kamar Haka, " karka damu da Kiran waya sai dai muyi magana da Kai ta WhatsApp xan bayyana maka koni waye alokacin Dana ga dama" tana tura Masa taga yayi seen da sauri yayi Mata reply da voice note saboda typing dinma gani yake b'ata lokaci ne, " Dan Allah waye Kai Kuma ya akayi na ganka da matata ahoto ?" Murmushi Saudat tayi ta tura Masa, " ni masoyinta ne ko ince Saurayinta " reply Sadiq yayi da, " matar auren CE zatayi saurayi? " Saudat ta K'ara tura Masa, " kana mamaki ne ? To ay hoton profile Dina shi zai warware maka zarginka " wani takaici ne ta rufe Sadiq musamman Daya K'ara Duba profile din yaga hoton Farida rungume da namiji daga ita sai miniskirt da bra, runtse idonsa yayi sannan yayi reply, " Eh na yarda Amma Dan Allah zaku Kai wane tsahon lokacin Kuna tare? " Alamun smile 😊 saudat ta tura masa sannan ta Kara tura Masa da, " zamuyi kamar shekara biyu da rabi zuwa uku, yanxu Haka ma Ina gabda zuwa indauketa mu tafi England saboda banasan ta haifarmun baby a Nigeria " jikin Sadiq har rawa yake da ya gama karanta abinda saudat ta tura masa baiyi reply ba sai Kira da taji ya K'ara shigowa, kin d'auka tayi sannan ta Nemo wani video da akayiwa Farida tana kwance Kan gado cikin bargo shikuma namijin na zaune akusa da ita ya juya baya daga shi sai boxers" Sadiq na gani ya Fara karanto innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un " baiyi reply ba saudat ta Kuma turo Masa da wasu hotunan na Farida kusan kala uku sannan tace masa, " nasan Kaine mijinta inasan ka saketa banasan na tafi da ita da aurenka akanta na baka Nan da kwana biyu" saudat na tura Haka ta kashe data ta rufe wayar gaba d'aya ta karya layin gida biyu sannan ta bushe da dariya harda hawaye.

          Ranar Sadiq haka ya kasa sukuni gaba d'aya baya walwala duk Wanda ya ganshi sai ya fahimci irin tashin hankalin da yake ciki, lokaci-lokaci yake K'ara Kiran number da aka Masa magana Amma abun haushi akashe yaji ta, Daya dawo gida ma ko abinci kasa ci yayi, saudat kuwa sai wani tambayar sa take tana nan-nan da shi kamar batasan me yake faruwa ba, washegari Asabar Saudat ta tambayi Sadiq zuwa  saloon ba Musu ya barta agida ta fita ta barshi kasancewar ranar ba aiki, tana tafiya Kai tsaye Tasha ta wuce irin Yan jagaliyan Nan ta samu masu kwana abakin gada ta tsara Masa duk yanda zai cewa Sadiq sannan tayi Masa alkawarin kud'i dubu Ashirin sai da ta zaro su ma ta nuna Masa sannan ta karasa tsara Masa plan din, sabon layin da ta siyo shi tasa acikin wayar wani sabon WhatsApp din ta bud'e tasanya Kati awayar sannan tayi dialing call din number Sadiq mik'awa Saurayin tayi shikuwa ya karb'a ya ma ke ya Fara magana bayan Sadiq ya d'auka, " kana tare da MD Guy Saurayin Farida Kuma uban babyn da zata Haifa Nan bada jimawa ba " rass gaban Sadiq ya yanke ya Fadi har rawar murya yake yace, " Kai ne ka kana ina a Ina zan ganka " tsuke fuska yayi yace, " dakata bana kiraka bane Dan mu had'u ba hasalima wannan ne Kiran karshe da zan yi maka, gargad'i nake karayi maka da ka sakarmun Farida banasan na tafi da matarka da aure akanta idan ba Haka ba Kuma zanyi duk Abinda na ga dama " Sadiq ransa yakai matukar b'aci yace, " zan Kama sai ka shiga hannuna zakayi mun bayanin cin amanata da kukayi kaida ita" murmushi Saurayin yayi sannan yace, " ka Makara domin cikin y'an kwanaki kadan zamu wuce nida ita kasan gidanka na garin Kano akwaishi da kyau gaskiya ya k'ayatu shiyasa naji d'adin dawowar ka Lagos aiki yanda zamuci karenmu ba babbaka, haushi d'aya naji data cemun ta koma gidan su dan zanyi kewarta na y'an kwanaki, idan ka gama bambamin banzar ka bud'e datarka zaka ga sauran shagalin da muke Sha da matarka " Bai jira jin ta bakin Sadiq ba Saudat tayi Masa inkiya ya katse atake ta Mika Masa dubu Ashirin dinsa data ta bud'e ta tura wa Sadiq duk sauran hotunan Farida da videos din da yayi saura awayarta, wad'annan videos din har sunfi na baya Muni su kansu hotunan duk Wanda ya gani ko baisan Farida ba Sai yayi ta'ajibin abinda ya gani, ta tari napep ta tafi aciki ta cire layin wayar ta karya shi biyu ta watsar acikin wata katuwar kwata ta bud'e wayar ta cire battery din ta itama ta watsa ta tana murmushin farinciki

    Saloon ta wuce aka gyara Mata Kai harda wankin k'afa akayi Mata sannan ta dawo gida, ita kanta yanayin da ta samu sadiq ba k'aramin d'aga Mata hankali yayi ba, ta tsorata matuk'a Bata taba tsammanin Haka yake da fushi da zafin zuciya ba shakkar yi Masa magana takeyi tana tsoron karya huce akanta, d'aki ta wuce tayi waya da mommy ko me suka tattauna sai gata ta fito tana yiwa Sadiq kuka Wai Kano zata tafi gobe mommy ba lafiya jikinta yayi zafi, saboda condition din da yake ciki ko  tambayar ta abinda yake damun mommy din baiyi ba yace mata, " Allah ya kaimu " tana shiga d'aki ta buga tsalle ta Fara had'a Kaya acikin jaka ahankali ta furta, " yessss aikinki na kyau mommy, idan na kashe auren wancen tsinanniyar sai Kuma boka ya taimaka Mana mu zubda shegen cikin jikinta "

         A daren Saudat tayi booking din jirgi ta samu tashin yamma zasuyi, gaba daya ta rasa gane Kan Sadiq ko abinci baya ci daga tunani sai tunani lokaci d'aya taga duk ya zabge ya rame kamar Wanda yayi ciwo, tana san tambayar sa tanajin shakkarsa hakan ne yasa ta barwa zuwa wayewar gari.
         Washegari bayan sun gama breakfast shima da kyar ta tursasa Masa yasha ruwan tea, gani tayi ya had'a Kai da gwiwa Yana tunani, marairaice fuska tayi kamar batasan me yake faruwa dashi ba tace, " dear Wai me yake damunka tun jiya ko wani laifin nayi maka bansani ba? " D'ago da Kai yayi ya kalleta yace, " ba abinda kikayi mun damuwata ce ni kad'ai " fuskar tausayi tayi tace, " au yanxu akwai abinda zai shafeka Wanda bansani ba?" A'a ba nufina kenan ba Amma banaso kema na saki cikin damuwa" cewar Sadiq.
       " Babu wata damuwa Dear, ka sani ko zan baka wata shawarar " Saudat ta fad'a tana Kara kwantar da Kai, ajiyar zuciya ya sauke yace, " ki k'yale kawai baby " fushi Saudat ta yi duk yanda Sadiq yaso boyewa Saudat sai da ta tursasa Masa ya Gaya Mata, aykuwa Yana karasa jawabinsa tayi tsalle ta dire tace, " to Kai me kace Kuma wane mataki ka yanke? " Ransa ne ya K'ara b'aci Tunowa da videos din Farida da yayi yace, " nace Masa bazan sake ta ba shi Kuma kamashi zanyi......." Kutumar uba " Saudat ta katse Sadiq sannan ta cigaba da cewa, " wallahi-wallahi bazai yuwu na cigaba da zama da fasik'a mazinaciya ba sai dai ka zab'a koni ko ita Haka kawai taje ta kwaso Mana masifa muna zaune kalau bazai yuwu ba, wallah indai zaka cigaba da zama da ita sai dai ka bani takarda ta " tashin hankali Wanda ba'a sa Masa Rana cikin firgici Sadiq yace, " Wai me kike fad'a ne Saudat, kina ganin zan iya rabuwa dake ne wallahi bazan iya rayuwa babu ke ba Dan Allah ki taimaki rayuwata karki gujeni " afakaice Saudat ta juya tana murmushin jin dad'i Amma sai ta basar ta tsuke fuska tace Masa, " a'a zabi ay ya rage naka ka zab'a ko ni ko Farida domin babu yanda za'ayi inaji Ina gani inkashe kaina da sauran Raina, idan ka zabe ni to dele ne ka sauwakewa Farida idan Kuma Farida ka zab'a to kuwa ayau zan din Nan idan na tafi bazan K'ara dawowa gidan ka ba" idan Saudat nayiwa Sadiq maganar saki ji yake kamar tana kokarin tarwatsa sauran rayuwarsa ne, ganin yayi shiru Yana nazari yasa Saudat tashi ta shiga d'aki tana hargitsa kaya tana surutai acewarta baza ta cigaba da zama dashi ba muddin zai cigaba da auren Farida.
           Sadiq bin Saudat yayi Yana Bata hakuri Yana magiya karta ta tafi kamar wani yaronta ko sauraronsa batayi ba ta cigaba da had'a kayanta har tsugunna Mata yayi Yana magiya ko ajikinta,  tana gama had'a Kaya ta fad'a toilet tayi wanka ta fito ta Fara Shirin sa Kaya kamar jiranta yakeyi Haka ya dinga binta da kyar dai Sadiq ya shawo Kan Saudat ta saurareshi, ita abin ma har dariya yaso Bata Amma sai ta maze ta bishi sukaje falo, d'auke kanta gefe tayi tace, " inajinka me zaka fad'a mun?" K'asa-k'asa da murya yayi cikin sigar lallashi yace, " ki fahimceni kinsan idan na saki Farida ran umma zai b'aci Amma na Miki alkawarin babu ni babu ita kinsa fa dama nace su wuce da ita gida kinga shikenan basai sun ganni bama  bare su tareni da wata maganar......." Ringtone din wayarsa ce ta katse Masa maganar da yakeyi, number umma ya gani guntun tsaki yaja ya cigaba da lallashin Saudat, wani Kiran ne ya Kara shigowa still number dai umma ce, Saudat ce ta kafeshi da ido tace, " Wai waye yake damunka da waya ne? " Cikin jin tsoron ta yace, " Wallahi umma ce ni bansan me suke nema Dani ba " harara ta watsa Masa tace d'auki muji me zasu ce maka yanxu ko Dame suka xo" jikinsa har karkarwa yake ya d'auki wayar umma fuska atsuke ba walwala.

          Kwanan Farida uku a hospital aka sallameta jikinta yayi kyau garau da ita jinin ma cikin ikon Allah ya sauka Bata da damuwar komai, umma tun tanasa ido taga Kiran Sadiq har taga abun ya mik'a abun tun Yana Bata  haushi ya koma bata tsoro kullin ta Kira wayarsa baya d'auka harta hakura ta daina kiransa, kwanan Farida d'aya a hospital tasa umma ta Kira su Asabe ta kwatanta Musu asibitin, sabon mukulli suka sayo suka rufo Musu gidan Kai tsaye asibitin su ka wuce, bayan sun Duba jikin Farida da Inna tazo kawo abinci Umma tasasu suka bita cen gidan da zama.
      Abunda ya d'aurewa Inna Kai bayan gama wayarsu da Sadiq da kamar awa biyu ya turowa Farida kud'i ta Account dinta kud'i ne masu yawa, Amma abun haushin ya Kira yaji jikinta yak'i Kuma idan ta Kira sai yaki d'auka, ita Kuma umma tana fargabar tarar Farida da maganar tunda dai Dr yace Kar adinga Gaya Mata maganganun dazasu b'ata Mata rai, tasan dai ruwa baya tsami banza yanda yake kaunar  Farida yake ji da ita Amma har ta Kira tace Masa tana gadon asibiti baixo ba Bai Kira ba tasan akwai wata ak'asa, sai dai abunda yake K'ara Bata tsoro taya har Sadiq zai shafe wasu kwanaki Bai neme ta awaya ba Kuma baixo gurinta ba, dad'in dad'awa ace har ya canja gurun aiki Bata sani ba gaskiya akwai lauje cikin nad'i, Tunowa tayi da maganganun da Saudat ta Gaya Mata abun yayi Mata matuk'ar ciw

       Kamar kullin Farida na kwance tana bacci wajen k'arfe 11:00am su Asabe na tsakar gida sunayiwa su Inna wankin Kaya abin duniya ne ya ishi umma na takaici gurun Inna ta lek'a ta zauna tana zayyane Mata duk abunda ya faru cikin bak'in ciki tafashe da kuka me ratsa zuciya, Inna ce take ta lallashin umma tana Bata Baki sai da tayi me isarta sannan tace, " Sadiya ki duba fa kiga Sadiq rabonmu dashi yau wata kusan biyu Ashe Har gurin aiki ya canja ya koma Lagos Bamu sani ba, Yarinyar Nan na Kira nace Masa tana asibiti akwance Amma Banda kud'i da ya turo ban Kuma ji ta bakinsa ba yanxu Sadiq Yana neman Albarka kuwa " ita kanta Inna tasan hakan Ba dai-dai bane Amma sai ta cewa umma, " haba-haba ki daina fad'an Haka ni Ina ganin ko akwai abinda Farida tayi Masa ne? " Umma ido ta zubawa Inna tace, " haba Sadiya duk laifin da zatayi Masa ay cuta tafi komai Kuma idan ita tayi Masa laifi mu laifin me mukayi masa? "  Farida na kwance sama-sama take jin hirar tasu farkawa tayi ta riski hirar tasu, umma ce tace, " Mik'omun wayar na K'ara jarrabawa " kamar wasa Inna ta Fara Kiran layin Sadiq Kira biyu tayi sai ana uku ya d'auka fuska ba walwala ya Fara magana, " Salamu alaikum " Inna mik'awa umma wayar tayi  da sauri ta karba ta Fara magana, " Sadiq ya kake gaba d'aya ka manta Damu me mukayi maka? " Adakile yace, " ba komai " murmushi Umma tayi tace, " Bari na mik'awa Farida sai ka dubata da jiki tunda gata Nan Naga ta farka daga bacci yanxu " Bata jira cewar sa ba umma ta mik'awa Farida, cikin d'oki da murna Farida ta tashi zaune ta karb'i wayar ta Fara magana, " Ina wuni ya Sadiq " ya Bata amsa da, " ke dakata dallah, dama ko basu kirani ba zan Kira Ashe dama Haka kike bansani ba mazinaciya maciya Amana na yarda dake Ashe ke fasik'a ce bansani ba, kin cuceni da Kika sa na hada jikina da naki munafuka me halin kwarai afili aboye kinfi kwarkwasa mugun nufi, naji since mun ciki gareki to tun wuri kije ki nemi ubansa acen kwararo inda kikayi shi saboda ba cikina bane banzan karb'i shege ba magana ta dake ta karshe kije na sakeki *SAKI D'AYA* wata uwar K'ara sukaji Farida ta kwallah da karfi ta yanke jiki zata Fad'i Inna dake kusa da ita da sauri ta tarota ta Fad'o jikinta.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                 59&60 


           Cikin tashin hankali Umma take Fad'in, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un Farida, Farida me ya same ki lafiya yau me zamu gani haka??? " Kamo farida sukayi suka kwantar akan gado ruwa Umma ta fita da sauri ta deb'o aka yayyafa mata, ajiyar zuciya sukaji ta sauke Amma Sam Bata cikin hayyacinta, Inna ce take jijjiga ta tana Fad'in, " Farida lafiya Wai me yake faruwa dake ne Dan Allah ki tashi Farida karki mun Haka " Umma ce tace, " yanxu ba lokacin surutu bane Bari na d'auko hijab Dina mu tafi asibiti " ta na karasa maganar ta fice daga d'akin, su Lami dake  waje  gaba d'aya jikinsu yayi sanyi dajin me yake faruwa da sauri Umma ta cewa Musu, " maza kuje titi ku Kira mun me Adaidaita sahu " cikin firgici Umma ta d'auko hijabi tuni Inna ta sa nata Banda hawaye babu abinda takeyi, Inna cikin kuka take ta kalli farida dake kwance take cewa, " Allah ka dubani ka Duba lamarina ya ubangiji karka d'auke mun Farida ita kad'ai ta ragemun, ita nake gani naji dad'i itace farinciki na itace komai nawa " Inna ta K'arasa maganar cikin kuka Umma dake gefe ita dinma hawaye takeyi saboda ba k'aramin tausayin Inna ne taji ya kamata ba, suna Nan tsaye su Asabe suka dawo da me napep, Inna da umma ne suka Kama Farida suka shiga da ita, Nana Khadija hospital suka gayawa Me napep din cikin k'ank'anin lokaci suka K'arasa asibitin, ganin halin da Farida take ciki yasa cikin gaggawa suka amshe ta suna Bata agajin gaggawa sai bayan kamar awa guda Dr ya fito ya ke sanar dasu Umma, " Alhamdulillah  Yarinyar ku ta farfard'o sai dai Bata buk'atar hayaniya, Naga har ta Fara bleeding Amma me kuka Gaya Mata Wanda ya d'aga Mata hankali haka?" Cikin tashin hankali Umma tace, " likita Amma babu abinda ya samu cikinta? " Kallan tsaf yayiwa Umma yace, " idan kun damu da ita da abinda ke cikin ta meyasa zaku dinga d'aga Mata hankali, kun manta kwanaki yanda na dinga jaddada muku akan ku kiyaye b'acin ranta da abinda zai sata cikin damuwa? " Gaba d'aya shiru sukayi ba Wanda ya tanka, fad'a sosai Dr yayi Musu akarshe yace, " mun gode Allah cikin jikinta na Nan Bai zube ba kuma itama tana cikin k'oshin lafiya sai dai ku K'ara kiyayewa gaskiya idan har Kuna San lafiyar ta " jinjina Kai su Umma sukayi suka tashi suka koma d'akin da farida ke kwance, ta na kwance fuskarta tayi fayau numfashinta na sauka k'asa-k'asa.

         Lokacin da Sadiq ya gayawa Farida kalmar saki Saudat na gefe a zaune tanajin ihun Farida ta murmusa da sauri ta karbe wayar daga hannun Sadiq ta kashe ta gaba d'aya tana zubawa Sadiq idanu, gaba d'aya jikinsa yayi sanyi kamar Wanda aka sokawa takobi haka yaji har cikin jikinsa, gabansa ne ya dinga dukan uku-uku ga gumi ya rufeshi tashi yayi jiki asanyaye ya wuce bedroom Kan gado ya fad'a Yana runtse idanunsa video Farida ne ta dawo Masa ido afili ya furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un " Idanunsa ne suka ciko da kwallah ya dafe kansa cikin takaicin abinda Farida ta aikata yake Fad'in, "  meyasa Zaki mun Haka Farida meyasa, kece mutum ta farko Dana Fara bawa yarda Amma Kika ci Amanata kin cuceni kin ha'inceni meyasa Zaki Sanya rayuwarki cikin miyagun halaye gaskiya kin yaudareni me na rageki dashi me na Gaza Miki? Why, Why Farida? " Saudat na daga falo takejin surutan Sadiq sama-sama da kamar bazata bishi ba Amma jin abin nasa yayi yawa yasa yasa ta tashi ta bishi ciki, marairaicewa tayi tana lallashinsa kamar gaske cikin zuciyarta kuwa haushinsa ne fal ya cikata, sai da kyar ta samu ta shawo kansa cikin kissa da kisisina, cikin zuciyarta kuwa tana Fad'in, " wato da sauran aiki agaba na kenan lallai Farida ba karamin gurbi ta samu azuciyar Sadiq ba Amma Kuma ay komai yazo karshe tunda yau zanje gurun mommy"  ta K'arasa maganar tana tashi da niyyar had'a Kayan da yayi saura Bata gama had'awa ba.

          Bayan da su Umma suka ga yanda jikin Farida yayi wata baranda suka koma suka zauna jugun-jugun kowa da abinda yake lissafawa zuciyarsa, Umma ciro waya tayi ta dinga Kiran number Sadiq Amma a kashe cikin damuwa Umma ta cewa Inna, " nifa lamarin yaron Nan ya Fara bani tsoro Anya kuwa babu wani Abu boyayye Wanda Bamu sani ba, Yarinya daga karbar waya sai ta yanke jiki ta Fad'i Anya Sadiq Bai Gaya Mata wata maganar ba, nifa na Fara zargin kishiyarta Anya babu wani kulallen Abu kuwa, Sadiq na cikin hayyacinsa yake yin irin wad'annan abubuwan? " Umma ta K'arasa jero tambayoyin tana zubda kwallah, Inna ma kanta ya Fara kullewa ayanayin zaman auren Farida da Sadiq Amma sai ta cewa Umma, "  A gaskiya Kan abin aduba lamarin Sadiq ne Amma gaskiya ni bana zargin Saudat tunda Kinga bamuji Farida ta tab'a kawo Mana kushenta ba Kuma ance alamar karfi Yana ga me k'iba, Saudat nada kyawawan halayen da be kamata mu zargeta game da  mugun Abu ba, sai dai kuma abinda yake bani tsoro da Sadiq yanda yake bijiro da wasu bak'in abubuwa Wanda mu kanmu bamusanshi dasu ba, gaskiya abun akwai rikitarwa " murmushin takaici umma tayi tace, " Sadiya D Ian ban Gaya Miki irin cin zarafin da Saudat tamun rannan ba ko gaba d'aya na tsorata da lamarin Yarinyar Nan Kuma ta matuk'ar bani mamaki, wallahi da labari za'a bani akan haka sai na karyata Wanda ya gayamun duk girmansa kuwa, mutum ki rabu da mutum ki Kama Allah Amma gaskiya nikam na Fara zargin wani abu Sadiq zan shedeshi dari bisa d'ari bawai Dan Nina  haifeshi ba, sai dan yanda ya taso da kishin mahaifiyar sa tun Yana yaro ko kallan banza wani yayimun wallahi sai inda k'arfinsa ya k'are Amma Wai yau ni matar Sadiq ke zagi agabansa tana Gaya mun maganganu Kuma ba abinda ya aikata akanta kinsan kuwa ruwa baya tsami banza, kema Kuma shaida ce Sadiya " Umma ta fad'a tana goge hawayen daya zubo Mata, jikin Inna sanyi yayi matuk'a, tabbas maganar Umma gaskiya ce Amma abun akwai d'aure Kai, ahankali Inna tace, " tabbas ni kaina na rasa yanda zan fasalta maganar Amma Inaganin abinda zamu yi mu dage da Addu'a babu abinda ya fi k'arfin ubangiji" asanyaye Umma tace haka ne".
       Allurar baccin da akayiwa Farida Bata saketa ba sai kusan magriba su Umma na zaune ta farka da sauri suka k'araso gurunta suna Mata Sannu, hawayene ya gangaro daga idanun ahankali ta bud'e Baki ta farayiwa su Inna magana, " Umma bansan me nayiwa Saudat ba bansan abinda na tsare Mata, Inna Ashe Saudat ba kaunata take ba, mayasa take nema illata rayuwa ta umma burinta ya cika shikenan burinta ya cika umma tasa ya Sadiq ya sakeni saki d'aya " matsanancin kuka ne ya ci k'arfin Farida, gaba daya zaro idanu sukayi suna jin maganar da Farida ta fad'a kamar amafarki Inna ce tayi k'arfin halin tambayar Farida, " maganar da Kika fad'a ce ban fahimce ta ba Farida mijinki Sadiq kina nufin shi ya sakeki??? " D'aga Mata Kai Farida tayi cikin kuka tace, " Inna ya Sadiq ya sakeni " shiru Inna tayi asanyaye ta furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un " Umma da bak'in ciki ya hanata magana zuciyarta har suya take kujera ta koma ta zauna wayarta ta d'auko number Alh karami ta kira ( _wan mahaifin Sadiq_) saboda tashin hankalin da Umma take ciki ko gaisawa basuyi ba umma ta Fara zayyanawa Alh karami bayani tun Daga ranar da suka d'auko Farida daga gidanta har zuwa sakin da Sadiq yayi Mata, sai da ta kammala sannan Umma ta samu ta fashe da wani irin kuka me tsuma zuciya, sai da ta tsagaita sannan Alh karami ya Fara magana cikin jimami, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un yanxu kuna wane asibitin?" Gaya masa sunan asibitin tayi yace Mata gashinan zuwa, bayan sun gama wayar umma kukanta taci ta gode Allah sai da ta gama taje kusa da farida tana lallashinta, gaba d'aya jikin Inna yayi Sanyi tausayin su duka biyun ya kamata, rarrashin su ta dingayi tana basu Baki har ta samu ta D'an shawo kansu.

        Wajen karfe uku na yamma saudat ta kammala shiri ta tsab ta shirya cikin doguwar riga, har airport Sadiq yayi Mata rakiya gaba d'aya yanayinsa baya cikin walwala duk Wanda ya ganshi ahaka sai ya fahimci haka, Yana dawowa gida kwanciya yayi ya fad'a cikin sabon tunani.
          Bayan tafiyar sadiq Kiran mommy Saudat tayi ta sanar da ita tana airport zasu taso, murna sosai mommy tayi tace zata tura driver yaje ya d'auko ta, tana gama waya da mommy wata number ta Kira ana d'auka ta Fara magana, " Aliyos ya garin ?" Daga cen b'angaren aka amsa, " lafiya klau manyan k'asa dama ana jinku? " Murmushi Saudat tayi tace, " Ana jinmu Mana Ina fatan kana Kano wani aiki zan baka Amma banasan asamu matsala, Kuma banasan ya wuce kwana biyu " dariya Aliyos ya k'yak'yata sannan yace, " au dama akwai ranar da Zaki Fara shakkun aikina? "
           " A'a ba Haka bane kawai dai jadadda maka nayi " cewar Saudat.
   " Karkiji komai jiya na koma Kano nayi tafiya ta kwana biyu ne sai naji aikin naki nawa Zaki biya? " Inji Aliyos.
               Guntun tsaki Saudat tayi tace, " Kai dad'i na dakai San kud'i kamar me, kasan dai kud'i ni ba matsala ta bace kabari anjima zan nemeka idan na shigo garin ko zuwa safiya " 
      " To shikenan ranki ya dad'e " Aliyos ya fad'a, da Haka sukayi sallama sannan ta maida wayar jaka.
          Bayan wani lokaci jirgin su Saudat ne ya sauka zuwa wasu mintuna mutane suka Fara sakowwa, Saudat na sakkowa ta Fara Rana ido tana neman driver gidan su, da sauri yakaraso gurunta ya na Mata barka da zuwa, jaka ta mik'a Masa ya karba Yana ja Yana tafe tana binsa abaya har suka K'arasa gurun motar, daga Nan suka wuce gida, lokacin da suka K'arasa gida Daddy na gida gaba d'aya da murna iyayenta suka tarbeta cikin farin ciki da jin dad'i, y'ar Hira suka tab'a sannan Saudat ta haye sama D'akin ta Nan agyare kamar da, wanka ta shiga bayan ta fito tana shafa Mai taji shigowar Kira cikin wayarta, sabuwar number ce ta gani kamar bazata d'auka ba sai da ta kusa yankewa sannan ta d'auka, " hello " daga cen b'angaren aka amsa, " hajiya Saudat barka da sauka garin Kano ya Kika bar mutanen Lagos din? " Dammm gaban Saudat ya Fad'i Amma sai ta dake tace, " waye yake magana " dariya taji yayi sannan yace, " saurin me kikeyi ne Saudat kina magana da ME DUNIYA " zaro idanu Saudat tayi kamar Yana gabanta cikin zuciyarta tace, " au dama baka mutu ba wato sarari ha'intata yayi kenan " Amma afili sai tace, " me duniya lafiya kake kirana " tsuke fuska yayi yace, " Au haka ma zakice ki bini asannu wallah Kar laifinki ya k'aru, wato tambaya ma kike lafiya nake kiranki, Saudat kinsani yi Miki aiki bayan buk'atarki ta biya sai Kuma Kika tura Dan a kashe mu, abinda Baki sani ba Allah ya tsairatar damu nida abokin Aikina, sai dai ankashe abokaina had'u gaskiya ke butuluce Saudat nayi Miki duk Abinda Kika umarce ni Amma karshen Kika sakamun da TSANTSAR BUTULCI, kina tunanin zan kyaleki ne Kuma wallahi-wallahi abinda Kika sa nayi wa kishiyarki sai na ninnaka Miki shi, kinsanni Sarai kinsan waye me duniya kinsanni bani da kyau, tun lokacin da abin ya faru naso daukar mataki akanki Amma dole na hakura saboda harbin bindigar da yaso yimun illah, sati na guda Ina bibiyar ki a Lagos dama Kuma banyi niyyar kamaki acen ba Kano nayi niyyar kamaki, y'an mata yanxu wasan namu zai Fara " k'it Saudat taji ya kashe wayar, jikin saudat har rawa yake gaba d'aya ta tsure da sauri ta Kira number da me duniya ya kirata Amma akashe, ta jima asanyaye tana tunanin ta yanda zata b'ullowa lamarin me duniya.

          Lokacin da Alh karami ya karaso Asibiti gurunsu Umma ya matuk'ar jimanta lamarin Umma k'arin bayani tayi Masa wannan Karan ma harda hawayenta, sai da umma tayi kukanta ta goge hawayen ta sannan Alh karami ya Fara magana, "  gaba d'aya na fahimci maganar ki na Kuma fukanci komai babu abinda zamu ce sai innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un domin Sadiq Yana cikin wani hali na jarabtar rayuwa, amma babu abinda yafi k'arfin Addu'a, akwai wani aramma me almajirai abayan layinmu inshallah zanyi Masa magana atayamu da Addu'a, sannan akwai wata islamiyya itama zanyi Musu magana insha Allah komai zai warware, kema ya kamata ki dage da yiwa d'anki Addu'a domin bakin mahaifiya kamar yankan wuk'a yake sai kiga cikin ikon Allah anshawo Kan matsalar cikin lokaci k'ank'ani " Umma taji d'adin bayanin Alh karami hakan ne ya Kara Mata kwarin gwiwa.
          Washegari Saudat Fita tayi taje ta samu Aliyos kud'i ta bashi masu yawan gaske, sannan ta tsara Masa aikin da take san saka shi, suna gama magana Saudat ta d'auki Aliyos ta kaishi har kofar gidan su Umma saukeshi tayi ta wuce gida.
        Sallama Aliyos yayi asoron gidan Asabe ce ta fito bayan sun gaisa yake tambayar ta, " da Allah umma Zaki mun sallama da ita Abokin Sadiq ne" Jim Asabe tayi tace, " basa Nan sun tafi asibiti " da sauri yace, " Ashsha waye ba lafiya? " Cikin jimami ta sanar dashi, tambayar ta yayi sunan asibitin ta sanar dashi, sallama yayi Mata ya tafi.
      Kai tsaye Asibitin ya wuce tambaya yayi aka nuna Masa d'akin da Farida take, har k'asa ya tsugunna ya gaida su Inna sannan yace, " nasan baku shaidani ba ko umma, Abokin aikin Sadiq ne " gaba d'aya suka maida hankali su guransa umma tace, " yaro amma wayeka ba ko gun Aikinku Daya?" Murmushi Aliyos yayi yace, " da farko sunana Babangida nima a Lagos nake aiki jiya nazo na d'auki hutu ne, umma gaskiya Sadiq na cikin wani yanayi shine nace Bari nazo na sanar daku ko kunsan halin da yake ciki, umma Sadiq yayi zurfi a harkar shaye-shaye da neman Mata yanxu Haka idan Yana Miki magana gani zakiyi kamar bashi yake magana ba saboda ta Fara tab'a Masa kwakwalwa, nayi iya bakin kokarina ganin na hanashi wad'annan halayen Amma abun yaci tura" jikinsu umma gaba d'aya yayi sanyi, Farida tayi mamakin da taji yace Wai Sadiq na shaye-shaye da neman Mata Amma Bata tanka ba tunda yace shi abokin Aikinsa ne wata kila yafisu sanin duk abubuwan da yake yi acen, sun jima suna Dan Hira dasu inna duk akan matsalar Sadiq sannan yayi Musu sallama lokacin da zai tafi dubu Ashirin ya mik'awa umma kin karb'a sukayi ba yanda baiyi ba amma fir suka k'i amsa sai akan kujerar Daya zauna ya Dora Musu ya fice, washegari ma sai da ya dawo aranar ne Dr yace zai sallami farida bayan anyiwa su Inna Bill Aliyos ne ya biya kud'in, godiya su Umma suka dinga yi Masa har kofar gida ya raka su sannan ya tura yaro ya Kai Musu kayan abinci da kayan abinda ba'a rasa ba na yau da kullin.
       Yana barin kofar gidan ya Kira Saudat suka Fara waya, " Ina fatan kina cikin Shiri ko, saboda zuwa yamma zan taho Miki da ita " saudat ta bashi amsa, " ay ba karbarta zan ba tare zamu taho da gayen da zai tafi da ita idan ka kawon ita inyi Yaya da ita Aliyos? " Murmushi Aliyos yayi yace, " yooooo nasani nidai ba in K'arasa Aikina ba kawai na sani" 
     "Shikenan Bari na kirashi sai anjima" cewar Saudat.
       Suna gama waya number MD Guy ta Kira, cikin zumud'i ya d'auka, " ya ake ciki ne nifa tun d'azu nake jiran wayarki " 
    " To sarkin zumud'i ka shirya anjima yace mun zai taho da ita" inji Saudat, dariya yayi yace, " Nan kofar gidan ku zanzo....." Da sauri Saudat ta katseshi, " Kai me yasa wani lokacin baka da Kan gado ne, wane irin kofar gidanmu to ka saurareni da kyau kaje State road ka tsaya acen zanzo mu tafi nace Masa ya samemu a farm center, Koda Wasa karka sake kazo kofar gidanmu" murmushi ya karayi sannan yace, " wannan me sauki ne Bari na shirya Dan Ina wani hotel Kuma da y'ar tazarar tafiya atsakanin gurun" da Haka suka gama wayar Saudat ta ajiye wayar tana murmushin farin ciki afili ta furta, " na kusa sauke wannan nauyin sai inji da wancen shegen garin " tana Nan zaune mommy ta shigo d'akin nata, ganin Saudat na shiri tace Mata, " Ina Kuma Zaki kike shiri Haka ni tunda Kika zo ma ko maganar magungunan ki Baki mun ba kamar basu suka kawo ki ba" D'an Sosa Kai tayi tace, " mommy Ina sane dasu nasan wad'annan masu sauk'i ne, akwai wani Abu da nake gudanar wa ki barni yau nake Shirin kammala shi sai muyi maganar daga baya " mommy batace Mata komai ba ta sa Kai ta fice.
      Bayan sallar magriba Aliyos ne yayi parking din wata mota me d'auke da bak'ak'en glass ( _Tinted glass_) wasu matasa ne daga cen gefensa sallama yayi soron gidansu Umma, Lami ce ta fito ta gaisheshi, bayan ya amsa ya mik'a Mata wasu manyan ledoji sannan yace ta Kira Masa Farida tace Abokin Sadiq ne yazo kara dubata.
     Bayan wasu mintuna Farida ta fito karasawa tayi suka Fara gaisawa, bayan sun gaisa hannu tasa a aljihu wani hankie ya ciro ya shammaceta ya kad'a afuskarta tuni matasan Nan dake gefe suka turata cikin mota da sauri Aliyos ya zagaya ya taka motar ya manna da gudun gaske, Yana karasawa farm center motocin Su saudat ya hango, bayan yayi parking da sauri MD Guy ga karasa bakin kofar har lokacin Farida Bata hayyacinta, haskata yayi da wayar sa wani kyau yaga tayi Masa cikin murmushi ya furta, " wonderful " cak ya d'auketa ya sata acikin motarsa saboda farin ciki dubu dari ya karawa Aliyos, itama Saudat jaka ta bud'e ta mik'a masa kud'in Aikinsa Yana karbewa ya taka motar saa ya wuce ya barsu agurin, Saudat kallan MD Guy tayi tace, " shikenan ba gara bin zago mun warware ko ? " Murmushin jin dad'i yayi ya mik'awa Saudat hannu alamun gaisawa yace, " daz great " lekawa ta window tayi tana kallan Farida tana murmushin da ita kad'ai tasan manufarta, motarta ta koma ta shiga shima MD Guy ya shiga tashi motar ya dau hanya, kowanne su hanyarsa daban.

    Saudat na cikin driving kira taji ya shigo cikin wayarta number ta gani lokaci d'aya gabanta ya hau fad'uwa, kamar bazata d'auka ba sai Kuma tayi ta maza ta d'auka tun kafin ta Fara magana taji muryar Mommyn ta cikin kuka tana magana, " Saudat kina Ina ne muna cikin tashin hankali wasu mutane ne suka xo har gida suka tafi damu" ji tayi anfisge wayar an mik'awa daddy shima cikin damuwa ya Fara magana.

      " Y'an Mata na Dan Allah duk Abinda mutanen Nan zasu umarce ki karki Musa Musu indai Bai Sabawa Addini ba, banasan suyi Miki illa....." Nan ma ji tayi anfisge wayar atake aka Fara Bata umarni,  " taka birki ki fito daga motar nan " jikin Saudat har rawa yake ta fito daga motar wata bak'ar mota ta gani ta k'araso gurunta, Bata ga fuskar Wanda ya fito daga ciki ba sai da ya karaso guru ta, hura Mata iskar sigarin bakinsa yayi sai alokacin ta gane shi har ta bud'e Baki zata Fara magana ya zabga Mata Mari guda biyu agigice ta Fara bashi hakuri,  " Dan Allah me duniya kayi hakuri karka cutar Dani da iyayena " K'ara kifa Mata wan Marin yayi sannan ya hankad'a ta mota ya shiga yaja motarsa da gudun gaske.


     _Wannan shi ne Ali yaga Ali🤣🤣 Farida fans how market🤣🤣 farinciki ne ko bak'in ciki?_🤪🤪
