*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                     61&62




            Bayan wasu mintuna da fitar Farida tun su Inna na sauraron shigowar Farida har suka ga dad'ewar tayi yawa, umma ce ta magantu ta cewa Asabe, " Asabe lek'a gun Farida Naga ta dad'e ita da jikinta ba karfi ba " tashi Asabe tayi taja hijabinta dake Kan kyaure ta sa tayi waje, tana fita wayam ta gani y'an dube-dube ta Fara yi da hange-hange Anna babu Farida babu Abokin Sadiq kanta ne ya d'aure da mamaki da kalli hudu me kalanzir tace, " Dan Allah baka ga inda mutumin Nan yayi me mota ba? " Wane mutum ?" Shima ya tambayeta, shiru tayi Masa jiki a sanyaye ta koma gida cikin sanyin murya tace, " Umma bangansu ba" gaba d'aya ido suka zuba Mata lokaci d'aya suka Watso Mata tambaya, " bangane Baki gansu ba har ita faridan ?" Sunkuyar da Kai Asabe tayi tace, " wallahi har ita banganta ba " azabure suka mik'e kowa Yana salallami umma ce tace, " to yau maga ikon Allah ace Yarinya daga fita su gaisa sai a neme su arasa, mutumin Ina ya tafi da Yarinyar Nan? " Ina saboda tashin hankali jikinta har karkarwa yakeyi, umma ce taja mayafinta ta fice hankali atashe tana tambayar mutanen unguwar duk Wanda ta tambaya sai dai yace yaga fitowar farida Amma baiga tafiyarsu ba, Inna dasu Lami gaba d'aya Haka suka bazama nema Amma Ina ba su Farida babu alamarsu, Inna Banda hawaye babu abinda take yi jikinta gaba d'aya ya sare da lamarin b'atan farida, basu suka koma gida ba sai fin Sha d'ayan dare gidajen masu unguwanni na kusa dasu babu inda basu je ba har na gaba da unguwar su, cigiyar Farida har masallatai Haka Inna da umma suka dinga bi suna kaiwa ko Allah zaisa adace, kuka Kam sun ci shi kamar me Inna da zuciyarta ta bushe ko hawayen Bata iyayi sai ajiyar zuciya, yanda suka ga Rana Haka suka ga dare, alawala umma da Inna suka yi kowacce na gabatar da sallar nafila, su kansu su Asabe basuyi wani baccin kirki ba Asubar fari umma ta tafi gidajen radio tana Kai cigiya, gaba d'aya basa cikin kwanciyar hankali cikin lokaci kad'an Inna ta zabge ta rame ta lalace kamar wacce tayi jinyar shekara, kowa yaga halin da bayin Allah Nan suke ciki sai sun tausaya Musu matuk'a, umma ta Kira wayar Sadiq yafi a irga tun tana sa ran samun shi har ta yanke kauna suka dukufa da Addu'a sun mik'a lamuransu gurun ubangiji, gaba d'aya farin ciki ya kau acikin gidansu kamar wad'anda akayiwa mutuwa, kowa jugun-jugun zaka ganshi lokaci d'aya duk sun kad'e saboda tashin hankali, umma ce ta Kira dangin mahaifin Sadiq da nata y'an uwan duk ta sanar dasu halin da suke Ciki, duk Wanda yaji halin da suke ciki sai ya tausaya Kuma ya jajinta Musu matuk'a, kamar yanda Alh karami ya yiwa umma Alkawari Haka yasa aka dukufa da rok'on Allah ba dare ba Rana su kansu su Inna Abinda sukeyi kenan saboda basu da wata mafita data wuce wanna.

          Me duniya na cikin driving Saudat nayi Masa magiya tana bashi hakuri, wani mugun kallo ya watsa Mata cikin tsawa ya Fara yi Mata magana, " ke karamar y'ar akuya karki mun akuyanci aciki mota da hakurinki da babu shi duk d'aya ne, karkisa na Fara Miki iziya tun acikin mota  shiru Saudat tayi tana goge kwallah lokaci-lokaci, tafiya sukayi me tsayin gaske sannan suka dinga shiga cikin surk'ukin daji motar me duniya na gaba yaronsa na driving din ta Saudat Yana biye dasu abaya, parking din mota yayi ya fito daga ciki shima yaron nasa motar Saudat yayi parking da ita hannu me duniya ya mik'a Masa,  wani bak'in kelle ya mik'awa me duniya motar ya bud'e da karfi ya fisgo hannun Saudat har sai da ta kusa kaiwa k'asa, janyota yayi d k'arfin gaske ya d'aure Mata ido da bak'in kyallen booth ya bud'e ya hankad'a ta ciki ya rufe sannan suka koma motarsu suka cigaba da tafiya, wata sabuwar tafiyar sukayi Saida suka kusa shafe awa guda sannan suka K'arasa, gidan da suka Kai Farida Nan suka shiga da saudat kafin su shiga sai da me duniya ya rufe idanunsa shi da yaronsa, suna shiga falon da k'arfin gaske ya hankad'a Saudat har sai da ta bugu da center table din dake tsakiyar falon, mommy ce ta tashi da gudu zata je ta rungumo Saudat me duniya ya buga mata uwar tsawa, " dakata, dakata bankawo ta Dan ku rungumi juna ba, na kawota ne Dan ta fansheku Kai kuraye ku rufewa tsofaffun Nan ido ki maida su har cikin gidan da muka d'auko su, shi Kuma me gadin gidan ka bud'e band'aki ka fito dashi ka kwance shi, daddy najin haka ya tsugunna har k'asa Yana rok'on su harda hawayen sa, " Dan Allah ka taimaka karka cutar da Yarinyar Nan ita kad'ai garemu wallahi-wallahi ko nawa kake so zan biyaka Kai idan duka dukiyata kake da buk'ata na maka alkawarin mallaka maka ita " dariya me duniya yayi yace, " tsoho ba kudinka nake buk'ata ba wannan itace abinda nake buk'ata wata kila bakasan me ta aikataba ko, ta tab'o me duniya Kuma acikin duniyar Nan sai na nuna Mata me duniya ya cika D'an duniya, tsoho da tsohuwa alkawari nayiwa kaina ko kwarzane babu abinda zai same ku, yanda na d'auko ku lafiya Haka Zan maidaku lafiya, Kai kuraye ja muje" mommy da daddy kuka suka sa lokaci d'aya cikin tashin hankali, ita kanta Saudat babu abinda takeyi sai hawaye ganin me duniya da gaske yake, cire kyalle Idanta tayi taje har k'asa ta tsugunna tana rok'on me duniya, ido ya zura mata sannan ya k'yak'yace da wata iriyar dariya lokaci d'aya ya tsuke fuska, k'arfinsa ya tara guri d'aya yasa k'afarsa ya doke bakin Saudat, daga gurin da take sai da takai har karshen bangon d'akin ji kake yaraf ta yanke ciki ta Fad'i, wata irin kururuwa su daddy suka da niyyar su je gurin Saudat cikin matsananciyar tsawa me duniya ya bawa kuraye umarni ya tasa keyarsu, babu yanda suka iya saboda hannunsu da kafafunsu adaure suke, fito yayi wasu k'aratan samari hudu suka fito suka d'aurewa su Mommy idanu da bak'in tsumma sannan suka jasu suka kaisu cen cikin mota, suna tura su ciki wani gabjejen cikinsu ya ciro wani Abu akwalba ya fesa Musu tuni kowanne su ya fice daga hayyacinsa, biyu ne suka zauna agaba sannan d'aya ya koma cikin booth Yana faken su Daddy, wuta suka bawa motar sannan suka mik'i hanya cikin surk'ukin dajin.

      Bayan  fitar su daddy me duniya wani fiton ya karayin sai ga wasu samari biyu sun fito, Yana d'aga Kan kujera ya nuna Musu Saudat da sauri suka K'arasa zasu d'agata d'ayan ya kalli me duniya yace, " master Wai ko ta wulla ne akaita jeji? " Murmushi me duniya yayi yace, " bugu tasa ku kaita d'aki ku sakar Mata AC ku rufo kofar " Yana gama basu umarni d'aya ya kama hannunta ya juyo da sai ga jini ta hanci ta bakin ta, tallafo kanta yayi ya cewa me duniya, " Amma fa tayi jagwal da yawa danma dai Kar rashin imanin yayi yawa da anyi Mata service " na kusa dashi ne yace, " ke ware dubi fa ahaka zakayi Mata service" ya karasa maganar Yana nuna masa hak'oran Saudat da yaga sun lek'o, ba ko kyankyami ya tura yatsansa bakin Saudat sai ga hakora biyar sun fad'o, gaba d'aya suka bushe da dariya har me duniya sai da sukayi me isarsu sannan suka tsagaita suka kinkimi Saudat sukayi d'akin da me duniya ya basu umarni.

        MD Guy tun ahanya yake Kiran wani abokinsa me suna Nabeel sai da ya Kira kusan Sau hudu sannan Nabeel ya d'auka cikin yanayin maye, MD agaggauce ya Fara Masa magana, " ya Abokina kana Ina ne ?" Cikin maybe yace Masa, " gidan Jennifer " kansa ne ya kulle yace, " Kai banasan iskancin kamun bayani yanda Zan gane zuwa nake sanyi Kuma bani kadai bane " ayanayin maganar Yan giya yace, " kaaaajeee guesthouse dinaaaa a sultan road Zan k'araso Ina kashe wata sabga ne " MD ko ta kansa baibi ya kashe wayar Kai tsaye yayi sultan road gidan Nabeel, tafiya yayi me D'an tsayi sannan ya karasa me gadin na ganin MD ya taso Yana Masa barka da zuwa kasancewar yasan MD Sarai tunda Abokin Nabeel ne na kut da kut Kuma yawancin zuwansa ma agidan Nabeel yake sauka, ba Musu ta bud'e Masa mota ya shiga da ita har zuwa lokacin Farida Bata farka ba, cak ya d'auketa ya shiga da ita cikin gidan d'akin daya Saba sauka Nan yayi ta Farida, akan gado ya shimfid'ar da ita ido ya zuba Mata Yana kallo kamar zai cinyeta, gefen bakinta ya samu ya manna Mata kiss sannan ya wuce ya shiga toilet Dan ya watsa ruwa, bayan ya fito zama yayi gefen Farida ya zuba Mata idanu kamar zai cinyeta afili ya Fara surutu kamar wani Sabon kamu, " na dad'e Ina mafarkin wannan ranar Ashe akwai ranar da zanganta, na dad'e Ina buk'atar ki pretty Amma na rasa me yasa Baki da waye ba gaki kalar manyan Yara Amma sai ki dinga Abu kamar wacce batayi school ba, da kinbada hadin Kai tayaya za'ayi Miki Haka agusar Miki da hankali umm baby" ya K'arasa maganar Yana shafa gashin Farida da ya rufo Mata fuska, ya Jima ahaka Yana surutansa sannan ya fita falo ya bud'e firji ya deb'o drinks cikin farin ciki da kwanciyar hankali domin jinsa yake wasai kamar wani Sabon Ango.

       Farida ba ita ta farka ba sai wajen k'arfe goma Sha biyu na dare, tana bud'e idanta kanta taji ya Sara Mata da mamaki take kallan d'akin da take, lokaci d'aya ta Fara tunano abunda ya faru da ita zumbur ta mik'e tsaye, d'an kwalinta dake gefe da sauri ta janyo shi ta d'aura wata kofa ta gani ahankali ta Fara tafiya cikin tsoro da tashin hankali, saboda duk atunanin ta gidan yankan Kai aka kawo ta, k'irjin ta ke bugun uku-uku lokacin da ta sa kafarta falon, wani kamshi ne me had'e da wani irin wari ne ya daki hancinta lokaci daya cikinta ya Fara hautsinawa, cikin k'arfin hali ta cigaba da tafiya tana jiyo maganganu sama-sama, tana k'arasawa tsakiyar falon cak ta tsaya tana kallan ikon Allah, maza biyu ta gani sai Mata ma guda biyu kowanne mace d'aya tana gefensa da wata banzar shiga, jikinta ne ya cigaba da karkarwa ahankali ta furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un" daga Haka kasa magana tayi ta daskare agurin kamar wacce aka rikewa k'afafu, daya daga cikin y'an Matan ce ta zabura aharzuke ta Fara magana, "ke, ke din wacece Kuma me kikeyi agidan Nan " Farida tsuru tayi babu amsa, da sauri MD ya mik'e cikin fara'a ya Fara yiwa Farida magana, " u r highly wlcm my pretty " ya karasa Yana nufar gurin da Farida take, wani uban Ashar budurwar Nan ta kunduma sannan ta Fara banbamin fad'a, " wato dama yaudarata kakeyi ko MD ni zakayiwa haka Ashe kana da wata budurwar bansani ba Wallahi ke Kuma yau Zaki ci ubanki " tana rufa Baki tayi kan Farida aykuwa MD ya fisgota Yana kifa Mata Mari cikin hasalla ya rufe ta da fad'a, " wallahi Kika sake Kika tab'a ta sai inkashe ki inkashe banza da wofi, wannan da kike gani farin cikina ce Zan iyayin komai akanta"  Nabeel ne ya shiga tsakiyar su Yana lallashin ta da kyar ya samu ya lallameta suka fita ita da budurwarsa merry.
         Farida dake cen gefen kujera arakub'e tana maida numfashi tsoro ne ya Kara namaye ta jin an ambaci sunan MD cikin zuciyarta tace, " badai MD din da Saudat take fad'a ba ne...... " Bata K'arasa maganar taji ya tunkaro ta da niyyar tab'a ta da sauri ta dakatar dashi tana tambayar sa, " Kai din waye Kuma inane Nan " murmushin jin dad'i yayi yace, " sunana MD Nan gidan Abokina ne " waye ya kawo ni Nan Kuma me ye dalilin dayasa ka kawo ni Nan " Farida ta jefo Masa tambaya.
     " Nine na kawo ki da taimakon kawayarki Saudat, Kuma so da kauna ne yasa na kawo ki gurina Ina fatan zamu dauwama cikin farantawa junanmu rai " ko mamaki Farida batayi ba saboda lamarin Saudat ya daina Bata mamaki, K'ara jefo Masa wata tambayar tayi, " kana tsoron Allah kuwa, Kuma idan yarka ko kanwarka akayiwa ya zakaji? " Shiru yayi Bai Bata amsa ba sai ma dogon tsaki da yaja Dan ya farajin haushin maganganun ta, shiru me ya ratsa Farida ta tunkari hanyar fita da niyyar itama ta fice daga gidan, da sauri MD ya dank'o ta ta fad'a jikinsa turashi take kokarin yi Amma kamar dutse take wa wata dabara ce ta Fad'o Mata cikin sanyin murya tace, " dakata dakata sakeni Bari kaji muyi magana ta masalaha " cikin jin dad'i MD ya saki Farida kujera ta koma ta zauna sannan tace, " kace kana so na ko? " Gyad'a Mata Kai yayi sannan ta cigaba.
      " Gud,  to na amince na  yarda Amma da sharad'i, banasan ka kara hulda da kowacce irin mace ka amince?" Da sauri yace Mata," na yarda na amince, "  sharad'i na biyu bazaka Kara yunkurin tab'a ni ba harsai mun bar gidan " inji Farida.
      Cikin zumud'i ya amsa, " karki damu na amince ay gobe-gobe zamu tafi Abuja " murmushi itama tayi kamar gaske tace," Ina buk'atar hutawa yanxu Bari naje nayi sallah " d'akin data ganta ciki Nan ta koma bayan tayi alwala rasa abin sallah tayi drawer d'akin ta Duba sai zanin gado ta nad'a tayi sallolin da ake binta, ta Jima tana Addu'a da Kai kukanta gurin ubangiji sannan ta  koma gefe ta takure guri daya tana tunanin yanda zata b'ullowa halin da take ciki.
     Bayan shigarta d'akin Nabeel ne ya kalli MD yace, " Wai me Yarinyar Nan take nufi " murmushi MD yayi yace, " Wai wayo take San yi min abinda Bata sani ba na gama komai na tafiyarmu England da ita " shek'ek'e Nabeel ya kalleshi yace, " ahakan zaku tafi England Yarinya sai taurin Kai" murmushi MD ya karayi yace, " Kai tuni na kammala komai azuwan fita zanyi da Mata Kuma Patient ce zamuje ganin likita Kai duk takaddun bogi na kammala fa da taimakon Dr Samuel nace ma gobe 8 jirginmu zai tashi, idan kuma Naga ta bada Kai sai inbuga Mata maganin baccin Dan Sarai nasan wacece ita, d'azu ma tayi Haka ne Dan tamun dabara" Nabeel yace, " to nidai idone nawa".


           Mak'ota y'an uwa da Abokan arxiki sai zuwa ake ana yiwa Umma da Inna jaje, y'an unguwar gaba d'aya sunji b'atan Farida sai yabonta suke suna yabon kyawawan halinta, duk Wanda ya shiga yiwa su Inna jaje da tausayinsu yake fitowa, Dan ma Allah yasa Musu tawakkali Amma duk da Haka daka kallesu zata fahimci tashin hankalin da suke ciki, ga Sadiq kullin suka Kira number sa akashe Bata shiga.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   63&64


         
          Wani irin sanyi ne ya tashi Saudat lokacin fuskarta gaba d'aya ta haye ta kumbura suntum, bakinta yakumbura leb'an ta kamar ganda idan ba magaba Saudat tayi ba bazaka tab'a ganeta ba, wani irin ciwon Kai ne yake damunta da rarrafe ta rarrafo ta fito falon, suna zaune duk sunyi mankass da alamu duk abige suke ganin yanayin da suke ciki yasa tayi tunanin ko basu lura da ita ba, da sauri take rarrafawa har ta Isa bakin kofar, murmushi me duniya yayi sannan ya kallin Saurayin gefen sa Yana bashi umarni da ido, da sauri ya mik'e yaje gun da Saudat take har ta na Shirin fita kafarta ya dank'o ji kake k'iiiiiii Yana janta a k'asa cikin azaba take kwallah ihu, agaban me duniya ya ajiyeta sannan ya koma ya zauna, wannan Karan Saudat kuka ta fashe dashi cikin kuka take magana, " me duniya Dan Allah kayi hakuri ka yafe mun wallahi badan komai na tura akashe ku ba sai Dan Kar asirina ya tonu, Wallahi na tuba bazan K'ara ba ka ji k'aina ka yafemun " dariya me duniya yake Yana kyaykatawa sannan ya kalli wani figeggen saurayi yace, " Kai Ola aje aymata service Kuma a tabbatar da anyi Mata video idan tayi Muku gardama ku jik'a Mata aiki " kuraye ne yayi karaf yace, " haba master wannan ay buk'ulu zakayi Mana, kasan dai su Ola virus garesu abarmu mu Fara tab'awa Mana sai suje su jik'a aikin ".
        Me duniya ido ya zurawa Saudat ganin yanda take hawaye share-share shi abun ma mamaki ya bashi, murmushi yayi yace, " Wai har kin Fara karaya ne?  Wannan fa somin tab'i ne y'an Mata, Kai kuaraye gatanan kuje na barmuku wuk'a da nama " jin wannan kalmar ta me duniya yasa Saudat ta rushe da wani irin kuka me cin zuciya, ba Wanda ya saurareta bare yasan tanayi haka suka figeta suka jata su kusan biyar suna shiga suka banke kofa Banda ihun Saudat ba abinda yake tashi, girgiza Kai me duniya yayi afili yace, " Saudat kenan bazan kashe ki ba Amma tabbas sai na nakasa rayuwarki ta yanda ko kalmar duniya kikaji an anbata sai kin saki fitsari agurin, Dani kike xancen".

       Bayan da su daddy suka dawo hayyacinsu Mommy zubewa tayi a get Banda kuka da ihu ba abinda take yi kuka take tana birgima kamar karamar Yarinya, shi kansa Daddy Banda hawaye ba abinda yakeyi Amma Dan ya kwantar mata da hankali yasa yake ta lallashinta Yana k'arfafa Mata gwiwa har ya samu suka shiga cikin gida, b'angaren sa ya shiga zama yayi ya rafka uban tagumi ba abinda yake damunsa sai tunanin halin da Saudat take ciki, shi kad'ai yake magana afili saboda gaba d'aya abun ya d'aure Masa Kai, " meyasa yaran Nan zasu min Haka since basa buk'atar kud'i to me suke buk'ata daga gurinta, Yarinyar Nan me ta tsare musu ne ita kadai Allah ya mallakamun Amma suke San lahanta mun ita, Ya Allah karka basu ikon cutar da ita " ya Jima ahaka Yana surutai sannan ya Kira number wani commissioner of police, sun Jima suna tattaunawa sai daga baya sukayi sallama.
      Mommy Haka ta dinga safa da marwa Tama rasa abinyi gaba d'aya kanta ya kulle, number hjy Daso ta Kira tana d'auka ta fashe Mata da kuka cikin tashin hankali hjy Daso take tambayar ta, " hjy me yake faruwa ne ko wani Abu ne ya faru da Alhaji ? " Jan majina tayi sannan ta zayyanawa hjy Daso duk abinda yake faruwa, sun d'auki lokaci suna tattaunawa daga karshe hjy Daso tayi Mata alkawarin zuwa ta rakata gurin wani hatsabibin boka domin yayi Mata aiki Su sako Mata Saudat.

( _Shi dama Wanda ya Saba biye-biyen bokaye to ba abinda zai sameshi ya fauwalawa Allah lamarinsa Allah ka Kara tsare mana imanin mu Aameen_)
  
     Sadiq tun bayan tafiyar Saudat ya kashe wayoyinsa gaba d'aya jinsa yake wani iri Yana ganin kamar ya aikata wani babban laifi, jin kansa yake yayi Masa nauyi a b'angare d'aya Kuma ga bakin cikin video da aka turo Masa na Farida, washegari ko aiki Bai je ba abinda yake yi sai tunani idan abun ya b'ata Masa Rai sai dai hawaye ya gangaro daga idansa.

   Wayewar gari da safe MD ne ya fito tsab dashi saboda zumud'i tun asuba ya yi wanka, d'akin da Farida ke ciki ya kwankwasa lokacin tana Kan dadduma tana Addu'a, ad'an tsorace ta bud'e kofar tana ja da baya, ko kyakykywan kallo me duniya Bai Mata ba ya mik'a Mata wasu Kaya doguwar Riga ce da mayafinta, kauda Kai gefe yayi yace Mata, " kiyi wanka ki shirya saboda da wuri zamu wuce " shi a dole bayasan ya kalleta irin ya d'auki nasihar da tayi Masa din nan, karb'a tayi ta koma ta zauna Banda azabar yunwa ba abinda take ji saboda da daddare bayan ta koma d'aki MD ya biyota ya kawo mata abinci tsoro ne ya hanata bud'ewa ta karb'a, shi kuma daya gaji da bugu yayi tafiyarsa, bayan wani lokaci Farida wanka tayi ta Sanya kaya Kan gado ta koma ta kwanta jikinta duk ba kwari saboda yunwar data cinyeta, bayan garin ya Fara haske nocking ta k'araji ahankali ta taso ta bud'e kofar MD ta gani hannunsa d'auke da tire Wanda yake cike da kayan breakfast, ajiyewa tayi ta zuba Musu idanu tunanin su Inna ne ya Kara Fad'o Mata aykuwa ta rushe da wani irin kuka tafi minti talatin tanayi Banda ajiyar zuciya ba abinda takeyi, bayan ta goge idanta ahankali ta Fara cin abinci Abinka da me jin yunwa tuni ta cinye tas.
          MD dake zaune shi da Nabeel Yana jiran kammalawar Farida lokaci-lokaci yake Duba agogo, Nabeel ne ya kalleshi yace, " mutumina da alama fa kayi zurfi akan Yarinyar Nan, ji yanda duk ka bi ka wani damu nifa Ina jiye maka tsoron karta tona maka asiri a airport fa " ajiyar zuciya MD ya sauke Yana dafe Kai sannan yace, " ka Bari Abokina Nima fa na farajin tsoron Haka Anya ba d'aga tafiyar Nan zanyi zuwa wani lokacin ba kafin Nan na shawo kanta"
      " To kana ganin zaka iya shawo kanta bayan kace taurin Kai gareta ka daiyi tunani" cewar Nabeel.
    " Eh to Ina ganin Haka Amma yau inajin Abuja kawai Zan wuce da ita Dan Ina tsoron infita da ita ko a Kuma airport ta bani matsala musamman turai kasansu da sanin darajar D'an Adam " inji MD.
          " To hakan ma yayi Amma baka ganin asarar kudinka zakayi?" 
   Hararar Wasa ya watsawa Nabeel yace, " nawa ne kud'in da har zanyi asararsu, ka manta hodar Nan tana bada citta " dukan Wasa Nabeel ya Kai Masa yace, " shegen Kaya kana wuta mutumina " gaba d'aya suka sa dariya.
       Online ticket yayi niyyar siya suka ce Masa Koda ya siya bazai samu damar tashi aranar ba saboda garin antashi da hazo ganin Haka yasa ya yanke shawarar tafiya ta mota, bayan ya kammala koma gurin Farida yaje har lokacin hawaye takeyi, ko ajikinsa yi yayi ma kamar Bai San tanayi ba, agaggauce yace Mata, " ki taso yanxu zamu wuce  Abuja " Yana gama maganar ya sa Kai ya fice, wani Sabon kukan  Farida ta fashe dashi, sannan ta d'aga hannu sama tana Addu'a, ta Jima tana Addu'a da neman d'aukin ubangiji, jiki a sanyaye ta fito falo kirjinta na dukan uku-uku,  shi kansa Nabeel yana kallanta har wani had'iyar yawu yake, sanin halin MD ne yasa Bai nuna zalamarsa afili ba, Yana ganinta ya fito get ita dinma binsa tayi tana goge kwallar idanta, gaba ya bud'e Mata shima ya shiga Yana yiwa me gadi horn, motar sanye take da tinted glass yanda na waje bazai gano abinda na ciki yake yi ba, me gadi na bud'e Musu kofa ya Danna hancin motarsa ya fice da gudu, farida kuka take sosai uffan Bai ce Mata ba sai ma wakar daya daga kunna, karshe gani yayi ta takure gefe d'aya da alama sanyi take ji hakan ne yasa ya kashe AC, jikinta Banda karkarwa ba abinda yakeyi saboda yanda zazzabi ya rufe ta, tafiya suke sosai har sun Fara barin cikin gari sannan bacci yayi awon gaba da Farida dama abinka da Wanda yake da bashin bacci, gashi cikin Farida Yana yawan sata bacci sai ta shafe wani lokaci tanayi, murmushi MD yayi ya cigaba da driving dinsa.

        Hjy Daso sammako ta bugo taje tasamu mommy bayan sun tattauna mommy ta shiga d'akin daddy yana zaune karatun kur'ani yake gefe ta samu ta zauna bayan ya kammala tace, " Daddyn Saudat hjy Daso ce tazo zata rakani gun wani malami ta ce Yana taimako sosai" kallan tsaf yayi mata yace, " da hankalinki me zai kaiki gun wani malami " tsuke fuska tayi tace, " taimako nake so ayi Mana akan Saudat ay Allah ma yace tashi intaimake ka "
    " Dama akwai taimakon da Wani zai maka Wanda ya gagari ubangiji " cewar Daddy.
    Wani dogon tsaki mommy taja tace, " nifa ba shawara nace kabani ba na Gaya maka ne saboda karka Duba kaga bana nan, zaka zauna kana gayamun kabali da ba'adi " mommy na gama fad'an Haka ta fice daga d'akin, ita da hjy Daso suka fice mommy na driving hjy Daso na gefe, da motar mommy suka fita hjy Daso ta bar tata motar agidan mommy.

         Tafiya sukayi me nisan gaske sannan suka Fara shiga cikin daji tafiya sukayi itama me nisa sannan sukayi Parking din motar su abakin wata bishiya, hjy Daso ce ta cewa mommy, " to anan motar mu zata dakata fa wannan itace k'a'idar boka ba'a shigar Masa da mota cen wajen fadarsa yanxu da kafarmu zamu K'arasa cen saman dutse acen fadarsa take " da mamaki mommy ta kalleta tace, " au kina nufin wancen dutsen zamu hau me uban girma Kuma har wata fa da gareshi " murmushi hjy Daso tayi tace, " kina Wasa da wannan bokan mu K'arasa ki gani " ba Musu mommy ta rufe mota hjy Daso na gaba tana biye da ita abaya.

          Saudat tayi suma yafi uku ba k'aramin cin mutumci su Ola suka yiwa Saudat ba, ahaka suka tsallake suka baro ta,  Saudat ba ita ta farka ba sai awajen asuba duk Wanda ya ga halin da Take ciki sai ya tausaya mata, jina jina haka me duniya ya shiga ya sameta akwance kallan tsaf ya yiwa Saudat sannan yace, " sannu hajiya Saudat " kallanshi Saudat tayi da jemammun idanuwanta bata ce Masa uffan ba, me duniya Dan murmushi yayi yace, " alfarma d'aya ce Zan Miki muddin Kika bani had'in Kai to ayau Zaki tafi gidanku yanda muka d'auko ki Haka zamu mayar dake" Saudat najin haka ta Fara gyad'a Masa Kai akan ta yarda ta Amince.
      " Inasan ki bani lambar mijinki ko ta kishiyarki "  cewar me duniya.
    Wannan Karan ma gyad'a Kan ta karayi ahankali tace, " a kkkawwo inrubuta " sai ka kasa kunne sannan zaka fahimci abinda take fad'a, mik'a Mata wayarsa yayi hannunta na karkarwa ta karba number sadiq ta sa masa, saving din number yayi sannan ya Kara mik'a Mata yace ta sa Masa number Farida, ba Musu wannan Karan ma ta sa Masa number yayi saving dinta, Abinci yasa aka kawo Mata kamar mayuwanciya haka ta dinga ci hannu baka hannu kwarya tana ci me duniya na Mata video, tana shanyewa aka kai Mata ruwa ta Sha sai alokacin ta D'an ji dai-dai, me duniya ne ya kalleta yace, " ke na manta bani number tsohonki " wannan Karan ma sa mishi number tayi sai yayi saving , sawa yayi aka fito Masa da ita falo saboda ko tafiya Bata iyayi, akan kujera suka zaunar da ita daga ita sai pant da bra me duniya ya janyo kujera daga gabanta ya zauna, kuraye ne ke rik'e da waya Yana jiran umarnin me duniya Dan ya Fara d'aukan video Saudat, sai da ya gyara zama yayi sannan ya yiwa kuraye umarni, tambayar ta me duniya ya dinga yi tana bashi labari tun Daga lokacin da ta kirasu Akan aikin da zasuyi mata har zuwa sace Farida da tasa akayi har Video da tasa akayiwa Farida da turo Mata da sukayi, har zuwa plan din data shirya na karbar kudin fansar Farida da aikowar da tayi akashesu aka kashe abokan aikin sa, gaba d'aya yasata tana ta tonawa kanta asiri tana fad'a tana kuka tana sunkuyar da kanta k'asa Ola dake gefe zai d'aga wani tafkeken dank'o ya lafta Mata, bayan sun gama yi Mata video gaba d'aya tayi wuri-wuri duk ta jema atunanin Saudat me duniya zai barta ta tafi kamar yanda ya fad'a, cikin sanyin murya tace, " Dan Allah me duniya ka taimaka ka kaini gida " Danna wayarsa yake sai da ya gama turawa su Daddy da Sadiq gaba videos din har nasu ola da ita sannan ya d'ago da Kai ya kalleta yace, " karki damu Zan maida ki a yau din nan " wani dad'i ne ya ratsa zuciyar Saudat tana Nan zaune me duniya ya Kira Ola yace, " wannan wayar da mukayi video da ita ka dauketa inzaku maida Saudat gida Ku had'a ta da ita, sannan karku manta ku sa Mata kaifi " Ola da kuraye har sunyi gaba da Saudat suka juyo suka ce masa, " master kaifin wane iri " Kan Saudat ne ya kulle ta rasa gane wannan zaurancen da sukeyi, me duniya D'an Jim yayi yace, " kuyi Mata sama rabi k'asa rabi atakaice hannu da k'afa " tun Bai rufe Baki sukayi gaba Saudat wacce ta rasa bakin magana ta zubawa sarautar Allah ido.

        Alh karami sun tashi tsaye da rok'on Allah makarantun islamiyya data Allo Haka ya dinga bi Yana bada sadaka akan Allah ya biya Musu bukatansu musamman matsalar Sadiq da b'atan farida, haka b'angaren su Inna tun iya k'arfinsa Haka suka dage da tsaiwar dare ba dare ba Rana suna Mik'awa Ubangiji lamuransu, gaba d'aya sun zama abun tausayi kowacce daga cikinsu ta rame atsaye babu me cin abincin kirki.
       Mommy sai da suka shafe kusan minti Ashirin suna tafiya sannan suka hau Kan saman dutsen, suna hawa wata kofa suka gani me Jan ganye adaidai Nan Hjy Daso tacewa mommy, " ni daga Nan Zan dakata arnen dutse baya buk'atar D'an rakiya ki shiga za'ayi Miki bayanin komai " mommy a dan tsoroce zata Fara magana da sauri hjy Daso ta girgiza Mata Kai alamar kaita musu saboda duk Abinda suke fad'a akunnen sa, hakanan mommy ta shiga cikin kofar tana shiga wata katafariyar kujera ta gani zagaye da ita wasu Korai ne cike suke da jini wani haske ne taga ya dunkule a Kan kujerar sannan taga wuta ta Kama akai daga cikin wutar taga wani mutum ya bayyana siffarsa me abin tsoro, wad'ansu irin yare taji anayi Wanda ta rasa Wanda yakeyi, sai da akayi yare yakai kala biyar sannan taga wannan mutumin ya bushe da mahaukaciyar dariya yace, " nasan duk Abinda yake tafe dake Kuma buk'atar ki zata biya Amma da sharad'i, sharad'in kuwa shine, idan har aiki yaci Yarinyar ki ta dawo Zaki kawo Mana ita zamu tsaga jikinta mu Debi jininta mu bawa Aljanin da yayi aikin, Abu na gaba Kuma sai dai kiyi hakuri da duk yanda Zaki Sami yarki Dan bazakiji dad'in ganin irin illar da sukayi mata ba, Kuma bana buk'atar kud'in Daya fito daga gurin mijinki tunda ba y'arsa bace dole sai da kudin hannun mahaifinta Zan Mata aiki " daga Haka arnen dutse Bai Kara magana ba mommy Jim tayi tana D'an waige kafin tayi magana boka ya K'ara fashewa da dariya yace, "  kina tsoron Kar Aminiyarki hjy Daso taji ko saboda mijinta shine mahaifin yarki, karki damu ba abinda zataji saboda Nan ba duniyar bil adama bane tunda Kika shigo waccen kofar kin bar duniyarku har sai kin fita daga ciki shi ne Zaki je duniyar ku " mamaki ne ya kama mommy had'e da tsoronsa cikin sanyin jiki ta gyad'a kai, wad'ansu yarirrika ya dinga Yana gamawa ya kalli mommy yace, " mun tura gidan hjy Daso d'akin mijinta ga kudinsa ankawo Mana buk'atar ki ta gama biya saura tamu buk'atar idan Kika kauracewa alkawarinmu keda yarki zaku fuskanci mummunan hukunci, b'ata da tabewa sun tabbata agareki fice ki bamu guri " jikin mommy har karkarwa yake ba shiri ta doshi kofar data shigo, bayan fitarta suka gangara daga saman dutsen suka nufi wajen motar su har lokacin Bata daina mamakin arnen dutse ba.

       Farida ta Dade sosai tana bacci har zuwa lokacin basu K'arasa garin Abuja ba suna cikin daji, ji tayi gaba d'aya ta gaji da zaman, tana kallan MD wani haushinsa ne ya kamata shikuwa ko ajikinsa cikin  murmushi yace, " pretty kin gaji ko sannu kinsha zama Amma ay munkusa k'arasawa baifi Nan da 30 minutes ba " juya kanta gefe tayi ko kallansa Bata karayi ba, wajen kafarta taji ta shuri  kwalba tuni wani tunani yaxo mata sunkuyawa tayi kamar me Shirin gyara takalmin ta, da sauri ta d'auki kwalbar cikin zafin nama ta maka wa MD akansa kafin ya Ankara ta Kuma buga Masa ragowar kwalbar da tai saura a hannunta, motar ce ta kubce Musu ta fad'a daji Allah ya taimakesu motar sai ta bigi bishiya ta mutu Dan kanta hayak'i ne ya turnike motar cikin tashin hankali Farida ta kokarin bud'e glass din motar da kyar ta samu ta fito ta window lokacin tuni MD ya fice daga hayyacinsa jini sai fita yake daga kwnsa, gudu takeyi Bata San inda take jefa kafafuwanta ba tana zuwa titi ji kake kuuuuuuuuu lokaci d'aya na hango Farida agefen hanya kanta na zubda jini da alama tuni ta suma, mutumin dake driving ne ya Fara fitowa cikin tashin hankali yake Fad'in innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un, wasu dattijan ne suma suka fito da alama Mata da Miji ne cikin tashin hankali suna salallami, cak mutumin Nan ya d'auki farida cikin tashin hankali tsohuwar take Fad'in, " Asim Dan Allah kayi ka sata a mota mu nemi asibiti acikin garin Nan mu kaita " da hanzari yasa Farida da batasan ma me sukeyi ba cikin mota sannan ya tada motar suka wuce.

       _Karku manta Ashe dai Saudat ba y'ar Daddy bace 🙊 lallai akwai k'ura_🤣

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  65-66


              Sai da suka gangara bakin mota sannan hjy Daso ta cewa mommy, " ya Kika ga aikin boka inafatan kun gama magana dashi? " Mommy jikinta a sanyaye tace, " eh yace buk'ata har ta biya Amma abinda ya sanyaya mun jiki da yace mun inyi hakuri da duk yanda Zan samu Saudat, wallahi shi ne abun yake ban tsoro Kar inje ko wani abun sukayi Mata " hjy Daso itama shirun tayi saboda tasan halin boka Sarai idan yace maka abu ba ko tantama sai kaga abun ya bayyana, Amma Dan ta k'arfafi mommy sai tace Mata, " haba karki damu babu abinda zai sameta kinji, wuce mu K'arasa nasan tuni ya tura an d'aukota " mommy har lokacin jikinta ba kwari ta wuce mota itama hjy Daso ta shiga suka d'auki hanya gabanta sai fad'uwa yake yi.
       Sadiq na cikin bacci ji yayi kansa ya mugun Sara masa lokaci d'aya yaji wani irin zafi ya rufeshi tuni jikinsa ya hau wata irin karkarwa, bargo ya janyo ya rufa Yana rawar d'ari ya kusa awa guda a haka sannan yaji ya daina jin wannan zafin, kansa yayi wasai sai dai gumi da yake tayi har kayan jikinsa suka jik'e sharkaf ya jima a Haka sannan yaji idanunsa sunyi dummmm hade da wani d'an yaji-yaji, shikansa abun tsoro ya Fara bashi sai bayan wani lokaci yaji jikinsa yayi Masa garau dashi, tunane-tunane ya shiga yi kala daban-daban saboda ganin abun yake kamar amafarki, Abunda ya Fara Fad'o Masa azuciyarsa Umma, Yana gama ayyanota afili ya furta  " Farida " lokacin ne tunanin wani Abu mummuna akanta ya Fad'o Masa, tunawa yayi da maganar video ta kamar Wanda aka tsikara  azabure ya tashi ya d'auko wayarsa data shafe kwanaki akashe ya kunna, da sauri ya shiga gallery duk atunaninsa mafarki ne, dan ya tabbatar ne ya bud'e gallery Dan ya Duba, cin karo da yayi da hoton Farida ne yasa ya runtse idanunsa afili Yana furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un " Saudat ce ta Fad'o Masa D'an guntun tsaki yaja sannan ya Kira number ta akashe, number Umma ya Kira bugu biyu da shiga umma ta d'auka, " Sadiq Dina Kaine ka kirani da kanka? " D'an Jim yayi kamar me nazari sannan yace, " Eh nine Umma " saboda farin ciki Umma fashewa tayi da kuka, kasa magana yayi sai dai Sam baiji dad'in abinda yaji daga gurin Umma ba, ahankali yace, " Umma Ina Farida " kamar subutar Baki shi kansa Saida gabansa ya Fad'i lokacin da yayi tambayar, cikin kuka Umma tace, " Bata Nan Sadiq wani Abokinka ya sace Mana ita, tun alokacin mun kiraka yafi a irga wayarka akashe...." Katse Umma yayi cikin tashin hankali, " what? Wane abokin nawa?" Cikin shashshek'ar kuka Umma tace, " Abokinka Babangida Mana" tunani yayi kaf Abokansa baida me suna Babangida, cikin sauri yacewa Umma, " Kashe Umma bani mintuna kad'an Ina zuwa, Umma gani take abun kamar amafarki Amma da taji yace takashe sai take ganin kamar Sadiq zai Kuma guje Mata, gaba d'aya hankanlinsa ya tashi dukda yanajin haushin ta Amma maganar Umma ta dake shi ganin maganar yake kamar Almara wai ansace Farida, da sauri ya shirya ya fice Kai tsaye gurin aiki yaje ya d'auki excuse da farko Bai Sami yanda yake so ba sai da kyar sannan ya samu suka bashi sati d'aya, daga Nan ticket din jirgi ya siya ya Fara had'a kayan da zai tafi dasu.

          Kamar yanda me duniya ya bawa su Ola umarni tafiya sukayi da Saudat suka d'add'aure ajikin bishiya, Saudat gaba d'aya ta gigice da ganin irin abinda zasu aikata Mata, ta saki fitsari agurin yafi uku kuka take harda majina fuskarta gaba d'aya tayi jage-jage, kuraye ne ke rik'e da wata zabgegiyar Adda sai shek'i takeyi, ganin Haka ba k'aramin haukata Saudat yayi ba, haukace Musu tayi da kuka da ihu tana neman d'auki, iya k'arfinsa ya dage ya tattakura ya datsewa Saudat kafad'a, wani irin azababben ihu ta kurma gaba d'aya jikinta ya saki badan adaure take ba da tuni ta sulale a k'asa sumammiya, su Ola na tsaye wani bak'in hadari suka gani ya taso ya mulmule gurin da suke, daga cikin hadarin ne wata iska me zafin gaske ta tunkaro gurunsu ganin Haka yasa suka Fara niyyar arcewa, Amma tuni ta cimmusu Haka ta dinga d'aukan su d'aya bayan d'ayansu tana bugawa da k'asa kafin wani lokaci tuni tayi filla-filla dasu kowanne anraba kansa da gangar jiki, sai dai kaga cen kafa cen hannun cen gangar jiki, guguwar gurin Saudat ta koma ta nannad'eta cikin k'iftawar ido ta d'auke ta daga gurin.

         Kai tsaye Asim police station ya wuce  yayi report, sai da aka yi rubuce-rubuce sannan aka had'asu da police suka wuce Hospital, sosai likitoci suka nuna kukawarsu akan Farida sai da suka shafe lokaci me tsayi sannan suka Sami nasarar tsaida jinin da ke zuba daga kanta ba, Asim na ganin Dr ya shiga office da sauri ya bishi da sallama ya shiga office din nasa, tun kafin Asim yayi magana Dr ya sharce gumin da tsatstsafo daga goshinsa yace, " Alhamdulillah mlm Bismillah" murmushi Asim yayi yace, " mun gode Allah likita Ina fatan babu wata damuwa " Jim kadan Dr yayi yace, " gaskiya akwai damuwa nasarar da mukayi cikin jikinta na Nan Bai zube ba sai dai Yana barazanar zubewa idan har ta kuma fuskantar tashin hankali, Abu na gaba sanadiyyar buguwar da tayi ta samu lalura...... " Cikin tashin hankali Asim ya katse Dr, " likita badai haukacewa tayi ba? " Girgiza Kai Dr yayi yace wa Asim, " Ba haukacewa tayi ba sai dai bazata iya tuna abunda ya faru da ita abaya ba, har sai taga wani Abu da zai razanata ko Kuma ta Sami shigen makamancin irin wannan  buguwar, ko taga wani Wanda take matuk'ar kauna ko Kuma Wanda Take matuk'ar tsoro, Amma yanxu idan ta farko babu abinda zata iya tunawa sai dai duk Abinda kukace tayi shi zatayi Wanda kukace ta Bari shi zata bari " wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya  Asim ya sauke Yana goge gumin daya keto Masa, kasa magana yayi ahankali yace, " Allah ya Bata lafiya " jiki a sanyaye Asim ya taso ya tafi gurun da tsofaffun Nan suke zaune yace, " Abba sannunku da hutawa " cikin had'in Baki suka Fara tambayar sa yanda sukayi da likita, duk Abinda Dr ya fad'a Masa shi ya zayyanawa su Abba, gaba d'aya suma jikinsu yayi sanyi asanyaye suka ce, " Allah ya Bata lafiya ya tashi kafad'unta " ya amsa da, " Aameen " kallan tsohuwar yayi yace, " Ummee Bari na maida ku gida inyaso sai su Rukayya su dinga zuwa suna zama da ita" ba Musu Haka Asim ya kwashi su Ummee ya maidasu gida, sai da sukaje gidan ne ma sannan suka sanar dasu halin da suka tsinci kansu aciki, gaba d'aya yayye da kannen Asim Haka suka jimanta abinda ya faru sannan Ummee tace," Rukayya da Halima  zaku dinga zuwa da safe zuwa dare Kuna zama agurinta, Fahima da rumaisa kuma ku dinga kwana da ita" cikin girmamawa suka amsa Mata.


              Bayan la'asar Umma da Inna suna zaune kamar amafarki sukaji sallamar Sadiq, daga Inna har Umma rasa me magana akayi daga cikinsu saboda abun ya matuk'ar basu mamaki, shi kansa Sadiq duk sai yaji wani irin kunya ta kamashi, har k'asa ya tsugunna ya gaida su Umma tuni hawaye ya Fara gangarowa daga idan Umma ita kanta Inna wani irin yanayi ta shiga Faridanta ce ta Fad'o Mata, tuni hawaye ya wanke fuskarta gwanin tausayi bayan sun gaisa yace wa Umma, " D'azu naji kinamun bayani Umma me yake faruwa? " Umma labari ta Fara bashi tun Daga d'auko farida daga gidansa har kwanciyar ta a asibiti, da zuwan Wanda yace shi abokinsa ne lokaci d'aya Umma taji haushin Sadiq ya kamata da ta tuno Sakin da Farida tace yayi Mata, cikin b'acin Rai ta cigaba da cewa, " ka bani mamaki, ka bani mamaki Sadiq Ina hankalinka da tunaninka dama duk soyayyar ta karya ce da kake nunawa Farida, karshe ka yi Mana wulakanci ka watsa Mana k'asa a ido ka saketa wallahi da nasan Haka zakayiwa Farida da ko zakayi me bazan yarda ta aureka ba, tunda baka buk'atar ta arayuwarka gashi Nan ay yanxu Bata Nan sai kuci karenku babu babbaka kaida waccen fitsararriyar Yarinyar Saudat, hmmmmm Kai yanxu Sadiq ko tirsasaka akayi ka saki Farida ka sake ta, yanda take kaunarka ayshikenan babu komai mudai fatanmu Allah ya bayyana Mana ita " Sadiq sai lokacin Umma da ta rufeshi da fad'a sannan ya tuno duk Abinda ya faru har sakin da yayiwa Farida ta waya, ji yayi gabansa na dukan uku-uku cikin sanyi murya zai fara magana Umma ta katseshi cikin tsawa saboda yanda ranta ya gama b'aci jikinta har rawa yake saboda fad'a, Inna ce ta dafa hannun Sadiq tace, "  Sadiq mu K'arasa ciki ka ajiye Jakarka ka huta muje sai na d'ora maka ruwan wanka, ba Musu Haka yabi bayan Inna jiki a sanyaye, da kanta ta karbi jakarsa tasa Masa a d'akin Umma sannan ta d'ora Masa ruwa, har zuwa lokacin Umma Bata daina fad'a ba saboda abun yanxu ne ya dawo Mata sabo, bayan yayi wanka abinci Inna ta Kai Masa kocali daya yasa abakinsa yaji Abincin ya fita daga kansa kamar karamin yaro Haka ya fashe da kuka Yama rasa da wanne zaiji, abun duniya duk yabi ya isheshi.

           Har su mommy suka shigo cikin garin Kano Bata da wani walwala, har suka k'araso gida horn take yiwa me gadi ba jimawa yaje ya bud'e Musu kofar, da gudu ta karasa ciki agajiye suka bud'e suka fito daga motar, ahankali suka K'arasa ga mamaki jini suka hanga akan tyes mommy ce Kan gaba suna karasawa Saudat suka hango yashe akwance cikin jini da gudu mommy ta K'arasa gurunta tana kwallah Mata Kira, cikin kuka ta d'auki Saudat ta rungume tana jijjiga ta ita kanta hjy Daso mamaki da tsoro ne ya kamata ganin Saudat cikin jini ba hannu d'aya, Daddy dake falo azaune sama-sama yake jin kamar ihun mommy, yaji shigowar motarta Amma Bata K'arasa ciki ba da sauri ya tashi ya bud'e kofar falon, turus yayi cikin tashin hankali ya kama Saudat shida mommy suka maidata cikin mota da gudun gaske Daddy yayi hospital da ita, hjy Daso jikinta ya gama sanyi motarta ta d'auka tabi bayansu saboda sunyi Mata nisa wayar mommy ta Kira ta tambaye ta hospital din da aka Kai Saudat.
         Kafin su K'arasa hospital din tuni Daddy ya Kira commissioner of police din ya Gaya Masa kusan tare suka K'arasa asibitin AKTH  cikin gaggawa aka karb'i Saudat emergency aka shiga da ita mommy da Daddy zuciyarsu gaba d'aya a jagule take, hawaye daddy ya share cikin zuciyarsa yake jin bak'in cikin abinda aka aikata wa Saudat, mommy ce ta karaso gurin Daddy cikin kuka tace, " shikenan sun cirewa Saudat hannu wallahi ko duniya ce gatansu sai anhukunta tsinannanun yaran Nan, me tayi Musu me ta tsare musu da alama duk y'an shegu ne matsiyata y'ay'an karuwai......" Hannu Daddy yasa abakin mommy ya katse sauran maganar da zatayi, Yana riketa tana ta banbamin fad'a da alama Bata sanin duk abubuwan da takeyi, mutane har sun Fara taruwa suna kallonsu daddy yaja mommy suka koma mota.


        Me duniya na zaune jin shirun nasu Ola yayi yawa yasa ya tashi ya fita tafiya yayi me nisan gaske sannan ya karasa gurin tun Kan ya karasa ya hango gurin duk amutse kamar Wanda akayi dambe agurin, ji yayi ya yi tuntub'e da Abu Yana kallan kafarsa yaga  kan kuraye da saurin gaske ya matsa baya cikin tashin hankali, sai alokacin ya dinga gano sauran jikinsu idan yaga kai cen sai yaga kafa da hannunwa anmusu filla-filla, cikin tashin hankali me duniya ya fita aguje cikin gidan ya koma jakar kud'in sa ya d'auka cikin nadama da Dana sanin kasancewar sa D'an fashi me miyagun hali ya tafi Tasha zai koma cen garinsu, shi kansa tunani yake ko zai gane gidansu tunda rabonsa da gida kusan shekara goma Sha takwas tunda aka kawo shi almajiranci ya gudu daga gurin malamin su.

    
       Kwanan Farida biyu a hospital ta farfard'o, da ido take binsu d'aya bayan d'aya tana kallan su sai jero Mata sannu sukeyi babu Wanda ta amsawa, kulawa dai-dai gwargwado suna bawa Farida, tana Shan magunguna da allura jikinta na Kara warwarewa, duk Wanda ya ce mata sannu sai dai ta kalleshi tayi Masa murmushi, Rukayya ce ta koya Mata cewa yauwa da taji mutum yace Mata sannu zatayi karaf ta amsa da yauwa, ahankali duk ta gane su da sunayen su saboda yanda suka ja ta ajiki basa kyamatarta kamar y'ar uwarsu Haka suke jinyarta tsakani da Allah.
         Lokacin da Farida ta cika kwana hudu a asibiti fahima na zaune suna Hira take tambayar Farida, " Aunty meye sunanki na aynihi " Dan Jim Farida tayi sai ta girgizawa fahima Kai murmushi fahima tayi tace to duk Wanda ya Kara tambayar ki kice sunanki kice Masa sunanki, " Humaira " cikin jin dad'i Farida tayi murmushi tana gyad'a wa Fahima Kai, tun Daga ranar duk Wanda yace Mata meye sunanki sai dai ta ce masa Humaira, jikinta yayi kyau kwarai ta warware dinkin dake kanta ne kawai yayi saura.
             Satin Farida biyu a hospital suka sallameta daga asibiti gida Ya Asim ya wuce dasu, gidan su Asim ba laifi suna da kud'i dai-dai gwargwado, Haka suka cigaba da kula da Farida suna Bata kulawa, Asim ba inda Bai Kai cigiyar Farida ba har gidajen radio ko Allah zaisa asamu iyayenta ko mijinta Amma shiru babu labari, Haka suka hakura suka zubawa sarautar Allah ido, suna cigaba da kula da Farida, ita kanta Farida ta saki jiki dasu sosai musamman ya Asim da yake nannan da ita duk shopping din da zashi da ita yake zuwa dasu halima, suma Kuma Ummee da Abba kulawa suke nuna Mata kamar y'arsu ta cikinsu ahaka har cikin Farida ya Kara girma, ya fito sosai kamar ba cikin fari ba lokacin ya shiga bakwai, shiyasa ma Asim ya rage fita da ita saboda tayi nauyi sai dai ya tambaye su itada su Rumaisa abinda suke so sai ya siyo Musu.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   67&68

_Not Edited_


             Alh karami Yana zaune gaban wani aramma sun gama tattaunawa game da matsalar Sadiq, wani ruwan Addu'a aramma ya d'auko acikin wata jarka, bayani ya Fara yiwa Alh karami, " Alh shi wannan tofin da kake gani yaron da ka gayamun zaku sashi gaba kullin yasha Kofi uku d'aya safe d'aya Rana d'aya dare, zakuyi Haka har tsawon kwana uku cikin ikon Allah duk wani kaidi da sihiri da aka aikata akansa zai warware, Amma fa sai kunyi da gaske saboda ba'asan a tsallake Koda na wuni d'aya ne " wata Yar jarkar swan aramma ya d'auko ya cigaba da cewa, " wannan Kuma a take zaku bashi ita ya shanyen tofi ne Wanda aka had'a da ganyen magarya, ga wannan ganyen ku dinga tafasa Masa ita yanayin wanka, Abu na gaba da Zan jadadda Muku ku gargad'eshi da ya dage da azkar ya dage da ibada, agaskiya harda Sakaci da Addu'a ansan sihiri babu Wanda baya kamawa tunda anyiwa annabinmu Annabi Muhammad (S.A.W) Kuma ya kamashi, to Amma idan kana azkar Koda anyi maka shi abun zaizo maka cikin sauk'i Allah ya tsare mu ya karemu daga sharrin masu sharri, mugun abin su ya koma Musu cen kansu Allah ya Kara tsaremu da imaninmu Baki d'aya yasa muyi kyakykyawan karshe " jiki a sanyaye Alh karami ya amsa da, " Aameen " sannan ya cigaba da cewa, " Allah gafarta malam wani hanzari ba gudu ba, maganar da nake maka yaron baya garin Nan har gurin aiki ya canja Amma mahaifiyar sa ma Bata sani ba " girgiza Kai Aramma yayi yace, " Kai Allah shi kyauta, Amma kuwa dole ku binciki gurin aikin nasa tunda wannan tofin da kake gani Yana da matuk'ar amfani " Alh karami na Shirin magana wayarsa ta Fara ringing,  ciro wayar yayi number Umma ya gani kallan aramma yayi yace, " kaga uwar yaron ce ma take kirana Bari naji daga gareta ".
         Alh karami da sallama ya d'auki wayar Umma tunda ta yaji alamun muryar ta tana cikin farin ciki, bayan sun gaisa take sanar dashi dawowar Sadiq, cikin jin dad'i suka kammala wayar tas ya sanarwa da aramma duk yanda sukayi da umma, shi dinma farinciki yayi afili Yana tayasu murna, atake Alh karami ya kwashi jarkar Addu'ar da aramma ya bashi Kai tsaye gidan umma ya nufa.

        Lokacin da ya shiga gidan har an idar da sallar magriba, gaisawa sukayi gaba d'aya har Inna, Sadiq na gefe arakub'e ya sunkuyar da Kai k'asa atunaninsa wani sabon fad'an Alh k'aramin zai K'ara yi Masa, Amma ga mamaki sai yaji Yana ta yiwa su Umma bayanin tofin Daya karb'o, atake ya Mik'awa Sadiq na y'ar k'aramar robar yace ya shanye, ba Musu Sadiq ya karb'a ya shanye tass tun kafin ya ajiye robar jikinsa ya hau karkarwa har ya gagara zama kwanciya yayi da sauri Umma zata Kai hannu jikinsa Alh k'aramin ya dakatar da ita, bayani wani lokaci Sadiq ya daina karkarwar ahankali ya yunkura ya tashi zaune jikinsa duk yayi gumi.
      A fili Alh k'aramin ya furta, " Alhamdulillah Allah ya Kara tsarewa ya kare mu Baki d'aya " duk nasihar da Aramma yayi Masa Haka ya zayyanawa Sadiq sannan ya d'ora da tashi nasihar da Jan kunnensa.
       Jikin Sadiq gaba d'aya yayi sanyi wata irin nutsuwa ya k'araji tana ratsa jikinsa, jin jikinsa yake kamar wani Sabon Mutum, duk nasihar da Alh k'aramin yayi Masa ta ratsa shi sai a lokacin shima yaga illar da yayiwa kansa game da rashin Azkar din da baya yi ba.


         Da kyar da lallashi Daddy ya shawo Kan mommy ta daina surutai ba, wayar hjy Daso ce ta shigo cikin shashshek'ar kuka ta d'auka, " Hello " daga cen b'angaren hjy Daso ta amsa, " hjy Kuna daga wane b'angaren na k'araso cikin Asibitin " cikin sanyin murya mommy ta ce, " muna emergency " kashewa hjy Daso tayi ta K'arasa emergency, futowa tayi daga motar tana D'an dube-dube da alama mommy take nema, Daddy dake cikin mota ya cewa mommy, " ga hjy Daso cen jeki gurunta Dan Allah ki had'iye damuwarki kinsan komai Allah ya k'addarawa bawansa Yana daga cikin jarrabawarsa, Addu'a zamu cigaba da yi mata Allah ya Bata lafiya" mommy Bata ce uffan ba ta bud'e motar ta fita, tare da hjy Daso suka gangara bakin wata bishiya ita kanta hjy Daso ta tausayawa mommy ganin halin da take ciki, lallashinta ta yi da banbaki har mommy ta D'an saki jikinta.
         Ba'a nemi su Daddy ba sai wajen k'arfe goma Sha biyu na dare lokacin har anyiwa Saudat dressing ansa Mata oxygen numfashinta ya dawo dai-dai, lokacin da su mommy suka K'arasa bakin godan da Saudat take kwance, sun dad'e tsaye akanta mommy sai goge hawaye take shi kansa Daddy ganin da yayi wa Saudat k'arfin hali yakeyi hawaye ya ciko idansa Amma gudun Kar mommy ta samu dama ya had'iye kwallarsa.
       Nurse ce ta k'araso gurunsu bayan ta tambaye su ya me jiki tace wa daddy, " Alh Kaine mahaifin patient din ko? " Da sauri Daddy ya amsa, wasu rubutu tayi a file din Saudat sannan tace, " idan na koma office inasan magana da Kai " amsa Mata Daddy ya karayi, jinin da aka sawa Saudat ne ya kare ta cire Mata shi sannan ta sa Mata ruwa ta zuba wasu allurai aciki sannan ta wuce office dinsu.

         Duk wasu tambayoyi da ake buk'ata daga gurin Daddy da mommy duk sun bada had'in Kai, su Daddy ne suka dinga fasaltawa y'an sanda yanayin me duniya da fasalinsa, wayar Saudat suka karb'a saboda tsaurara bincikensu Dan ganin sun kamo me duniya da tawagarsa.
        Daddy har office yabi  nurse din sannan ta Fara yi masa bayani, " Alh Yarinyar ku ta zubda jini me tsanani Allah ne ma yayi da kwananta agaba, sannan akowanne yanayi zamu iya K'ara bukatar wani jinin " jinjina Kai daddy yayi yace, " baku da matsala duk Abinda ake da buk'ata ayimun magana " nurse din ta amsa da, " ok yayi kyau " sannan ta cigaba da cewa, " Alh ita wannan patient din tana da aure ne ko babu, saboda akwai gwajin da mukayi Mata ya Bamu positive so munasan ganin mijinta saboda shima muna da buk'atar tattaunawa dashi kuma muyi Masa irin nata test d'in " Kan Daddy ne ya kulle cikin rashin fahimta yace, " wane irin test ne wannan da har za'ayiwa mijinta " shi Daddy saboda tashin hankali ma ya manta Bai Kira Sadiq ya sanar dashi halin da Saudat take ciki ba, nurse din ce ta ajiye biron hannunta tace, " am sorry to say Alh Yarinyar ku tana d'auke da HIV Amma baiyi karfi ajikinta ba Kuma......." Cikin tashin hankali Daddy ya katseta cikin tsawa, " What, What are u  Trying to say, kina nufin Saudat my daughter tana da kanjamau, This is nonsense and impossible kinsan me kike fad'a kuwa??? " Ita kanta nurse din ta tsorata da yanda taga Daddy ya rufeta da fad'a, ana cikin Haka, Dr din dake Duba Saudat ya shigo nurse din ce ta Fara yi Masa bayanin maganar da sukeyi da saudat, ya fahimci tashin hankalin da Daddy ya shiga ne yasa har ya rufe nurse din da fad'a, Dr Faruq ne ya cewa nurse, " ki bani guri zamuyi magana dashi " kamar me jira da sauri ta tashi ta fice, kujera Dr. Faruq ya ja ya zauna sannan ya nunawa Daddy kujera yace,  " Have a Sit " ba Musu Daddy yaja kujera ya koma ya zauna, cikin lallami da kwantar da hankali Dr. Faruq ya zayyanawa Daddy bayanin ciwon Saudat, share gumin fuskarsa yayi afili ya furta, " tabbas sai dai idan mijinta ne ya shafa Mata Amma y'ata na Bata tarbiyya dai-dai gwargwado, wallah kotu ce zata rabani da shi D'an Iskan yaro ahaka kamar na Allah Ashe abayan fage fasiki ne "  cikin tausayawa Dr. Faruq yace, " Alh ka Kira shi muna buk'atar ganinsa Amma Dan Allah abi komai cikin laluma bada tashin hankali ba " Daddy ko ta Kan Dr baibi ba fuuuuu ya fice daga office din.

           Bayan komawar hjy Daso gida Alh muntari ta samu zaune da laptop Yana dannawa, agajiye ta zauna Kan kujera jikinta duk a sanyaye, kallanta yayi Yana ture laptop din gefe yace, " wannan duk gajiyar ce Haka Nan da gidan Alh Kamal din shi ne har kikayi wannan gajiyar haks? " Cikin damuwa hjy Daso ta d'ago ta ce wa Alh muntari, " Alh daga asibiti nake " cikin halin ko inkula yace, " waye ba lafiya? " D'an kwalin kanta ta zame da kitson kanta kwaya hudu sai shafa y'an jelolin kitson da suke mak'ale a keya take sannan tace, " Allah ne ya saukarwa su Alh Kamal iftila'i wallahi Saudat ce......" Tun Bata K'arasa ba cikin tashin hankali Alh muntari ya katse ta, " me ya samu Saudat din?" Tun Daga labarin da mommy ta bawa hjy Daso na sace sun da akayi har zuwa ganin da suka yiwa Saudat yashe cikin jini ta bashi labari, katseta yayi cikin fushi Yana banbamin fad'a, " meyasa tunda ya faru Baki Gaya mun? " Hjy Daso Bata komai a ranta ba tace, " ta bani Amana ne tace karna gayawa kowa...." Azafafe ya katse ta, " Amanar banza da wofi wallahi sai na d'auki mataki, na rantse da girman Allah duk Wanda ya yiwa Saudat wannan abun sai ya gane kuransa ko waye shi Kuma ko D'an dingan uban waye yau sai Naga gatansa a garin Nan, yanxu shikenan annakasta ta wallahi-wallahi sai na bawa kowa mamaki jinin Saudat bazai tafi hakan Nan abanza ba " Yana gama maganar ya d'auki key din mota yayi gaba daga shi sai jallabiya.
          Hjy Daso ido ta zura Masa har ya fice daga falon gaba d'aya mamaki ya Bata tafa hannuwa take afili tace, " yau Naga ikon Allah ji yanda yake hak'ik'ancewa sai kace shi ne ubanta, Kai ko ubanta ma ya fauwalawa Allah lamuransa Amma Kai abokin ubanta sai tada jijiyoyin wuya kake yi" komawa tayi kujera ta kwanta saboda ba k'aramin gajiya tayi ba.

        Daddy d'akin da Saudat ke kwance ya koma jiki a sanyaye, mommy na ganinsa ta taso tana tambayar abinda suka sanar masa da farko yaso b'oye Mata Amma ganin boyewar baida fa'ida yasa yayiwa mommy bayani dallah-dallah, d'ora hannu tayi aka ta kurma ihu da sauri nurse suka k'araso gurin Dan atunaninsu patient ce ta farka, Daddy ne ya dinga lallashinta Yana Bata baki da kyar ya samu tayi shiru, fita yayi daga gurin ya koma cen bakin wata bishiya ya had'a Kai da gwiwa, bayan fitar Daddy Alh muntari ne yakira Mommy tana d'auka ya rufeta da masifa ta inda yake shiga ba ta Nan yake fita ba, shiru tayi daga karshe yace ta kwatanta Masa b'angaren da suke a cikin AKTH din, ba Musu ta kwatanta Masa gabanta sai dukan uku-uku yake, saboda tasan halin Alh muntari sarai dama ya Jima Yana Mata zancen karbar Saudat da boka da malamai take rufe masa Baki gudun Kar asirinta ya tonu agurin Daddy, bayan wasu mintuna Alh muntari ne ya shiga cikin hospital din duk Wanda yaga yanayin da yake ciki zai fahimci Yana cikin tashin hankali, tura kofar yayi da k'arfin gaske abakin kofar ya tsaya hannunsa rik'e da kofar, idanunsa ne suka Kai Kan Saudat dake Kan gado da hannu d'aya, juyawa yayi ya kalli mommy ya Fara zazzaga Mata masifa, Daddy ne ya shigo Dan ya d'auki wayarsa ya Kira Sadiq ga mamaki Yana zuwa wajen d'akin da Saudat take yake jiyo Sautin muryar Alh muntari da wasu kalamai da Bai gane su ba, maganar da yaji mommy nayi ce tasashi suman tsaye cikin tsoro da mamaki ya jingina da window d'akin Yana jin karashen zancen.

       Farida ta murje tayi k'iba sosai hasken fatarta ya Kara fitowa kamar ba ita ba jikinta kamar ka tab'a jini ya fito, Ummee tausayin Farida da kaunarta ne ya mamaye zuciyarta shiyasa take nunawa Farida kulawa, a babban private hospital Abba yasa ake bud'ewa file na Anti-nal, Asim da Rukayya ne ke Mata rakiya da daddare tayi awon cikinta ta dawo, scanning din da tayi guda biyu duka ya nuna twins ne sai dai na farko ya nuna duk maza ne na karshen Kuma ya nuna mace da namiji ne, murna wajen mutanen gidan yak'i musaltuwa kowa da irin hidimar da yake mata daga ci sai wanka iya abinda takeyi kenan, sai bayan magariba su Rumaisa su rakata motsa k'afa, danma Ummee tayi da gaske da farko farida cewa take ta gaji sai da Ummee ta Mata dabara sannan take yarda su fita, yau ma kamar kullin Farida na zaune bayan tayi magriba tana Shan kankana Rumaisa ta shigo da mayafi ahannunta, Farida na ganin Haka tasan abinda suke nufi shiyasa ta tsuke fuska tayi ma kamar Bata gansu ba, sai da ta bawa kowa dariya har Ummee ta gane nufin farida, Ummee ce tace, " kawata tashi ku je ki D'an tattaka Mana " turo Baki Farida tayi da tulelen cikinta agaba tace, " Ummee wallahi ciwon k'afa nake abari sai gobe kawai " Halima ce tayi karaf tace, " haba humaira ki daure Mana bakiji me Dr yace ba " mirsisi tayi Musu ta cigaba da shan kankanar ta " ganin da gaske Farida take yasa Ummee ta shiga lallab'a ta da kyar Farida ta yunkura ta tashi har suna Mata tsiyar, gaba d'aya family su Asim ba Wanda baisan da Farida ba, Kuma ko had'uwa akayi babu me nuna Mata wariya Haka Kuma basa tab'a barinta ta tafi guri ita kad'ai acewar Abba Amana ce ita agaresu.

         Mommy na zaune taga Fad'owar Alh muntari azabure ta tashi tana D'an waige ganin babu kowa yasa tace Masa, " Alh shigo Mana " azafafe ya ce, " bazan shigo ba, shashashar banza da wofi, su waye sukayiwa 'yata haka " jiki na rawa mommy ta zayyanawa Alh muntari aykuwa ya shiga banbamin ta inda yake shiga ba ta Nan yake fita ba daga karshe yace Mata, " tunda kink'i ki kaso auren naki ki dawo gareni ke da 'yata wallahi a yau basai gobe ba tafiya zanyi da ita, kinsan dai Tun Saudat na Yarinya nace Zan karb'e ta kikak'i ko to yau babu ruwana da mijin naki yaji da kar yaji uwarsu d'aya ubansu d'aya " mommy itama haushi ne ya kamata tace, " Kai muntari baka isa is tonan asiri ba kamanta shekarun baya da kamun cikin ba cewa kayi na zubar ba, nace maka bazan zubarba sai kace na likawa mijina idan na haihu sai muyi aure........" Cikin tsawa yace,
          " da Kika haihun me ya Hana ki aurenin" harara mommy ta watsamai tace, " kud'i saboda Alh Kamal yafika kud'i nesa ba kusa ba, yanxu ne dai kaima ka faso gari ayanxu ko kisa ne Ina iya kashe Alh Kamal Dan in auri uban y'ata " Daddy dake jikin window gabansa ne yake dukan uku-uku, saboda tashin hankali jikinsa har karkarwa yake, mommy ce to cigaba da cewa, " dama tuni na had'a Kan takardun kadarorinsa Dana kamfaninnikansa suna d'aki na sai ka saki hjy Daso mu haure ruwa mu fice k'asar waje ".

_za kusha kurakurai ban tace shi ba._

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   69&70

     _kuyi hakuri da yanda zaku sameshi._

       Tun lokacin da Farida ta bugawa MD kwalba aka hayak'i ne ya turnike motar ya jima cikin wannan halin ne ya Fara farfard'owa hayak'i ne ya turnike shi daga gaban motar wutace ta Fara ci, gaba d'aya jikin MD Babu kwarin da zai iya nemarwa kansa mafita, tari yake tun iya k'arfin sa  har numfashinsa ya d'auke, kafin wani lokaci wuta ta Kama gaba d'aya motar babu abinda ya tsira aciki.
   
         Daddy ya Jima a tsaye Yana sauraron duk Abinda su mommy suke tattaunawa, jikinsa har karkarwa yake saboda bak'in ciki ransa ya mugun b'aci, mamaki ne ya gama kasheshi cikin zuciyarsa yake tunanin dama akwai ranar da Hjy Zata nuna Masa *TSANTSAR BUTULCI* jikinsa ne ya yi sanyi tsoro ya kamashi afili ya furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un ni zata kashe ta kwashe dukiyata batare da sanina ba, Allah na gode maka da fallasa mun wannan kullin b'oyen da bansan dashi ba " juyawa yayi Kai tsaye motarsa ya shiga da gudun gaske ya figeta gudu yake kamar zai tashi sama, lokacin da ya karasa gida horn yake kamar zai b'alle kofar, da sauri me gadi ya bud'e Masa shi atunaninsa ma ko Saudat ce Rai yayi halinsa tunda yaga yanda aka fita da ita.
       Turo motar sa Daddy yayi da gudun gaske ya shiga ciki, ko K'arasa kashe motar Daddy baiyi ba ya fita da sauri, part d'in mommy ya nufa cikin bedroom d'inta yake bincikawa drower ta ya bud'e Yana bincike wani Dan file ya gani da sauri ya zaro shi, bud'ewa yayi yana dubawa ga mamakinsa duk takardun filayensa ne dana kamfani, had'asu yayi ya kwaso ya koma part dinsa ya bud'e wata katuwar Akwatunsa wasu takaddu ya dinga deb'owa Yana had'awa sannan ya janyo kofar ya fito, Yana zuwa get d'akin maani me ya gadi ya kwankwasa Masa kofa, Yana fitowa wasu kudaden ya deb'o masu yawan gaske Wanda shi kansa baisan yawansu ba agurguje yayiwa me gadin bayani na sallamar da yayi Masa, maani harda hawayen sa saboda yanda yakejin dadin aiki acikin gidan saboda Daddy mutumin kirki ne akwai Jan mutane ajiki gashi baya wulakanta na k'asa dashi.

      Maani d'aki ya shiga ya had'o kayansa acikin jaka ya fito Yana tafe ya waiwayen gidan, ganin talatainin dare yayi yasa maani ya kwankwasa wani gida da Kaninsa yake gadi aciki, bud'e Masa yayi da mamaki yake tambayar sa ko lafiya sai da ya zayyane Masa sannan ya bashi guri ya kwanta.
    
        Daddy takardun sa yasa cikin mota sannan yasa key ya rufe get din Kai tsaye number commissioner of police ya Kira ya zayyane Masa duk Abinda yaji sannan yace Masa su had'u acen asibitin AKTH, suna gama waya da commissioner Miss call din mommy ya gani ba adadi, guntun tsaki Yaja da kamar bazai d'auka ba sai wata zuciyarta tasa shi ya d'auka, da sallama ya d'auka daga b'angaren mommy ta amsa, " Daddy tun d'azu nake kiransa baka d'auka ba Ina ka shiga ne ga nurses suna tambayar ka " Daddy ya amsa, " eh wayar Bata kusa Amma gani Nan Ina hanya " cikin kissa mommy tace, " Alh na adaren Nan yanxu fa biyu ta wuce na dare fa " Dan Jim Daddy yayi kamar bazai tanka Mata ba sai yace, " eh ay babu Abinda zai taru " mommy tace, " to Allah kawo ka lafiya" Daddy Bai Bata amsa ba ya kashe wayar Yana jadadda k'arfin hali irin na mommy.

       Lokacin da Daddy ya K'arasa tuni commissioner da yaransa sun Riga Daddy zuwa, Asibitin fayau yake babu mutane sosai sai d'aid'aiku Daddy ne Kan gaba sai Kuma y'an sandan da suke biye dashi, lokacin da suka shiga nurses din da ke duty binsu sukayi da kallo nocking Daddy yayi mommy da saurinta ta taso ta bud'e tana Masa barka da zuwa, turus tayi ganin y'an sanda abayan Daddy jinin jikinta Tasha cikin in'ina ta Fara magana, " Daddyn Saudat lafiya na ganka da.........." Cikin zafin nama daddy ya kifeta da Mari kafin ta K'arasa d'agowa ya K'ara kifa Mata wani Marin ransa ya gama b'aci tana kokarin magana Daddy yayi ball da ita harsai da maku da jikin gadon da Saudat take bacci, commissioner ne ya Fara yiwa Daddy magana cikin lallashi, " easy sir karka d'auki doka ahannu ka barmu zamu hukuntata dai-dai da laifin da ta aikata, cikin huci Daddy yace,
            " Officer da ka barni da ita na illatata tunda ni kashe ni take sanyi matsiyaciya kije na sakeki saki uku, Kuma babu ni babu ke har abada nida ke kotu ce zata rabani " mommy shiru tayi babu bakin magana tuni suka tasa keyar ta Daddy sai huci yake, saudat na ganin za'a tafi da mommy ta Fara kuka tana Kiran Daddy har aka fice da ita, Daddy tausayin Saudat ne yakamashi kwallah ta ciko idon sa ganin zai iya zubda hawayensa yasa ya turo kofar shima ya fice daga d'akin, Saudat kuka ta fashe dashi me tsuma zuciya, wani irin sauti taji azabure ta d'ago kanta tsoro ne ya kamata ganin wani irin hallita daga bakin kofa na tunkaro cikin d'akin, hallitar jikinta irin na mutane ne kanta irin na saniya hannuwanta d'auke suke da y'an yatsu uku-uku kafafunta irin na doki, idanunsa kwala-kwala atsaye dudu batafi girman Dan wata bakwai ba atakaice baifi akife ta Dan bokiti ba, wage bakinta ta farayi ta Fara yiwa Saudat dariya tana kyakyatawa sannan tace, " Arne ya turo ni, boka ya turo ni, inzuki jininki inbashi, tunda ke dai kin ki zuwa, anyi alkawari da mahaifiyar ki zata kaiki abiyani jininki na aikin da nayi Miki Amma kun karya alkawarin" tana gama maganar ta buga uban tsalle tayi Kan Saudat.

     _Maleria na neman Sani gaba wallahi need ur prayers._😞👏🏼

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*
 
                   71&72


        _Not Edited_


            Sadiq na kwance a d'akin da na da abun duniya duk yabi ya isheshi, tun da ya dawo babu wata hirar azziki da take had'asu shida umma ko zama yayi acikinsu ta dinga tado maganar Farida tana zazzaga Masa fad'a, cikin abinda Bai wuce kwana uku ba tuni Sadiq ya zabge ya rame ya lalace ko abinci baya iya ci, daga tunani sai tunani idan abun yayi Masa zafi sai dai yayi ta hawaye, tunanin Farida ne ya addabi zuciyarsa ta b'angare d'aya Kuma Yana mamakin abinda ta aikata duk da abun na bashi haushi Amma neman ganinta yake ido rufe, duk lokacin da Umma ta tasa shi tana yi Masa fad'a sai yayi kamar zai nuna Musu video da hotunan wayarsa Amma sai yaji Sam bazai iya ba, kamar kullin da daddare bayan kowa yayi bacci Sadiq na kwance Kan katifarsa sai juye-juye yakeyi ya rasa me yake damunsa, wayarsa ya d'auko ya kunna data Dan rabonsa da online tun ranar da aka tura masa videos din Farida bayan ya gama gani zuciya ta d'ebeshi ya rufe datar Bai kara bud'e ta ba, sakwanni ne rututu suke shigowa ta ko ina sai da aka shafe kusan minti talatin sak'onin suka tsagaita da shigowa, sak'onin da aka yi Masa magana ta private yake bi na groups kuwa ko ta kansu baibi ba, wad'ansu Yana bada reply wasu Kuma wucewa yake, wata bakuwa number ya gani anturo masa videos da pictures rututu, videos din Farida ne suka Fad'o masa, guntun tsaki yaja ya fita daga ciki, ya Jima Yana Duba sak'onin WhatsApp dinsa wata zuciyar ce tace, " ka koma kaga me suka Kara turo maka ko wani video ne na Farida " kamar Wanda aka tsikara da sauri ya koma ya Fara bud'e videos da pictures, wani video ya kunna ya Fara gani, tsaida hankalinsa da nutsuwarsa yayi guri d'aya mamaki ne ya kamashi jin Abubuwan da Saudat take furtawa da bakinta, ga wad'ansu banzayen hotuna daya gani an d'auki Saudat, gaba d'aya ya manta da duniyar Saudat ko kad'an tunaninta Bai tab'a Fad'o masa ba.
          Sadiq ba karamin tashin hankali ya shiga ba, saboda b'acin Rai jikinsa har karkarwa yake, acikin dare ya tashi ya fito tsakar gidan sai kaiwa da kawowa yake, lokaci-lokaci yake kaiwa bango naushi, Inna dake Bata Jima da idar da nifila ba lamarin Sadiq ya matuk'ar Bata tsoro, fitila ta d'auka ta haska agogo alokacin 3:15 am cikin damuwa ta taso ta fito tsakar gidan ta Fara tambayar sa, kamar me jira dama Aykuwa Sadiq ya Fara magana, " Inna ta cuceni ta ha'inceni ta yaudareni wallahi taci Amana Inna, ita ba kawar Arxiki bace BUTULU ce muguwa Kuma azzaluma , wallahi-wallahi hukuma ce zata rabani da ita duk kud'i da ikon ubanta sai ta fuskanci mummunan hukunci......" Katse shi Inna tayi cikin rashin fahimta, " Kai Sadiq lafiyarka kuwa cikin dare kake wannan surutan kodai gamo kayi ne? " Kafin ya Bata amsa Umma ce ta taso  itama cikin tashin hankali saboda atunaninsa ko lamari Sadiq ya K'ara dawowa, ta na karasowa babu Wanda tayiwa magana sai da damk'i hannun Sadiq ta yanda bazai iya guduwa ba sannan tace, " Sadiq me yake damunka ince ba mugun mafarki kayi ba ko, kayi Addu'a kafin ka kwanta kuwa ??? Shi Sadiq ma tambayoyin nasu haushi yaso yabashi cikin damuwa yace, " Umma wallahi ta cuceni tasa na rabu da matata Kuma na tabbata itace ta sace ta nida ita har Abada Kuma wallahi ko k'aruna ne ubanta sai nayi shari'a dasu...." Umma ta katseshi cikin tambaya har Kara lek'a fuskarsa take tace, " wai wace kake magana ne " cikin tsananin jin haushin Saudat yace, " Saudat Mana umma wallahi Saudat annamimiya ce makira ce ita ba kawar Arxiki bace, Farida tayi Mata hallaci Amma ita ta nuna Mata TSANTSAR BUTULCI irin na D'an Adam, wallahi umma ko sama da k'asa zata had'e sai Saudat ta fito mun da Farida, nida ita ko a lahira bana fatan mu had'a hanya na saketa saki uku, Kuma ayau d'in Nan basai gobe ba sai ta sanar mun inda takai Farida" sakin Baki sukayi suna mamakin jin maganganun Sadiq Umma na Shirin magana azubure ya fige hannunsa Yana Shirin fita, Inna ce tayi ta maza ta Sha gabansa, Umma karasowa tayi cikin tsoro saboda su duk atunaninsu Sadiq baya cikin hayyacinsa sa Inna ce ta Fara magana, 
             " Sadiq koma menene yake faruwa mu Bari zuwa safiya idan gari ya waye" Inna na direwa Umma ta Dora, " haba Sadiq abunda yayi dare shi yayi safiya minti nawa ya rage garin ya waye kayi hakuri mana, Kuma idan kaje gidansu kace Musu me, wai ma meye hujjarka ta cewa ita ta sace Farida??? " Sadiq shiru yayi Yana Danna wayarsa videos din Farida ya kunnawa su Umma ya mik'a Musu.
     
           Salati suka kwasa lokaci d'aya suna salallami, Inna fashewa tayi da kuka ta Fara zagin Farida tana debe Mata Albarka, uffan Be ce Musu ba ya Kuma kamo Musu videos din Saudat Wanda me duniya ya tura masa, lokacin da suke gani gaba d'aya jijiyoyin jikinsu yayi sanyi tsoro da mamaki duk ya cika zuciyarsu, cikin b'acin Rai Umma ta Fara magana, " kaga tsinanniyar Yarinyar annamimiya wallahi Ina goyon bayan ka Sadiq Dan ubanta sai ta fito Mana da y'armu ta sace Mana ita harda ciki Allah sarki Farida Allah sa kin fad'a hannu na gari " Umma ta karasa maganar tana fashewa da kuka, dama tuni Inna take hawaye Amma kukan Umma sai ya Kara karya mata zuciya, d'aki tashiga ta zauna tana share kwallah, fita Sadiq zaiyi Umma ta lallasheshi akan ya Bari zuwa Asuba, Sadiq na shiga d'akin sa Kai ya had'a da bango Yana tunani, Dana sani k'arara yake furtawa afili cikin nadamar abinda ya aikata, gabannin Asuba wayar wani abokinsa me suna Bilal ce ta shigo Masa bayan sun gaisa yace sanar da Sadiq hutun da aka bashi ya K'ara har anfara maganar, sai alokacin Sadiq ya tuna saboda Sam ya manta da batun komawar sa Lagos, Bilal Abokinsa ne na kut tun farkon training dinsu suka had'u dashi, Cikin damuwa Sadiq ya fara kutsira Masa matsalar da yake ciki, abaya Bilal yasan da maganar b'atan Farida na farkon aurensu Amma jin wacce Tai silar abun shi ne yafi bashi mamaki, sun Jima suna waya sannan suka yi sallama.

          Tun kafin D'an Aljanin ya K'arasa jikin Saudat ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa ta rufe idonta tana kurma ihu da agajin neman taimako, abinda Saudat Bata sani ba duk irin ihun da zatayi babu Wanda yasan tanayi, tsalle Aljanin yayi ya d'afe Kan cinyarta hakoransa ya kafa yake ta zuk'ar jini, d'ayar k'afar ya koma ya samu babban D'an yatsanta d'aya shima ya zuk'i jinin kadan sannan ya sauko daga gadon jelar bayansa sai tsalle take bakin sa jina-jina da jini Kai tsaye ficewa yayi, Saudat d'ago da kanta tayi tana D'an juye-juye amamakinta ta zaci wani abun zauyi Mata Amma sai taga akasin Haka, tayi tunanin zai yanketa ko ya karceta Amma sai taji Yana kafa hak'oransa sai ji tayi wani sanyi na ratsata har cikin kanta sai da ya fice ta daina jin wannan sanyin, d'ison jinin ta gani da sauri taja zaninta ta goge shi shima na D'an yatsan nata tasa zani ta goge, tumawa tayi da mommy aykuwa ta rushe da kuka cikin damuwa take ta surutai marasa fasali, nurse najin ta damesu da surutu taje tayi mata allurar bacci.

              A cen Abuja shirye-shiryen bikin wata jikar su Abba akeyi harda su farida acikin y'an anko sai dai ita doguwar Riga aka dinka Mata saboda yanda ta bud'e ta shirga uwar k'iba, idan ba farin sani kayiwa Farida ba lokaci d'aya bazaka ganta ka shaida ta ba, ga wani irin fari da tayi saboda yanda hutu da kwanciyar hankali ya K'ara ratsata, saura Sati biyu bikin Amma idan kaga yanda suke shirye-shirye sai ka rantse gobe ne bikin Kuma su duka ne Amaren, har tsokaranar Farida suke suna cewa bazata je gurin dinner da fati ba, idan suka cika ta sai tace Musu, " Ummee ki kice su Bari wallahi insuka dameni sai in had'a su da Ya Asim " Ummee sai dai tayi murmushi tace, " Humaira bazan hanaki ba Wallahi tunda su suka tab'o ki " Farida kallansu take ta fakaici idan su tace, " dole mutum yayi biyayya inba Haka ba ya zauna agida " babu yanda suka iya saidai suyi gum Dan tabbas da Zata Kai zugarsu tsaf Ya Asim na iya hanasu zuwa dinner din musamman idan yaga suna ta d'okin zuwa.

             Sadiq na jin Kiran sallah tun kafin ayi assalatu ya yayi alwala ya fice daga gidan, sai da yayi raka'anal fijr bayan wani lokaci Allah tada sallar, bayan sun fito daga masallacin Kai tsaye wani D'an makotansu me napep ya yiwa magana ba Musu ya d'auke shi har cen unguwar su Saudat, sai dai abinda ya bashi mamaki da jimami ganin sank'ameme key rufe a k'ofa wayar say ya ciro ya Fara Kiran Saudat Bai sameta ba, har zai Fara buga gidan makotansu sai tunanin Kiran Daddy yazo Masa, number sa ya Nemo da sauri ya Fara kira, Kiran farko ba'a d'auka ba sai da ya Kira na biyu har ta kusa yankewa sannan ya d'auka da alama ma bacci ya Duba da yanayin muryar Daddy, fuska adaure Kai tsaye Sadiq jefowa daddy tambaya yayi ko gaisuwa babu, " nazo gidan ka neman Saudat na samu arufe, na kirata bansamu layinta ba inada muhimmiyar magana da ita " mamaki ne ya kama Daddy saboda yasan Sadiq na matuk'ar bashi girma da girmamawa Koda ta waya ya kirashi cikin mutumci suke gaisawa, Amma sai yayi Masa uzuri atunaninsa ko Dan yanxu farar safiya ce shiyasa yaji muryar Sadiq haka shiyasa yace Masa, " wacce irin magana ce Haka ko Zan iya jinta " cikin k'aguwa da maganar Daddy Sadiq yace, " zaka iya ji tunda maganar na tabbata kaima zata shafeka tunda y'arka ce ta janyo ta " sororo Daddy yayi ya kasa magana Sadiq ya cigaba da cewa, " Kuna Ina yanxu Ina fatan ba Gaya maka abinda ta aikata tayi ba da har ka b'oye ta " Daddy jinjina Kai yayi cikin zuciyarsa yace, " lallai to da wata a k'asa " afili sai yace, " ba damuwa kazo guest house dina dake tarauni Bari na turo ma da address din yanzu " Sadiq Bai jira cewar Daddy ba ya kashe wayarsa ya cewa me Adaidaita sahun su wuce tarauni, sun Fara tafiya ba jimawa sai ga sak'on Daddy ya shigo, bayan Sadiq ya karanta tiryen-tiryen ya dinga gayawa me Napep din har suka K'arasa bakin get din gidan, Daddy da kansa ya karasa ya bud'e Masa kofa.

       Bayan da aka tasa keyar mommy Kai tsaye bompai akayi da ita cikin cell idanunta sunyi zuru-zuru saboda tashin hankali, bak'kik'irin gurun yake Banda wari da zarni babu abinda yake tashi, sai cinnaku da kud'in cizo ga uban zafi, ana tura mommy ciki lokaci Daya ta Kara fita daga hayyacinta, Abinka da ba'a saba jin sauro da zafi ba tuni jikinta ya hau kaikayi ta hau soshe-soshe, wasu Mata dake cen gefe suna jiyo abinda mommy takeyi Banda dariya ba abinda suke mata, hannu ta zira cikin zaninta ta zaro wayarta aboye juyawa tayi ta Nemo number Alh muntari ta Fara Kira Yana d'auka ta Fara magana, " Alh kana Ina ka taimaka kazo kasa bashi afiddani daga cikin azabar Nan Ashe duk maganar damukayi Alh najimmu shi ya Kira Yan sanda yanxu haka Ina bompai Kuma......." Katse ta yayi cikin sauri, " to ni me Zan Miki wallahi ni kinganni dama Dana fito daga gurinki to hotel na wuce na tabbata ko Alh Kamal yaje nemana to daga Nan ma k'asar Zan Bari Dan muddin Alh Kamal ya kama na tabbata allura zata tono garma, 'ya ce ki rike dama ni bata Saba Dani to na bar Miki ki rike " Yana gama maganar mommy taji k'it ya kashe, cikin takaici ta Kara Kiran wayar  taji ta akashe gaba d'aya, wani uban ihu ta kurma ta fashe da kuka.

    _Nagode sosai da Addu'o'inku Allah bar zumuncinmu_👏🏼👏🏼👏🏼
