*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  73&74


            
             Wata uwar tsawa wani police ya dakawa mommy ba shiri taja bakinta ta tsuke, Dan kwalinta ta cire tana bige saurayen da suke kawo Mata yayima a jiki, Tunowa tayi da halin da tabar Saudat tausayin yarta ne ya kamata, Karan farko kenan da taji ta tsani rayuwar data shuka abaya, hawayen bak'in ciki take yi tana Kara kallan gurin da take arufe, Tunowa tayi da gidan Daddy da alfarmar da take ciki tuni ta Fara Dana sani acikin zuciyarta.

         Gaba d'aya fuskar Sadiq a tsuke take babu alamar walwala atattare dashi, Haka suka K'arasa cikin falon Sadiq Bai K'arasa zama ba ya jehowa Daddy tambaya, " Ina Saudat take? " Daddy Jim yayi yace, " mlm Sadiq ay ka Bari mu gaisa ko? " Ransa ne ya K'ara baci azafafe yace, " ba wannan ne ya kawo ni ba, tambayar ka nake ina Saudat Ina ka b'oye ta?" Ganin Sadiq da gaske yake yasa Daddy gyara zaman sa yace, " tana asibiti wasu azzalumai sun sace ta sun cire Mata hannu guda, kafin wannan ma naso na kiraka akan wata magana to abubuwa sai suka Sha mun Kai, Amma gwara da akayi haka....." Sadiq katse shi yayi, " wallahi da zanga mutanen da nace su K'arasa cire d'ayan hannun Kai idanma da k'afa duk su had'a da ita " Daddy dukda yasan matsayin Saudat agurinsa Amma Saida yaji ba dad'i game da kalaman Sadiq cikin jin haushi yace, " kasan agaban wa kake kuma me kake fad'a kuwa? " Ido cikin ido Sadiq ya kalli Daddy yace, " da mahaifin Saudat nake magana ko Dan ni bakasan me ta aikata mun ba, Duba kalli ka gani wallahi sai y'arka ta fito mun da matata, idan cikin wuta suka kaita na rantse da Allah sai ta bita ta d'auko ta" ya karasa maganar Yana mik'awa daddy video Saudat.
               Daddy gaba d'aya jikinsa yayi sanyi tsoro ne ya kamashi, dabadan yaga abinda su me duniya sukayiwa Saudat ba da tuni ya karyata Sadiq yace editing ne akayi da fuskar Saudat, dafe kansa yayi Yana karanta innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un sannan yace wa Sadiq, " Abu na farko da zance da Kai Sadiq kuyi hakuri wallahi bantab'a tunanin Saudat zata aikata abinda na gani Kuma naji yanxu da kunnena idanu na ba, Sadiq nayi tunanin halinta ya banbanta Dana mahaifiyar ta Ashe ba Haka bane duk halinsu D'aya, duk Abinda kayi niyya bazan Haka ba sai dai shawara d'aya Zan baka karka d'auki doka ahannu ka yi report ga hukuma su suka San matakin da zasu d'auka akanta, Sadiq mahaifiyar Saudat ta yaudareni  amma nayi niyyar cigaba da kulawa da Saudat duk da ba nine aynihin mahaifinta ba....." Da sauri Sadiq ya katse daddy, " What ? " Girgiza kansa Daddy yayi idanunsa na cikowa da kwallah yace, " Saudat ba y'ata bace, sai dai har yanxu Ina kaunarta acikin zuciyata ko ba komai na shak'u da ita na ta'allaka soyayyar mahaifi akan ta, Zan cigaba da kulawa da ita da dukiya ta ko ba komai nasan Allah zai bani lada tunda da d'a da dukiya ba'ay Musu mugunta, wata kila wannan itace tawa k'addarar arayuwa wata kila bazan tab'a ganin nawa kwan aduniya ba shiyasa Allah ya kawo Saudat cikin rayuwata, Zan Mata nasiha da fadakarwa idan taji ta d'auka tayi wa kanta saboda bazan iya yakice Saudat daga jikina ba, Allah ya sani Sadiq, Allah yana gani har cikin zuciyata Ina kaunar Saudat har cikin Raina Kuma zan cigaba da d'aukanta amatsayin y'ata ta cikina " Daddy ya K'arasa maganar cikin tausayawa kansa Yana hawaye, jikin Sadiq sanyi yayi tausayin sa ya kamashi kasa Masa yayi Daddy ya d'ago da runannun idanunsa yace, " Amma Sadiq ya akayi ka shafawa Saudat HIV tsakani da Allah kana ganin ka kyautatawa rayuwarta....." Cikin k'araji Sadiq ya mik'a a zabure yace, " bangane HIV kana nufin nine me HIV ko Saudat? "Kallan mamaki yayi wa sadiq yaga yanayinsa ga dukkan alamu Sadiq ya tsorata da jin abinda ya fad'a Daddy yace, " likitoci since tana da HIV to a Ina Saudat zata kwasa tunda Kaine mijinta kuma a tarbiyyar Dana bawa Saudat bansanta da kule-kule ba" da sauri Sadiq yace, " ka bincikata da kyau saidai in a duniya ta kwasa cen yawon banzanta Amma ni Allah ya sani bantab'a aykata zina ba, da kake zancen tarbiyya da kasan zata aykata abinda na nuna maka ne" tabbas yanayin Sadiq shi zai tabbatar maka da abinda yake fad'a gaskiya ne, Sadiq ne ya mik'e tsaye yace, " Ina ne hospital d'in da Take?" Shima Daddyn tashi yayi tsaye yace, " a AKTH take Bari na d'auko hulata sai na fito muje " daddy na juya baya Sadiq ya K'ara Kiran DPO din yake sanar dashi su wuce AKTH shima Yana tafe, daddy Bai b'ata lokaci ba ya fito suka shiga mota suka wuce Aminu Kano Teaching Hospital.


           Saudat taci kuka har ta gode Allah har aron wayar wata nurse tayi ta Kira number Daddy no answer ta Kira number mommy ita Kuma akashe, komawa tayi ta kalli gefen hannu ta taga wayam tashi tayi dakyar dake takawa saboda yanda kafafunta suke Mata tsami-tsami, jikin window din d'akin taje ta tsaya ta glass tana kallan kanta plaster jikin kafad'arta ta shafa afili ta furta, " Wai ni Saudat yau nice da hannu d'aya me duniya ka cuceni " hawaye ne ya gangaro Mata tasa hannu ta goge shi, Sadiq ne ya Fad'o Mata gaba d'aya kewarsa ta dameta ahankali tace, " Sadiq bakasan me yake faruwa Dani ba na tabbata da ka biyoni kaga halin da nake ciki " kamar me mafarki ana turo kofa Daddy ta gani ajiyar zuciya ta sauke cikin shagwaba tace, " daddy Ina kuka je kuka kyaleni Kai da mommy Dan Allah daddy ka dawomun da mommy..." Katse ta Sadiq yayi cikin tsawa, " Munafukar banza da wofi sai kinfito mun da matata wallahi ki gayamun inda take ko inkarya ki agurin Nan " Sadiq na rufe Baki yasa k'afarsa ya kwashe Saudat ba zato har sai da ta Kai k'asa ji kake kanta ya bada k'uuumm " yaran DPO ne suka shigo da sauri suka rik'e Sadiq suna bashi hakuri da banbaki, in charge dinsu ne ya nuna Saudat yace, " wannan itace criminal din ko, good zamu barki a hospital kafin zuwa Nan da ki warware zamu bar ma'akatanmu mutum biyu su dinga kulawa dake kafin mu wuce dake inda yafi cancanta dake " tashi Saudat tayi jiki ba kwari tace, " Sadiq yau ni ka Mara wallahi ni bansan inda matarka take ba daddy Dan Allah kasa Baki kuma wallahi sharri akamun...." Cikin shammata Sadiq ya zabgawa Saudat Mari har saida ta ga wani haske ya gilma mata, cikin kallan tsana yace Mata, " ki fito mun da ita tun da sauran ki matsiyaciya kwarkwasa kije na sakeki saki uku Kuma nida ke kotu ce zata rabamu " Yana niyyar Kai Mata wani Marin da sauri Daddy ya rike shi, cikin k'arajin kuka Saudat ta fad'a jikin Daddy ta fashe kuka me tsuma zuciya, kukan da Saudat take yi har cikin ransa ji yayi Sam babu dad'i kyaleta yayi sai da tayi me isarta sannan ya maida ta kan gado ya zaunar da ita, statement din Sadiq suka d'auka suka tabbatar Masa da zasu bar police biyu mace da namiji agurinta saboda tsaro, bayan sun gama d'auka cen hukumar hospital din suka samu suka gabatar Musu da dalilin zamansu da Kuma patient din da suke zama agurinta, Nan ma rubuce-rubuce sukayi aka cike sannan suka wuce Sadiq shima yayi tafiyarsa.

      
         Duk Abinda ya faru tsakaninsa da Daddy da Kuma Saudat sai da ya bawa su Umma labari, gaba d'aya hankalinsu ya tashi fargabarsu D'aya Farida ko awane hali take, shi kansa Sadiq bashi da wani sukuni ga wata zazzafar soyayyar farida da yake ji baccin kirki baya yi, ko abinci su Inna suka bashi sai dai ya zuba Masa ido Amma Loma d'aya baya iyaci, gaba d'aya ya fige ya rame ya lalace kamar Wanda yayi jinya har yafi su Inna zabgewa.
         Kwanan sa biyu da zuwa gurin Saudat gun aiki suka matsa Masa lamba ba yanda ya iya haka ta tattara ya koma, Inna da umma sai nasiha suke masa da gargad'i akan ya rik'e ibada da azkar sannan ya cigaba da Addu'a akan lamarin Farida, sosai yaji nasiharsu sai dai Sam yayi-yayi ya cire tunanin Farida Amma abun yaci tura kamar K'ara Masa wutar santa ake acikin zuciyarsa.
              Satin Sadiq guda da tafiya aka sallami Saudat Kai tsaye bompai aka kaita bangaren da ake ajiye masu laifin Y'an garkuwa da mutane, da farko Saudat taurin Kai tayi Musu sai da taji matsa jikinta yayi laushi sannan ta hau jero Musu bayani, da bincike yayi bincike har sai da ta bada number MD Guy duk iya binciken da zasu yi basu sameshi ba karshe har Abokina Nabeel suka kamo duk babu nasara sai da suka tabbatar da gaskiyar Nabeel sannan suka daina hukunta shi Amma basu sake shi ba, tracking number MD sukayi itama ba ta active sai da suka zurfafa bincike ta computer sannan suka ga hanyar da MD yabi ta Abuja daga inda suka samu matsala adaidai gun da su MD sukayi accident, baxa jami'ansu sukayi lungu da sak'o har sai da suka je gurun da motar MD take, ko kad'an basu kawo MD ne aciki ba sai da suka sa Nabeel agaba suna nuna Masa y'an abubuwan da suka tsinta ba jikin motar Wanda Bai kone ba, duk Abinda suka nuna Masa baya ganeshi sai da number motar MD sannan ya ce Musu itace, basu tabbatar da haka ba harsai da Nabeel ya kaisu har gidansa ya fito Musu da wad'ansu takardu sannan suka amince, Kai tsaye kotu suka mik'ashi ba b'ata lokaci alkali ya yanke Masa hukuncin zaman gidan kaso na shekara goma, saboda ya bawa me laifi goyon baya akan sace y'ar mutane ba tare da ya sanarwa da hukuma ba.

         Saudat mik'a ta akayi gaban alkali itama ya yanke Mata hukuncin d'aurin Rai da Rai, lokacin da aka sanar da Sadiq mutuwar Farida kamar mahaukaci Haka ya koma yaji d'adin hukuncin da akayiwa Saudat amma babban tashin hankalinsa ta ya zai sanar da su Inna, Haka ya wuni ya kwana ba ci ba Sha sai kuka da ambaton sunanta, masallatai ya dinga bi Yana  Kai kud'i anayiwa Farida Addu'a, kafin Sadiq ya yarda da abinda police suka Gaya Masa harsai da yaje suka nuna Masa gunda motar MD ta kone, Kuma ko da ya gani zuciyarsa Taki aminta da mutuwar Farida, abokan aikinsa sun matuk'ar tausayawa halin da ya tsinci kansa aciki, ciwo yake a tsaitsaye har ya so ya kwantar dashi danma ya fauwalawa Allah lamuransa kwana yake Yana sallah da Addu'o'i, duk wannan abun da ake ciki ko kad'an ya kasa sanarwa dasu Umma saboda tunanin halin da Inna zata shiga, shiyasa ya yanke hukuncin sai ya nemi hutun sati daya yaje ya Gaya Musu cikin lallami da nasiha.

     Wata rana aiki aka turasu Abuja na kwana biyu, har ya d'auki excuse saboda zuwa kano Amma aka hanashi, zuwansu Abuja washegari shida abokin aikinsa victo sun fita siyayya a FARHAN STORE zasu shiga kenan ya jiyo Sautin murya kamar na Farida, da sauri ya waiga gurin acikin mota ya hango me kama da ita da chocolate a hannunta ta juya tana yiwa ta  bayanta magana, " Rukayya wallahi idan na haifi y'an biyu na da rowa kece nace Miki miyi musanyar chocolate Amma ki k'i ko ba komai Zan rama ba gobe ne dinner din ba Wallahi sai dai ki zauna a........." Bata K'arasa maganar ba yaga mutumin da ke gefenta ya ja motar da gudu zai fice daga harabar gurin, Sadiq kamar sabon kamu Haka ya zube takalma yabi bayan motar da gudu mutane suka bishi da kallo Amma inaa kafin ya karasa tuni sun dad'e da ficewa sunyi Masa nisa.

     _lallai Sadiq kana ruwa_🤣

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  75&76

       _zaku Sha kura-kurai ban tace shi ba._


          Cikin k'araji da tashin hankali Sadiq ya kurmawa Farida Kira kamar Yana iya hangota, horn masu mota suke masa ba kyakyautatawa ko kad'an baima San sunayi ba, hunnu bibbiyu ya d'ora aka abun duniya duk ya dameshi, da gudu Victor ya k'araso cikin zafin nama ya janye Sadiq sannan ya rufeshi da fad'a, " kasan me kakeyi kuwa baka ganin akan titi kake? " Fisge hannunsa Sadiq yayi kamar me Shirin kurma ihu yace, " wallahi ita na gani wannan ya wuce Kama wallahi Farida ce, nidama nasan Bata mutu ba Wallahi D'an Iskan Nan sace ta yayi " ido Victor ya bishi dashi saboda idan da sabo ya Saba da jin surutun da Sadiq ya ke kullin ba dare babu Rana hatta agurin aiki bashi da Hira sai ta Farida, Amma yau abin ya bawa Victor tsoro saboda yanda yaga Sadiq ya fita da gudu baiyi tsammanin zai sameshi akusa ba, cikin tsoron abunda zai iya faruwa gaba yace, " Sadiq kayi tunani Wanda ya mutu baya dawowa, Amma ni abun ya fara bani mamaki har ka Fara fita da gudu inda nasan parents dinka Zan Gaya Musu suyi maka Addu'a irin Wanda kukeyi a Addininku " wani hashin Victor Sadiq yaji ya ja dogon tsaki ya d'auke kansa cen gefe, kansa ne yaji Yana Sara Masa dafeshi yayi Yana runtse idonsa, Victor gajiya yayi da tsaiwa yace, " mu shiga mu yi shopping namu yamma nayi " ko kallan gurin da Victor yake Sadiq baiyi ba, sai da ya maimata Masa sau biyu cikin k'aguwa Sadiq yace, " jeka ni na fasa siyan komai " d'aga kafad'a Victor yayi irin ko ajikinsa din Nan sannan ya wuce cikin store d'in.
       Sadiq gaba d'aya duniya tayi Masa zafi har ya rasa nemarwa kansa mafita, wani irin zafi zuciyarsa take Masa mak'ogaronsa na d'aci, wani tunani yayi da sauri ya ciro wayarsa number DPO ya Kira ba'a Jima ba ya d'auka bakin Sadiq har  rawa yake yafara Masa magana.

        " Barka da wannan lokacin yallab'ai........." Duk Abinda Sadiq ya gani lokaci d'aya ya zayyanawa DPO, sun Jima suna tattaunawa sannan sukayi sallama, Victor fitowa yayi da ledoji banya guda biyu a hannunsa Yana Shirin mik'awa Sadiq yaga ya mik'a zai shiga cikin Store d'in, mamaki ne ya kama Victor da sauri ya bishi yana magana, " me zakayi Kuma duk Abinda muke buk'ata na siyo Mana " fuska babu walwala Sadiq yace, " inasan inga ma'akan cen ne " Bai jira cewar victor ba ya wuce ciki.
        Wani matashin saurayi ya Fara cin karo dashi bayan shigar sa, Yana zaune da computer a gabansa, k'asa-k'asa Sadiq yay Mai sallama da fara'a ya amsa Masa Yana Masa barka da zuwa, ID Card dinsa ya ciro ya nunawa Saurayin sannan ya gabatar Masa da kansa, jikinsa ne ya hau kerma cikin tsuke fuska yace Masa, " Akwai wacce muke nema Kuma  yanxun Nan ta fice daga store dinku, so inasan inga Video Record dinku na yau especially na yamman Nan " shi Saurayin duk atunaninsa ko me laifi su Sadiq suke nema babu ko Musu ya Kira wani da alama shine babban su agurin, abinda Sadiq ya Gaya Masa shi ya sanar Masa ba Musu suka janyi wata computer dake gefe suka kunna Masa tariyewa suka dinga yi har sai da yaga anzo dai-dai lokacin da su Farida suka shiga gurin, da sauri yace Musu suyi playing dinsa ya fara kallo " wasu y'an Mata ya gani sun fara shigowa guda biyu sai Farida da wani namiji tana tafe da kyar take d'aga k'afa, suna tafe suna Hira kamar Mata da Miji, muryar Farida yaji radau tace, " Ya Asim yaushe zaka kaimu gidan Aunty nafisa? Wallahi mun kwana biyu bamu je ba" Ya Asim ne ya Bata amsa, " gaskiya kin daina tafiyar nesa saboda kinyi nauyi Nan ma da Kika ga na kawo ku Dan ba nisa ne, yanxu idan nace muje sai ki biyo ni haba Humaira ki dinga nemarwa kanki sauk'i Mana " Rumaisa tayi karaf tace, " Wallahi ya Asim Haka Aunty Humaira take Amma da cewa Ummee tayi mu fita ta motsa kafa da daddare sai kaji tace ta gaji " guntun tsaki Farida tayi tace, " to y'an sa ido waya sa daku"  babu Wanda ya kulata saboda sanin halin rikicin ta da sukayi musamman da cikinta ya tsufa k'aramar magana ce take b'ata Mata rai, b'angaren su sweet da chocolate sukayi kowa na d'aukan Wanda yake so.

       Sadiq mamaki ne ya kamashi yana kallan ikon Allah saboda yaji suna Kiran Farida da sunana Humaira Amma ga muryarta iri Daya da ta Farida har kammaninsu iri Daya hasko Farida akayi da sauri yasa hannu ya yayi stopping video, wayarsa ya zaro ya Nemo wani hoton Farida na gabannin auransu ma'akatan store din ya nunawa yace, " Abokina Dan Allah ka Taya ni gani ta da wannan da ta cikin wayar Nan ba mutum Daya bane? " Har rige-rigen karbar wayar sukayi suna dubawa ido suka zubawa hoton Farida sai kalli ta computer sai su kalli ta wayar Sadiq ogan ne yace, " Kai ni Ina ganin yanayi kawai sukayi Amma wannan kana gani tafi k'iba da haske waccen kuwa ay karamar Yarinya ce Kuma batakai hasken waccen ba, da cemun kayi ya da kanwa ne Zan iya yarda " guntun tsaki Sadiq yayi yaron yace, " haba me gida kalli da kyau mutum d'aya ne baka ganin ga tawadar Allah Nan akan hancin wannan ita ma Kuma wannan gashin Nan kawai dai Dan anan tana da ciki ne shiyasa kaga ta canja " Sam Sadiq ya manta da tawadar Allah farida ta goshi da hanci sai da Saurayin Nan ya fad'a da sauri ya Kara Kai idanunsa Kan ta jikin computer, rad'au yaga tawadar Allah Farida azabure yace, " wallahi itace, itace na rantse da Allah " playing video yayi manager yace, " yallab'ai Wai laifin me ta aikata muna fatan bazai shafi gurin kasuwancin mu ba " sosai Sadiq yayi zurfi gurin kallan video Farida harsai da manager ya Kara maimaita tambayar sa sannan yace, " ka Bari na kammala kalo zanyi Muku bayani.


        Haka Sadiq yayi ta binsu da ido har suka kammala siyayyar su suka taho gurin da ake biyan kud'i, Ya Asim na wajen biyan kud'i yaga ankira Rukayya kafin ta d'auka afili tace, " kunga Yar latsi Wai Iman ce take kirana " d'auka tayi sai da suka gaisa Iman take tambayar Rukayya katin dinner dinta, gutun tsaki tayi tace, " kina da matsala hajiya ta nace Miki Ranar Saturday ne Kuma a WALEEDAH EVENT CENTER za'ayi karki damu kixo gurun idan kinzo bakin get sai ki kirani na kawo Miki sai mu shiga tunda nace ki zo gida ki kwana sai mu wuce kink'i " Iman ce ta Bata amsa sannan sukayi sallama, ta gama waya ba jimawa suka fice daga cikin store din.

         Da sauri Sadiq ya kalli manager yace, " a Ina wannan Event din yake, tsoro ne ya kama manager yace, " fisabilillahi yallab'ai tsoro nake ji Kar azo wani Abu ya faru ace mune sila Wai me zakayi Mata ne ? " Wata uwar tsawa Sadiq ya buga Masa harsai da sauran mutanen gurin suka jiyo Victor na tsaye Bai ce Masa uffan ba sannan Sadiq yace Masa,  " matata ce b'ata tayi kana tunanin Zan zo Ina tambayar ku akan abinda Bai shafeni ba "  manager ganin sadiq da gaske yake yace, " ba mu da nisa sosai baifi d'ari da hamsin ne zuwa gurin ba ko d'ari biyu duk me Napep din da ka gayawa zai kaika gurin " Sadiq na gama jin bayanin manager ya mik'a Masa hannu suka gaisa Bai Kara ce Musu komai ba ya juya ya fice da sauri kamar me Shirin tashi sama, Baki asake suka bishi da kallo cikin zuciyarsu kowa da Abinda yake ayyanawa, Victor binsa yake abaya Anna ganin saurin ba Sadiq ba na misali bane yasa ya cigaba da tafiya sannu-sannu, me Napep ya tsaida Musu suka wuce cen masaukinsu inda suka sauka.

         Saudat lokacin da za'a tafi da ita daga kotu rungume daddy tayi tana kuka me tsuma zuciya, daddy duk dakiya irin tashi sai da ya zubda kwallah, shi kansa zuciyarsa ba dad'i duk da yasan Saudat ke da rashin gaskiya, kuka take jikinta har karkarwa yake sai da kyar ma'aikatan prison suka b'anb'anreta suka wuce da ita ana kaita ba jimawa aka Kai mata uniform d'inta to a saka, fuskar Saudat duk ta canja saboda kuka sai ajiyar zuciya take saukewa, d'akin da aka kaita acen lungu ta rakab'e sai kallanta suke, laraba tsigai itace duk shigabarsu ita take hukunta su yanda take so.
        Daddy kwana yayi zuciyarsa na masa suya da k'una ganin irin halin da Saudat ta tsinci kanta aciki, satin da ta zagayo yayi dai-dai da ranar visiting dinsu Kaya daddy ya had'a niki-niki yaje ya ga Saudat ya Bata sannan ya bada na sadaka da ya bayar abawa mazauna cikin gidan gyara hali da gyaran tarbiyya.
        Bayan kwana biyu bompai ya je Akan case din mommy, lokacin da aka fito da mommy idanu daddy ya zuba Mata Yana mamakin yanda yaga duk ta jeme tayi bak'i ta lalace, magana yayi da commissioner akan ya janye case dinsa da mommy acewarsa taje ita da Allah idan kairan ta shuka kanta inma sharran ne kanta, mommy da fuska duk tayi mele irin na masu bleaching ga wasu manyan kuraje da suka cika Mata fuska kudaje sai binta suke, har k'asa ta tsugunna tana yiwa daddy godiya, agurguje ya sanar Mata da hukuncin da aka yankewa Saudat kuka mommy take harda majina, dubu Hamsin Daddy ya mik'a Mata yace ta rage wata matsalar dasu, Bai jira jin ta bakinta ba yayi tafiyarsa yabar mommy a tsugunne tana hawaye.

       Labara tsigai shek'ek'e take kallan Saudat tace, " ke kawo mun kayan Nan da aka kawo Miki " harara Saudat ta watsawa laraba tace, " uban nawa ya kawo mun kice na Baki " murmushi laraba tayi ta gyara d'aurin Dan kwalinta tace, " kafin ki shigo gidan Nan take ubanki Amma yanxu ubanmu yake ke zuwaira ku karb'o kayan Nan idan Taki ku lillisamun ita, Amma kuyi a sannu kunsan ance Mana tana da kanjamau " sak Saudat tayi tana hawaye har su zuwaira suka warce katuwar ledar da Daddy ya had'o Mata na kayan tea, maganar da tafi damunta kazafin da sukayi Mata na cutar kanjamau.
      Tun Daga ranar ko fita su saudat sukayi duk inda ta ratsa zakaga Matan gurin na gudu Wai karta shafa Musu kanjamau ko abinci sai kowa ya gama karba take zuwa ta karb'o duk irin yunwar da takeji haka zata jira duk sai ansallame su, ko acikin d'akin cen karshen bango take kwanciya babu me Hira da ita ko magana tayi babu me Bata amsa, kafin wani lokaci tuni Saudat ta rame ta lalace ta fita daga hayyacinta ga ciwon kafafuwan da take fama dasu lokaci-lokaci suke kumbura sai Kuma awayi gari sun sace, abunda ta Fara Bata tsoro wasu manyan kuraje da suke fito Mata har ruwa sukeyi me wani irin d'oyi, ba shiri y'an dakinsu Saudat suka sallama kwana da ita a d'aki yanxu tsangwamar tata sai abunda yayi gaba, hatta ma'aikatan gidan ganin wannan kurajen nata yasa suka Fara baya-baya da ita ko magani suka kawo Mata sai dai su wurgar Mata a k'asa ta d'auka.

      Ranar litinin shine ranar da Sadiq ya had'u da Farida, tun Daga ranar kullin wuni yake akan titin event din da yaji Rukayya tayi magana akai, ranar juma'a kamar kullin Yana zaune yaga anbude gurin anayi Masa gyare-gyare cikin dabara yake tambayar masu gyaran ko akwai bikin da za'ayi atake suka tabbatar Masa da washegari akwai dinner wani d'an minister k'arfe goma na dare za'a Fara atashi 1:am, wannan labarin ba karamin dad'i yayiwa Sadiq ba jinshi yake cikin nishad'i kamar Wanda akace Masa ungo ga Farida ku wuce.
         Washegari tun safe Sadiq ya kasa ya tsare tsallaken titin da Waleedah event yake, megadin gurun tsoro ne Fara kamashi ganin kullin Sadiq na zaune agurin tunda Bai sanshi ba Bai tab'a ganinsa ba sai a y'an kwanakin Nan, cikin k'arfin hali ya Fara tambayar Sadiq, ID card dinsa har biyu ya ciro ya nunawa Dan tsohon yace  wa tsohon akwai wani da suke San kamawa, ganin Haka yasa Dan tsohon ya saki jikinsa da Sadiq har suka yita Hira, da Rana tayi Sadiq ya sai Musu abinci da drinks sallah ita ke tashin Sadiq daga gurin Yana idarwa zai dawo ya zauna ya cigaba da da ko.
             Kimanin k'arfe goma da rabi lokacin duk y'an Matan gidan sun shirya cikin anko gwanin ban sha'awa Farida ma ba karamin kyau tayi ba dukda simple makeup akayi Mata ta fito fess da ita, har tana Shirin shiga mota domin tafiya dinner Ya Asim ya hangota da sauri ya K'arasa gurunsu hannunsa rik'e da key yace, " Wai kin manta yau ne Zaki koma ganin Dr " yamutsa fuska tayi tace, " Ya Asim share kawai mu Bari sai next week" d'aure fuska yayi yace, " aykuwa indai bazaki asibiti ba to ba inda zaki " ita d'inma yamutsa fuskar tayi suna had'a ido ya gyad'a Mata Kai alamar da gaske yake, da hannu ya nuna Mata mota ba Musu ta wuce tana zumb'urar Baki su Halima sai rik'e dariyar mugunta suke suna ganin fitarsu da mota me zasu yi Banda dariya, sun San idan Yana Nan sukayi dariyar Yana iya cewa suma su wuce su tafi suyi Musu rakiya.

     Ganin sojoji da police suke tsaron hall din yasa Sadiq ya koma cen masaukinsu shima ya sanyo damararsa ya saje dasu, babu wanda ya kawo komai akansa musamman da suka tabbatar da ba sojan bogi bane, Sadiq Yana gurin gaba d'aya hankanlinsa baya jikinsa sai waige-waige yakeyi, cen bayan wani lokaci su Rukayya ya gani sun fito daga mota da sauri ya K'arasa gurunsu Yana Shirin yi Musu magana, kasancewar kowacce ta hango Saurayin ta babu wacce tabi ta Kan Sadiq, atare da samarinsu suka wuce cikin hall din, Dafe kansa Sadiq yayi cikin takaici ya Kaiwa bango duka, ganin anfara lura da yanayinsa yasa ya basar ya cigaba da harkar gabansa saidai yanayi Yana lek'en hall din saboda hankalinsa yaki kwanciya.

       Lokacin da Dr ya gama check up din Farida yake shaida Mata cikinta Yana 38 weeks kuma babies dinta suna cikin k'oshin lafiya, murna gurin Ya Asim kamar shine mahaifin twins d'in, daga Nan Kai tsaye gurin dinner suka wuce har bakin hall din Ya Asim ya ajiye Farida da kyar ta yunkura ta fito Tasha doguwar rigar leshi gwanin sha'awa da ita, rik'e Mata jaka yayi har suka Fara shiga hall din idan ka gansu zaka rantse Ya Asim mijinta, daga bakin k'ofar ta tsaya tana hutawa Rukayya ce rik'e da kujera ta ajiye wa Farida tana Mata tsiya, kamar Wanda aka tsikara Sadiq na hango Rukayya ya dunfari gurin idanunsa ne suka sauka akan Farida dake zaune ya Asim na mik'a Mata jaka, da karfi Sadiq yake Fad'in, " FAREEDER " ya fad'a yafi a irga ganin Kiran bazai kaishi ba saboda Karar DJ yasa ya kutsa Kai tsaye zuwa yayi ya rike hannunta d'ayan hannun nasa ya tallab'o hab'arta Yana Fad'in, " FAREEDER " gaba d'aya gurin kallo ne ya dawo kansu shi kansa Ya Asim mamaki abun ya bashi Farida ido ta zurawa Sadiq........✍🏻

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   77&78


     _Not Edited_


          Farida ido ta zurawa Sadiq kamar me tuno wani Abu, ya Asim ne ya fisge hannun Sadiq daga jikin Farida azafafe yace, " Kai baka da hankali ne meyake kanka da zaka zo ka rik'e matar aure kana hauka ne " cikin zafin zuciya Sadiq ya bugawa Ya Asim tsawa, " dakata karka shiga hurumina da matata FAREEDER ki Gaya Musu ke matata ce" Gaba d'aya mamaki ya kama mutane cikin mamaki Rumaisa tace, " Farida, Kuma matarka lallai kana da k'arfin hali " Farida da duk budurin da akeyi idanunta nakan Sadiq, kanta ne yayi wani irin sarawa ta dafe kanta ta kwallah wata irin kaya tana Shirin yanke jiki ta Fad'i Ya Asim da Sadiq sukayi sauri suka taro ta cikin tashin hankali, gaba d'aya gurin dinner ya hautsine su Rukayya kuka suka fasa ganin halin da Farida take ciki, da sauri su Sadiq suka d'auki Farida suka sa ta a mota, Ya Asim ne yake driving Sadiq na baya Farida na hannunsa cikin tashin hankali Sadiq yake ta surutai wasu ma baisan Yana fad'a ba, " Farida Dan Allah karki tafi ki barni bansan yanda zanyi ba idan Kika mun haka zan iya haikacewa, karkiyi Mana Haka farida ki tuno Inna ki tuna halin da zata shiga " Ya Asim da haushin Sadiq ya cika shi juyowa yayi ya buga uban tsaki yace, " dallah malam ka rufe Mana Baki, wallah karka ga kana da kaki duk Abinda ya samu Humaira hukuma ce zata rabamu " Sadiq ko takan ya Asim baiyi ba ya zubawa Farida idanu, tafiya sukayi me nisa sannan suka K'arasa hospital din da, cikin gaggawa suka karb'i Farida suka shiga da ita tuni har ta Fara zubda jini, Sadiq komawa yayi gefe ya dafe Kai abun duniya duk ya dameshi, Ya Asim dake gefe suna had'a ido zai zabga Masa harara, wayar su Abba ce ta shigo wayar Ya Asim, d'auka yayi cikin damuwa yace, " Hello Abba " daga daya b'angaren aka amsa, " Asim Kuna wane Asibitin zamu fito yanxu " fuska ba walwala yace, " muna  Health Tune Clinic " katse wayar akayi sannan ya cire wayar a maida aljihu.

       Dr. Muhammad ne ya fito ya buk'aci ganinsu Sadiq har rige-rigen shiga sukeyi, bayan sun shiga wata takarda Dr Muhammad ya ciro sannan ya Fara magana, "  Patient dinku tana cikin wani yanayi jininta ya hau sosai sai munyi Mata CS yanxu Dan haka Ina mijinta yake zaiyi signing awannan takardar " cikin hadin Baki Ya Asim da Sadiq suka amsa kowa Yana Fad'in, " nine mijinta " mamaki ne ya kama Dr. Muhammad Yana kallansu yace, " bangane ku duka kune mazajenta ba, baku fahimci abinda nake fad'a bane, Ina nufin mijinta mahaifin unborn babies dinta " wannan Karan ma hada Baki sukayi suka amsa, " nine Dr. Ba wannan bane " jinjina Kai Dr yayi abun ya matuk'ar bashi mamaki Yana Shirin yin magana cikin zafin nama Sadiq ya fisge takardar da biro yayi signing, fuska ba walwala yace, " pls Dr. Kuyi ku cetomun rayuwar Farida da abinda yake cikinta Dan Allah ku hanzarta " mik'ewa Dr yayi Yana gyara glass dinsa yace, " insha Allah " haushi ne ya cika ya Asim wani dogon tsaki yaja atare suka bugaw juna wata uwar harara, Haka suka fice Daga office din kowa najin haushin D'an uwansa.

      Fitarsa da lokaci kad'an iyalan gidan Abba suka k'araso alokacin dare har yayi saboda Sha 12:00 am ta wuce, duk wanda zaiyiwa Sadiq kallo na tsanaki zai tabbatar da irin tashin hankalin da yake ciki, cikin damuwa Abba ya kalli Ya Asim yace, " Asim me yake faruwa da Humaira ne, Rukayya ta faramun wani zance " Ya Asim kamar me jira cikin damuwa ya zayyanawa Abba komai tun Daga shigar Sadiq gurunsu har fad'uwar da Farida tayi, jinjina Kai Abba yayi ya juya gurin da Sadiq yake yace, " saurayi Bismillah inasan magana dakai " kujera Abba yaja ya tura gefe alamun Sadiq ya zauna aykuwa ba Musu Sadiq ya zauna Yana Kara tsuke fuska, kallan tsaf Abba yayiwa Sadiq yace, " bawan Allah meye sunanka? " Atakaice Sadiq yace, " sunana Abubakar Sadiq " inasan insan garin da kake da alakarka da Humaira" cewar Abba.
              Gyara zama Sadiq yayi yace, " sunanta ba Humaira bane sunanta Farida Kuma matata ce sace ta akayi " karaf Ya Asim yace, " ita kayan sawa ce da za'a sace maka ita " harara Sadiq ya wurga Masa Abba ya dakatar da  Ya Asim sannan ya cewa sadiq, " shin ko kana da shaidar da zata tabbatar Mana da haka " tun kafin Abba ya rufe Baki sadiq ya ciro wayarsa ya Fara dube-dube hannunsa har rawa yake, hotunan farida ya dinga nunawa Abba, Abba ya Jima Yana jinjina Kai sannan yace, " azahirin gaskiya wannan ba wata kwakwkwarar shaida bace da zata sa mu yarda da Kai har mu d'auki Yarinya mu baka ba sai munji daga bakinta idan Allah yasa komai ya lafa, saboda duniya ba tabbas inkuma kanada wata muhimmiyar shaidar to ka kawo Mana ita, sannan sai munyi tattaki mun dangana da hukuma ta shiga maganar tukunna " haushin Abba ne ya kama Sadiq ko uffan Bai cewa Abba ba sai da ya tashi har ya Fara tafiya ya juyo yace, " Baba Dan Allah karmuyi haka daku na biyo ku ta lallami banasan Abu yazo ya b'aci Ina ganin girma da darajar ka saboda Kai dattijo ne wallahi idan duniya zata had'u akaina banga Wanda zai hanani tafiya da Farida ba " jinjina Kai Abba yayi Yana karewa Sadiq kallo tabbas jarumta da k'arfin halin Sadiq sun burgeshi murmushi yayi yace, " idan har ka K'ara kawo Mana wata shaidar da zata sa haka ba " Sadiq gurun zamansa ya koma ya zauna Yana huci lokaci-lokaci yana kallon d'akin da aka shiga da Farida.

     Umme ce ta kalli Abba tace, " ni Ina ganin yaron nan kamar Yanada kamshin gaskiya tunda Yarinyar Nan da ciki muka tsinceta kuma yace mijinta ne gashi har hotunanta ya nuna maka " kallan ta Abba yayi sannan yace, " Mata kenan kudai tunaninku daban yake, yanxu ke wannan Yar shaidar ta Isa tasa mu d'auki Yar mutum sukutum mu bashi ya tafi da ita ? " Shiri Ummee tayi tana nazari Rumaisa tace, " wallahi Abba ni banma yarda dashi ba kila ma sojan na bogi ne yayi haka ne Dan ya razana mutane adauke ta abashi " Rukayya ta d'ora da, " ke tunfa a gun dinner dama yaso tsaidamu Ashe da abinda ya kulla to aniyarsa ta bishi cen Kuma wallahi shi da Humaira saidai kallo daga nesa " ta K'arasa maganar tana watsawa Sadiq harara aykuwa karaf suka had'a ido ta Kara balla Masa wata uwar hararar, duk Rabin hirar da sukeyi akan kunnensa damuwarsa Daya awanne hali Farida take ciki, wayar sa ya ciro ya Kira number Umma akashe ita Kuma number Inna Yana Kira is not reachable guntun tsaki yayi ya maida wayar ya ajiye.

       
         Sun shafe kusan awa biyu azaune kowa Yana tunanin halin da Farida ta shiga, Ya Asim haushin Abba yaji aganinsa kamar Abba ya goyi da bayansa ne shiyasa ya tashi daga cikin su ya koma cen gefe Yana karajin haushin Sadiq ya baibayeshi, suna Nan zaune Dr Muhammad ya fito daga cikin d'akin da sauri suka K'arasa gurinsa har rige-rigen tambayar sa suke, " Dr ake ciki ne " goge fuska yayi da hankee yace, " Alhamdulillah munyi nasara anciro Mata two baby boys dinta Kuma suna cikin k'oshin lafiya, nurses zasuje su gyara su Nan bafa jimawa ba zaku gansu" Sadiq ne yayi karaf yace, " Dr. Ita mommyn fa " yanayin Dr. Ne ya canja cikin damuwa yace, " munyi Mata allurar bacci sannan maganar gaskiya Koda ta farko Bata bukatar hayaniya akan ko wani Abu da zai dameta, sannan kwakwalwarta ta dau zafi tana buk'atar hutu inasan ku ware mutum daya zai zauna da ita ya dinga kulawa da ita saboda bazai yuwu ace ku duka zaku dinga shiga gurunta ba, ko y'an dubiya pls and pls adakatar dasu bama buk'ata Ina fatan kun fahimceni " gaba d'aya gurun tsit yayi ahankali Abba yace, " ba komai likita zamu kiyaye " murmushi Dr yayi yace, " nagode bisa ga had'in Kan da kuka Bamu yanxu ku dakata za'a kawo Muku baby ku gani, itama Kuma o
Patient ankaita Room 3 ta jikin Window zaku iya lek'awa ku ganta inyaso sai ku wuce gida ki bar wacce zata kula da ita da babyn " umme ce tace, " likita ayi Mana alfara mu bar Mata masu jinyar tunda kaga yaran biyu ne ga uwarsu aikin zaiwa mutum d'aya yawa " jinjina Kai Dr yayi yace, " No problem hakan ma yayi Amma sai ankiyaye " Abba ya amsawa Dr. Sannan ya wuce cikin office dinsa.

     Bayan wasu mintuna nurses  biyu ne suka fito kowacce hannuta d'auke da baby Ya Asim da Sadiq ne Kan gaba sai su Rukayya sannan su Abba, Ya Asim ne ya mik'a hannu ya karb'a Sadiq ma ya mik'a hannu ya karb'a, tasbihi ya dingayi ya afili bakinsa yaki rufuwa saboda farinciki, mamakin irin tsananin kamar da yakeyi da jariran yake yi Abba ne ya karewa yaran kallo ya d'ago ya kalli Sadiq cikin zuciyarsa yace, " tabbas ko makaho ya shafa yasan wad'annan yaran y'ay'an ka ne " shima Ya Asim jikinansa ne yayi sanyi yajima atsaye idan ya kalli Sadiq shi kansa Yana mamakin kamannin yaran da Sadiq yakeyi.

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   79&80


    _Not Edited_


         Su Rukayya Baki har kunne yak'i rufuwa waya suka d'auko aka dinga snapping yaran shima Sadiq wayarsa ya ciro ya d'auki babyn hannunsa, Abba ne ya mik'a hannu cikin murmushi ya karb'i jaririn gaba d'aya farin ciki ya kamasu, kowa ka kalleshi zaka ga TSANTSAR farincikin da yake ciki, Abba ya Jima Yana yiwa babbyn hannunsa Addu'a sannan ya mik'awa Ummee ta karb'a ya karb'i na hannun Ya Asim shima Addu'a yayi Masa ya mik'awa su Rukayya har rige-rigen karb'a ake kowa na zumud'in ya d'auka, Halima ce tace wa d'ayar nurse din, " sister wannene Hassan aciki " da hannu ta nuna na hannun Umme tace, " wannan shi aka Fara cirowa sai wancen " gingina Kai sukayi Rumaisa tace, " to tayaya zamu dinga gane su? " Gaba d'aya rufowa sukayi Kan yaran suna Kare Musu kallo babu wani banbanci ajikinsu, Sadiq ne ya zura Musu idanu murmushi yayi yace, " wannan ga rowa Nan Akan hannunsa " ya nuna Hassan din sannan yace, " wancen Kuma babu " sai alokacin suka farga aykuwa kowa dariya yasa agurin, nurses din ne suka karb'i babyn suka kaisu cen d'akin da Farida Take akan bed dinsu ta kwantar dash, Rumaisa ce tabi bayansu har zasuyi Mata magana camera waya ta nuna Musu alamun tana San Kara d'aukan pictures din yaran, hotuna kuwa ta d'auke su kala daban-daban gwanin sha'awa, lokacin da Rumaisa ta fito haka ta dinga nuna Musu kowa yanajin kaunar yaran, Ya Asim gaba d'aya jikinsa yayi sanyi sai bin Sadiq yake da ido Yana kallo Amma jikinsa ya gaba d'aya ya gama bashi Sadiq shine mijin Humaira, shiyasa ma yaji gaba d'aya wata irin kunya ta Kama shi, duk sai yaji nauyin Sadiq yake ji Yana tunanin wane irin kallo Sadiq zaiyi masa,  hotunan da Rumaisa ta d'auka Sadiq ya buk'aci da ta tura Masa ba Musu ta tura Masa ta xender, acikin Daren Nan su Rukayya suka dinga posting din twins ta WhatsApp Instagram Facebook da dai sauransu.

       Sadiq gefe ya koma ya zauna ya zubawa yaran idanu shi kad'ai yake murmushi na farin da jin dad'i azuciyarsa yake Fad'in, " yanxu duk wad'annan y'ay'ana ne, ikon Allah kenan Allah na gode maka ubangiji ka bawa Farida lafiya ". 
            Daren ranar Nan babu Wanda ya rintsa sai da sukayi sallar asuba sannan Su Abba suka koma gida, kafin sallar asuban kuwa sun karb'i waya yafi a irga daga cikin Yan uwa da Abokan arxiki, sai bayan da Abba yayi sallar Asuba sannan ya dinga Kiran Yan uwa Yana sanar musu da abinda Farida ta Haifa, Sadiq saboda rashin yarda lokacin da zai tafi masaukinsu sai da yayiwa Victor waya yace yaje gurunsa address din hospital din ya bashi bayan D'an wani lokaci sai ga Victor ya k'araso, gargad'arsa yayi sosai da ya sa Mai ido akan d'akin da Farida take Dan shi Sadiq gani yake idan yace gida kafin ya dawo zasu iya tafiya da ita.
     Yana fita daga hospital din cen gidan ya wuce agaggauce yayi wanka ruwan tea kawai yasha Kai tsaye AFNAN COLLECTION'S ya wuce kayan jarirai Haka ya dinga loda kala-kala duk Abinda ya gani na baby ne sai dai ta janyo ya d'iba, bayan ya gama bill sukayi Masa yayi Musu transfer ya wuce da Kaya niki-niki, Victor na ganinshi mamaki ne ya kamashi Baki asake ya tari Sadiq Yana tambayar sa, dakatar dashi yayi ya wuce katsaye d'akin da Farida take wance ya shiga, tana kwance bacci take lakadan hankali kwance Haka suma twins din, ya dad'e akan Farida Banda murmushi ba abinda yakeyi, wayarsa ya ciro ya dinga d'aukan ta a hoto, Rumaisa dake gefe a zaune kallan Sadiq take ita abin nasa ma har so yayi ya Bata dariya.
      Bayan sun gaisa dashi mik'a Mata kayan yayi sannan yace, " Wai tun jiya Bata farka bane " ajiye kayan Rumaisa tayi tace, " wajen 7:00 am ta farka Tasha tea tayi wanka so sai suka Bata magani da allura Amma since like 10 to 11 zata iya farkawa " murmushi Sadiq yayi yace, " Kuma Bata tambayi kowa ba? " Tambayar taso bawa Rumaisa dariya sai ta maze tace, " a'a babies din ta kawai ta gani tayi Musu Addu'a ba Wanda ta tambaya " D'an Jim kad'an yayi Bai Kara cewa komai ba ya Jima a tsaye sannan ya fice daga cikin d'akin.

          Yana fita yaci Karo da su Rukayya hannunsu d'auke da Kaya Niki-niki cikin zolaya Rukayya tace, " Abban twins andawo kenan " ganin suna San maidashi abun zolaya yace baice Mata komai ba da murmushi ya wuce abinsa su ma din murmushi sukayi suka shiga wajen Farida.
         Lokacin da ya koma gurun Victor gaba d'aya mamaki ya gani akan fuskarsa, ganin Haka ne yasa ya zayyanawa Victor duk Abinda ke faruwa, idanu Victor ya zurawa Sadiq sannan yace, " Kai bross kana wuta fa mutumina, Amma how comes Wanda ya mutu zai dawo abun akwai mamaki? " Murmushi Sadiq yayi yace, " Man shine ur eyes, inbaka manta ba na tab'a Gaya maka jikina ya bani matata na Raye " dariya Victor yayi ya kaiwa Sadiq hannu suka tafa yace, " shegen Kaya " gaba d'aya suka sa dariya.


         Lokacin da mommy ta fita daga bompai rasa gurin zuwa tayi wayarta tasa ta dinga Kiran k'awayenta duk wacce tacewa zataje gidanta kowacce saidai ta kafta Mata rashin mutumci da bak'ak'en maganganu domin duk Abinda ya faru Alh muntari ya sanarwa da hjy Daso bayan ya gama Gaya Mata Kuma ya dankara Mata saki, damuwar hjy Daso kudaden da take tatsa gun Alh muntari Amma dake ta fishi sanin Kan tsiyar sai ta kwashe kadarorinsa na Nan cikin gidanta ta Kira Saurayinta Mubarak mubby boy ta sanar dashi, mubarak yaro ne k'aramin baifi 27yrs ba hotel ta Kama Musu acewar ta kafin su tafi honeymoon, gaba d'aya kadarorin ta damk'a Masa ta fita zata je ta d'auko motar Alh guda d'ayar daya Bari agidan, abinda Bata sani ba ta fita mubby boy ya Kira budurwarsa billy London girl atake suka samfe daga hotel din, hjy Daso na dawowa hannu ta Dora aka ta kurma ihu tana surutai ganin zata Tara Musu jama'a yasa security suka fice da ita, wannan tashin hankali shine yasa kwakwalwarta samun motsi tabi hanya tana surutai marasa fasali.
         Labarin da hjy Daso ta bawa kawayensu kafin haukacewarta ne yasa kowa ya juya Mata Baya, da kyar da magiya wata kawarsu ta sauki Mommy agidanta itama sati Daya ta bawa Mommy, kwanan mommy biyu ta nemi dillalai tana neman d'aki agobe zata cika sati ta samu d'aki awani gida me zaman mutane had'u ashekara dubu Ashirin, D'an komatsai da ba'a rasa ba mommy ta saya ganin zaman bazai kaita ko Ina ba yasa ta nemi aiki agurin wata me restaurant.

         Ranar da mommy taje ta gano Saudat kwana tayi tana kuka abun duniya duk yabi ya dameta, ganin ciwon Saudat na gaba yasa hukumar gidan yarin tasa aka Kai Saudat asibiti Amma duk wani gwaje-gwaje da akayi babu abinda suka gani lokacin da daddy ya samu labari yaje ya ga Saudat abin ya daga Masa hankali Jin abinda likitoci suka fad'a yasa Daddy ya dage da neman magungunan musulunci, Kai tsaye d'aukan ta yayi yasa aka kaita Al-furqan Islamic chemist. 
         Ana Fara yiwa Saudat rukiyya ta yanke jiki ta Fad'i andauki lokaci me tsayi ana karatun sannan Aljanin ya Fara magana, sai da aka fafata dashi sosai sannan ya bar jikin Saudat har sai da yaga malamin Yana Shirin halaka shi, Saudat sai da ta shafe wata guda agurin anayi Mata magani cikin ikon Allah ta warke garau da ita kurajen nata duk suka tafi sai dai tabbai ciwo d'aya bataji ajikin ta, duk wannan zaman da tayi ma'aikatan prison suna tare da ita suna gadi har ta warware ta warke gaba daya, kafin su wuce da ita prison sai da daddy ya kaita Asibitin makka aka yanka Mata hakoran da suka fita na bakin ta sannan aka wuce da ita lokacin da zasu tafi da ita tayi kuka kamar ranta zai fita Haka ma mommy.

       Mommy gaba d'aya kunyar daddy take ji Duba da yanda ya kashewa Saudat kud'i dukda yasan ba yarsa bace, ta roki gafararsa yafi a irga gaba d'aya nadama tazo Mata tana Dana sanin biyewa San zuciyarta gashi ta jefa kanta da y'arta cikin damuwa da kuncin rayuwa, Daddy yayi kokari matuk'a ya kashe kud'i masu yawa wajen neman magungunan Saudat Kuma ko afuska Bai tab'a nunawa Saudat canjin da zata gane shi ba mahaifin ta bane, sai dai duk lokacin da ya tuno haka ransa ya na mugun b'aci sai dai yayi godiya ga ubangiji bisa jarrabar da yayi Masa.

         Saudat gaba d'aya ta koyi darasin rayuwa tayi nadama da Dana sani zamanta agidan yari ya koya Mata hankali da ilimin rayuwa, sai yanxu take nadamar abinda ta aikatawa babbar Aminiyarta tayi kuka kamar me ta na Jin inama ace Farida Bata mutu ba ta nemi yafiyarta, sannan tana rok'on sanadin da zai hadata da Sadiq shima ta rokeshi gafara bisa abubuwan data aika Masa b'oye Dana fili.
          Abokan zaman sun rage tsangwamar da suke mata sosai saboda tsawatarwar da akayi Musu Dan har zama suyi Hira sukeyi da sauran aikace-aikacen da ake sasu duk tare sukeyi, shiyasa damuwar Saudat ta D'an ragu ba kamar da ba.

        Sadiq na zaune banda time ba abinda yake dubawa k'arfe goma nayi ya tashi yashiga d'akin da Farida take kwance, su Rukayya na zaune suna Yar hirarsu k'asa-k'asa gefen gadon Farida ya tsaya ba Wanda ya tankawa,farida ba ita ta farka ba sai wajen 10:30 salati ne abakinta sannan ta bud'e idanta yunkura tayi zata tashi su Rukayya suka taso zasu taimaka mata, kafin su k'araso tuni Sadiq ya d'agota Sannan yace Mata, " sannu Farida " cikin had'in Baki Su Rukayya suma suka Fara jero Musu sannu, ido ta zubawa su Rukayya kamar tanasan tuno wani Abu Amma sai ta kasa juyowa tayi da idanta Kan Sadiq da murmushi afuskarta cikin sanyin murya tace, " Ya Sadiq Kaine agurina ko mafarki nake " ta K'arasa maganar idanunta na cikowa da kwallah, gefen gadon Farida ya dawo kusa da ita ya zauna yace, " nine Farida Dan Allah Kiyi hakuri nasan nayi Miki laifi Amma ki yafemun karki K'ara guje mun " zatayi magana su Ya Asim suka shigo cikin d'akin ganinta a zaune ba karamin Dad'i yayi Musu ba Ya Asim ne ya Fara cewa, " Humaira kin tashi ya jikin naki?" Da kallan mamaki da kallesu sai tace, " da sauki " murmushi Ya Asim yayi yace su Ummee na Nan zuwa anjima kinji " bayan Sadiq ta D'an shige sai kuma wani tunani ya Fad'o Mata, hannun Sadiq ta rik'e gam tace, " Ya Sadiq su waye wad'annan ni bansan su ba Dan Allah karka tafi ka barni tsoro nakeji karsu tafi Dani, wallahi Saudat zata iya turo su har Nan su tafi Dani Dan Allah Ya Sadiq ka daukeni mu tafi gurunsu Inna wallahi tsoro nake MD ne zai gudu Dani " ta K'arasa maganar tana tusa kanta abayan Sadiq, daga su Rukayya har su Ya Asim mamaki ne ya kamasu sororo sukayi suna kallan juna cikin mamaki, shi kuwa gogan naku wani farin ciki ne ya kamashi ta wani b'angaren Kuma tausayin Farida ne ya kamashi, bubbuga bayanta ya dingayi cikin lallashi yace, " babu inda zani Farida kinji Kuma babu Wanda zai tafi dake, Kinga Saudat ma Bata Nan tana cen nasa anrufe ki kwantar da hankalin ki kinji " kamar karamar Yarinya ta d'ago da kanta tana gyad'a Masa Kai.


      _Gsky book ya taho gangara zanyi missing din ki su Farida musamman Aunty Saudat._🤓

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   81&82

     _Shi d'inma ban tace shi ba._



       Shiru tayi ta lafe ajikin Sadiq kamar me tsoron had'a idanu da Ya Asim sai sauke ajiyar zuciya take yi ta rik'e Sadiq Gam kamar za'a kwace ta, mamaki gaba d'aya ya gama Kama su Rukayya cikin k'arfin hali Ya Asim yace, " Humaira yanxu mune Baki sani har ni Yaya Asim d'in? " Farida tsumu tayi jikinta sai karkawa yake yi Bata Masa uffan ba, Rumaisa sa ce tace, " haba Aunty Humaira idan Wasa kike Mana Dan Allah ki daina wannan ba Wasa bane " Farida d'agowa tayi tana kallan Rumaisa wani Abu take San Tunowa Sam sai taji kanta yayi nauyi ta kasa tuno komai, kasa magana tayi sai ido da ta bisu da kallo ana cikin Haka sai ga Ummee tayi sallama ta shigo carko-carko ta gansu a tsaye kujera Rukayya ta Mika Mata suka gaisa da Sadiq kallan Farida tayi tace, " Humaira ansamu Kai k'alau ya k'arfin jikin? " Amsa Mata tayi da, " da sauki " ta amsa Mata ne Amma bawai Dan ta gane wacce tayi Mata sannun ba, Rumaisa ce tace, " Ummee Wai Humaira ce batasan mu ba wata kila ke tunda ta ganki ta daina Mana wannan wasan, murmushi Ummee tayi tace, " haba kun sa ta agaba kun isheta da surutu ba dole tace Bata gane ku ba ko Humaira, hala damunki sukayi " Farida kallan Ummee tayi ta gyad'a Mata Kai badan ta yarda da abinda taji ta fad'a ba.
       Suna Nan zaune nurse ta shigo bayan sun gaisa ta bawa Farida magunguna sannan tayi Mata allurai, ita kanta Ummee lamarin Farida na Bata mamaki tasan dai Tasu tazo d'aya da Humaira Amma taganta yanzu sai d'ari-d'ari take yi da ita Amma Sai tayi Mata uzurin kodan Bata gama warwarewa bane, bacci ne ya Fara fisgar Farida bayan wasu y'an mintuna yayi awon gaba da ita, ahankali Sadiq ya zame ya kwantar da ita, su Rukayya ficewa sukayi cikin jimami da mamaki itama Ummee tabi bayansu, Sadiq shima fitowa yayi dama tuni Ya Asim ya riga su fitowa kujera ya samu ya zauna Yana Danna wayarsa.
    
     Suna Nan zaune Dr. Najeeb ya wuce su bayan shigarsa da minti Ashirin ya turo wata nurse yace ta Kira Masa Y'an uwan Humaira, Sadiq ne ya tashi zau tafi yaga Ya Asim Bai biyoshi ba juyowa yayi yace, " Asim Bismillah muje Mana " jiki ba kwarai yace, " yallab'ai na bika kuwa " murmushi Sadiq yayi yace, " haba haba muje Mana " ba Musu Ya Asim ya tashi suka shiga gurin Dr. Najeeb.

       Bayan sun zauna gaisawa sukayi da Dr. Tambayar su yayi me jiki suka amsa Masa da, " me jiki da sauki Alhamdulillah " ajiye wayar hannunsa yayi sannan ya Fara magana, " jiya na manta bansanar daku wani bayani game da patient dinku ba, kamar yanda bincike ya nuna ada ta tab'a samun buguwa akanta Wanda yayi sanadiyyar da kwakwalwarta ta samu y'ar matsala har ta kamu da ciwon mantuwa, to Alhamdulillah sakamakon ganin wanin Abu da tayi Wala Allah na firgici ne ko damuwa ko tsoro ko kuma kauna shi ya taimaka Mata yanxu kwakwalwarta ta dawo yanda take Kuma duk Wanda ta sani abaya tana ganinsa zata ganeshi kamar dai bacci tayi ta farka, sannan wad'anda tayi sabuwar rayuwa dasu dole sai sunyi hakuri saboda ta dinga Jin kamar ta sansu kamar Bata sansu ba, zata dinga ganin kamar acikin mafarki tayi rayuwa dasu so sai a hankali yau da gobe idan ana D'an Bata labarin abubuwan da suka gudanar da ita, zata dinga Tunowa da kad'an da kad'an, Amma Kuma komai baya buk'atar garaje pls kubi sannu " sai yanxu Ya Asim ya tuno da matsalar da aka ce Musu ta samu tun bayan bigetan da sukayi, Sadiq ne ya Fara magana, " insha Allah Dr we should try our best, Allah ya  Kara Mata lfy Aameen " sannan Ya Asim yace, " Dr duk munji bayaninka insha Allah zamu kiyaye bayan wannan babu wata matsalar ko ? Gyad'a Musu Kai yayi yace, " babu wata damuwa da yardar Allah saboda nurse tace mun jinin ta ya sauka yayi dai-dai " gaba d'aya suka amsa da, " Alhamdulillah " tattaunawa suka K'arasa yi da Dr sannan suka fito waje, tun Daga wannan lokacin Ya Asim da Sadiq suka jone waje d'aya suka zama abokai.

       Da daddare suna zaune akunyace Ya Asim yace, " Sadiq Dan Allah karkamun mummunar fahimta bisa ga yanda na hak'ik'ancewa cewar ni mijin Farida ne, nayi hakan ba Dan komai ba sai Dan Kare martabarta Kuma....... " Da murmushi Sadiq ya katse shi sannan yace, " Dan Allah karmuyi Haka dak Asim azamana daku na fahimci karamci da mutumcinku inda Kai mutumin banza na tabbata da ka nuna halinka akanta, so mubar wannan maganar idan ban gode muku ba kana ganin Zan BUTULCE Muku ne, kamanta Karin maganar mlm bahaushe da yace D'an halak baya manta halacci " murmushi Ya Asim yayi yace, " shikenan nagode Abokina " daga Haka suka cigaba da hirar su cikin girmama juna.

        Wayarsa Sadiq ya ciro number su Umma yake ta trying da farko ya Kira akashe sai bayan kamar minti Ashirin ya Kara Kira cikin sa'a yaji tana shiga, tana gabda tsinkewa yaji Umma ta d'auka da sallama, amsawa yayi cikin farin ciki da Annushuwa daga b'angaren umma ta amsa, " lallai Saddik'u lafiya najika cikin farin ciki Haka rabona da Jin muryarka Haka har na manta " murmushi Sadiq yayi yace, " Ranki ya Dade wani abun farin ciki ne ya samemu gaba d'aya shiyasa Kika jini Haka " murmushi Umma tayi tace, " Allah Sadiq Gaya Mana me yake faruwa ne ko sun ce maka angano gunda Farida take ne " har zai Bata da amsa da, " eh " sai yayi wani tunani azuciyarsa na cewar saidai kawai yayi Musu surprise, basarwa yayi yace, " wani aiki ne akace za'a tura mu Amma allowances din da za'a dinga Bamu awaya ya fi dubu d'ari biyar " cikin jin dad'i Umma take San barka sannan ta dinga jero Masa Addu'a dacewada dawowa lafiya, sun danyi waya me tsayi sannan Umma ta mik'awa Inna itama suka gaisa, Yana Jin muryar Inna tausayinta ne ya kamashi dakewa yayi suka gaisa itama sun tab'a Hira sannan take ta yi Masa Addu'o'i kariya da samun nasara, ban sun gama Umma ta Kara mik'awa wani tunani Umma tayi cikin damuwa tace, " Sadiq wani hanzari ba gudu ba, gurin aikin da zasu turaku ba gurun yak'e-yak'e bane ko ? " Murmushi yayi yace a'a Umma ba cen bane kedai kuyi tayi Mana Addu'a " jiki ba kwari Umma tace, " to Allah ya tsare ya kareku " amsawa yayi da Aameen sannan yace, " Umma anan cikin gidan akwai  abinda kuke buk'ata ne? " Dan Jim kad'an tayi tace, " eh ba'a rasa ba Bari zuwa safiya abinda babu na Kira na Gaya maka " sun Jima suna Hira daga karshe sukayi sallama ya kashe wayar.

          Tashi Sadiq yayi ya shiga gurun Farida tana zaune Ruwan tea Rukayya ta had'a Mata tana Sha, Hassan ne ya fara kuka Rukayya ta Mik'a Mata shi, nono ya Fara Sha har ya kusa koshi usainin shima ya fara kuka karbar Hassan Rukayya tayi ta kwantar dashi sannan ta Mika Mata usaini Yana gabda gama Shan nono Sadiq ya shiga d'akin Rukayya ce ta fita ta basu guri, bayan ya kamalla Sha karbarsa Sadiq yayi Yana Mata murmushi, sunkuyar da kanta tayi cikin sanyi murya tace, " Ya Sadiq yaushe zamuje gurun su Inna nagaji da zaman asibitin Nan so nake naje nagansu nasan hankalin su ba akwance yake ba " kallan ta yayi cikin so da kauna yace, " sai abinda likita yace, Kinga jikinki ba kwari yanxu kina Kara warwarewa zamu tafi ko, Kuma karki damu su Inna babu abinda zai samesu sai Alheri Kiyi hakuri likita ya Bamu sallama shikenan Kinga sai mu tafi da yaranmu, ummm Wai Farida Anzama mother " ya K'arasa fad'a cikin zolaya, turo Baki tayi tace, " wallahi Ya Sadiq banaso, yauwa ba cewa kayi ma ba cikinka bane na tuna bani yarana " ta K'arasa fad'a tana mik'a hannu alamun ya Bata, tsuke fuska yayi ayanayin rashin wasa yace, " banasan ki K'ara fadar Haka inba Haka ba zamuyi fad'a da ke " kallansa take yanda ya hakikance Yana magana da kakkausar murya sannan tace, " tab lallai ya Sadiq kana da k'arfin hali to wallahi nikuma maganar Nan azaune take babu Wanda ya isa ya baka su ba tunda da bakinka ka fad'a " kallan mamaki yakewa Farida uffan Bai ce Mata ba ya janyo Mata wata hirar tun tana basarwa tana d'auke Kai har Kuma ta saki jiki sukayi ta hira, Sadiq bashi ya bar Asibitin ba sai da dare ya Fara yi sannan yayiwa su Rumaisa sallama ya tafi.

       Sadiq ya jinjinawa karamci da mutumci irin nasu Abba tunda aka kwantar da Farida duk wasu abubuwan buk'ata su sukeyi tun ranar da Sadiq ya sai kayan jarirai ya Kai da Abba yaji labari tatas yayi Masa yace su Dan Allah suke wa Farida komai ya bar kud'in sa Kar ya Kara siyan Koda abin biyar ne da sunan zai kawo wa Farida, Yan uwansu ummee haka suke yawan Kiran waya suna Duba farida, abinda ya hanasu zuwa ma saboda ance Musu ba'a buk'atar y'an dubiya shiyasa suka hakura Amma su Ummee da Sadiq ba karamin sabo sukayi ba, har Abba na tsokar Sadia wai ragwan maza Yana yiwa mace kuka, sai dai suyi ta dariya cikin Raha.

       Kwanan Farida hudu a hospital din aka sallameta saboda yanda jikinta yayi kyau gurin stitches dinta ma babu wata matsala sai sauran magungunan ta da aka Bata sannan akayi Musu bill, alokacin babu yanda Abba baiyi da Sadiq Dan ya Bari su biya Amma sadiq fir yak'i, Yana zuwa gurin Accountant tace Masa Asim ya biya, Sadiq yaji dad'i matuk'a Haka suka rankaya gaba d'aya suka wuce gida, kulawar da Farida take samu sai wacce ta karu, abinda zai baka dariya har Sadiq aka koma Nan su Rumaisa suka dinga yimai tsiya suna zolayasa, idan yaji abun yayi yawa sai dai ya tashi ya basu guru, Victor ne ya kirashi yace yaje hospital din ance ansallame su, address din gidan ya turawa Victor lokaci kad'an ya K'arasa cen gidan.
       Sadiq ne ya fita cen waje ya sameshi bayan sun gaisa Victor yace, " Sadiq gurun aiki sun Fara complain akan bakasan zuwa duty " canja fuska yayi saboda shi ya manta da wani batun aiki, guntun tsaki yayi yace, " ba nace ka Gaya Musu matata na hospital an Mata CS ba? " Kallansa Victor yayi yace, " yau akayi Mata CS ko jiya, Sadiq duk wata karya da zanyi Dan na kareka nayi ta to nidai na fita maganar gaskiya ka dawo aiki to Wai akanka aka Fara haihuwa " tsuke fuska Sadiq ya karayi yace, " look karka gayan magana fa, insha Allah gobe zanje shikenan " eh shikenan Mana kasan Kuma Monday zamu wuce ko " cewar Victor.
      " Eh Ina sane Allah ya kaimu " Sadiq na fad'a ya juya zai koma da mamaki Victor yace, " Sadiq Ina zaka koma gidan mutane? " Nuna gidan yayi da hannu yace gurun yarana da Mata ta " Bai rufe baki ba Victor ya fisgo hannunsa yace, " haba Be a Man karka bada maxa fa, ka manta Kai waye ne kaifa soja ne Amma sai ka dinga wasu irin abubuwa " da wad'annan maganganun Victor yajanye Sadiq suka wuce.
         Sadiq duk haushin komawarsa aiki yakeji dandai babu yanda zaiyi ne da ba abinda zai sashi  komawa gurun aiki, farida ce ta tayar Musu da balli kullin maganar ta d'aya Ya Sadiq ya kaita gurun su Inna, wani lokaci har cewa tayi idan bazasu kaita ba ita zata yi tafiyarta, danma Ummee na lallamarta tana nuna Mata ta Bari jikinta yayi kwari tukunna, idan abun ya Fad'o Mata sai dai ta zauna taci kuka ta gode Allah.

         Ranar juma'a Sadiq yayiwa Yara hud'uba da sunan mahaifinsa Dana farida, Hassan yaci sunan mahaifinsa sai usaini yaci sunan mahaifin farida ana kiransu Areef da Irfan aranar ne Farida ta sasu agaba Banda kuka babu abinda takeyi, hankalin Sadiq ba karamin tashi yayi ba, da kyar ya lallameta ya shawo kanta yace ta Bari zuwa washegari zasu tafi, jin hakan ne yasa Farida ta daina kukan saboda gaba d'aya hankalin ta yayi gurunsu Inna musamman da yak'i Bata su Koda ta waya ne su gaisa, akwai lokacin da har ce Masa tayi idan mutuwa Inna tayi ya gaya Mata yace Mata duk suna Nan babu wacce ta mutu, farida Yan kwanakin da tayi har ta Fara tuno da wasu abubuwan da suka faru da ita tsakaninsu dasu Rukayya shiyasa suke sata agaba suyita zolaya.

       Washegari Farida ta Riga kowa shiryawa ji take kamar ta janyo tafiyar, Abba ne yace zasu shirya gaba d'aya Dan suyi Mata rakiya mota biyu suka sa aka wanke, Haka suka shirya tsaba Farida ta Riga kowa zuwa bakin mota suna ta tsokaranarta da zolayarta ita kuwa Baki yak'i rufuwa, Sadiq ko kad'an Bai cewa su Umma zai kawo farida ba ya dai ce Musu zaizo da Baki abokan aikin sa agirka abinci da D'an dama, mota suka shiga da kayan farida Dana babies d'in ta himm masu uban yawa tunda y'an uwa Haka suka dinga kawowa Farida kayan jarirai musamman da Yan biyu suke da farin jini, tafiya suke sosai suka mik'i hanyar Kano.


      _To fans na gobe in Allah ya kaimu fa ba tabbas._

[10/20, 8:31 PM] Ummou Aslam😍Bint Adam❤️: *بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

😭 *TSANTSAR BUTULCI*😭

    _Ameera Adam_
                _AmeeraAdam60_
              *@Wattpad*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  83&84

      _Not Edited_



         Tun suna tafiya ana hirarraki har kowa ya fara gajiya yayi shiru, tafiya sukayi me nisan gaske sannan suka Fara shigowa cikin garin kano, Farida murna bakinta har yaki rufuwa farin ciki take yi a bayyane su Rukayya sai zolayarta sukeyi, duk  irin gudun da motar su takeyi Farida gani take yi kamar ba'a sauri har ayyana yanda zata samu su ummu da Inna take yi, ita kad'ai take murmushi tana Kara kallan yanayin garin da yanda mutane ke sabgar gabansu, Irfan dake hannun halima ne ya Fara kuka karbarsa tayi ta bashi yasha daga Nan yayi bacci, lokacin da suka isa unguwar su Sadiq 11:30 am alokacin farida ji tayi kamar ta fita da gudu tun kafin su K'arasa kofar gida, motocinsu ne sukayi parking a kofar gidan su Sadiq sannan suka Fara fitowa, Sadiq na Shirin yiwa Farida magana Ina da gudun gaske ta fad'a cikin gidan ko sallama babu tana kwalawa su Inna kira, " Innaaaaaa Innaaaaaa Ummaaaaa kuna inaaa?" kamar amafarki Inna da umma suka ji muryar Farida, umma ce ta Fara fitowa Bata K'arasa fitowa ba Farida da gudu ta rungume Umma tana mak'aleta, Inna kusan mutuwar tsaye tayi da kyar ta janyo kafafuwanta ta iya fitowa aykuwa Farida ta fad'a kanta cikin ihu da murna, Inna harda hawayen farin ciki kallan Farida tayi cikin k'auna tace, " Farida yau ke muke gani agabanmu " Umma ce ta Kara matsawa kusa da Farida tana rik'e hannunta tace, " y'ar Nan Ina Kika shiga Kika barmu Kai Alhamdulillah mungode Allah " Sadiq dake baya yace, " wato ni babu Wanda yake ta tawa ko? " Inna ce ta saki Farida cikin jin dad'i tace, " haba D'ana karka Fad'i haka " murmushi yayi yace, " umma muje d'aki da bak'i muke tafe " Umma labule ta d'aga tana wa su Rukayya barka da zuwa sannan tayi Musu iso ciki, Inna d'aki ta shiga zata deb'o Musu mahutai, Sadiq yayi wa Abba da Ya Asim jagora zuwa d'akin sa na da.

           Umma fitowa zatayi kaiwa su Ummee ruwa da drinks Inna ta Mik'a Mata muhucin da kanta ta wuce kitchen ta deb'o Musu pure water da drinks, Farida na lafe jikin Umma har k'asa umma tatsugunna ta gaida Ummee cikin girmamawa Ummee ta amsa tace, " Rukayya mik'a Mata mazajen ta su gaisa Mana " kamar amafarki Umma ta ga abun mamaki anmik'o mata jarirai har guda biyu, bakinta wasai ta sa hannuwa biyu ta karb'a saboda farin ciki har rasa abin fad'a tayi, tana kallan fuskar su wani irin annushuwa ta dinga ji ba abinda take fad'a sai, " Alhamdulillah "

           Inna shigowa tayi da sallama bayan ta zauna su Rumaisa suka gaishe ta, cikin girmamawa ta kalli dattijuwar zata gaishe ta, Inna ido ta zurawa dattijuwar Haka ma Ummee kallan Inna take ta kasa d'auke idanta, asanyaye tace, " Ina kwana " itama Ummee ta amsa jiki ba kwari, " lafiya kalau mun sameku lafiya? " Inna ta amsa da lafiya klau " kowannensu kallan Dan uwansa yake da sun had'a ido sai su janye idanunsu, Umma cikin farin ciki tace wa Inna, " Sadiya Kinga y'ayan Farida " mik'a Mata Umma tayi ta karb'a cikin jindad'i Amma sai tayi ka ra Bata nuna farin cikinta afili sosai ba, tana karb'a basu Jima a hannunta ba ta Mik'awa umma su, Inna kallan Ummee take kamar zata tanka Mata Amma sai taga kamar Kama ce, itama Ummee abunda yake cinta kenan azuciya, Umma ce ta cewa Rukayya, " ke Rik'e masu gidan Nan Azubo Muku abinci " Umma na Shirin tashi Sadiq ya lek'o yace, " Umma ku lek'o ku gaisa da Abba Yana d'akina " Inna ta amsa da, " To Bari mu zo " hijabi Umma ta janyo akan hannun kujera tasa, Inna jiki ba kwari ta tashi ta shiga d'akin ta ta d'auko hijabi, tare suka shiga d'akin Sadiq da sallama suka shiga cikin d'akin suka zauna daga bakin kofa, Ya Asim ne ya Fara gaishe su suka amsa sannan suka gaida Abba cikin girmamawa, da murmushi afuskar Abba ya amsa, " lafiya kalau mun sameku lafiya? " Umma ce kad'ai ta amsa ta iya amsawa, Inna d'agowa tayi saboda yanda taji muryar Daddynta kau ta karad'e dodon kunnenta ga Kuma zallar ainihin kamanninsa Nan, azuciyata tace, " idan Waccen namun Kama da Ammi wannan ma kenan Mana ce? " bakin Inna har rawa yake tace, " Daddy " sai alokacin Abba ya d'ago da kansa ya kalli Inna shima kafeta yayi da ido cikin Farin ciki yace, " Allah me iko wa nake gani kamar Halimatu na " hawaye ne ya ciko idanun Inna Nan da Nan ta Fara zubda kwallah tace, " ni ce Daddy, dama Zan K'ara ganinku arayuwa ta " Ya Asim, Sadiq da Umma sororo sukayi suna kallan Abba da Inna, Abba ne ya d'aga hannun sama yace, " Alhamdulillah Allah mun gode maka da ka amsa Addu'ar mu ka bayyana Mana y'ar mu tsawon shekaru da muka rasa ta " Inna kuka take sosai harda shashshek'a sai yanzu su Umma suka fahimci inda maganar su Inna ta dosa, Abba ne ya kalli Sadiq yace, " jeka kiramun Ummee " da sauri Sadiq ya fita yaje ya kira Ummee,  da sallama ta shiga d'akin cen gefe ta samu guri ta zauna idanunta na Kan Inna dake goge hawaye, Abba ne ya kalli Ummee yace, " kin gane waccen? " Dama kamar jira Ummee take yi tayi karaf tace, " Wallahi tunda na shigo take mun Kama da halimatu na banyi magana bane Dan Kar mutum yace ka Sanya Masa ido, Amma kamarsu tayi yawa sai dai nasan nida halimatu na sai adarussalamu tunda tafiya ta Mik'a tun Ina sa ran ganinta yanxu har na cire, ganin wannan baiwar Allah ba karamin famamun ciwon zuciyata yayi ba " Ummee ta K'arasa maganar tana fashewa da kuka, kowa sai da ya tausayawa Ummee yanayin data tsinci kanta aciki, Inna ganin bazata iya jurewa ba yasa ta taso da sauri ta rungume Ummee tana fashewa da kuka me tsuma zuciya, itama Ummee kukan take gwanin tausayi, hatta Umma sai da ta zubda kwallah saboda lamarin akwai bugun zuciya ga duk me tausayi.

        Sai da sukayi kukansu me isarsu sannan Inna ta d'ago da Kai tace, " Ammi na ni kaina ban tab'a tunanin Zan K'ara saku afuskata ba, saboda na neme ku nayi nema har na gode Allah bansame ku ba, Ammi yau ku nake gani alokacin da banyi tsammani, Ammi Ashe Zan K'ara ganinku a rayuwa ta " mamaki ne ya kama Ammi tace, " idan Wasa kike mun ki daina bazan jure irin wannan wasan ba " Abba ne ya jinjina Kai kicin godiyar ubangiji yace, " Y'ar ki Halima ce agabanki Allah yayi nashi ikon " Ammi kafe Inna tayi da kallo tace, " Halimatu na kece? " Rungume Inna ta karayi tsam kamar wani zai kwace ta, Ya Asim ne ya ce, " Abba Wai Aunty Sadiya dai da kullin kuke bamu labarin ta itace wannan " Eh ita ce Asim, yau kusan shekara Ashirin da bakwai rabonmu da ita sai yau Allah yayi nasa ikon, lallai shigowar Humaira ko ince Farida rahama ne agaremu gaba d'aya " cewar Abba.

       Sai alokacin Umma tayi magana, " Allah shi ne abun godiya Ammi muma Nan ko yaushe hirar ku ta Fad'o Mana munayinta, a fuska ne kawai bamusan ku ba Alhamdulillah mahakurci mawadaci, Sadiya Kinga ranar hakurin da nake cewa kiyi komai lokaci ne, tabba duk Abinda hakuri Bai baka ba rashinsa ma bazai baka shi ba " Sadiq cikin zolaya yace, " wato dama Abba da biyu ka rik'e mun Mata shiyasa muka fafata daku kafin ku yarda ni mijinta ne, su o'o har Yana Shirin Wuff da Mata ta, Ashe ita gida ta fada" gaba d'aya d'akin aka sa dariya.


           Jin shirun yayi yawa yasa Farida ta fito  tsakar gida tana cewa, " Umma nifa ban gaji da ganinku ba wata da watanni bana Nan Inna na zaci har goyani zakiyi " ta K'arasa fad'a tana shiga d'akin Sadiq, hango Inna tayi ajikin Ummee fuskarsu duk tayi jawur Abinka da fararen fata, zama tayi gefen Umma tana rik'e hab'a tace, " Inna kukan me Kuma Kika keda zakiyi murna Amma ji fuskar ki duk ta kumbura, nasan Ummee da tausayi hala itama tayaki tayi " murmushi Umma tayi tace, " Farida dole Sadiya tayi kuka ta had'u da mahaifanta ke ya Kika ji da Kika ganta ba murna kikayi harda tsalle ba? " Cikin rashin fahimta Farida tace, " Umma mahaifanta Kuma su waye mahaifan nata " Farida ta K'arasa fad'a tana y'an dube-dube, Abba ne yace, " ja'ira Ashe ba banza tun da kike figarmu ba, mune Nan mahaifan nata " Farida Jin maganar tayi amma badan ta yarda ba shiyasa tace, " Alhaji Abba Ina da Wanda Zan figa bayan Kai ne, ay anan ma zaka bar Mana Ummee sai tayi wata guda ma mayar da ita " ganin ta d'auki maganar a Wasa umma ta Fara yi Mata bayani, ta'ajibi da mamaki ne ya kama Farida tace, " Ikon Allah, Allah me yanda yaso Allah sarki rayuwa, Abba baka ga wuyar da ta Sha ba wajen neman inda kuke, to Ammi ya akayi kuka dawo Abuja " Inna cikin Wasa tace, " ke karki damesu da tambaya ay Kya Bari su huta " su Rukayya ne sukayi sallama suka shigo suna Fad'in, " to tunda duk kun dawo Nan muma Bari muzo ayi damu " zama sukayi suka da Farida, kallan Rukayya tayi ta d'aka Mata duka acinya tace, " y'ar uwa Ashe soyayyar mu ajini take " Rukayya hararar Wasa ta watsawa Farida tace, " to Dan mugunta shi ne zakici zalina " Rumaisa tace, " bar muguwa Wai dabara zatayi Miki " dariyar farida tayi tace, " dallah da gaske nake Muku ku sa wasa " Abba ne ya katse Musu hirar Yana gabatar Musu da Inna gaba d'aya mamaki ne ya kamasu Rumaisa tace, " Ummee wallahi sai yanxu nake ganin kamarsu da Aunty Mami " Ammi tace, " Ay jini ba Wasa ba " Rukayya ta ce, " kuma wallahi Nima Naga yanayin kamar Aunty Sadiya da Abba " cikin zolaya Farida tace, " ba wani Inna tafi shi kyau " harara Inna tayiwa Farida tace, " karya kike Yarinya ubana ya fini kyau ke ko Rabin kyansa Kika samu Ay kinji Dadi " gaba d'aya aka sa dariya Sadiq Dan tsuke fuska yayi cikin Wasa yace, " Haba Dan Allah wama zai had'a, Ummou Areef Wai kin yarda Abba yafi ki kyau " ta kalli Sadiq tana Kara kallan Abba tace, " Ina fa kawai dai Inna ce da abinta Wai ta samu su Ammi yau ta juya mun baya Allah sarki ni yau Inna ta maidani kwandan shara " gaba d'aya suka kwashe da dariya Umma ta cewa Farida, " Banda Abinki wata kusan ay tafi wata ko " Farida ta marairai ce murya tace, " ay shikenan Umma bani da ta cewa sai inkoma cen inda na fito ay " Rukayya tayi karaf tace, " a'a sai dai ki koma cen duniyar da MD ya Fara jefa ki " su Rumaisa suka ce me  zasuyi aykuwa suka kwashe da dariya Halima tace, " kin tuna a asibiti farkon farkowarta girma take bani kamar wata uwarta Aunty sama Aunty k'asa Amma tunda ta felle shikenan, nikuwa kaina har girma yake karayi tunda nasan ta girme ni Amma tana ban girma " guntun tsaki Farida tayi tana watsa Musu harara tace, " y'an Iskan gari Ashe dama tama ni kukeyi a'a sai dai inkoma duniyar uwar MD " Aykuwa gaba d'aya d'akin aka sa dariya Banda su Inna da basusan Kan labarin ba.

       Hirar ce ta barke atsakinsu sai da sukayi Sallar Azahar sannan suka zubo Abinci suna ci, labari har ya riski Makota na dawowar Farida masu gulma da tsegumi har anfara gutsutsutsura maganar kowa da yanda yake isar da nashi zancen, har da masu cewa dama yawonta ta tafi tunda gashi ta dawo kalau da ita ta shirga uwar k'iba, y'an ganin kwaf har zuwa suka farayi da sunan Taya su Inna murna, Kai harda masu cewa dama Saurayinta ne ya sace ta Dan yaga ta haihu ne ya gudu ya barta, shiyasa suka dinga turuwar zuwa ana d'aukan jariran ta sai sunga jariran ne sai bakin kowa ya mutu su fita jiki a sab'ule, Bayan sunyi sallah suna zaune Inna tace, " Ammi Ina su baba Hussain da kawu , Kuma Wai dama da kuka bar Sudan Abuja kuka dawo " Ammi tace, " wannan labari ne mai tsayi Amma Bari na Baki shi a takaice " gaba d'aya gyara zama sukayi suna sauraron Abinda Ammi zata Fara fad'a.

     _Wannan Shafin sadaukar ne ga Duk mahaifan mu Ubangiji ya ja da ransu ya basu jinkiri me Amfani, wad'anda suka rasu Allah ya jaddada rahamarsa agaresu._

        _Tun bayan tafiyarku Nigeria keda mijinki baifi da wata uku ba sai fad'a ya Fara shiga tsakanin mu da makotan k'asar mu, tun ana ganin abun kamar Wasa har ya Fara zama babba da farko munyi tsammanin Fad'an bazai shigo har cikin k'asa ba saboda babu abinda yake faruwa acikin Sudan sai Daga baya suka Fara kawo Mana farmaki, to kuskuren da akayi da aka bijirewa shawarwarin shuwagabanni, saboda alokacin shugabanni sun fito sunyiwa jama'a nasiha Akan karsu d'auki doka ahannunsu, gwamnati zata yi bakin kokarinta wajen ganin ta tattauna da shugaban waccen k'asar domin asami masalaha, to da farko anfara d'aukan abinda suka fad'a kunsan halin mutane kowa da yanda tunaninsa zai bashi, sai kawai mutane suka yanke shawarar suyi kungiya da zu ga me yawa domin su je su rama abinda akeyi Musu, hakan kuwa sukayi  ko mu Nan bamusan da wannan Shirin nasu ba sai da ta kwab'e har aka hasko alabarai sannan muka gani, Ashe suna zuwa ciki suka farma mutane da Sara suka Kan uwa da wabi babba da yaro, ganin Haka yasa suma Yan cen garin suka dinga d'aukan makamai suna maida martani, kunsan Abinka da Wanda Bai shiryawa hakan ba da Wanda yaxo da shirinsa kunsan abun bazai tab'a zuwa d'aya ba, lokaci k'ank'ani mutanen mu suka cinmusu suka raunata mutane da dama suka yi Musu asarar dukiya me yawa, Kai masu Karar kwana ma du anan Rai yayi halinsa, Haka suka juyo suka dawo sai da aka dinga nunawa a gidajen television sannan yawancin mutane sukasan abinda yake wakana, shugabanni k'asar mu ransu yayi mugun b'aci Haka suka sa aka dinga kamo wad'anda suka aikata laifin ana hukunta su sannan Saida suka biya waccen k'asar diyya, tunda Haka ta faru babu wani Abu Daya K'ara faruwa Ashe suma gangamin shiri sukeyi na gaske Bamu sani ba, Dan yawancin mutane sun sakankace tunda sai da muka shafe wata biyu ko sama da Haka ma sannan wata rana suka Fara kawo Mana hari._

         _A farko-farkon kawo harinsu mutane suka dinga bi na hanya duk Wanda suka gani su far Masa ko su kashe shi har lahira ba ruwansu da karamin yaro ko tsoho, alokacin kowa sai zaman gida ya koma y'an kasuwa aka kowa ya koma gida da zama ma'aikata ma Haka, y'an sanda da sojojinma kwantan b'auna suke Musu suna kashe su, da suka ga mutane sun ganewa zaman gida sai suka dinga bi asibitai, gidan yari da gidan marayu suna jefa bama-bamai, anan ne hankalin mutane ya Kara tashi kowa ya rasa yanda zaiyi Abu ya Fara neman gagarar gwanti saboda ba k'aramin shiri sukayi ba, kwana biyu anji k'ura ta lafa har mutane sun Fara fita sai jimukayi bom ya tashi Ashe cen babban banki suka kai Hari, ganin Haka yasa mutane suka dinga turuwar zuwa karb'ar kud'ad'ensu na sauran bankuna, Haka magidanta suka dinga shiga gida da buhunhunan kud'ade cikin gida, kamar sunsan abinda mutane sukeyi aykuwa wata rana Daddynki ya shigo da tashi dukiyar kenan mukaji labarin sunkai Hari a manyan bankuna biyar Kuma kowanne cike yake da mutane tunda kowa fafutukar neman hakkinsa yake, acikin wannan harin harda y'an uwana hudu da y'an uwan mahaifinki biyu, wannan harin ba karamin tashin hankali yasa mu ciki ba ki baccin kirki a gidajen mu bama iya yi, shugabanni k'asar mu ransu yayi mummunan b'aci Haka suka samu wancen shugaban sai cewa yayi shi Yana zargin ma ba y'an k'asar sa ke wannan aika-aikar ba, saboda cewa yayi basu da kud'in da zasu sai irin wannan mugayen makaman Amma Sai ya bada umarnin duk Wanda aka samu akashe shi._

            _Wannan tashin hankalin da muke ciki banda gaba babu abinda yakeyi saboda alokacin sai muka fahimci ba iya d'aukan fansa sukeyi ba, bayan fansa harda sata sukeyi, sai suka Fara shiga gidajen mutane idan suka kwashe dukiyarku sai su cinna Muku wuta duk ku kone, ana cikin Haka sai baba Hussain ya bada shawarar muyi hijira daga k'asar zuwa wata k'asa, da farko k'in yarda y'an uwan mahaifinki sukayi sai da gaba d'aya gari kowa ya Fara hijira da dukiyarsu da iyalansu sannan suka Amince, lokacin da zamu tafi Banda sark'ok'ina da dukiyar mahaifinki babu abinda muka d'auka sai y'an kayan sawarmu, ita kanta dukiyar Saida mukayi Mata dabarar da idan ka ganta bazaka tab'a tsammanin kud'i bane sunnan suka tattara  kwai da fure muka fito, Ina Shirin zuwa gun mahaifana inyi Musu magana su fito mu wuce labari ya riskemu ansawa unguwar mu bom ko kaza Bata shura ba, alokacin cewa nayi bazan tafi ba gwara Nima ahada Dani da kyar mahaifinki ya shawo kaina na bisu, munaji muna gani Haka mukabar komai namu musamman babban gida iyalan cikinsa Haka suka fito kwai da fure suka bar komai nasu dai dukiyoyinsu da yan kadarori to abinda Zan ce muku mun tafi dasu motoci ne, suma muna zuwa wani gari da mahaifanki suka sayi wani karamin gida suka had'a su suka siyar, kud'ad'en su sannu ahankali suka dinga kaisu banki suna ajiyewa._ 

     _Shekarar mu ukune agarin suka yanke shawara atsakaninsu akan mu hauro k'asar Nigeria to da farko dai sun Sami hatsaniya musamman daga gurin baba Hussain sai daga baya suka amince muka tarkato kwai da fure muka dawo k'asar Nigeria, awani kauyen Abuja muka sauka arugar wasu fulani, mutanen rugar mutanen kirki ne munyi sabo dasu sosai Dan har mun Fara jin yarensu duk abinda suka fad'a muna ji sai dai wani ne bama fahimta kuma bama iya maida Musu da yaransu na fillanci, iyayenki kasuwanci suka kafa cikin ikon Allah, Allah ya albarkace shi suka dinga samun riba Mai Albarka, Shekarar mu uku arugar Nan muka dawo cikin garin Abuja kowa ya sayi mahallinsa shida iyalinsa a mahalli me kyau cikin rufin asiri, to kinji abinda ya faru a takaice._"

            Inna ce tace, " amma alokacin ba na dinga neman layinku bana Samu gaba d'aya har nasu baba Hussain kowa na Kira bana samu " murmushi Abba yayi yace, " ay takaita Miki tayi Amma idan tace zata Baki labarin duk abinda ya faru ay sai awuni akwana ana d'aya " jinjina Kai Ammi tayi tace, " kwarai kuwa domin munga gararin rayuwa " Inna ce tayi murmushi tace, " Ammi inajin duk rayuwar da kuka gani kuwa kun kaini, nayi tsammanin ma zaku tambayeni ya akayi nazo Kano nida nake agarin Adamawa " Ammi ce tace, " mu Nan nida dangin mahaifinki har Adamawa mukaje da tambaya da tambaya har gidansu Hashim munje Amma akace Mana kun bar garin keda mijinki da yaranki saboda yayi kisan shi ne ya tasaku agaba kuka gudu, alokacin ce Mana sukayi Shekarar ku hudu da tafiya ko me kama daku basu tab'a gani ba, tundaga lokacin muka sawa zuciyarmu hakurin rashin ki tunda bamuda tabbacin kina Raye ko bakya Raye, Haka damuwa da hadu ta samu agaba nida mahaifinki danma shi yake k'arara karfafarta da rarrashina har na hakura na fauwalawa Allah lamurana, Amma Allah shi ne shaida tunda na d'ora ido na akan Farida yanayin kamanninki kina budurwa ne ya Fad'o mun nidai kallanta kawai nake ga Kuma kaunarta da nakeji har cikin zuciyata " Abba ne yace, " Allah me iko kenan me tsara yanda yaso alokacin da yaso ".

          Inna kallan Umma tayi idanunta na cikowa da kwallah sannan ta kalli Abba tace, " Daddy wannan itace mahaifiyar Sadiq sirikar Farida ko ince uwar Farida, Abba aduniya Bayin Allah Nan sunyimun komai banida bakin gode musu, sun karramamu sun taimakemu Kai komai na rayuwa sunyi mana, kaga wannan " Inna ta nuna Sadiq ta cigaba da cewa, " kafin asamu irinsu sai anbincika Ammi banida bakin da Zan fasalta irin abun alherin da suka mana, ko farida kafin haduwa ta dasu ne nasan wahalar ta Amma yanda uwa ke kula da y'arta Haka Umma take kula da Farida har gobe, Bari inbaku labarin irin tamu rayuwar da muyi abaya da taimakon da su Sadiq sukayi Mana,........" Tundaga farkon dawowarta daga Sudan har had'uwar su da Sadiq da taimakon da yayi Musu har zuwa dawowar su gidan umma Ka ma da auren Farida zuwa b'atan da tayi duk ta basu labari, sosai suka tausaya Musu su Rukayya Banda hawaye babu abinda sukeyi har Ammi ba'a barta abaya ba itama Saida ta share hawayenta, Inna ta cigaba da cewa, " Daddy sai da nayi danasanin auren Hashim yafi sau dubu bawai Dan Yanada mugun hali ba, hasali ma har Allah ya d'auki rayuwar sa Yana matuk'ar k'auna ta....." Ammi da Daddy suka katse ta da Fad'in, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un Hashim ya rasu? Allah ya gafarta Masa " gaba d'aya gurun suka amsa da, " Ameen " sannan Inna ta d'ora, " Nayi Dana sanin ne saboda tunda matsalolin suka dinga b'ullo mun sai na yarda da cewar saboda ba asan ranku akayi shi ba duk da dai bawa baya gujewa k'addarar sa " murmushi Abba yayi yace, " Halima ko kad'an Bamu rik'e ku da wani Abu ba tunda mukayi na farko shikenan Kuma wannan akidar da Kika sani abaya tun Daga kanki ta rushe sai dai idan Yara su suka had'a kansu sai Muku manya mu shiga maganar ".

        Da haka wata hirar ta ka K'ara barkewa, atake Abba ya dinga kiransu Yana sanar dasu labarin ganin Inna da alakarta da Farida, aykuwa y'an uwan kwai da fure sukace Allah ya kashe su washegari zasu duro garin Kano Wanda yayi dai-dai da ranar Sunan Twins wato ranar lahadi, Abba da farko ce wa yayi shi da Ya Asim tafiya zasuyi fur su Umma sukace baza'ayi haka ba, Akan dole shima ya amince sai zuwa wayewar gari.

          Da daddare suna zaune suna Hira saboda hirar tasu gaba d'aya Taki karewa sai anyi shiru Inna zata cewa Ammi kaza itama Ammi sai anyi shiru zata cewa Inna kaza, Sadiq shida ya Asim suna cin Abinci aka Kira shi awaya bakuwar number ya gani sai da ta kusa tsinkewa ya d'auka, sai da suka gaisa da Wanda ya Kira sannan sukaji Sadiq ya na furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un "
