*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                   87&88

        _Not Edited_


            Gaba d'aya ido suka zubo Masa suna kallo har ya katse wayar cikin had'in Baki suka Fara tambayar sa, " lafiya me yake faruwa ne? " Cikin sanyin jiki yace, " wallahi wani makocinmu ne na unguwar tudun yola ya kirani Wai suna zaune suka hango wuta ta Kama a gidan mu " gaba suka furta, " innalillahiwa'ina'ilahirraji'un " da sauri Sadiq ya d'auki takalminsa Yana Shirin tafiya Ya Asim yace ya jira shi su tafi, suna fita Sadiq ya Kira fire service ya sanar dasu da Address din gidan, lokacin da suka K'arasa kusan tare suka K'arasa da y'an kwana-kwanan da k'arfin gaske aka doke key din, cikin kwarewar aiki suka kutsa ciki kansu tsaye, sun D'an d'auki lokaci suna artabu da wutar Amma Sam taki mutuwa kamar ma fetir suke Kara Mata, har sun Fara kosawa da siddabarun wutar sannan  ta Fara lafawa, sai da taci iya abinda tayi niyya sannan Dan kanta ta mutu lokaci guda, makotan Sadiq sai jajin ta Masa sukeyi bisa abinda ya faru, bayan kura ta lafa shiga gidan mutane suka farayi bayan da Sadiq yayi Musu jagora, abunda ya bawa kowa mamaki da tsoro yanda suka ga wutar bangare Daya taci wato part d'in Saudat, su kansu fire service din sai da kansu ya kulle da mamakin wannan lamarin, ganin babu abinda ya Sami part d'in Farida, sunjima suna kallan wannan yanayin sannan suka fito, Sadiq ya sai wani key din suka rufo gidan suka taho, ahanyarsa ta dawowa ne ya Nemo number Daddy ya kirashi duk abinda yake faruwa sai da gayawa Daddy, shi kanshi Daddy baiji dad'in abinda ya faru ba cikin jimami yace wa Sadiq, " Bana gari Sadiq Amma insha Allah gobe-gobe Zan shigo cikin garin " Sadiq ya amsa da, " Allah ya kaimu " har suna Shirin yin Sallama Sadiq ya tuna bai gayawa Daddy maganar Farida ba, murna ce ta cika Daddy farin cikinsa yak'i b'oyiwa bakinsa har kunne yake Taya murnar bayyanar Farida abangare d'aya Kuma farin cikinsa na fitowar Saudat ne tunda yasan muddin aka sanar da hukumar gidan prison da alkali akwai lokacin da zasu dibar Mata da ta K'are shi za'a sallameta " sun D'an jima suna waya sannan suka yi sallama, Kai tsaye gida suka wuce lokacin dare ya Fara tsalawa su Inna har sun Fara bacci.

         Washegari tun bayan sallar Asuba Sadiq da Ya Asim basu shigo cikin gida ba Kai tsaye siyayyar ragunan suna suka wuce da cefanan kayan Abincin suna dasu drinks basu suka shigo cikin gidan ba sai wajen 9:00 am lokacin Farida ta gama wanke lallen da aka rangad'a Musu ita dasu Rukayya, wanka ta shiga bayan ta fito Rumaisa ta fesa Mata makeup wani dakakken maroom din leshi ta d'auko ta saka Rumaisa ta buga mata d'aurin D'an kwalli lokaci d'aya ta koma tamkar Amarya, su Rukayya ma sunyi kyau sosai kowa ka kalli fuskar sa Yana cikin farin ciki musamman Inna bakinta gaba d'aya yak'i rufuwa, Makota ne suka shigo Taya su Umma aiki aketa cikiniyar d'ora girki, wata kawar umma ce tace aje gidant ayi girkin acen saboda asamu sararin gurun zaman jama'a, hakan kuwa akayi umma da Abokan arxikinta suka tafi cen gidanta aka D'ora girki, Inna na gida ita dasu Farida ita Kuma Umma tana cen Kan hidimar girki, suna Nan zaune mutanen Abuja suka Kira Abba suna tambayar Address din unguwar su Sadiq saboda sun iso cikin garin Kano, Sadiq ya bawa wayar ya dinga Musu kwatance sai gasu cikin lokaci k'alilan sun k'araso wajen mota biyar sukayi sallama sukayo cikin gidan da yawansu gwanin sha'awa, Inna na tsakar gida taji shigowarsu wad'anda basu ganeta ba sai binta suke da kallo wad'anda suka ganeta cikin murna suka rungume ta, y'an uwan Abba su biyar din Nan gaba d'aya sai da sukaxo da matansu harda tsirarun jikokinsu, masu kuka nayi masu hawaye nayi masu rik'e Inna suna duddubata nayi, nanfa Hira ta b'alle gida ya kacame anata hirar yaushe gamo, masu zolayar Farida suma suna tayi.


          Lokacin da Umma ta shigo cikin gidan sannu da zuwa tayi Musu Inna duk Wanda suka gaisa dashi sai ta gabatar Masa da Umma, idan kaga gidan su Sadiq zakayi tunanin biki akeyi dangin mahaifin Sadiq Dana umma suma Rana Bata gama bud'ewa ba suka dinga sallama gidan Umma har kadan yayi sai da wasu suka dinga shiga Makota, bakin Sadiq har kunne sai baza babbar Riga yake Yana gaisawa da mutane, yawan mutanen da suka halarcin sunan ko alokacin bikinsu basu taru Haka ba, y'an uwan Inna sai godiya suke jerawa dangin mahaifin Sadiq Dana Umma, lokaci d'aya sukayi sabo da junansu ana ta sabgar arziki da juna, su hafsa ma sun iso ita y'an uwanta gaba d'aya awannan zuwan da sukayi ne ake sanar da sa ranar ta ita da wani cousin brother dinta na b'angaren mamanta, kowa ya kalli Farida sai yaji ta burgeshi ita da babies d'inta.

           Kimanin k'arfe 3:00pm suka shirya Gaba d'aya suka tattara suka tafi hall d'in da Sadiq ya kama me suna *ELHAM EVENT CENTER* dake Kan titin Zoo Road, Yan uwa sunyi damk'am a hall d'in wasu na zaune wad'ansu na tsakiyar fili suna cashewa irinsu Rukayya masu jiran kad'an itada su hafsa tuni suka zage suna rawa, Sadiq da Farida sai wajen k'arfe 4:00pm suka K'arasa gurun Ya Asim ne ya kaisu, sun matuk'ar yin kyau doguwar rigar leshi tasha black color da ratsin stones golden color, hannunta rik'e da Irfan sai Sadiq da yasha farar shadda Tasha bak'in aiki hannunsa na Rik'e da Areef, suna shigowa hall din Gaba d'aya idanu ya hau Kan su atake DJ ya saki sassanyar wakar Nan ta Me&You Together for Ever, lokaci d'aya mutane suka mik'e tsaye kowa kallansu yake gwanin birgewa cikin ban sha'awa mai hoto da me video camera sai d'auka suke har sun kusa K'arasawa gurun zamansu zasu zauna DJ ya dakatar dasu yace su fito tsakiyar fili, hakan kuwa sukayi alokacin ya sauya wak'a Farida gaba d'aya idanun mutane yasa duk jinta take wani iri, Abokan Sadiq ne suka fito suka Fara zazzaga Musu kud'i sannan y'an uwa da Abokan axiki suka fito suna yiwa su Farida liki, hotuna aka dinga d'auka kala-kala sai da aka cashe sosai sannan mutane sukaje suka zauna, shima Sadiq da Farida sukaje mazauninsu suka zauna masu rawa suka cigaba da tattakawarsu, wasu manyan Mata na hango kowacce taci wanka tana taku dai-dai suka shigo shikin hall din kallan su aka dingayi musamman ganin irin wankan da suka d'auka, kujeru suka samu suka zauna daga nesa Farida ce ta hango Mum Aslam murmushi tayi Mata itama ta mayar Mata da martani, tashi sukayi sukaje aka daddauki hotuna da videos, Farida ce ta kalli Mum Aslam tace, " Sis Ashe da gaske zaku zo din na dauka ma da Wasa kuke " mum Aslam tace, " eh gamu munxo munyi Miki bazata " murmushi Farida ta karayi tace, " to ay bangane sauran ba naji kince _First Class Writers_ dukkansu zasu xo, sai fa kimun bayaninsu Dan duk muna gaisawa afuska ne bazan iya ganesu ba " mum Aslam ta kallin manyan Matan dake hannun hagun ta tace, " ga y'an _First class_ nan" sai ta Kara juyawa ta kalli na d'ayan b'angaren tace, " wad'annan sisters d'in groups din da muke ciki ne da Kika turo da Kika  gayyacesu akwai y'an, _bak'in zobe fans, D/auta novels, hadaka Writers, tsantsar Butulci fans, hazab fans, rukayatul islaha fans, kuncin rayuwa fans, mutuwar aure fans, mmn maleek novels, Dafta A'iha fans, princess Ayshatou palace, Sannu-sannu fans, official marcy novels, makauniyar rayuwa fans, sanyin idaniya fans, Mrs yarima fans, babu maraya novel, um/khadeeja novels daga marubutan mu_ Kai me jego bazasu lissafu ba fa gamu Nan dai gaba d'aya mu munzo tayaki murnar barka da arziki " Murmushi Farida tayi tace, " nagode kwarai Allah ya bar zumunci gaskiya sisters kun mun bazata banyi zatan zakuxo ba Wallahi" juyawa tayi gurun Sadiq ta gabatar Musu da bak'inta cikin sakin fuska suka gaisa dashi Yana Musu godiya, jariran suka dinga d'auka ana baza Musu hotuna, suna gama hotuna aka sakar Musu wakar zee baby sannu ahankali suka Fara tattakawa cikin kasaita, nokewa Farida tayi tace bazata taka ba aykuwa Mum Aslam ta janyota cikin Jin kunya ta Fara takawa aykuwa sauran y'an uwa na ganin Haka suka taso suna Mara Mata baya, mum Aslam ce ta Fara balla jaka tana zubawa Farida liki kawayen ma suka dinga deb'o kud'i suna lika Mata, DJ ya samu abinda yake so Haka ya dinga Wasa su Yana Sanya zafafan wak'oki', sai da suka cashe sannan suka koma mazauninsu, kafin suje tuni ankai Musu abinci da abinsha, suna ci suna Hira suna Yaba kyan da me jego tayi ita da babies d'inta, sai yamma likis suka je sukayiwa Farida sallama tare da mik'a Mata wata katuwar leda sukace gift din da suka kawowa twins ne, godiya sosai Farida tayi Musu sannan suka juya suka tafi har sunkai bakin kofar fita suka hango Inna har k'asa suka tsugunna suka gashe su ita da umma sannan suka fice daga halla d'in.


          Ana cikin wannan shagalin kiran Daddy ne ya shigo wayar Sadiq, d'auka yayi bayan sun gaisa Daddy yace, " Sadiq na shigo gari tun safe fa Ina zamu had'u" Sadiq yace, " Daddy yanxu Ina gurun walimar suna Amma ay ankusa tashi ko zuwa magriba sai nazo na sameka " ok to yayi Nima dama wani Abokina ya zo Nan garin zanje gurunsa mu gaisa indai ka gama kawai ka kirani " cewar Daddy.
      " To shikenan " Sadiq ya bashi amsa.
        Su Umma anatashan shagali fuskar Nan tasu wasai, anci ansha taro yayi taro gwanin sha'awa kowa yanata harkar gabansa, Alh maheer ( _wanda aka tab'a zubawa Inna shi taki_) Yana zaune cikin hall din wayarsa ce tayi kara number Abokinsa ya gani ta shigo wayarsa, tashi yayi ya fita sannan ya d'auka, Daddy ne ya sanar dashi Yana bakin get din fita yayi ya sameshi bayan sun gaisa Daddy yake cewa, " Kai mutumina Wai me ya kawo ka Kano babu labari? " Alh maheer yayi dariya sannan ya kwashe labarin da su Ammi suka bashi ya gayawa Daddy, ya d'ora da cewa, " Ashe Yarinyar ma su Ammi suka bigeta ta zauna agidansu to yanxu walimar sunan ake aciki " da mamaki Daddy yace, " Kai mutumina Wai harsu Abba da baba Alhaji duk suna ciki? " Alh maheer yace, " ay dukkan mu muna ciki tunda anan suke taron sunan " Alh maheer ne yayiwa Daddy jagora ya Isa ciki ya dinga gaisawa dasu saboda yasan yawancin Yan uwan Alh maheer, su Umma na ganin Daddy cikin mutumci suka gaida shi, Alh maheer yace, " Abokina kaga kanwar tawa da na baka labari" ya karasa fad'a Yana nuna Inna, da mamaki Daddy yace, " wannan fa ba mahaifiyar Farida bace? " Sororo Alh maheer yayi yace, " kasanta ne dama?" Daddy jinjina Kai yayi yace, " Allah me iko, lallai rashin sani yafi dare duhu, Ina Visa card din da nace maka inaso kusan Shekara biyu da ta gabata ta mutum biyu to ay tata ce " Nan fa hayaniya ta kaure kowa da abinda yake fad'a su kansu su Inna mamaki ne ya kamasu Jin cewar Daddy babban Abokin Alh maheer ne, daga cen nesa ya hango Sadiq da Farida zaune da yaran su ahannu, wani tunani ne ya Fad'o Masa cikin zuciyarsa yace, " Yaron Nan ba cewa akayi Saudat na HIV ba, karfa ya shafawa Y'ar mutane tunda bance ko magani ya Fara karb'a ba " Alh maheer ne yaga Daddy yayi zuru shi kadai yace, " lafiya Abokina ?" Daddy tashi tsaye yayi yace, " mu dan je waje inasan magana da Kai " ba Musu ya tashi suka fice.

   _kachokan Shafin Nan sadaukarwa ne gareku TSANTSAR BUTULCI FANS kuyi yanda kuke so, yau baitin wak'a me dad'i nakeso a suburbud'omun ta comments da zafafan comments, jiyama banji comments da yawa ba Amma fa iya wuya inayinku._❤️

😭 *TSANTSAR BUTULCI*😭

                _AmeeraAdam60_
              *@Wattpad*

*SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFIYATA ALLAH YA KARA NISAN KWANA AMEEN.*

                  89&90

     _Not Edited_
      
      🔚🔚🔚🔚🔚🔚🔚

         Gaba d'aya Alh maheer ya saurari abinda Daddy ya sanar dashi jiki ba kwari ya kalli Daddy yace, " Kai Amma lamarin baiyi Dad'i ba insha Allah Zan samesu mu tattauna cikin ruwan sanyi ba tare da tashin hankali ba " Daddy yaji Dad'i kwarai yanda Abokin nasa ya fahimceshi dama Kuma yayi tsammanin haka, suna D'an jima suna hirarsu lokacin da suke niyyar shiga cikin Hall din sukaji har antashi, yan uwa da abokan arziki sai fitowa sukeyi, Yan uwan su Inna na Abuja mazan duk hotel suka je suka Kama Matan ne suka wuce tare dasu Inna har gida, dangin mahaifin Sadiq Dana Umma duk da yamman suka wuce gidajensu, agajiye su Farida suka koma gida kowa jiki yayi tsami musamman wad'anda suka Sha rawa suka mo re.

     Washe gari y'an uwan Inna suka tarkata gaba d'aya suka wuce cike da kewar Yar uwarsu cikin so da k'auna, Alh maheer ne kawai yace bazai wuce ba saboda Yanada abunyi sai Kuma Ammi da tace tana tare da Inna tare da ita zasu taho Abuja, bayan kowa ya watse Alh maheer ne ya samu Inna ad'akin ta cikin hikima ya zayyane Mata duk abinda Daddy ya sanar Masa, ba karamin tashin hankali Inna ta shiga ba, bawai Dan tana tunanin Farida na da cutar ba sai Dan fargabar su biyun suna da ita abun tashin hankali ne, bayan sun gama maganar Sadiq ya Kira yace Masa yanasan yayi magana dashi bayan azahar, Daddy ya Kira ya sanar dashi zasu zauna wajen k'arfe biyu na Rana da kwarin gwiwarsa yace wa Alh maheer zai karaso gidan kafin lokacin.

            Sadiq mamaki ne ya kamashi jin za'a zaune meeting tsakaninsa dasu Alh Maheer, zaune suke gaba ɗaya  afalon Umma kowa tsakanin umma, Farida da Sadiq kowa da abinda yake ayyanawa azuciyarsa, Alh maheer ne yayi sallama gaba ɗaya suka amsa sannan ya cigaba da cewa, " Maƙasudin wannan taro namu munyi sa ne dangane da wata muhimmiyar magana da Abokina kuma Amini gareni ya sanar dani jiya amma waƙa abakin me ita tafi daɗi zamuso suma suji daga gareka " Alh maheer ya ƙarasa maganar yana kallon Daddy.
      Gyaran murya Daddy yayi sannan ya zayyanawa su Inna duk abinda ya sani game da Saudat da kuma yanda sukayi da Sadiq a asibiti, yana gama jawabi Umma kuka tasa cikin takaici ta fara magana, " Amma Allah ya tsinewa wannan yarinya da ta cutar mun da ɗana guda tilo, yanxu shikenan Sadiq kanjamau gareka Allah ba azzalumin bawa ba gashi nan Allah ya wanke baiwarsa Farida, dama kace mun tun kafin ta haihu ka maida matarka to wallahi sakinta zakayi bazaka cuci yar mutane ba, kai wannan Saudat Allah ya isa tsakaninmu da ita " ta karasa maganar tana zubda kwallah me ciwo, Daddy yasan duk abinda Umma ta faɗa gaskiya ne kuma kowacce uwa zatayi hakan koma fiye da haka amma sam baiji daɗin irin tsinuwar da Umma take mata ba.
      A zabure Sadiq ya miƙe tsaye ya fara magana, " wallahi ni babu wanda zai sani insaki matata ni banida wata cutar HIV Allah umma ni ba mazinaci bane kinsan tarbiyyar da kika bani ko ke xaki iya shaida ta akan haka.... " Sadiq bai ƙarasa ba Umma ta ƙarɓe maganar da, " Sadiq idan kai baka kwaso muku ba ay gashi ita ta janyo muku masifa " Umma ta faɗa cikin takaici da tashin hankali.
       Daddy ne yace, " ku kwantar da hankalinku abi abin cikin ruwan sanyi kamar yanda muka fara, dan Saudat nada HIV bashi yake nuna shima Sadiq yana da ita ba har sai binciken likitoci ya bayyana mana hakan tukunna " sai yanxu hankalin Umma ya ɗan kwanta sannan Sadiq ya koma ya zauna.

           Farida tunda aka fara maganar hawaye take cikin zuciyarta take ayyana wai yaya Sadiq nada cuta me karya garkuwar jiki, duk ta ajiye Saudat awani shafi na daban bai hanata jin tausayinta ba musamman da sadiq ya gaya mata nakasar da ta samu a hannu, Inna ce tayi maganar sakin da Sadiq yayi wa Farida, Sadiq na shirin maganar Alh maheer ya rigashi, " wato dama tun kafin su taho an ɗanyi taƙaddama akan maganar sakin da tacewa su Ummi yayi mata, har munje mun samu malamai sunyi mana cikakken bayani akai da suka tabbatar mana da aurensu yana nan kuma koda da sakin ma tunda yace tuni yamaida matar sa ay kunga magana ta ƙare." Gaba ɗaya sun gamsu da bayanin Alh maheer sun ɗan jima suna tattaunawa daga ƙarshe Daddy, Sadiq da Alh maheer suka ɗunguma Sukayi Asibiti, Sadiq banda muzurai da ɓata Fuska ba abinda yakeyi har suka je asibiti babu wanda yacewa uffan acikinsu, jininsa aka ɗauka aka tafi dashi ɗakin gwaji bayan wani lokaci aka dawo musu da sakamako wanda ya tabbatar da bashi da wannan cuta me karya garkuwar jiki, sai lokacin Fuskar Sadiq ya ɗan murmusa daga asibitin Daddy ne ya buƙaci da su wuce domin sanar da dawowar Farida domin asan wane matsayi Saudat take dashi.

         Duk wani shige da fice Daddy ne yayi mata shi bayan wani lokaci alkali ya buƙaci da aƙara wani zaman domin tabbatar da hukunci ga Saudat haka zalika ya buƙaci ganin Farida azaman da za'ayi na gaba, Satin Farida uku ya kama shi ne ranar zaman kotu, Farida gaba ɗaya ji tayi bataji daɗin ganin Saudat ba sai taji gaba ɗaya tausayinta ya kamata, alkali ƴan tambayoyi ya yiwa Farida sannan ya yankewa Saudat hukuncin zaman gidan yari na shekara biyar da tarar kuɗi naira dubu ɗari biyu, lokacin da za'a tafi da Saudat farida ce ta ƙarasa gurunta idonta cike da hawaye, Saudat ita dinma hawayen ne a idonta cikin jin kunyar Farida ta fara neman gafarar Farida tare da karɓar Areef dake hannun Farida, cikin tausayawa  Farida tace, " na yafe miki tawan duniya da lahira " da mamaki Saudat ta kalli Farida tace, " Duk irin mugun abun dana ƙulla miki amma har yanxu sunana bai canja agurinki ba? " girgixa mata kai Farida tayi tana goge hawayen idonta tace, " bai canja ba kuma bazai taɓa camjawa ba har yanxu ke tawa ce Saudat " guntun tsaki Sadiq yaja ya ɗauke kai gefe, suna cikin haka aka musu maganar xa'a tafi da Saudat, miƙa Areef tayi ta juya gurun Daddy ta rungumeshi tana hawaye, ganin zata ɓatawa gandurobar lokaci ta figeta da ƙarfi aka wuce da ita shima Daddy na zubda kwalla, bayan wani lokaci tuni kotun ta watse har sukaje gida Farida na jimamin halin da Saudat take ciki.

         Mommy sosai take samu agurin Auntyn ghana musamman da ta kwantar da kanta tana yi mata aiki sosai ba algus, lokaci-lokaci take ziyartar Saudat tana kai mata ɗan abun arxiki.
      Satin Farida huɗu da haihuwa suka tarkata gaba ɗaya sukayi garin Abuja, Abba ne ya turo musu ya Asim ya kwashesu gaba ɗaya har Umma, Farin ciki gurun ahalin gidan baya musaltuwa ƴan uwa na nesa dana kusa sai xuwa suke gurin Inna ana hirar yaushe gamo, Umma tuni Tuni tayi sabo da su suma kuma suna karramata yanda ya kamata, Sadiq tunda yaji su Farida sun wuce Abuja ya shiga yi musu sunturi musamman da tayi arba'in, ko nisan tafiya bayaji daga lagos zuwa Abuja tun yana kara har ya fara yi musu jurwaye me kamar wanka gaba ɗaya nunawa sukayi kamar basusan me yake nufi ba, Wata rana Farida da  Ammi suna hira Amm ce tace, " ƴar nan ya naga kwana biyu kin zabge ne? " shafa wuyanta Farida tayi tace, " yoo Ammi ko su Areef ay sa sani rama " hararar wasa Ammi ta watsa mata tace, " ja'ira ni zaki wa hikima kicr dai gidan mijinki kike son komawa kawai " tsuke fuska Farida tayi cikin wasa tace, " to zan bar miki ɗakinki tunda abin naki yaxo da haka Ammi ku tsofaffun nan kun fiya zolaya " dariya Ammi tayi tace, " Shakiyiya kamar da gaske nan idan yaxo ba uwar kwalliya kike ci kije gurunsa ba " ganin Ammi da gaske take yasa Fareeda tace, " Eh kema ay gaki ga tsohon mijinki nima wannan karan ƙafa ta ƙafarsa, haka kawai kun riƙe masa mata sai sintiri kuke sashi " ta ƙarasa maganar tana ficewa daga ɗakin.
           Lokacin da Ammi tace wa su Umma idan Sadiq ya ƙara zuwa ya tafi da matarsa umma cewa tayi a'a shima aja masa aji dan Umma har lokacin idan ta tuno wulaƙancin da Sadiq yayiwa Farida tana jin haushi, sai da Abba yayi magana sannan Umma ta amince, aykuwa sati uku da yin maganar da yayi wani zuwa Aka shirya farida da kayanta dana su Areef suka wuce murna gurin Sadiq bata musaltuwa, kwanansu uku da zuwa yasa aka nemo masa me aiki wacce zata dinga taya Farida rainon su Irfan.

          Watan farida biyu da tafiya Daddy ya sauka garin Abuja, har gida yaje ya samu abokinsa Alh Maheer bayan sun gaisa Alh Maheer yace, " Abokina ɓatan hanya kayi yaushe rabonka da Abuja ? " Daddy dariya yayi yace, " Ay wannan zuwan da kaga nayi na musamman ne matar da zan aura na biyo " dariya Alh Maheer yayi yace, " kai mutumina kace ɗankwali yaja hula " ya ƙarasa maganar yana kallan Daddy, Daddy shima dariyar yayi sannan yace, " da gaske nake wallahi ƙanwarka Sadiya na biyo mahaifiyar Farida " murmushi Alh maheer yayi cikin zolaya yace, " wato akaro ba biyu ma bazan samu Sadiya ba kenan, mutumin nan kamun shigar sauri ya na iya dole inbar maka " Daddy cikin farin ciki yace, " dama cen ba rabonka bace " gaba ɗaya suka kwashe da dariya.
       Alh maheer shi yayiwa Daddy iso har gurunsu Abba sannan aka saka Inna cikin maganar da farko ƙin amincewa tayi amma da tayi tunanin abinda ya wuce abaya sai ta amince ko dan ta faranta wa su Ammi rai, wata guda aka sa komai, da komai su Farida sun samu labari sunyi kuma farin ciki da jin haka musamman da sukan wanda Inna zata aura da ƙyawawan halinsa, lokaci nayi aka sha biki duk da ba wani shagali akayi ba sai ƴan uwa da suka hallara lokacin da za'a kai amarya harda su Umma aka tarkato aka yo garin kano, yawancin baƙin Abuja agurun Umma suka sauka washe gari kowa ya watse dama farida bata biyo ƴan kawo Inna ba Sadiq ne ya hana yace zai kawo ta kanshi suyi zuwa na musamman, gida sai Umma ita kaɗai saboda ƙaɗaici harda hawayenta gaba ɗaya gidan yayi mata iri ba daɗi, Farida ce ta matsawa Sadiq tace amaida Umma lagos da suka je mata da maganar ƙin amincewa tayi tace idan ya dawo kano da aiki ta amince zata koma gidan nasa da zama.

       _*BAYAN SHEKARA ƊAYA DA RABI*_

         Lokacin tuni Farida ta yaye su Areef suna ta gudunsu ko'ina gwanin sha'awa har Sadiq ya gama aikin da aka turasu lagos ya dawo kano, katafaren gida ya gina a unguwar gadon ƙaya musamman aka warewa Umma sashenta ta koma cikin farin ciki zamansu da Farida gwanin sha'awa kamar ba sirika da matar ɗa ba.
        Inna ce ɗauke da tsohon ciki haihuwa ko yau ko gobe, Daddy siyayya yake harda ta banza kowa zuba kunnuwa yake yaji haihuwar Inna musamman su Ammi da suke cike da farinciki, ana cikin haka Inna ta haifo kyakykyawan jaririnta ɗa namiji me kama sak da Daddy, fasalta murna gurin Daddy abune mara musaltuwa ranar suna yaro yaci suna Muhammad anyi shagali sosai ƴan uwa da abin arxiki sun hallara wajen taya Inna murna, lokacin da Saudat ta samu labari ba ƙaramin murna tayi ba, abu ɗaya ne yake damunta shi ne rashin sanin abinda ya raba auren daddynta da mommy duk wanda ta tambaya sai dai yace mata haka Allah ya hukunta, Inna ta murje tayi kyau ta ƙara ƙiba kamar ba ita ba.

         _*BAYAN SHEKARA SHIDA*_

        A wannan lokacin Farida yaranta huɗu, Areef&Irfan sai Aneesa me shekara guɗu da Inteesar wata shida, Farida tayi ƙiba ta canja sosai Sadiq ya ƙara samun matsayi agurin aiki yayinda Inna keda yara uku Muhammad, Fatima sai Ansar, tuni Saudat ta fito daga gidan yari kuma Daddy da kansa ya taho da ita gidansa suna zaune lafiya da su Inna tun tana jin nauyi da kunyar su Farida ta sake jiki dasu, har cikin zuciyarta take jin ƙaunar ƴaƴan Farida da sauran ƙannanta, wani mutumi ta haɗu dashi agurin karɓar magani sun daidaita shi matansa uku yana ɗan rufin asirinsa, manyansa ya turo neman auranta gurin Daddy, sai da Daddy yasa aka bincika masa halinsa sannan aka tsaida lokaci aka sha biki su Farida sune manyan ƙawayen Amarya ta tare a gidansa dake karkasara.

       Da farko sadiq ko gaisheshi Saudat tayi baya amsawa sai da Farida tayi mai nasiha sannan yake amsa gaisuwarta sama-sama, wata rana Saudat ta dawo daga awo ta zarce ta gidan Farida sunsha hira sosai suna zaune taga farida ta ɗauko wani abu a jarka tana sha, kallanta Saudat tayi tace, " meye wannan ɗin nima abani " harara Farida ta watsa mata tace, " dallah ke yanxu me zaki da wannan ki bari ki sauka lafiya kigani  " suna cikin hirar Sadiq ya shigo gaisheshi Saudat tayi ba laifi ya amsa mata ba yabo ba fallasa har yana tambayarta ya me gidan, har zai wuce ciki Farida tace, " Dear wai nikam ina ajiyata da nace maka ta Saudat ce " murmushi yayi yace, " tana nan biyoni ki karɓar ta " da gudu Farida tabi bayansa har ɗakinsa yaje ta ɗauko wani ɗan mitsitsin kit tana zuwa ta miƙawa Suudat sannan tace mata karta buɗe sai taje gida, godiya Saudat ta fara jero mata da harara farida tabita, Tashi Saudat tayi zata tafi lokacin Sadiq ya fito Farida tace, " Dear muje muyi mata rakiya ko " da har zai buɗe baki Farida kifta masa ido har gate suka raka Saudat tana kallansu cikin birgewa da ban sha'awa, suna dawowa Su Muhammad ne suka fito daga sashen Umma da gudu Inteesar da taho gurin Farida ta ɗauketa itama Aneesa ganin haka ta tafi gurin Sadiq da gudu, riƙe mata hannu yayi suka ƙarasa ciki gaba ɗaya, shigarsu keda wuya kira ya shigo wayar Farida, Rukayya ce ke kiranta da sallama Farida ta ɗauka, daga cen ɓangaren Ruƙayya tace, "  matar soja kinyi ɗa Rumaisa ta haihu " cikin murna Farida tace, " Allah da gaske maman humaira " guntun tsaki Ruƙayya taja tace, " ke nifa daɗina dake iskanci zan miki ƙarya ne ta haifi ɗanta baby boy ko awa ɗaya ba'ayi ba " ko kashe wayar batayi ba ta kwalla ƙara cikin murna tana maƙale Sadiq dake gefe, da ido ya bita yana murmushi sai da ta gama yace mata, " wai ke bakya girma be bakya ganin kin aje su Areef " cikin Farin ciki tace, " eh man duka-duka nawa nake wallahi murna ce tasa haka, kasan fa ashekara ta bakwai suke da Aure sai yanxu Allah ya kawo ba dole nai murna " cikin kwaikwayon muryarta Sadiq yace, " eh ni kuma sai ki nemi ki karya ni ko? " kafin tayi magana Areef yace, " Daddy ay sojoji karfi garesu, Mommy kuwa ba soja bace nasan ka fita ƙarfi dan zaka iya ƙarya ta biyu" gaba ɗaya suka sa dariya suna mamakin surutu irin nasu Areef.

       _TAMMAT BIHAMDULILLAH_😁

      _ALHAMDULILLAH ANAN NA KAWO KARSHEN WANNAN LITTAFI NA TSANTSAR BUTULCI KURA-KUREN CIKI ALLAH YA YAFE MUN, ABINDA MUKA JI NA ƊAUKA ALLAH YA BAMU IKON AMFANI DASU, NASAN ANSAMU ƳAN KUSKURE ACIKI INA FATAN ZA'AMUN AFUWA MUSAMMAN TA FANNIN EDITING😄 DA ƘINYIN TYPING AKAI-AKAI._

     *GODIYA TA MUSAMMAN GAREKU MASOYANA MASOYAN TSANTSAR BUTULCI INA ALFAHARI DA JIN DAƊI MUSAMNAN YANDA KUKA DINGA NUNA MUN KULAWA BA GAJIYAWA HAKA NAN BA DARE BA RANA KUNA BIBIYAR ABINDA NAKE RUBUTAWA KUNA KARANTAWA KUMA KUNA NUNAMUN JIN DADƊINKU, INA GODIYA KWARAI ALLAH YA BAR SO DA KYAU INAYINKU 100% HANNU BAZAI IYA LISSAFAKU BA SHIYASA NAYI JIMMILAI NAKE MIƘO MUKU GODIYATA AJIMLACE.*

          _ADUNIYAR WATTPAD BAN MANTA DAKU  BISA GA NUNA JINJINARKU DA KULAWARKU GARENI ALLAH YA BARMU TARE CIKIN SO DA ƘAUNA._

      _ƘUNGIYATA ABAR ALFAHARINA TA *FIRST CLASS WRITERS* ALLAH YA ƘARA ƊAUKAKA UBANGIJI YA ƘARA HAƊA KAWUBANMU INA JI DAKU IRIN SOSAI ƊIN NAN KUNA TAFIYA CIKIN ƘASAITA INA BINKU DA TURARE ALHERIN ALLAH YA KAI MUKU ADUK INDA KUKE._🤓

       TO FA ƳAN UWA SAI MU JONE A SABBUN LITTAFAI NA MASU ZUWA AMMA GA ƊANƊANO DAGA CIKINSU. 😎


*بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم*

*D'AN BA K'ARA........*👹
            ( _gajeren labari ne_)

                  1&2

           " Mejidda mejidda wai kina Ina sai kwala Miki Kira nake kin shanyani daga d'aukan kallabi tuni kin shafe lokaci har na Fara gajiya, ko so kike mu rasa motar  kina sane dai yau juma'a ga Rana da d'aga " goggo larai ce ke tsaye da shirinta da alama fita zasuyi, mejidda ce ta karaso mayafinta ahannu tana kokarin d'aura D'an kwali, cikin mita da tsiwa take yiwa goggo larai magana, " haba goggo ke kincika raki duk kibi ki tayiwa mutane sanabe muje ni ko a soro na sa hijabin " rike haba goggo larai tayi cikin takaicin abinda mejidda tayi Mata tayi kwafa tace, " Allah ya shiryaki Y'ar Nan kinji Allah ya nunamun ranar da zanga auranki, mejidda kinsan dai banasan laifinki Amma ni meyasa bakya shayin fad'a mun magana ne? " Mejidda ko ta Kan goggo batabi ba ta cigaba da sabgar gabanta.

      Har sunje kofa suka ji me kifi na talla da sauri goggo ta tsaidashi, har ya kusa Shan kwana ya dawo guda uku aka zubawa goggo suka Fara ciniki da ita, sunyita ciniki ita da me kifi har suka karkare a dari hudu da hamsin, goggo har ta zuge kajarta ( _D'an aljihun da akewa tsofaffi ajikin sket dinsu na ciki_ ) ta ciro kud'i tuni me kifi ya yanka kifi Yana Shirin zubawa acikin leda aykuwa mejidda tai karaf tace, " goggo wannan mitsi-mitsin kifin Zaki siya dari hudu da hamsin wallahi ya daki hancinki wannan kifin da Bai wuce d'ari da hamsin ba, chabdi lallai ma kudin ne sukayi Miki yawa"

     Aykuwa da sauri me kifi ya kulleta Yana watsa Mata harara, goggo jin kalaman mejidda tuni ta hau ta zauna ya mutsa fuska gogg tayi sannan tace, " Kai yaro jeka da kifinka wallahi na fasa siya tunda mejidda tace yayi tsada idan munje rumfar D'an Tani ma saya inajin nashi sunfi wannan arha dama ajiyeshi zanyi sai yamma idan mun dawo, kila ma ragowar na jiya ne Kar naje ya buga kafin mu dawo, Dan yanda naga wannan jagulallen kifin Nan da alama kwantai ne" me kifi sakin Baki yayi Yana kallan goggo larai, tuni me jidda ta karb'e zancen, " kema goggo da wayace ki tsaidashi sai wani zubawa mutane ido yake kamar mujiya, ni irin dama masu tallan kifin Nan ban yarda da kifinsu ba, gaskiya mu wuce Kar muje arufe hanyar masallaci kinsan dai basa d'aga k'afa " goggo najin Haka ta yi gaba me jidda da mukulli ahannunta tasa Masa key ta rufe sukabar me kifi atsaye da yankakken kifi a hannu mejidda harda bangazar robar kifinsa da gefen hannunsa bisa tsautsayi kifin hannunsa rabi ya zube tayi gaba abinta ko ajikinta.

    Su Inna na zuwa titi suka tari bus din da zata kaisu Rimi asibiti, me jidda akan maleji ta zauna goggo na kujera sannan suka tafi, bayan wani lokaci motar dasu goggo suka hau ta cika makil har maleji, fad'a ne ya tirke tsakanin  me jidda da wata Yarinya ba kunya goggo ta shigar Mata, saboda ko kadan goggo batasan laifin ta Dan  mahaifi da mahaifiyar me jidda sun rasu shiyasa ta taso a hannun goggo asangarce ba kwab'a sai abinda tayi niyya shi take yi, kuma komai tayi goggo bazata Mata fad'a ko tsawatarwa ba, har suka K'arasa goggo nata masifa gidan babban D'an goggo sukaje, shi ma Kuma dama me jidda ce ta damu sai sunje saboda shima kawu haruna mutum ne bame fad'a ba akwai shi da sanyin hali shiyasa tasu tazo d'aya da me jidda.

        Su goggo basu suka dawo ba Sai yamma likis, goggo na bud'e kofa wasu y'an Mata suka k'araso da sauri gurinta kowa da karar Daya kawo Mata ta mejidda, tsuke fuska tayi ta zage su tass sannan ta koresu, haushin goggo ne ya kamasu suka tafi suna gunguni suna watsawa mejidda mugun kallo, sanin halin mejidda ba kanwar lasa bace shiyasa yasuyi gigin tab'a ta ba, Amma yanda suke Jin haushin mejidda yaci su nad'a mata dukan tsiya.

      Bayan sun shiga cikin gidan goggo tabarma ta shinfid'a tana maida numfashin gajiya, mejidda ma gefen Goggo ta kwanta ta d'ora kanta akan cinyar Goggo.
            Karni-karnin kifi Goggo ke ji Dan waige takeyi tana neman inda zataji yake karnin, bayan sun huta goggo tashi tayi zata d'ora Musu girki tana d'auko tukunya ta bud'e sai ganin yankakken kifi tayi aciki tana kallan kwayar idan d'aya ya kirfta Mata ido.

     _Ummou Aslam Bint Adam_😉

       👸🏻 *GIMBIYA FADEELA*👸🏻

*Written By:-*
    _Ameera Adam_
                _AmeeraAdam60_
              *@Wattpad*

             *_Gargad'i 👂🏻👈🏼 Ban yarda acanja mun littafina ta kowacce siga ba batare da izinin ba._*

*_SADAUKARWA GA MAHAIFINA ALLAH YA KARA GIRMA DA NISAN KWANA AMEEN_.*❤️

                      1&2


         Katafaren falo ne na Alfarma Wanda ya k'ayatu da kayan Ado na alatu Wanda yasha zubi da tsarin da ya danganci b'angaren Sarauta, kujeru ne zagaye da falon da wasu luntsuma-luntsuman kushin da aka ajiyesu guru-guri ta ko'ina kamshi ne yake tashi acikin falon, wata kyakyawar Mata ce zaune da tsohon cikinta me kimanin wata bakwai sanye take cikin kayan alfarma na Alkyabba, shekarunta gaba d'aya bazasu wuce talatin da takwas ba zuwa Arba'in, zaune take Kan luntsumemen carpet ta mik'e kafafuwan ta da suka D'an kumbura, gefen hagu da damanta wasu y'an Mata ne guda biyu ashekaru basufi Sha Takwas ba zuwa Ashirin, kowaccen su matsa Mata kafafuwa take yi lokaci-lokaci suna cewa, " Sunnu da hutawa ranki shi dad'e " uffan Bata ce Musu  sai ta ga dama ne take gyad'a Musu Kai ta cigaba da sabgar gabanta.

            Wani kwando ne ajiye agefenta cike yake da kayan marmari, lokaci-lokaci take d'auka tana ci tana Danna wayarta, suna Nan zaune wata baiwar ce ta shigo cikin falon da sallama hannunta d'auke da faranti anjera jug da kofunansa, sai da ta rankwafa ta gaida gimbiya Haleema sannan ta koma gefe ta ajiye wannan faranti tana Kara duk'awa cikin girmamawa tace, " Allah ya taimakeki ya Kara Miki lafiya Allah ya kawo yarima cikin k'oshin lafiya, Allah tsone idan makiyanki Ranki shi dad'e ko akwai abinda ake da buk'ata? " Girgiza Kai gimbiya Haleema tayi baiwar ta tashi tana cewa, " na barki lafiya uwar d'akina " Bata amsa Mata ba har ta fita dama batayi tsammanin Haka ba tunda dama wannan halinta ne.

       Kallan y'an Matan da ke yi Mata tausa tayi tace, " ku tashi haka " ta fad'a atakaice, tashi sukayi cikin girmamawa suka ce, " mun barki lafiya " wannan Karan ma Bata amsa ba Bata Kuma kalli gurin da suke ba har suka fice Daga falon.

     Suna fita d'aya Daga cikinsu ce me suna Ramlat ta tsuke fuska akausashe ta kalli d'ayar tace, " Naga zak'ewar na neman wuce gona da iri ka taka asannu fa....... " D'ayar me suna Rahina itama azafafe tace, " karkace mun komai na fahimci abun naka ya Fara canja salo karka manta jiya ka so kayi ka zameta ta Fad'i badan Ina gurun ba shikenan " Ramlat cikin halin ko inkula tace, " to Kai da kake zak'ewa inace har yanxu ba'a gayawa Wanda aka sadaukarwa ba " da k'arfin gaske Ramlat ta bige bakin Rahina lokaci d'aya suka bace Daga gurin suka sauka atsakiyar wani daji cikin mummunar halitta, siffarsu kamar ta barewa take sai dai fukarsu irin ta karnuka ce, sai kahonhuna guda biyu akansu, hannuwa garesu irin na mutane kowanne su Yana da hannu guda hurhudu, Daga gadon bayansu zuwa gurun jelarsu gashi garesu irin na raguna sai kafafuwansu irin na doki, bakinsu dogo me tsinin gaske, kaurewa sukayi da matsanancin fad'a sai da  suka shafe lokaci sunayi sannan suka saki juna rijib suka fado cikin gajiya kowanne Yana maida numfashi.

      Bayan sun huta Ramlat ta kalli Rahina cikin wani irin hagitsatstsen yarensu ta Fara magana, "( harzogabashin martakubata faltaksikarunlamuha chakuskurani mukulafutis garujakab mukulasgisahugurjabtikasu kalbifatibsmhu farkusajika minsakilchuchakishi )" Wallahi sai dai duk abinda zakayi kayi Amma ni Nan nine Zan d'aukarwa baba fansar abinda akayi masa, da asadaukarmun ita da Kar asadaukarmun da ita alwashi naci Kuma babu Wanda ya isa ya hanani " azafafe Rahina tace, "( chakusaki minsalifawu kaskisisiyaki mulfasukawufai chuchakusha kalbis miyahu firsashi mulhu tusbashikulsisi musakki wasfifitakasu minsaligati Jessiijba )" wallahi baka isaba idan Kuma kace tanan zaka b'ullo sai mu zuba mu gani, kasan dai ba wannan alkarin muka d'auka ba ko? " Wagegen bakinsa Ramlat ta bud'e tace, " ( hasiwasifasimasi kaskuskisawosi marsakisahu giskasit kalmansikaru katriksilamu walmurkisu munsalsatufikasu yunkasufuwut paput papiksmati )" Nima bance Zan cutar da ita ba Amma tabbas Zan dauki Fansa, Abu na gaba da Zan Gaya maka duk abinda zamuyi mu bar yinsa cikin masarautarsu sai mun dawo tamu " Rahina Bata kula Ramlat ba rikid'a ta koma Y'ar matashiyar budurwar nan itama Rahina rikid'a tayi suka koma cen cikin masarautar zazzau cikin sauran bayi.
